/* ============================================================
   COMBAT CAPS & TUNABLES
   ============================================================ */
const CAPS = { hp: 240, dmg: 40, def: 70, spd: 4 };
const COMBAT = {
  baseCrit: 0.08,
  critMult: 1.7,        // crit damage multiplier (was 2.0; reduced to slow lethality)
  rngSpread: 0.5,       // ±50% damage roll
  hitRange: 18,         // pixels — melee range
  // === v28: Melee/ranged miss + crit constants ===
  // Every attack now has a chance to miss outright (separate from spread):
  // melee uses meleeMissBase scaled by speed advantage of defender; ranged
  // uses rangedMissAtMaxRange linearly scaled by (distance / weapon.range).
  meleeMissBase: 0.06,           // 6% baseline melee miss
  meleeMissPerSpdDelta: 0.05,    // each unit of defender_spd over attacker adds 5% miss
  rangedMissAtZeroRange: 0.02,   // point-blank
  rangedMissAtMaxRange: 0.40,    // edge-of-range
  // Crit applies to both melee and ranged; existing stats.crit + weapon.critMod
  // already define per-attacker chance, but we also clamp the combined number.
  critClampMin: 0.03,
  critClampMax: 0.55,
  // Weapon switching: when an enemy enters melee range and the bot is
  // currently wielding a ranged weapon, swap to a melee fallback profile.
  // Distance below which the swap triggers.
  meleeSwitchRange: 60,
  // === end v28 constants ===
  arenaW: 1280,         // 16:9 field, wider than v28 (960) so spawning teams
  arenaH: 720,          // can't shoot across the field at start. Spawn-to-spawn
  arenaSize: 1280,      // distance is 1080px (was 760px in v28); even at sniper
                        // max range (460px) bots must close ~620px before
                        // they're in firing range of the opposing spawn line.
  frameMs: 50,          // sim tick at 1×
  roundMaxFrames: 1500, // safety cap on a single ROUND (longer rounds need more)
  matchMaxFrames: 1500, // alias kept for back-compat
  // Armor formula: damage_taken = damage * (1 - def/(def + DEF_DENOM))
  // Lower DEF_DENOM = armor matters more. 35 is meaningfully tougher than 50.
  defDenom: 35,
  cdMult: 1.20,
  spawnRadius: 580,     // was 540 in v28.2; pushed further toward walls per
                        // v28.4 user request "bots need to start farther back."
                        // Team A spawn line at x=60, team B at x=1220, with
                        // ±6 jitter and 12px world-edge clamp this gives a
                        // comfortable ~40px clear of the wall for sprites.
};

/* ============================================================
   MATCH STRUCTURE — best-of-N rounds + lives per match
   Each "match" in the bracket (semifinal, final) is best-of-5
   rounds. Each round is a 5v5 elimination fight. Knights have
   3 lives per match — a round-loss costs them one. Run out of
   lives and they die permanently, dropping their gear.
   ============================================================ */
const MATCH = {
  bestOf: 5,           // number of rounds total
  roundsToWin: 3,      // first-to-N (must be ceil(bestOf/2))
  startingLives: 3,    // each player gets this many lives per match
  interRoundDelay: 0,  // milliseconds (0 = pause for user input)
};

/* ============================================================
   EQUIPMENT — weapons, armor, items
   Each player carries a loadout {weapon, armor, item}. When they
   die, the loadout drops at their corpse and surviving players
   may scavenge it. Picked-up gear is renamed
     "<owner>'s <gear name> (scavenged)"
   and persists with that name (cascading if scavenged again).
   ============================================================ */

// Each weapon defines combat modifiers and reach. `range` 0-30 = melee
// instant-hit; >30 = projectile-based ranged. `projSpeed` = px/frame for
// the projectile. `inacc` = baseline aim cone in radians (more = wilder).
//
// === v28 weapon rebalance ===
// Ranged weapons gained damage and inaccuracy; melee weapons stayed
// average-damage but their existing reliability translates well in the
// new combat math (new miss-roll layered on top of inaccuracy).
// Sniper railgun is now the highest-dmg highest-crit lowest-fire-rate
// option, properly anchoring the glass-cannon archetype.

/* Melee fallback profiles — used by AI's MELEE state when a ranged-weapon
   bot is being swarmed. We don't actually swap inventories (that would
   confuse the scavenging system); we just use one of these profiles for
   the resolveAttack call when the bot is in MELEE state.
   pickMeleeFallback() runs at match start, deterministic via SIM_RNG so
   reseeded matches reproduce exactly. */
const MELEE_FALLBACKS = [
  { id: "_fb_punch",  ico: "👊", name: "Bare Knuckles",   dmgMod: -2, defMod: 0, cdMod: -0.10, critMod: 0,     stun: 0,    range: 18, projSpeed: 0, inacc: 0 },
  { id: "_fb_dagger", ico: "🗡", name: "Belt Dagger",     dmgMod:  0, defMod: 0, cdMod: -0.05, critMod: +0.04, stun: 0,    range: 20, projSpeed: 0, inacc: 0 },
  { id: "_fb_kick",   ico: "🦵", name: "Boot Kick",       dmgMod: -1, defMod: 0, cdMod:  0,    critMod: 0,     stun: 0.05, range: 18, projSpeed: 0, inacc: 0 },
];
function pickMeleeFallback(p) {
  // Deterministic per (matchSeed, player.id). We use a hash so changing
  // player order doesn't reshuffle assignments.
  const h = ((p.id || 0) * 2654435761) >>> 0;
  return MELEE_FALLBACKS[h % MELEE_FALLBACKS.length];
}

// "passive" (active for whole match), "lowHP" (fires once when HP <30%).

// "Score" a piece of gear so the AI can compare its current piece to
// a scavengable upgrade. Higher = better for that slot.
// Crucially, this is now POSITIONAL — a Sniper values a Railgun more than
// a stronger-on-paper weapon they don't know how to use, so we don't get
// every survivor swapping to the absolute strongest piece.
function gearScore(slot, gear, ownerPosition) {
  if (!gear) return 0;
  if (slot === "weapon") {
    let s = gear.dmgMod * 2.5 + gear.defMod * 1.2 + (-gear.cdMod) * 6 + gear.critMod * 80 + gear.stun * 25;
    if (ownerPosition && gear.flavorPos === ownerPosition) s += 5;
    if (ownerPosition === "sniper" && gear.range > 200) s += 4;
    if (ownerPosition === "speed"  && gear.range > 100) s += 2;
    if ((ownerPosition === "tank" || ownerPosition === "brawl") && gear.range < 50) s += 3;
    return s;
  } else if (slot === "armor") {
    // Armor is now MORE valuable — defMod weighted 2.5x (was 1.5), spdMod 8x.
    // This is paired with the new defDenom=35 making each point of def
    // meaningfully reduce damage, so the AI's preference reflects actual impact.
    let s = gear.defMod * 2.5 + gear.spdMod * 8 + gear.critMod * 80;
    if (ownerPosition && gear.flavorPos === ownerPosition) s += 5;
    // Position-specific armor preferences
    if (ownerPosition === "tank" && gear.defMod >= 8) s += 4;   // tanks LOVE heavy armor
    if (ownerPosition === "speed" && gear.spdMod > 0) s += 4;   // speedsters love mobility
    if (ownerPosition === "sniper" && gear.critMod > 0) s += 3; // snipers love crit boost
    return s;
  } else if (slot === "item") {
    return 5;
  }
  return 0;
}

// Equip gear on a player and stamp the scavenged name if applicable.
// `gear` is a clone of a base WEAPON/ARMOR/ITEM definition with optional
// `displayName` and `originalOwner` fields when scavenged.
function gearDisplayName(gear) {
  if (!gear) return "";
  return gear.displayName || gear.name;
}

/* ============================================================
   PLAYER FACTORY (updated)
   Each new player spawns with a loadout matching their position by
   default — Tanks get Riot Shields & Plate Cuirass, Snipers get
   Railguns & Stealth Cloaks, etc. Items are randomized for variety.
   ============================================================ */
let _idCounter = 1;

function rollBaseStats() {
  return {
    hp:  RI(140, 200),  // was 70-120 — doubled for ~45s rounds
    dmg: RI(8, 22),
    def: RI(8, 30),     // was 5-25 — slightly higher floor
    spd: +(R(1.4, 2.6).toFixed(2)),
  };
}

function makePlayer(teamId, position) {
  const base = rollBaseStats();
  const bias = position.bias;
  const stats = {
    hp:  clamp(base.hp + bias.hp, 30, CAPS.hp),
    dmg: clamp(base.dmg + bias.dmg, 4, CAPS.dmg),
    def: clamp(base.def + bias.def, 0, CAPS.def),
    spd: clamp(+(base.spd + bias.spd).toFixed(2), 0.6, CAPS.spd),
    crit: clamp(COMBAT.baseCrit + bias.crit, 0.02, 0.30),
  };
  // Default loadout by position (with a fresh cloned object so scavenged
  // copies can be renamed without mutating the catalog).
  const defaultWeapon = WEAPONS.find(w => w.flavorPos === position.id) || WEAPONS[0];
  const defaultArmor  = ARMORS.find(a => a.flavorPos === position.id)  || ARMORS[0];
  const randomItem    = pick(ITEMS);
  return {
    id: _idCounter++,
    name: genFullName(),
    teamId,
    position: position.id,
    stats,
    // Loadout — start with default gear for the position
    weapon: { ...defaultWeapon },
    armor:  { ...defaultArmor },
    item:   { ...randomItem },
    // Career stats (persist across matches/seasons)
    career: { kills: 0, deaths: 0, matchesPlayed: 0, matchesWon: 0, seasonsPlayed: 0, scavenged: 0, longestStreak: 0, currentStreak: 0 },
    // Mortality
    deceased: false,
    deceasedSeason: null,
    deceasedBy: null,
    debutSeason: 1,
    // Match-time fields (reset per match)
    x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0,
    hp: stats.hp,
    alive: true,
    lives: MATCH.startingLives, // per-match lives (resets at match start)
    target: null,
    stunned: 0,        // frames remaining stunned (skip attacks)
    itemUsed: false,   // whether the consumable has fired this match
    boostMods: null,   // {dmg, spd, life} active boost from item
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   LEAGUE STATE — top-level game state
   ============================================================ */
const LEAGUE = {
  season: 1,
  bits: 0,
  // Roster: { fox: [5 players], bvr: [5 players], owl: [5 players], shk: [5 players] }
  rosters: {},
  // Permanent graveyard: array of deceased players (never reaped)
  graveyard: [],
  // Team records: { teamId: { wins, losses, championships } }
  records: {},
  // Bracket for current season:
  // { semi1: {teamA, teamB, winner|null, kills}, semi2: {...}, final: {...} }
  bracket: null,
  // Match in progress (live state during a fight)
  match: null,
  // Active match index in bracket order: "semi1" | "semi2" | "final" | null
  currentSlot: null,
  // History of past seasons: [{season, champion, runnerup, casualties}]
  history: [],
  // Active bets (cleared after each match resolves)
  bets: [],
  // Shop boosts pending: { teamId: { dmg, def, spd, ... } } — applied next match
  pendingBoosts: {},
  // === v28.1: per-knight targeted boosts (Personal Sandbag etc.) ===
  // { teamId: { knightId: { spd, dmg, ... } } } — applied next match, per-knight.
  pendingTargetedBoosts: {},
};

function initLeague(opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  // Genesis seed: stable across this league's lifetime. If supplied (e.g. for
  // tests / replay), use that. Otherwise generate a random one — the only
  // place real Math.random affects sim outcomes. Once set, persists in saves.
  LEAGUE.genesisSeed = opts.genesisSeed || ((Math.random() * 0xFFFFFFFF) >>> 0);
  // Reseed the sim RNG NOW so all setup randomness (rosters, names, etc.)
  // is derived from genesisSeed deterministically.
  SIM_RNG.reseed(LEAGUE.genesisSeed);

  for (const team of TEAMS) {
    LEAGUE.rosters[team.id] = POSITIONS.map(p => makePlayer(team.id, p));
    LEAGUE.records[team.id] = { wins: 0, losses: 0, draws: 0, championships: 0 };
  }
  LEAGUE.season = 1;
  LEAGUE.bits = 0;
  LEAGUE.graveyard = [];
  LEAGUE.history = [];
  LEAGUE.matchHistory = [];
  LEAGUE.lostItems = [];
  LEAGUE.bets = [];
  LEAGUE.pendingBoosts = {};
  LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts = {};
  LEAGUE.startedAt = Date.now();   // when this league was first booted (immutable)
  LEAGUE.lastTickAt = Date.now();  // wall-clock of most recent sim activity (updated each frame)
  buildBracket();
}

/* ============================================================
   BRACKET SCHEDULER — random seeding each season.
   Supports three formats (set via LEAGUE_CONFIG.bracketSize):
     4  → semi1, semi2, final
     8  → qf1..qf4, sf1, sf2, final
     12 → ql1..ql4 (qualifier round, 8 teams), then qf1..qf4, sf1, sf2, final
          (Top 4 ranked teams get byes; bottom 8 play qualifiers.)
   The bracket is stored as an ordered list of slots so advanceBracket()
   can step through them generically.
   ============================================================ */
function buildBracket() {
  const size = (LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG.bracketSize) || 4;
  const teams = shuffle(TEAMS.map(t => t.id));
  LEAGUE.bracket = { _order: [] };

  if (size === 4) {
    // Pick 4 random teams from the league
    const chosen = teams.slice(0, 4);
    LEAGUE.bracket.semi1 = { teamA: chosen[0], teamB: chosen[1], winner: null, casualties: [], depends: null };
    LEAGUE.bracket.semi2 = { teamA: chosen[2], teamB: chosen[3], winner: null, casualties: [], depends: null };
    LEAGUE.bracket.final = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["semi1","semi2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket._order = ["semi1", "semi2", "final"];
  } else if (size === 8) {
    const chosen = teams.slice(0, 8);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      LEAGUE.bracket["qf" + (i+1)] = { teamA: chosen[i*2], teamB: chosen[i*2+1], winner: null, casualties: [], depends: null };
    }
    LEAGUE.bracket.sf1 = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["qf1","qf2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.sf2 = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["qf3","qf4"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.final = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["sf1","sf2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket._order = ["qf1","qf2","qf3","qf4","sf1","sf2","final"];
  } else if (size === 12) {
    // Top 4 by championships (then by wins) get byes; bottom 8 play qualifiers
    const ranked = TEAMS.slice().sort((a, b) => {
      const ra = LEAGUE.records[a.id] || { championships: 0, wins: 0 };
      const rb = LEAGUE.records[b.id] || { championships: 0, wins: 0 };
      return (rb.championships - ra.championships) || (rb.wins - ra.wins);
    }).map(t => t.id);
    const byeTeams = ranked.slice(0, 4);
    const qualifierTeams = shuffle(ranked.slice(4));
    // 4 qualifier matches → 4 winners join the 4 byes for the 8-team ladder
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      LEAGUE.bracket["ql" + (i+1)] = { teamA: qualifierTeams[i*2], teamB: qualifierTeams[i*2+1], winner: null, casualties: [], depends: null, isQualifier: true };
    }
    // Quarterfinals: each bye-team paired with one qualifier winner
    LEAGUE.bracket.qf1 = { teamA: byeTeams[0], teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["ql1"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.qf2 = { teamA: byeTeams[1], teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["ql2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.qf3 = { teamA: byeTeams[2], teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["ql3"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.qf4 = { teamA: byeTeams[3], teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["ql4"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.sf1 = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["qf1","qf2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.sf2 = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["qf3","qf4"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket.final = { teamA: null, teamB: null, winner: null, casualties: [], depends: ["sf1","sf2"] };
    LEAGUE.bracket._order = ["ql1","ql2","ql3","ql4","qf1","qf2","qf3","qf4","sf1","sf2","final"];
  }

  LEAGUE.currentSlot = LEAGUE.bracket._order[0];
}

function advanceBracket() {
  // Step through the bracket order, populating teams from dependencies.
  const order = LEAGUE.bracket._order || ["semi1", "semi2", "final"];
  const idx = order.indexOf(LEAGUE.currentSlot);
  if (idx === -1 || idx + 1 >= order.length) {
    LEAGUE.currentSlot = null;
    return;
  }
  LEAGUE.currentSlot = order[idx + 1];
  // Resolve dependencies: pull team IDs from earlier slots' winners
  const slot = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
  if (slot.depends) {
    if (slot.teamA == null && slot.depends[0]) slot.teamA = LEAGUE.bracket[slot.depends[0]].winner;
    if (slot.teamB == null && slot.depends[1]) slot.teamB = LEAGUE.bracket[slot.depends[1]].winner;
    // 12-team qf slots have only one dependency (the qualifier winner fills slot B)
    else if (slot.teamB == null && slot.depends[0] && slot.teamA) {
      slot.teamB = LEAGUE.bracket[slot.depends[0]].winner;
    }
  }
}

function activeMatchTeams() {
  if (!LEAGUE.currentSlot) return null;
  const m = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
  return { slot: LEAGUE.currentSlot, teamA: m.teamA, teamB: m.teamB, match: m };
}

/* ============================================================
   ROSTER MAINTENANCE — replacement of deceased between matches
   Mortality is permanent; rookies replace dead at the SAME position
   so each team always has one of each archetype.
   ============================================================ */
function reapDeceased() {
  // Move dead players from active rosters to graveyard, then call up rookies.
  const callups = [];
  for (const team of TEAMS) {
    const roster = LEAGUE.rosters[team.id];
    for (let i = 0; i < roster.length; i++) {
      const p = roster[i];
      if (p.deceased) {
        // Already dead — make sure they're in graveyard, then replace
        const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
        const rookie = makePlayer(team.id, pos);
        rookie.debutSeason = LEAGUE.season;
        roster[i] = rookie;
        callups.push({ team: team.id, rookie, replaced: p });
      }
    }
  }
  return callups;
}

/* ============================================================
   ITEM SYSTEM (shop boosts)
   Carried over from v2 — purchased between matches, applied once.
   ============================================================ */
// Shop items have a rarity tier that gates pricing + visual treatment.
// "common" / "rare" / "legendary" map to gold/purple/red borders.
const SHOP_ITEMS = [
  // ===== COMMON (basic team buffs) =====
  { id: "armor",   ico: "🛡️", nm: "Iron Plating",   cost: 25,  rarity: "common", desc: "+10 DEF, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.def = (boost.def||0)+10; } },
  { id: "blade",   ico: "⚔️", nm: "Sharpener",      cost: 30,  rarity: "common", desc: "+5 DMG, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.dmg = (boost.dmg||0)+5; } },
  { id: "boots",   ico: "👟", nm: "Track Spikes",   cost: 35,  rarity: "common", desc: "+0.6 SPD, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.spd = (boost.spd||0)+0.6; } },
  { id: "potion",  ico: "🧪", nm: "Healing Tonic",  cost: 40,  rarity: "common", desc: "+30 HP, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.hp = (boost.hp||0)+30; } },
  { id: "lucky",   ico: "🍀", nm: "Lucky Charm",    cost: 50,  rarity: "common", desc: "+5% crit, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.crit = (boost.crit||0)+0.05; } },
  { id: "sabot",   ico: "🩹", nm: "Saboteur",       cost: 30,  rarity: "common", desc: "Opponent: -3 DMG",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.dmg = (boost.dmg||0)-3; }, target: "opp" },

  // ===== RARE (multi-stat / opponent debuff) =====
  { id: "warpaint",ico: "🎨", nm: "War Paint",      cost: 75,  rarity: "rare",   desc: "+8 DMG and +5 DEF, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.dmg = (boost.dmg||0)+8; boost.def = (boost.def||0)+5; } },
  { id: "stim",    ico: "💉", nm: "Combat Stim",    cost: 90,  rarity: "rare",   desc: "+0.4 SPD and +5 DMG, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.spd = (boost.spd||0)+0.4; boost.dmg = (boost.dmg||0)+5; } },
  { id: "sandbag", ico: "⚓", nm: "Sandbag (opponent)", cost: 80, rarity: "rare", desc: "Opponent: -0.5 SPD whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.spd = (boost.spd||0)-0.5; }, target: "opp" },
  // === v28.1: Personal Sandbag — targets ONE opposing knight ===
  // Subtle match-weighting for the bettor who knows their target. Costs
  // a lot, applies a per-bot SPD penalty for one match only. Different
  // from "sandbag" above: that nukes the whole opposing team uniformly;
  // this nukes one specific named knight (chosen via player-picker).
  { id: "sandbag_one", ico: "⚓", nm: "Personal Sandbag", cost: 150, rarity: "legendary",
    desc: "Pick ONE opposing knight: -0.8 SPD for next match. Quiet, surgical.",
    target: "specific_player",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.spd = (boost.spd||0)-0.8; } },
  { id: "fog",     ico: "🌫️", nm: "Smokescreen",    cost: 70,  rarity: "rare",   desc: "Opponent: -2 DMG and -3% crit",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.dmg = (boost.dmg||0)-2; boost.crit = (boost.crit||0)-0.03; }, target: "opp" },

  // ===== LEGENDARY (big stacked buff or curse) =====
  { id: "elixir",  ico: "🧉", nm: "Elixir of Endurance", cost: 150, rarity: "legendary", desc: "+50 HP and +10 DEF, whole team",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.hp = (boost.hp||0)+50; boost.def = (boost.def||0)+10; } },
  { id: "curse",   ico: "💀", nm: "Curse of the Loser", cost: 175, rarity: "legendary", desc: "Opponent: -10 HP and -5 DEF and -3 DMG",
    apply: (boost) => { boost.hp = (boost.hp||0)-10; boost.def = (boost.def||0)-5; boost.dmg = (boost.dmg||0)-3; }, target: "opp" },
];
const SHOP_BY_ID = Object.fromEntries(SHOP_ITEMS.map(s => [s.id, s]));

function applyBoosts(teamId, players) {
  // Apply pending team-wide boost (if any) to a team's players for this match.
  const boost = LEAGUE.pendingBoosts[teamId];
  if (boost) {
    for (const p of players) {
      if (boost.hp)   p.hp = Math.min(CAPS.hp, p.hp + boost.hp);
      if (boost.dmg)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, dmg:  clamp(p.stats.dmg + boost.dmg,  1, CAPS.dmg) };
      if (boost.def)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, def:  clamp(p.stats.def + boost.def,  0, CAPS.def) };
      if (boost.spd)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, spd:  clamp(+(p.stats.spd + boost.spd).toFixed(2), 0.6, CAPS.spd) };
      if (boost.crit) p.stats = { ...p.stats, crit: clamp(p.stats.crit + boost.crit, 0.02, 0.50) };
    }
    delete LEAGUE.pendingBoosts[teamId];
  }
  // === v28.1: Apply per-knight targeted boosts (Personal Sandbag etc.) ===
  // pendingTargetedBoosts: { teamId: { knightId: { spd, dmg, ... } } }
  if (LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts && LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts[teamId]) {
    const targeted = LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts[teamId];
    for (const p of players) {
      const tb = targeted[p.id];
      if (!tb) continue;
      if (tb.hp)   p.hp = Math.min(CAPS.hp, p.hp + tb.hp);
      if (tb.dmg)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, dmg:  clamp(p.stats.dmg + tb.dmg,  1, CAPS.dmg) };
      if (tb.def)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, def:  clamp(p.stats.def + tb.def,  0, CAPS.def) };
      if (tb.spd)  p.stats = { ...p.stats, spd:  clamp(+(p.stats.spd + tb.spd).toFixed(2), 0.6, CAPS.spd) };
      if (tb.crit) p.stats = { ...p.stats, crit: clamp(p.stats.crit + tb.crit, 0.02, 0.50) };
    }
    delete LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts[teamId];
  }
}

</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     SIMULATION — Part B
     Match init, team-aware AI, combat, match-end, bracket flow
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
'use strict';

/* ============================================================
   COMMENTARY FEED (in-memory, capped)
   ============================================================ */
const FEED = [];
const FEED_MAX = 60;

function feed(kind, msg) {
  const ts = LEAGUE.match
    ? `${String(Math.floor(LEAGUE.match.frame / 20)).padStart(2,"0")}:${String(LEAGUE.match.frame % 20 * 5).padStart(2,"0")}`
    : "--:--";
  FEED.unshift({ kind, msg, ts });
  if (FEED.length > FEED_MAX) FEED.length = FEED_MAX;
  if (typeof renderFeed === "function") renderFeed();
}

/* ============================================================
   SPORTSCASTER COMMENTARY — separate channel from the kill feed.
   Pulls from large tonally-categorized pools and pushes lines as
   they happen. Optional AI enhancement via ai-text-plugin (hook
   at the bottom): if the plugin is present and responds, it can
   replace the static line with a fresher take. Static pool
   ALWAYS fires first so there's no latency hole.

   Channels: "play-by-play" (raw event description) and "color"
   (analyst-style observation about the meta of the moment).
   ============================================================ */
const COMMENTARY = [];      // most-recent first
const COMMENTARY_MAX = 40;

function commentate(kind, line, opts) {
  if (!line) return;
  opts = opts || {};
  const ts = LEAGUE.match
    ? `${String(Math.floor(LEAGUE.match.frame / 20)).padStart(2,"0")}:${String(LEAGUE.match.frame % 20 * 5).padStart(2,"0")}`
    : "--:--";
  const entry = {
    kind,                                 // "play"|"color"|"chant"|"opener"
    line,
    ts,
    flavor: opts.flavor || null,          // "hype"|"deadpan"|"sad"|"chaotic"
    isAi: !!opts.isAi,
    id: COMMENTARY_MAX_ID++,
  };
  COMMENTARY.unshift(entry);
  if (COMMENTARY.length > COMMENTARY_MAX) COMMENTARY.length = COMMENTARY_MAX;
  if (typeof renderCommentary === "function") renderCommentary();
  // Async AI enhancement — does NOT block the sim. If the plugin returns
  // a different/better line, it updates the entry in place.
  if (opts.aiContext && typeof requestAiCommentaryEnhancement === "function") {
    requestAiCommentaryEnhancement(entry, opts.aiContext);
  }
  return entry;
}
let COMMENTARY_MAX_ID = 1;

/* ===== Sportscaster line pools ===== */

// Match opener — bracket-positioning + dramatic stakes
const COMM_MATCH_OPEN = [
  "Welcome to the Float Knights arena! {a} versus {b} — let's get into it.",
  "And we're underway. {a} on the left, {b} on the right.",
  "Fans on their feet — {a} and {b} square up at center circle.",
  "This is the matchup we've been talking about all season. {a} versus {b}.",
  "Whistle blows. {a} versus {b}. Five rounds, three-life format. Let's roll.",
  "Bell rings — {a} and {b} have taken the field.",
  "OK folks, {a} brings their full roster against {b}. Should be a barnburner.",
  "Coin's in the air, knights are moving. {a} versus {b}, here we go.",
];

// First-kill specific
const COMM_FIRST_KILL = [
  "FIRST BLOOD! {a} draws first.",
  "And just like that, {b} is on the board.",
  "Opening kill goes to {a}. The match has officially begun.",
  "{a} doesn't waste any time — first kill of the match.",
  "Early shot, early casualty. {a} sets the tone.",
];

// Streak callouts
const COMM_STREAK_BIG = [
  "{p} is in the zone now. That's {n} kills, no signs of stopping.",
  "Folks, {p} is doing things on this field that defy explanation.",
  "{n} kills! {p} has tipped from 'hot' into 'historic.'",
  "Get the engravers ready — {p} is at {n} and counting.",
];

// Last-knight comeback potential
const COMM_LAST_KNIGHT = [
  "{p} is the only {tm} left standing. Dramatic stuff.",
  "Down to one for {tm} — {p} carrying the team.",
  "Five-on-one. {p} versus the world. No pressure.",
  "Lone {tm} on the field — {p}. This is where legends are forged.",
];

// Match win — by margin
const COMM_MATCH_WIN_DOM = [
  "{w} take it in dominant fashion — {sa}-{sb}.",
  "Statement win for {w}. {sa}-{sb}, no contest.",
  "{w} make it look easy. Not the response {l} fans were hoping for.",
];
const COMM_MATCH_WIN_CLOSE = [
  "{w} edge {l} in a {sa}-{sb} thriller.",
  "Right down to the wire — {w} take it {sa}-{sb}.",
  "Heart-stopper. {w} survive {l} {sa}-{sb}.",
];
const COMM_MATCH_DRAW = [
  "Stunning result — {a} and {b} fight to a {sa}-{sb} draw. Coin flip decides it.",
  "We've got a tie. Coin in the air for the advancement. Brutal.",
  "Draw on the board. The committee gets the cruel honor of picking the survivor.",
];

// "Color commentary" — analyst observations, swap in occasionally
const COMM_COLOR_GENERAL = [
  "Look at the gear differential — {a} is rolling out heavier armor than usual.",
  "Both teams played fast in their opener. Watch for fatigue late.",
  "Power Core's been the difference all season. Don't sleep on it.",
  "If {a} can keep their snipers alive, they win this comfortably.",
  "Healers are gonna decide this round. Snipe accordingly.",
  "These two teams have a history. Should be feisty.",
  "Fans want chaos and the league is delivering.",
];

// Death-related — softer than the Worms-style death cries
const COMM_PERMADEATH = [
  "And that's it for {p}. {n} kills, {y} seasons, gone forever.",
  "Tough one — {p} permanently retired. The Hall awaits.",
  "End of an era. {p} won't suit up again.",
];

// Power Core captures
const COMM_CORE_CAPTURE = [
  "{tm} grabs the Core! Damage buff incoming.",
  "Power Core is {tm}'s — they get the next eight seconds at +25%.",
  "{tm} muscle through to the center. Buff acquired.",
];

function ccPick(pool, vars) {
  let s = pick(pool);
  for (const k of Object.keys(vars || {})) {
    s = s.replaceAll("{" + k + "}", vars[k]);
  }
  return s;
}

// passed in, and (if it produces a better line) mutates entry.line +
// re-renders.
function requestAiCommentaryEnhancement(entry, ctx) {
  // Real implementation lives in the events layer where it can reach
  // the imported ai-text-plugin global. This stub is the no-op fallback.
}

/* Phrase pools */

  "{a} bodies {b} clean off the field!",
  "{b} eats turf — {a} stands tall!",
  "{a} sends {b} to the great beyond!",
  "{b} is no more. {a} marches on!",
  "{a} ends {b}'s career permanently!",

];

  "CRITICAL HIT — {a} just ROCKED {b}!",
  "DEVASTATING blow from {a}!",
  "{a} found a weak spot on {b}!",
];

  "{p} is barely standing!",
  "{p} is on death's door!",
];

  "Whistle blows — {a} vs {b} is LIVE!",
  "Five-on-five action begins between {a} and {b}!",
  "{a} and {b} square up at the center circle!",

  "{w} clinch the match {alive}-to-zero!",
];

/* ============================================================
   DEATH CRIES — Worms-style last words. When a knight dies, one
   of these is shown as a float over the corpse and pushed to the
   feed for ticker pickup. Categorized by tone/situation so we
   can pick something that fits the moment (perma-deaths,
   round KOs while a streak ends, getting blown up by your own
   item, etc.).

   Each pool has 8-12 entries. We pick weighted by context to
   avoid repetition fatigue. The Perchance ai-text-plugin can
   be plugged in here later for fully-dynamic generation; the
   static catalog is the always-available fallback.
   ============================================================ */
const DEATH_CRIES_GENERIC = [
  "Tell my mother…", "Not like this!", "I felt that one.", "Worth it.",
  "Rude.", "Already?!", "Tag, you're it.", "I'll be back.",
  "MEDIC!", "Why me?", "That tickled.", "See you in the rematch.",
  "My armor was a lie.", "I dropped my snack.", "Catch ya later.",
  "Tell my team I love them.", "Was that legal?", "Fair enough.",
  "I regret everything.", "I had a family of imaginary squirrels.",
];
const DEATH_CRIES_CRIT = [
  "OW.", "MY BALLOONS!", "Did you really need to crit?", "OK calm DOWN.",
  "Right in the giblets!", "That was personal!", "Excessive!",
  "Read the room!", "Save some for the fans!", "Yowza.",
];
const DEATH_CRIES_LASTLIFE = [
  "Goodbye, cruel arena…", "It's been a journey.", "My final balloon… pop…",
  "Tell the children I tried.", "Carry on without me.", "I'll wait at the gates.",
  "The league will remember me… right?", "Enter my name in the Hall…",
  "Not the perma-death!", "Save my gear for someone good.",
  "I leave you my sword. And my debts.", "Three lives and STILL not enough?",
];
const DEATH_CRIES_STREAKBROKEN = [
  "ALL THAT WORK!", "My streak…", "Nooo, the bonus!", "I had it!",
  "I was COOKING!", "Not now, not now.", "The fans will riot.",
  "Take my streak with you.",
];
const DEATH_CRIES_SELFDESTRUCT = [
  "I held the grenade too long.", "Whoops.", "MY BAD!", "That was on me.",
  "Note to self: don't.", "I had ONE job.", "I fumbled the explosive.",
  "I take full responsibility.",
];
const DEATH_CRIES_STUNNED = [
  "Z…z…z…", "Wait, I was sleeping!", "Cheap shot!", "I wasn't ready!",
  "Stunlocked. Fitting.", "Eyes were closed!",
];

function deathCryFor(defender, attacker, ctx) {
  // ctx: { isCrit, isPermadeath, wasOnStreak, wasStunned, isSelfDestruct }
  ctx = ctx || {};
  let pool;
  if (ctx.isSelfDestruct) pool = DEATH_CRIES_SELFDESTRUCT;
  else if (ctx.isPermadeath) pool = DEATH_CRIES_LASTLIFE;
  else if (ctx.wasOnStreak) pool = DEATH_CRIES_STREAKBROKEN;
  else if (ctx.wasStunned) pool = DEATH_CRIES_STUNNED;
  else if (ctx.isCrit) pool = DEATH_CRIES_CRIT;
  else pool = DEATH_CRIES_GENERIC;
  return pick(pool);
}

/* ============================================================
   STREAK TAUNTS — fired when a knight hits a kill milestone.
   Shouted as a float over the knight + pushed to feed.
   ============================================================ */
const TAUNTS_STREAK_5 = [
  "I'M HEATING UP!", "FIVE DOWN!", "WHO'S NEXT?", "STAY DOWN!",
  "TOO EASY.", "HANDS UP, FANS!", "THIS IS MY ARENA NOW.",
  "FIVE-FOR-FIVE!",
];
const TAUNTS_STREAK_10 = [
  "TEN. KILL. STREAK!", "I AM INEVITABLE.", "TELL THE LEAGUE.",
  "DOUBLE DIGITS, BABY!", "SOMEONE STOP ME!",
  "PUT ME IN THE HALL OF FAME ALREADY!",
];
const TAUNTS_STREAK_15 = [
  "FIFTEEN. UNREAL.", "I'M ON ANOTHER PLANE OF EXISTENCE.",
  "THE GODS THEMSELVES BLINK.", "WRITE BOOKS ABOUT THIS.",
];
const TAUNTS_STREAK_25 = [
  "TWENTY-FIVE. RETIRE THE SPORT.", "I AM THE LEAGUE.",
  "MY NAME WILL OUTLIVE THE STADIUM.",
];
const TAUNTS_LAST_KNIGHT = [
  "ALONE, BUT NOT BROKEN.", "ONE KNIGHT REMAINS!",
  "I'LL TAKE ALL OF YOU.", "FOR THE TEAM!",
  "DON'T COUNT ME OUT YET!", "ONE SHALL STAND. TWO SHALL FALL.",
  "I AM the team now.",
];
function taunt(phrase, p, m) {
  if (!phrase || !p || !m) return;
  m.floats.push({
    x: p.x, y: p.y - 22,
    text: phrase,
    life: 50,
    color: "#f7c948",
  });
  feed("kill", `📣 ${p.name}: "${phrase}"`);
}
function phrasePick(pool, vars) {
  let s = pick(pool);
  for (const k of Object.keys(vars)) {
    s = s.replaceAll("{" + k + "}", vars[k]);
  }
  return s;
}

/* ============================================================
   MATCH INITIALIZATION (best-of-N rounds)
   - startMatch() sets up match-level state, resets lives, then
     immediately starts round 1.
   - startRound() fields all eligible players (lives > 0, !deceased),
     positions them, fires "start" items on round 1, sets phase = "round".
   ============================================================ */
function startMatch() {
  if (!LEAGUE.currentSlot) return;
  const b = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
  if (!b.teamA || !b.teamB) return;

  // SAFETY NET — ensure both teams have full living rosters BEFORE we filter.
  const callups = reapDeceased();
  for (const c of callups) {
    feed("spawn", `📣 Emergency call-up: ${c.rookie.name} replaces ${c.replaced.name} (${TEAM_BY_ID[c.team].name}).`);
  }

  // DETERMINISTIC SEED — same season + slot ALWAYS produces the same match.
  // This is the foundation for shared live matches: two clients running with
  // the same league state will see identical kill orders, dodges, drops, and
  // outcomes. Each match's seed is a hash of (season, slot, season-genesis).
  const matchSeed = computeMatchSeed(LEAGUE.season, LEAGUE.currentSlot);
  SIM_RNG.reseed(matchSeed);
  BIOME_RNG.reseed(matchSeed);

  const teamA = LEAGUE.rosters[b.teamA].filter(p => !p.deceased);
  const teamB = LEAGUE.rosters[b.teamB].filter(p => !p.deceased);

  // Reset match-level state on every player going into this match
  for (const p of [...teamA, ...teamB]) {
    p.lives = MATCH.startingLives;
    p.itemUsed = false;
    p.boostMods = null;
    p.balloonsPopped = 0;
  }

  // Apply pending shop boosts (consumed once per match)
  applyBoosts(b.teamA, teamA);
  applyBoosts(b.teamB, teamB);

  LEAGUE.match = {
    slot: LEAGUE.currentSlot,
    teamAId: b.teamA, teamBId: b.teamB,
    teamAplayers: teamA, teamBplayers: teamB,
    bestOf: MATCH.bestOf,
    roundsToWin: MATCH.roundsToWin,
    roundsWonA: 0, roundsWonB: 0,
    rounds: [],
    currentRoundIndex: 0,
    phase: "preRound",
    frame: 0,
    roundFrame: 0,
    killsByTeam: { [b.teamA]: 0, [b.teamB]: 0 },
    firstKillTeam: null,
    blood: [],
    corpses: [],
    floats: [],
    drops: [],
    projectiles: [],
    terrain: generateTerrain(),  // randomized obstacles for this match
    biome: pickBiomeForMatch(),  // visual variety per match
    scatter: [],                 // populated below
    clouds: generateCloudShadows(),
    ambient: [],                 // populated below
    winnerId: null,
    // === v28: game mode ===
    // Captured at match-start so changing LEAGUE_CONFIG.gameMode mid-match
    // doesn't fight in-flight state. The corresponding mode entities
    // (crown / flag / bases) are initialized below.
    gameMode: LEAGUE_CONFIG.gameMode || "deathmatch",
    modeScoreA: 0,
    modeScoreB: 0,
    modeWinner: null,
  };
  _initModeEntities(LEAGUE.match);
  // Scatter + ambient have to be generated AFTER biome is set
  LEAGUE.match.scatter = generateBiomeScatter(LEAGUE.match.biome);
  LEAGUE.match.ambient = generateAmbientParticles(LEAGUE.match.biome);

  feed("match", phrasePick(PHRASE_MATCH_START, {
    a: TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamA].emoji + " " + TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamA].short,
    b: TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamB].emoji + " " + TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamB].short,
  }));

  // === Sportscaster opener + pre-match chants ===
  const tA = TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamA], tB = TEAM_BY_ID[b.teamB];
  commentate("opener", ccPick(COMM_MATCH_OPEN, {
    a: tA.emoji + " " + tA.short,
    b: tB.emoji + " " + tB.short,
  }), { flavor: "hype", aiContext: { type: "matchOpen", teamA: tA, teamB: tB } });

  // Each team shouts a chant — pulled from per-team chants array
  if (tA.chants && tA.chants.length) {
    commentate("chant", `${tA.emoji} ${tA.short}: "${pick(tA.chants)}"`, { flavor: "hype" });
  }
  if (tB.chants && tB.chants.length) {
    commentate("chant", `${tB.emoji} ${tB.short}: "${pick(tB.chants)}"`, { flavor: "hype" });
  }

  // Begin round 1 immediately
  startRound();
}

function startRound() {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;
  m.currentRoundIndex++;
  m.phase = "round";
  m.roundFrame = 0;
  m.projectiles = m.projectiles || []; // ensure projectile array exists

  // Eligible players: not deceased AND have lives remaining
  const eligibleA = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => !p.deceased && p.lives > 0);
  const eligibleB = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => !p.deceased && p.lives > 0);

  if (!eligibleA.length || !eligibleB.length) {
    finalizeRound(/*forfeit*/ true);
    return;
  }

  layoutTeam(eligibleA, "left");
  layoutTeam(eligibleB, "right");

  // Spawn neutral items on the field. Round 1 gets the full set (3 Health
  // Packs + 2 Armor + 3 random); later rounds refresh if the field is
  // sparse (re-stock so survivors don't fight over scraps).
  if (m.currentRoundIndex === 1) {
    spawnNeutralItems(m, 8);
  } else {
    if (m.drops.length < 4) spawnNeutralItems(m, 4);
  }

  if (m.currentRoundIndex === 1) {
    for (const p of [...eligibleA, ...eligibleB]) {
      if (p.item && p.item.trigger === "start" && !p.itemUsed) {
        applyItemTrigger(p);
      }
    }
  }

  feed("match", `🔔 Round ${m.currentRoundIndex} of ${m.bestOf} — fight!`);
}

/* ============================================================
   POWER CORE — center-of-arena objective.
   Spawns ~10sec into the round and pulses with golden light.
   Walking into it grants your TEAM a damage buff for 8 sec.
   After capture, respawns 30 sec later. Gives AI a strong
   reason to push the center.
   ============================================================ */
function tickPowerCore(m) {
  if (!m.powerCore) {
    m.powerCore = { state: "spawning", spawnAtFrame: 200, x: COMBAT.arenaW / 2, y: COMBAT.arenaH / 2 };
  }
  const pc = m.powerCore;
  const frame = m.roundFrame;

  if (pc.state === "spawning" && frame >= pc.spawnAtFrame) {
    pc.state = "active";
    pc.spawnedFrame = frame;
    feed("item", `⚡ POWER CORE has spawned at the center of the arena!`);
  }

  if (pc.state === "active") {
    // Check if any alive knight is touching it
    const all = m.teamAplayers.concat(m.teamBplayers);
    for (const p of all) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      const dx = p.x - pc.x, dy = p.y - pc.y;
      if (dx*dx + dy*dy < 14*14) {
        // Captured!
        const team = TEAM_BY_ID[p.teamId];
        feed("item", `⚡ ${p.name} captures the POWER CORE — ${team.short} gets +25% DMG!`);
        m.floats.push({ x: pc.x, y: pc.y - 16, text: "CAPTURED", life: 40, color: team.color });
        // Apply team buff
        const teammates = (p.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers);
        for (const t of teammates) {
          if (!t.alive) continue;
          if (!t.boostMods) t.boostMods = {};
          t.boostMods.dmgMult = 1.25;
          t.boostMods.life = 160; // 8 sec at 20fps
        }
        if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast) {
          window.toast(`⚡ <b>${team.short}</b> captures POWER CORE — +25% damage for 8s`, "info", { ico: "⚡", ttl: 5000 });
        }
        // Sportscaster
        if (typeof commentate === "function") {
          commentate("play", ccPick(COMM_CORE_CAPTURE, { tm: team.emoji + " " + team.short }), { flavor: "hype" });
        }
        pc.state = "respawn";
        pc.respawnAtFrame = frame + 600; // 30 sec
        pc.lastCapturedTeam = p.teamId;
        pc.lastCapturedFrame = frame;
        break;
      }
    }
  } else if (pc.state === "respawn" && frame >= pc.respawnAtFrame) {
    pc.state = "active";
    pc.spawnedFrame = frame;
    feed("item", `⚡ POWER CORE respawns at the center.`);
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   GAME MODES (v28) — KOTH, CTF, Melee Tag

   Three new modes layered over the standard match runtime. Each
   mode hooks the match through three points:

     1. _initModeEntities(m) — called once at startMatch to set
        up crown / flag / bases / "It" assignment.
     2. tickGameMode(m) — called every tick from tickMatch to
        run mode-specific scoring + entity updates.
     3. checkModeWinCondition(m) — called every tick to detect
        the score threshold; sets m.modeWinner when done.

   Deathmatch is unaffected (the round-elimination flow keeps
   running). The new modes set m.gameMode to their id and use
   m.modeScoreA / m.modeScoreB for scoring instead of the
   round-win counts.
   ============================================================ */

function _initModeEntities(m) {
  const mode = m.gameMode || "deathmatch";
  const modeCfg = (LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig || {})[mode] || {};
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW, H = COMBAT.arenaH;
  const cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2;

  if (mode === "koth") {
    // Crown spawns at center, no current carrier.
    m.crown = {
      x: cx, y: cy,
      carrierId: null,
      carrierTeamId: null,
      // Sub-frame accumulator so tick-points-per-second translates cleanly
      // at 20fps sim rate (frameMs = 50ms).
      pointsAccum: 0,
    };
  } else if (mode === "ctf") {
    // Flag spawns at center; bases spawn at each team's spawn area.
    m.flag = {
      x: cx, y: cy,
      homeX: cx, homeY: cy,
      carrierId: null,
      carrierTeamId: null,
      droppedAtFrame: -1,  // -1 = at home, >=0 = dropped (will return after timeout)
      returnAfterFrames: Math.round((modeCfg.flagReturnSec || 8) * 1000 / COMBAT.frameMs),
    };
    m.basesById = {
      [m.teamAId]: { x: 80, y: cy, teamId: m.teamAId },
      [m.teamBId]: { x: W - 80, y: cy, teamId: m.teamBId },
    };
  } else if (mode === "tag") {
    // Pick a random "It" from team A's roster (deterministic via SIM_RNG).
    const all = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].filter(p => !p.deceased);
    if (all.length > 0) {
      const itIdx = Math.floor(SIM_RNG.next() * all.length);
      const it = all[itIdx];
      m.tag = {
        itId: it.id,
        // tagFreezes maps frozenPlayerId → frame the freeze ends.
        tagFreezes: Object.create(null),
      };
      // Mark the It bot so AI can branch on it (we read m.tag.itId in AI dispatch).
    }
  }
}

/* Master mode tick — dispatches to per-mode logic. Called from tickMatch
   after all per-player ticks have run, so player positions for this frame
   are already finalized. */
function tickGameMode(m) {
  const mode = m.gameMode || "deathmatch";
  if (mode === "koth") tickKOTH(m);
  else if (mode === "ctf") tickCTF(m);
  else if (mode === "tag") tickTag(m);
  // deathmatch needs no extra tick — round logic handles everything
  if (mode !== "deathmatch") checkModeWinCondition(m);
}

/* ----- KOTH ----- */
function tickKOTH(m) {
  const c = m.crown;
  if (!c) return;
  const cfg = LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.koth;
  const all = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].filter(p => p.alive);

  // If carrier is dead/null, find a new pickup (any bot within 16px)
  const carrier = c.carrierId ? all.find(p => p.id === c.carrierId) : null;
  if (!carrier) {
    c.carrierId = null;
    c.carrierTeamId = null;
    for (const p of all) {
      const dx = p.x - c.x, dy = p.y - c.y;
      if (dx*dx + dy*dy < 16*16) {
        c.carrierId = p.id;
        c.carrierTeamId = p.teamId;
        feed("match", `👑 ${p.name} grabs the crown for ${TEAM_BY_ID[p.teamId].short}!`);
        break;
      }
    }
  } else {
    // Crown follows the carrier
    c.x = carrier.x;
    c.y = carrier.y - 20; // floats above their head
  }

  // Carrier scores points over time (frame-rate-agnostic via accumulator)
  if (c.carrierTeamId) {
    c.pointsAccum += (cfg.tickPointsPerSec || 1) / (1000 / COMBAT.frameMs);
    while (c.pointsAccum >= 1) {
      c.pointsAccum -= 1;
      if (c.carrierTeamId === m.teamAId) m.modeScoreA = (m.modeScoreA || 0) + 1;
      else m.modeScoreB = (m.modeScoreB || 0) + 1;
    }
  }
}

/* ----- CTF ----- */
function tickCTF(m) {
  const f = m.flag;
  if (!f) return;
  const all = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].filter(p => p.alive);

  // Respawn grace — after a capture, the flag is briefly uncapturable
  // so the same carrier doesn't immediately re-grab and re-cap.
  if (f.respawnGraceUntil && m.roundFrame < f.respawnGraceUntil) {
    return;
  }

  // If carrier dead/null, check for pickup
  const carrier = f.carrierId ? all.find(p => p.id === f.carrierId) : null;
  if (!carrier) {
    f.carrierId = null;
    f.carrierTeamId = null;
    for (const p of all) {
      const dx = p.x - f.x, dy = p.y - f.y;
      if (dx*dx + dy*dy < 16*16) {
        f.carrierId = p.id;
        f.carrierTeamId = p.teamId;
        f.droppedAtFrame = -1;
        feed("match", `🚩 ${p.name} grabs the flag for ${TEAM_BY_ID[p.teamId].short}!`);
        break;
      }
    }
    // Return-to-base timer if dropped (carrier was killed)
    if (f.droppedAtFrame >= 0 && (m.roundFrame - f.droppedAtFrame) >= f.returnAfterFrames) {
      f.x = f.homeX; f.y = f.homeY; f.droppedAtFrame = -1;
      feed("match", `🚩 The flag returns to center.`);
    }
  } else {
    // Flag follows carrier
    f.x = carrier.x;
    f.y = carrier.y - 20;
    // Capture check: carrier touches their own base
    const myBase = m.basesById[carrier.teamId];
    if (myBase) {
      const dbx = carrier.x - myBase.x, dby = carrier.y - myBase.y;
      if (dbx*dbx + dby*dby < 24*24) {
        // Capture!
        if (carrier.teamId === m.teamAId) m.modeScoreA = (m.modeScoreA || 0) + 1;
        else m.modeScoreB = (m.modeScoreB || 0) + 1;
        feed("match", `🏆 ${carrier.name} captures the flag! ${TEAM_BY_ID[carrier.teamId].short} +1`);
        // Reset flag — and apply 60-frame grace so this carrier doesn't
        // immediately re-grab and re-cap on the next tick.
        f.x = f.homeX; f.y = f.homeY;
        f.carrierId = null;
        f.carrierTeamId = null;
        f.droppedAtFrame = -1;
        f.respawnGraceUntil = m.roundFrame + 60;
      }
    }
  }
}

/* ----- TAG ----- */
function tickTag(m) {
  if (!m.tag) return;
  const all = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers];
  const it = all.find(p => p.id === m.tag.itId);

  // If "It" bot died, pick a new one (highest-HP survivor)
  if (!it || !it.alive) {
    const survivors = all.filter(p => p.alive);
    if (survivors.length > 1) {
      const newIt = survivors.reduce((a, b) => (a.hp > b.hp ? a : b));
      m.tag.itId = newIt.id;
      feed("match", `🏷 ${newIt.name} is now IT!`);
    } else {
      // Only one survivor — they've won
    }
  }

  // Bleed damage on frozen bots — high enough that a single tag is often fatal
  const cfg = LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.tag;
  const dpsTick = (cfg.tagDpsWhileFrozen || 50) / (1000 / COMBAT.frameMs);
  // Find current "It" bot to credit as the bleed-damage attacker
  const itBot = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].find(p => p.id === m.tag.itId && p.alive);
  for (const id in m.tag.tagFreezes) {
    const target = all.find(p => p.id === Number(id));
    const expireFrame = m.tag.tagFreezes[id];
    if (!target || !target.alive) {
      delete m.tag.tagFreezes[id];
      continue;
    }
    if (m.roundFrame >= expireFrame) {
      // Freeze expired
      target.stunned = 0;
      delete m.tag.tagFreezes[id];
      continue;
    }
    // Apply bleed via applyDamage so death detection (handleKO) fires
    // properly — otherwise frozen bots accumulate negative HP but stay
    // marked alive=true, breaking the win condition.
    if (itBot && typeof applyDamage === "function") {
      applyDamage(itBot, target, Math.max(1, Math.round(dpsTick)), false, m);
    } else {
      target.hp = Math.max(0, Math.round(target.hp - dpsTick));
    }
    target.stunned = Math.max(target.stunned || 0, 4);
    if (target.hp <= 0) {
      delete m.tag.tagFreezes[id];
    }
  }
}

/* Apply a tag — called from melee resolveAttack when in tag mode and
   the attacker is "It". Per spec: tagger stays "It". Target freezes for
   3 seconds (during which they take heavy bleed damage), then unfreezes
   and resumes fleeing. Bots die from cumulative tag damage; the cycle
   continues until bots fall one by one. Last bot alive = winner.
   "It" gets reassigned only if the current "It" dies. */
function applyTagHit(attacker, defender, m) {
  if (!m.tag || attacker.id !== m.tag.itId) return false;
  const cfg = LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.tag;
  m.tag.tagFreezes[defender.id] = m.roundFrame + (cfg.tagFreezeFrames || 60);
  defender.stunned = (cfg.tagFreezeFrames || 60);
  feed("match", `⚡ ${attacker.name} TAGS ${defender.name}!`);
  // Route damage through applyDamage so handleKO fires properly on death.
  if (typeof applyDamage === "function") {
    applyDamage(attacker, defender, (cfg.tagDamagePerHit || 30), false, m);
  } else {
    defender.hp -= (cfg.tagDamagePerHit || 30);
  }
  // It does NOT pass on tag — the tagger keeps chasing.
  return true;
}

/* Detect mode-specific win condition — set m.modeWinner. */
function checkModeWinCondition(m) {
  if (m.modeWinner) return;
  const mode = m.gameMode;
  const cfg = (LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig || {})[mode] || {};
  if (mode === "koth") {
    if (m.modeScoreA >= cfg.pointsToWin) m.modeWinner = m.teamAId;
    else if (m.modeScoreB >= cfg.pointsToWin) m.modeWinner = m.teamBId;
  } else if (mode === "ctf") {
    if (m.modeScoreA >= cfg.capsToWin) m.modeWinner = m.teamAId;
    else if (m.modeScoreB >= cfg.capsToWin) m.modeWinner = m.teamBId;
  } else if (mode === "tag") {
    // Last-bot-standing wins (or last team standing in team-tag)
    const aliveA = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => p.alive).length;
    const aliveB = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => p.alive).length;
    const total = aliveA + aliveB;
    if (total <= 1) {
      // Whichever team has the survivor wins
      if (aliveA > 0) m.modeWinner = m.teamAId;
      else if (aliveB > 0) m.modeWinner = m.teamBId;
      else m.modeWinner = "draw";
    }
  }
  if (m.modeWinner && m.modeWinner !== "draw") {
    feed("match", `🏆 MATCH OVER — ${TEAM_BY_ID[m.modeWinner].short} wins ${mode.toUpperCase()}!`);
  }
}

/* Spawn neutral items at match start. Mix:
   - Health Packs (instant +30 HP on pickup, NOT a gear slot)
   - Armor pieces (random armor — instant equip if upgrade)
   - General loot (random weapon/armor/item)
   This gives the AI reasons to scout the map besides chasing each other. */
function spawnNeutralItems(m, n) {
  const margin = 60;
  const safeSpawn = () => {
    let x, y, attempts = 0;
    do {
      x = R(margin, COMBAT.arenaW - margin);
      y = R(margin, COMBAT.arenaH - margin);
      attempts++;
    } while (m.terrain && collidesTerrain(x, y, m.terrain) && attempts < 12);
    return { x, y };
  };

  // Always spawn 4 Health Packs (was 3) — bigger HP pool needs more healing
  const healthCount = Math.min(n, 4);
  for (let i = 0; i < healthCount; i++) {
    const { x, y } = safeSpawn();
    m.drops.push({
      x, y,
      slot: "health",
      gear: { id: "healthpack", ico: "❤", name: "Health Pack", heal: 60 },
      fromName: null, neutral: true,
    });
  }

  // Always spawn 3 Armor pieces (was 2) — armor is now strategically vital
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const { x, y } = safeSpawn();
    m.drops.push({
      x, y,
      slot: "armor",
      gear: { ...pick(ARMORS) },
      fromName: null, neutral: true,
    });
  }

  // Fill the rest with general loot (random slot)
  const rest = Math.max(0, n - healthCount - 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < rest; i++) {
    const { x, y } = safeSpawn();
    const slot = pick(["weapon", "armor", "item"]);
    const catalog = slot === "weapon" ? WEAPONS : slot === "armor" ? ARMORS : ITEMS;
    m.drops.push({
      x, y, slot,
      gear: { ...pick(catalog) },
      fromName: null, neutral: true,
    });
  }
}

/* Periodic mid-round resupply: every 25 sec of round time, spawn one
   fresh Health Pack at a safe position. Keeps long matches sustainable. */
function maybeResupplyHealth(m) {
  if (!m.lastResupplyFrame) m.lastResupplyFrame = 0;
  if (m.roundFrame - m.lastResupplyFrame < 500) return; // 25s at 50ms ticks
  // Don't over-saturate the field
  const healthOnField = m.drops.filter(d => d.slot === "health").length;
  if (healthOnField >= 3) return;
  m.lastResupplyFrame = m.roundFrame;
  const margin = 60;
  let x, y, attempts = 0;
  do {
    x = R(margin, COMBAT.arenaW - margin);
    y = R(margin, COMBAT.arenaH - margin);
    attempts++;
  } while (m.terrain && collidesTerrain(x, y, m.terrain) && attempts < 12);
  m.drops.push({
    x, y, slot: "health",
    gear: { id: "healthpack", ico: "❤", name: "Health Pack", heal: 60 },
    fromName: null, neutral: true,
  });
  feed("item", `❤ Resupply: a Health Pack drops onto the field.`);
}

/* ============================================================
   TERRAIN GENERATION
   3-7 square obstacle blocks clustered near the center vertical
   line of the arena. Each block is randomly nudged left/right so
   no two matches feel identical. Blocks are ~36×36px (roughly 2×
   a player's footprint) and AI must path around them.
   ============================================================ */
function generateTerrain() {
  const blocks = [];
  const count = RI(3, 7);
  const SIZE = 36;
  const cx = COMBAT.arenaW / 2;
  const cy = COMBAT.arenaH / 2;
  const usable = COMBAT.arenaH - 80;
  // Power Core spawns at exact arena center with a ~14px radius. Reserve
  // a 70px clear zone around the center so terrain can never overlap it.
  // If a placed block would land in this exclusion zone, push it outward
  // along the y-axis (away from center) until it's clear.
  const CORE_KEEPOUT = 70;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const yFrac = (i + 1) / (count + 1);
    let y = Math.round(40 + usable * yFrac - SIZE / 2);
    let x = Math.round(cx - SIZE / 2 + R(-90, 90));
    // Distance from block CENTER to arena center
    const blockCx = x + SIZE / 2, blockCy = y + SIZE / 2;
    const dx = blockCx - cx, dy = blockCy - cy;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (d < CORE_KEEPOUT + SIZE / 2) {
      // Push the block along its current vector from center until it clears.
      // If it's exactly on top of center (rare), pick a default down-direction.
      const ux = d > 0.5 ? dx / d : 0;
      const uy = d > 0.5 ? dy / d : 1;
      const need = (CORE_KEEPOUT + SIZE / 2) - d + 4; // 4px breathing room
      x = Math.round(x + ux * need);
      y = Math.round(y + uy * need);
      // Clamp inside arena bounds
      x = clamp(x, 8, COMBAT.arenaW - SIZE - 8);
      y = clamp(y, 8, COMBAT.arenaH - SIZE - 8);
    }
    blocks.push({ x, y, size: SIZE });
  }
  return blocks;
}

/* ============================================================
   BIOMES — visual variation across the play field.

   Each match is assigned a biome deterministically (via SIM_RNG so
   the same match seed always produces the same look). Biomes provide:
   - base + secondary floor colors for the gradient
   - mowing-stripe hue (subtle striped pattern, like a sports pitch)
   - scatter palette (decorative tufts/pebbles/petals scattered on floor)
   - accent palette (cracks, lines, wear marks)
   - sky tint (affects vignette mood)
   - ambient particle type ("petal", "ember", "snow", null)

   Biomes are drawn UNDER players, terrain, and projectiles. They are
   drawn EVERY frame (decorations are stored in match state, not
   re-randomized) — this is cheap because there are typically <200
   decoration sprites.
   ============================================================ */
const BIOMES = [];  // populated by the factory below — see CITY_PALETTES × SPORT_TYPES × EFFECT_TYPES

/* ============================================================
   GENERATIVE STADIUM SYSTEM — v28.9
   ----------------------------------------------------------------
   12 cities × 8 sports = 96 generated stadiums, plus the legacy
   environmental & seasonal venues defined above.

   Each city has a signature visual palette (floor colors, scatter
   set, ambient effect, sky tint, accent line color, lore name
   fragments). Each sport has a court-marking type and a set of
   venue-name terms. _buildBiome(cityKey, sportKey) merges them
   into a runtime biome object compatible with the existing
   render pipeline.

   The factory runs at module init time, populating BIOMES with
   all 96 city/sport combinations. Legacy environmental biomes
   (grass, desert, ice, volcanic, etc.) and seasonal venues
   (jungle, autumn, neon, halloween, etc.) remain in the array
   as-is, representing "barnstorming" or special-event venues.
   ============================================================ */

/* === v28.10: EFFECT_TYPES — third axis of stadium variation ===
   Each effect optionally overrides the city's ambient particle type
   and adds a thematic scatter overlay that re-skins the venue. The
   'default' effect leaves the city palette untouched; other effects
   produce themed venues like "Foundry", "Underdig", "Hallowtide", etc.
   Inspired by the original legacy biomes which are now subsumed by
   the factory.

   Effect overrides apply via merging — the city's base scatter is
   PRESERVED but augmented with the effect's added scatter layer.
   So a "Phoenix Sandstone Foundry Court" still has Phoenix's pebbles
   and cacti AND the Foundry's lava cracks and embers. The compounding
   gives each city/effect pair a distinct character.

   12 cities × 8 sports × 9 effects = 864 stadium configurations. The
   stadium is picked per-match via SIM_RNG; the lore page shows only
   the 'default' effect grid (96 entries) by default, with a picker
   that lets users browse other effect overlays (see Lore tab UI). */

/* Build a single biome from city + sport + effect.
   id format: "{cityKey}_{sportKey}" (default effect) or "{cityKey}_{sportKey}_{effectKey}".
   name format: "{City} {Adjective} {Venue}" (default) or "{City} {Adjective} {EffectPrefix} {Venue}".
   Scatter is merged: city.scatter + effect.scatterAdd.
   Ambient/sky/accents from effect override city when set. */
function _buildBiome(cityKey, sportKey, effectKey) {
  const city = CITY_PALETTES[cityKey];
  const sport = SPORT_TYPES[sportKey];
  const effect = EFFECT_TYPES[effectKey || "default"];
  if (!city || !sport || !effect) return null;
  const isDefault = (effectKey || "default") === "default";

  // Build merged scatter — start with city's base, add effect's overlay
  let mergedScatter = city.scatter;
  if (effect.scatterAdd) {
    mergedScatter = Object.assign({}, city.scatter, effect.scatterAdd);
  }

  // Compose name: skip effect prefix when it's the default effect
  const namePart = effect.namePrefix ? (effect.namePrefix + " ") : "";
  const name = city.name + " " + city.adjective + " " + namePart + sport.venue;
  const id = isDefault
    ? cityKey + "_" + sportKey
    : cityKey + "_" + sportKey + "_" + effectKey;

  return {
    id, name,
    emoji: effect.emoji || sport.emoji,
    floorTop: city.floorTop,
    floorBot: city.floorBot,
    stripeHue: city.stripeHue,
    scatter: mergedScatter,
    accents: effect.accentOverride || city.accents,
    skyTint: effect.skyTintOverride || city.skyTint,
    ambient: effect.ambientOverride !== null ? effect.ambientOverride : city.ambient,
    courtType: sport.courtType,
    // Metadata
    cityKey, sportKey, effectKey: effectKey || "default",
    cityName: city.name, cityCode: city.code,
    cityLore: city.lore, sportLore: sport.lore, effectLore: effect.lore,
  };
}

/* Generate all combinations: 12 cities × 8 sports × 9 effects = 864 stadiums. */
const CITY_KEYS   = Object.keys(CITY_PALETTES);
const SPORT_KEYS  = Object.keys(SPORT_TYPES);
const EFFECT_KEYS = Object.keys(EFFECT_TYPES);
for (const ck of CITY_KEYS) {
  for (const sk of SPORT_KEYS) {
    for (const ek of EFFECT_KEYS) {
      const b = _buildBiome(ck, sk, ek);
      if (b) BIOMES.push(b);
    }
  }
}

const BIOME_BY_ID = Object.fromEntries(BIOMES.map(b => [b.id, b]));


/* Pick a biome for a match. Uses BIOME_RNG (independent stream) so
   biome variety doesn't shift combat outcomes. */
function pickBiomeForMatch() {
  return BIOMES[Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * BIOMES.length)];
}

/* Generate decorative scatter for a match: an array of `{type, x, y, c1, c2,
   r, rot}` records. The coordinates avoid the spawn lanes (where knights
   stand at round start) and the center circle (where the Power Core lives).
   Generated once per match with BIOME_RNG (independent stream) so the
   layout is stable BUT doesn't affect combat outcomes. */
function generateBiomeScatter(biome) {
  if (!biome) return [];
  const out = [];
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW, H = COMBAT.arenaH;
  const cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2;
  const SPAWN_KEEPOUT = 80;
  const CORE_KEEPOUT = 75;
  for (const [typeKey, def] of Object.entries(biome.scatter || {})) {
    for (let i = 0; i < def.count; i++) {
      // Rejection sampling — keep clear zones spawn-free
      let x, y, tries = 0;
      do {
        x = Math.round(BIOME_RNG.next() * (W - 16) + 8);
        y = Math.round(BIOME_RNG.next() * (H - 16) + 8);
        tries++;
        if (tries > 8) break;
        const dxC = x - cx, dyC = y - cy;
        const distCenter = Math.hypot(dxC, dyC);
        if (distCenter < CORE_KEEPOUT) continue;
        // Avoid spawn columns (left + right edges)
        if (x < SPAWN_KEEPOUT || x > W - SPAWN_KEEPOUT) {
          // Allow some on the edges (only a fraction)
          if (BIOME_RNG.next() > 0.3) continue;
        }
        break;
      } while (true);
      out.push({
        type: typeKey,
        x, y,
        c1: def.color1, c2: def.color2,
        r: 1 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 3),
        rot: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
      });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/* Generate cloud shadows for a match. 2-3 large soft rectangles that drift
   diagonally across the field. Uses BIOME_RNG for the same isolation reason. */
function generateCloudShadows() {
  const clouds = [];
  const count = 2 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 2);
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    clouds.push({
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * COMBAT.arenaW * 1.5 - COMBAT.arenaW * 0.25,
      y: BIOME_RNG.next() * COMBAT.arenaH * 1.2 - COMBAT.arenaH * 0.1,
      w: 280 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 220,
      h: 140 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 120,
      vx: 0.18 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.18,
      vy: -0.04 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.08,
      alpha: 0.07 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.05,
    });
  }
  return clouds;
}

/* Tick cloud shadows — drift them and wrap when they exit. Called from
   tickMatch each frame. */
function tickCloudShadows(m) {
  if (!m.clouds) return;
  for (const c of m.clouds) {
    c.x += c.vx;
    c.y += c.vy;
    // Wrap when fully out the right side
    if (c.x > COMBAT.arenaW + 100) c.x = -c.w - 100;
    if (c.y > COMBAT.arenaH + 100) c.y = -c.h - 100;
    if (c.y < -c.h - 100) c.y = COMBAT.arenaH + 100;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   AMBIENT PARTICLES — biome-driven atmospheric effect.

   Snow falls in the Frozen Rink, embers rise in Volcanic Lot,
   petals drift in Cherry Blossom Garden, heat shimmer wavers in
   the Desert Pit. Stadium Pitch has no ambient (clear day).

   Particles are tracked in `m.ambient[]` with {x, y, vx, vy, life,
   r, type}. They are recycled when life expires or they exit the
   arena bounds. Cap at ~80 particles total to keep the cost
   bounded. Particles use BIOME_RNG since they're cosmetic-only.
   ============================================================ */
const AMBIENT_CAP = 80;

function generateAmbientParticles(biome) {
  if (!biome || !biome.ambient) return [];
  const particles = [];
  const initialCount = biome.ambient === "shimmer" ? 30 : 60;
  for (let i = 0; i < initialCount; i++) {
    particles.push(_spawnAmbientParticle(biome.ambient, true));
  }
  return particles;
}

/* Spawn a single particle. If `existing` is true, it spawns anywhere on the
   arena (so the field starts already populated). Otherwise it spawns at the
   recycle origin (top for snow/petal, bottom for ember). */
function _spawnAmbientParticle(kind, existing) {
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW, H = COMBAT.arenaH;
  if (kind === "snow") {
    return {
      type: "snow",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : -10,
      vx: -0.15 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.30,    // slow horizontal drift
      vy: 0.45 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.50,     // gentle fall
      r: 0.8 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.4,
      life: 9999,                              // recycled by bounds, not life
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,  // for sway
    };
  }
  if (kind === "ember") {
    return {
      type: "ember",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : H + 10,
      vx: -0.25 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.50,
      vy: -0.40 - BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.45,    // float up
      r: 1.0 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.5,
      life: 80 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 60),
      maxLife: 0,  // set below
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
  }
  if (kind === "petal") {
    return {
      type: "petal",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : -10,
      vx: 0.20 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.45,     // drift right with breeze
      vy: 0.30 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.45,
      r: 1.5 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.5,
      life: 9999,
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
      rotSpeed: -0.04 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.08,
      rot: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
  }
  if (kind === "shimmer") {
    // Heat-shimmer dot — short-lived, just a brief vertical wobble
    return {
      type: "shimmer",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: 80 + BIOME_RNG.next() * (H - 100),
      vx: 0,
      vy: -0.08 - BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.10,
      r: 0.6,
      life: 30 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 30),
      maxLife: 0,
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
  }
  if (kind === "bubble") {
    // Underwater bubble — rises slowly, slight wobble
    return {
      type: "bubble",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : H + 10,
      vx: -0.08 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.16,
      vy: -0.30 - BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.30,
      r: 1.0 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.8,
      life: 9999,
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
  }
  if (kind === "leaf") {
    // Drifting leaf — slow rotation + gentle fall
    return {
      type: "leaf",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : -10,
      vx: 0.10 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.30,
      vy: 0.20 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.30,
      r: 1.5 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.5,
      life: 9999,
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
      rot: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
      rotSpeed: -0.03 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.06,
    };
  }
  if (kind === "firefly") {
    // Glowing dot that drifts in a meandering path, pulsing in brightness
    return {
      type: "firefly",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : 80 + BIOME_RNG.next() * (H - 160),
      vx: -0.20 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.40,
      vy: -0.15 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.30,
      r: 1.0,
      life: 200 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 100),
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
  }
  if (kind === "spark") {
    // Neon spark — bright fast streak, fades quick
    return {
      type: "spark",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : H + 10,
      vx: -0.15 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.30,
      vy: -1.0 - BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.5,
      r: 0.8,
      life: 30 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 25),
      maxLife: 0,
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
      hue: BIOME_RNG.next() < 0.5 ? "magenta" : "cyan",
    };
  }
  if (kind === "bat") {
    // Flapping silhouette — flies across with sinusoidal wing motion
    return {
      type: "bat",
      x: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * W : -20,
      y: 40 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 200,
      vx: 0.7 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.6,
      vy: -0.10 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 0.20,
      r: 1.5,
      life: 9999,
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
  }
  if (kind === "drip") {
    // Cave water drip — short fall, fades as it lands
    return {
      type: "drip",
      x: BIOME_RNG.next() * W,
      y: existing ? BIOME_RNG.next() * H : -10,
      vx: 0,
      vy: 1.5 + BIOME_RNG.next() * 1.0,
      r: 0.8,
      life: 80 + Math.floor(BIOME_RNG.next() * 80),
      phase: BIOME_RNG.next() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
  }
  return null;
}

function tickAmbientParticles(m) {
  if (!m.biome || !m.biome.ambient) return;
  if (!m.ambient) m.ambient = generateAmbientParticles(m.biome);
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW, H = COMBAT.arenaH;
  const kind = m.biome.ambient;

  // Iterate backwards so we can splice safely
  for (let i = m.ambient.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const p = m.ambient[i];
    p.x += p.vx;
    p.y += p.vy;
    p.phase += 0.04;
    if (p.rotSpeed) p.rot += p.rotSpeed;

    // Sway for snow + petal + leaf
    if (p.type === "snow" || p.type === "petal" || p.type === "leaf") {
      p.x += Math.sin(p.phase) * 0.18;
    }
    // Bubbles wobble side to side as they rise
    if (p.type === "bubble") {
      p.x += Math.sin(p.phase * 1.2) * 0.20;
    }
    // Bats undulate (sin curve in their flight path)
    if (p.type === "bat") {
      p.y += Math.sin(p.phase * 0.5) * 0.12;
    }
    // Fireflies meander randomly — small velocity perturbation each frame
    if (p.type === "firefly") {
      p.vx += (Math.sin(p.phase * 0.3) - p.vx) * 0.04;
      p.vy += (Math.cos(p.phase * 0.5) - p.vy) * 0.04;
    }

    let recycle = false;
    if (p.life !== undefined) p.life--;

    // Bounds-based recycle
    if (p.type === "snow" || p.type === "petal" || p.type === "leaf") {
      if (p.y > H + 8 || p.x < -8 || p.x > W + 8) recycle = true;
    } else if (p.type === "ember" || p.type === "bubble" || p.type === "spark") {
      if (p.y < -8 || (p.life !== undefined && p.life <= 0)) recycle = true;
    } else if (p.type === "shimmer") {
      if (p.life <= 0 || p.y < 60) recycle = true;
    } else if (p.type === "firefly") {
      // Fireflies wander; recycle on life expiry or off-screen
      if (p.life <= 0 || p.x < -20 || p.x > W + 20 || p.y < -20 || p.y > H + 20) recycle = true;
    } else if (p.type === "bat") {
      if (p.x > W + 30) recycle = true;
    } else if (p.type === "drip") {
      if (p.y > H + 8 || p.life <= 0) recycle = true;
    }

    if (recycle) {
      // Replace with a fresh particle at the spawn edge
      const replacement = _spawnAmbientParticle(kind, false);
      if (replacement) m.ambient[i] = replacement;
      else m.ambient.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }

  // Cap (defensive — shouldn't grow but just in case)
  if (m.ambient.length > AMBIENT_CAP) m.ambient.length = AMBIENT_CAP;
}

/* === v28.8: Tactical formations ===
   Replaces the static horizontal line with role-aware formations.
   Six patterns inspired by real-world military / sports formations:
     - line:     classic horizontal line (default fallback)
     - wedge:    V-shape with tank at the point, ranged at back
     - phalanx:  tanks front, brawlers mid, snipers/healers back
     - echelon:  diagonal stagger, faster knights leading the angle
     - killbox:  spread tanks/brawlers wide, snipers anchor center
     - flyingv:  speedsters lead, tanks flank, healer/sniper trail

   Each function takes (players, baseX, cy, side) and returns an array
   of [dx, dy] offsets from baseX (positive = toward field center) and
   from cy. The side parameter mirrors X for the right-side team so
   formations always face the enemy.

   Selection is deterministic via SIM_RNG so reproducible per match.
   Players are sorted by role priority before assignment so formations
   place the right ROLE at the right SLOT regardless of roster order. */
const FORMATIONS = {
  line(n) {
    const spacing = 70;
    const sy = -(n - 1) * spacing / 2;
    return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => [0, sy + i * spacing]);
  },
  wedge(n) {
    // V-shape with tank at the point (slot 0), opening backwards.
    // Each subsequent slot is +60 dx behind, +/-50 dy alternating.
    const slots = [];
    slots.push([0, 0]);  // point — tank
    for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {
      const tier = Math.ceil(i / 2);
      const upper = (i % 2 === 1);
      slots.push([tier * 60, (upper ? -1 : 1) * tier * 50]);
    }
    return slots;
  },
  phalanx(n) {
    // Three ranks: front (tanks/brawlers), mid (strikers/speed), back (sniper/healer/grenadier).
    // Front rank close to enemy, back rank deep. Y-spread within each rank.
    const slots = [];
    const tiers = Math.min(3, Math.ceil(n / 2));
    let placed = 0;
    for (let r = 0; r < tiers; r++) {
      const remaining = n - placed;
      const inThisRank = (r < tiers - 1) ? Math.ceil(remaining / (tiers - r)) : remaining;
      const dx = r * 70;
      const sy = -(inThisRank - 1) * 60 / 2;
      for (let i = 0; i < inThisRank; i++) {
        slots.push([dx, sy + i * 60]);
        placed++;
      }
    }
    return slots;
  },
  echelon(n) {
    // Diagonal stagger — each slot offset by (+50, +60) from previous.
    const slots = [];
    const sy = -(n - 1) * 30;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      slots.push([i * 50, sy + i * 60]);
    }
    return slots;
  },
  killbox(n) {
    // Two spread flankers far up + back rank centered. Tank slot 0 anchors center back.
    const slots = [];
    if (n >= 1) slots.push([0, 0]);                    // anchor back-center (tank)
    if (n >= 2) slots.push([80, -180]);                // upper flank striker
    if (n >= 3) slots.push([80,  180]);                // lower flank striker
    if (n >= 4) slots.push([40, -100]);                // upper mid brawler
    if (n >= 5) slots.push([40,  100]);                // lower mid
    if (n >= 6) slots.push([-30, -50]);                // back upper sniper
    if (n >= 7) slots.push([-30,  50]);                // back lower healer
    while (slots.length < n) slots.push([20 * slots.length, 0]);
    return slots;
  },
  flyingv(n) {
    // Speedsters lead at +90 dx, tanks flank at +30, healer/sniper trail at -30.
    // Asymmetric V — opposite of wedge (which has tank at point).
    const slots = [];
    if (n >= 1) slots.push([90, 0]);                   // speed point
    if (n >= 2) slots.push([30, -80]);                 // upper flank tank
    if (n >= 3) slots.push([30,  80]);                 // lower flank tank
    if (n >= 4) slots.push([0, -40]);                  // upper mid striker
    if (n >= 5) slots.push([0,  40]);                  // lower mid striker
    if (n >= 6) slots.push([-50, 0]);                  // back center sniper
    if (n >= 7) slots.push([-90, 0]);                  // far back healer
    while (slots.length < n) slots.push([-30 * slots.length, 0]);
    return slots;
  },
};

/* Sort players by tactical role priority for formation slot assignment.
   Lower priority value = placed in earlier slot (typically frontline).
   Tanks first (slot 0 = "point" or "anchor"), then brawlers, strikers,
   speedsters, snipers, grenadiers, healers (always last/safest slot). */
const FORMATION_ROLE_PRIO = {
  tank: 0, brawler: 1, striker: 2, speedster: 3,
  sniper: 4, grenadier: 5, healer: 6,
};

function _formationSortPlayers(players, formationName) {
  const sorted = [...players];
  // For flying-V, speedsters get priority slot 0 (the point)
  if (formationName === "flyingv") {
    sorted.sort((a, b) => {
      const pa = a.position === "speedster" ? -1 : (FORMATION_ROLE_PRIO[a.position] ?? 99);
      const pb = b.position === "speedster" ? -1 : (FORMATION_ROLE_PRIO[b.position] ?? 99);
      return pa - pb;
    });
  } else if (formationName === "killbox") {
    // Killbox: tank center-back (slot 0), then strikers on flanks
    sorted.sort((a, b) => {
      const pa = a.position === "tank" ? -1 : (FORMATION_ROLE_PRIO[a.position] ?? 99);
      const pb = b.position === "tank" ? -1 : (FORMATION_ROLE_PRIO[b.position] ?? 99);
      return pa - pb;
    });
  } else {
    sorted.sort((a, b) => (FORMATION_ROLE_PRIO[a.position] ?? 99) - (FORMATION_ROLE_PRIO[b.position] ?? 99));
  }
  return sorted;
}

/* Pick a formation name deterministically per team per round.
   Uses SIM_RNG so the same seed produces the same formation. */
function _pickFormation(teamId, roundIndex) {
  const names = ["line", "wedge", "phalanx", "echelon", "killbox", "flyingv"];
  // Bias slightly toward line + phalanx (the safer/standard ones).
  const weights = [3, 2, 3, 2, 1, 1];
  let total = 0;
  for (const w of weights) total += w;
  let pick = SIM_RNG.next() * total;
  for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
    pick -= weights[i];
    if (pick <= 0) return names[i];
  }
  return "line";
}

function layoutTeam(players, side) {
  const cx = COMBAT.arenaW / 2;
  const cy = COMBAT.arenaH / 2;
  const baseX = side === "left" ? cx - COMBAT.spawnRadius : cx + COMBAT.spawnRadius;

  // === v28.8: Pick formation per team per round ===
  // Stamp on the match so the team panels can display the formation
  // name to viewers ("KIT in PHALANX vs OWL in WEDGE").
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const teamId = players[0]?.teamId;
  const formationName = _pickFormation(teamId, m?.currentRoundIndex || 0);
  if (m && teamId) {
    m._formations = m._formations || {};
    m._formations[teamId] = formationName;
  }
  const formationFn = FORMATIONS[formationName] || FORMATIONS.line;
  const slots = formationFn(players.length);
  // Sort players by role priority so the right ROLE goes to the right SLOT
  const ordered = _formationSortPlayers(players, formationName);

  // Side: left team's "+dx" (toward enemy) is +X. Right team mirrors.
  const dirX = side === "left" ? 1 : -1;

  for (let i = 0; i < ordered.length; i++) {
    const p = ordered[i];
    const [dx, dy] = slots[i] || [0, 0];
    p.hp = p.stats.hp;
    p.alive = true;
