    // dx in formation = "toward enemy"; for left team baseX+dx, for right baseX-dx
    p.x = baseX + dirX * dx + R(-6, 6);
    p.y = cy + dy + R(-4, 4);
    // Clamp to safe spawn area to avoid clipping
    p.x = Math.max(40, Math.min(COMBAT.arenaW - 40, p.x));
    p.y = Math.max(40, Math.min(COMBAT.arenaH - 40, p.y));
    p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0;
    p.target = null;
    p.attackCooldown = 0;
    p.facing = side === "left" ? 1 : -1;
    p.stunned = 0;
    p.balloonsPopped = 0;
    p.aiState = "IDLE";
    p.aiStateFrames = 0;
    p.aiNextRetargetFrame = 0;
    p.meleeFallback = (typeof pickMeleeFallback === "function") ? pickMeleeFallback(p) : null;
    p.attackedBy = null;
    p.attackedByExpires = 0;
    p._tauntedLastKnightThisRound = false;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   ITEM TRIGGERS — fired by "start" or "lowHP" conditions
   Sets temporary boostMods or applies one-shot effects.
   ============================================================ */
function applyItemTrigger(p) {
  if (!p.item || p.itemUsed) return;
  const id = p.item.id || (p.item.name && ITEMS.find(i => i.name === p.item.name)?.id);
  switch (id) {
    case "booster":
      p.boostMods = { spd: 0.5, life: 240 }; // 12s @ 50ms tick
      feed("item", `💨 ${p.name} engages booster pack!`);
      break;
    case "powercell":
      p.boostMods = { dmgMult: 1.25, life: 300 }; // 15s
      feed("item", `🔋 ${p.name} overcharges power cell!`);
      break;
    case "stim":
      p.hp = Math.min(p.stats.hp, p.hp + 25);
      feed("item", `🍶 ${p.name} jabs a healing stim — ${p.hp}/${p.stats.hp} HP.`);
      break;
    case "grenade": {
      // Hit nearest living enemy for 18
      const m = LEAGUE.match;
      if (!m) break;
      const enemies = (p.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamBplayers : m.teamAplayers).filter(e => e.alive);
      let best = null, bestD = Infinity;
      for (const e of enemies) { const d = distance(p, e); if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = e; } }
      if (best) {
        const grenadeDmg = 18;
        best.hp -= grenadeDmg;
        m.floats.push({ x: best.x, y: best.y - 14, text: `-${grenadeDmg}💥`, life: 30, color: "#f7c948" });
        feed("item", `🧨 ${p.name} chucks a frag grenade at ${best.name}!`);
        if (best.hp <= 0) {
          best.hp = 0;
          best.alive = false;
          best.balloonsPopped = 3;
          best.lives = Math.max(0, (best.lives || 0) - 1);
          updateKillStreak(p, best, m);
          p.career.kills++;
          m.killsByTeam[p.teamId]++;
          if (best.lives > 0) {
            feed("kill", `💥 ${p.name}'s grenade KOs ${best.name}! (${best.lives} ${best.lives===1?"life":"lives"} left)`);
            m.floats.push({ x: best.x, y: best.y - 16, text: "DOWN", life: 24, color: "#ef4d4d" });
          } else {
            best.deceased = true;
            best.deceasedSeason = LEAGUE.season;
            best.deceasedBy = p.name + " (grenade)";
            LEAGUE.graveyard.push({
              name: best.name, teamId: best.teamId, position: best.position,
              season: LEAGUE.season, killer: p.name + " (grenade)",
              killerTeam: p.teamId, slot: m.slot, round: m.currentRoundIndex,
            });
            m.corpses.push({ x: best.x, y: best.y, color: TEAM_BY_ID[best.teamId].color });
            dropGear(best, m);
            feed("kill", `💥 ${p.name} EXPLODES ${best.name} with the grenade!`);
          }
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    case "visor":
      // passive, handled in damage calc — just mark as "active" for the match
      // (no boostMods — crit bonus is read directly from the equipped item)
      break;
  }
  p.itemUsed = true;
}

/* ============================================================
   AI / TICK — team-aware
   - Targets only living opponents
   - Approaches & attacks within hit range
   - Crit + RNG damage roll
   ============================================================ */
/* ============================================================
   AI / TICK — state-machine driven
   States per player:
     ENGAGE      — pursue target, attack when in range
     SEEK_ITEM   — walk toward a desirable nearby drop
     FLEE        — low HP, run away from threats
     FINISH_OFF  — stick on a target that's actively attacking us (mercy)
   Combat:
     - Melee weapons (range <= 30) hit instantly when in range
     - Ranged weapons spawn projectiles with accuracy = f(distance, target velocity)
     - Projectiles are fire-and-forget; targets can dodge
   ============================================================ */
function tickMatch() {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m || m.phase !== "round") return;

  m.frame++;
  m.roundFrame++;

  // Slow-mo countdown — if we've triggered it, count it down per tick so
  // the loop's effective speed multiplier knows when to release back to
  // normal. The actual speed reduction lives in the main loop.
  if (m.slowMoFrames > 0) m.slowMoFrames--;

  // POWER CORE — periodic objective spawned at arena center.
  tickPowerCore(m);

  // === v28: Mode-specific tick (KOTH/CTF/Tag) ===
  // Runs alongside power-core. Updates crown/flag/freeze state and
  // accumulates mode scores. Deathmatch is unaffected.
  if (typeof tickGameMode === "function") tickGameMode(m);

  // === v28.1: Refresh per-bot mode roles based on current state ===
  // Cheap (one sort + iteration per team). Runs every frame so role
  // changes (carrier killed, flag dropped, It changed) propagate to
  // bot behavior without delay.
  if (m.gameMode && m.gameMode !== "deathmatch" && typeof MODE_AI !== "undefined") {
    MODE_AI.assignRoles(m);
  }

  // === v28.2: Team coordination pass for deathmatch ===
  // Computes focus-fire targets, frontline/backline assignments, and
  // in-trouble flags so per-bot AI can make team-aware decisions.
  // Runs every frame; cost is dominated by enemy-target scoring which
  // is O(N²) for N≈14 — negligible.
  if (typeof TEAM_AI !== "undefined") {
    TEAM_AI.computeTeamState(m);
  }

  // Cloud shadows drift across the field
  tickCloudShadows(m);

  // Ambient particles (snow, embers, petals, shimmer per biome)
  tickAmbientParticles(m);

  // RESUPPLY — periodic Health Pack drops to keep long matches sustainable
  maybeResupplyHealth(m);

  // Decay floats and blood
  for (let i = m.floats.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    m.floats[i].life--;
    m.floats[i].y -= 0.7;
    if (m.floats[i].life <= 0) m.floats.splice(i, 1);
  }
  for (let i = m.blood.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    m.blood[i].life--;
    if (m.blood[i].life <= 0) m.blood.splice(i, 1);
  }

  // Tick projectiles BEFORE players move so dodging is fair
  tickProjectiles(m);

  const allA = m.teamAplayers, allB = m.teamBplayers;

  // Per-player AI tick
  for (const list of [allA, allB]) {
    const enemies = list === allA ? allB : allA;
    const allies = list;
    const enemyAlive = enemies.filter(e => e.alive);
    for (const p of list) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;

      // Decay timers
      if (p.stunned > 0) p.stunned--;
      if (p.boostMods) {
        p.boostMods.life--;
        if (p.boostMods.life <= 0) p.boostMods = null;
      }
      if (p.attackedByExpires > 0) p.attackedByExpires--;
      else p.attackedBy = null;

      // Low-HP item trigger
      if (p.item && !p.itemUsed && p.item.trigger === "lowHP") {
        const hpFrac = p.hp / p.stats.hp;
        const itemId = p.item.id || (p.item.name && ITEMS.find(i => i.name === p.item.name)?.id);
        const threshold = itemId === "grenade" ? 0.25 : 0.30;
        if (hpFrac < threshold && hpFrac > 0) applyItemTrigger(p);
      }

      // Decide AI state
      decideAIState(p, enemyAlive, m);

      // Execute state
      executeAIState(p, enemyAlive, m);

      // Apply dodge nudge AFTER state movement so it weaves additively
      applyDodgeNudge(p, m);
    }
  }

  // Round end condition — varies by game mode
  const aAlive = allA.filter(p => p.alive).length;
  const bAlive = allB.filter(p => p.alive).length;
  const mode = m.gameMode || "deathmatch";
  if (mode === "deathmatch") {
    if (aAlive === 0 || bAlive === 0 || m.roundFrame >= COMBAT.roundMaxFrames) {
      finalizeRound();
    }
  } else {
    // Continuous-mode matches end ONLY when the mode reports a winner.
    // Tag mode also ends if everyone's dead. Time-cap is much higher
    // (10x roundMaxFrames) so the match can run to a natural finish.
    if (m.modeWinner) {
      // Mode declares the winner — synthesize a "round" to record
      m.roundsWonA = (m.modeWinner === m.teamAId) ? m.roundsToWin : 0;
      m.roundsWonB = (m.modeWinner === m.teamBId) ? m.roundsToWin : 0;
      if (m.modeWinner === "draw") { m.roundsWonA = 0; m.roundsWonB = 0; }
      finalizeRound();
    } else if (m.roundFrame >= COMBAT.roundMaxFrames * 8) {
      // Hard time cap — extremely long match, force whoever has more score
      if ((m.modeScoreA || 0) > (m.modeScoreB || 0)) m.modeWinner = m.teamAId;
      else if ((m.modeScoreB || 0) > (m.modeScoreA || 0)) m.modeWinner = m.teamBId;
      else m.modeWinner = "draw";
      finalizeRound();
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   AI STATE SELECTION (v28 rewrite)

   The previous version had three structural bugs:

     1. FLEE could persist indefinitely. If HP < 25% and the
        nearest enemy was always more than 40px away, the bot
        kept fleeing — straight at whatever wall was closest.
     2. ENGAGE's target lookup ran only when target was null/dead,
        so a bot that lost line-of-sight or got knocked away from
        their target would chase across the map even if a new,
        closer target had appeared.
     3. There was no neutral state: an alone-against-the-clock
        survivor (or a winning-team straggler) had no good state
        to fall into besides ENGAGE-with-no-target, where
        executeAIState would `break` and the bot froze.

   Fixes:
     - IDLE state. Default starting state. From IDLE we decide
       what to do this tick. ENGAGE/FLEE etc. eventually drop
       back to IDLE when their goals are satisfied.
     - FLEE is time-capped to 90 frames. After that, even at low
       HP, the bot returns to IDLE → ENGAGE (better to die fighting
       than die against a wall).
     - Target reacquisition runs every 60 frames regardless of state.
     - MELEE state. When an enemy gets within COMBAT.meleeSwitchRange
       and the bot is wielding a ranged weapon, it switches to MELEE
       which uses the cached meleeFallback profile for resolveAttack.
   ============================================================ */
/* ============================================================
   MODE AI (v28.1 rebuild)

   Per-game-mode AI decision-making. The previous version had every
   bot do the same thing (carrier→flee, defender→shoot anyone,
   It→chase nearest). That meant matches were dull stalemates of
   uncoordinated solo plays.

   This rebuild assigns each bot a role per mode based on real-world
   sport tactics, then routes decisions through that role:

     KOTH (basketball half-court possession):
       - carrier       — kites toward safe space, avoids pressure
       - escort        — screens between carrier and nearest enemy
       - pressure      — engages enemy carrier or distant threats
       - chaser        — when crown is uncarried, races for it
       - zone          — defensive holding pattern when no clear job

     CTF (football handoff + ultimate frisbee zone defense):
       - carrier       — runs toward own base with threat awareness
       - escort_lead   — runs ahead of carrier, picks off defenders
       - escort_rear   — trails carrier, blocks pursuit
       - chaser        — closest 2 defenders pursue carrier directly
       - zone_defender — camps own base perimeter to intercept caps
       - retriever     — when flag is uncarried, fastest runs for it

     Tag (predator-prey + freeze tag):
       - it_predator   — predictive aim-ahead chase, picks easy targets
       - prey_flock    — flees while staying near other prey (herd safety)
       - prey_break    — when isolated/cornered, scatters away from herd
       - prey_shielded — uses frozen bots / terrain as line-of-sight breaks

   Roles are reassigned every 30 frames OR when a triggering event
   happens (carrier killed, flag dropped, It changed). Within a role,
   the bot's per-tick movement and target selection follow that role's
   pattern. State and target are still set on the player object so the
   normal executeAIState machinery can run; the role just shapes WHAT
   target and WHICH state.
   ============================================================ */
/* ============================================================
   TEAM AI (v28.2 deathmatch rework)

   Per-team coordination pass. The previous AI was purely per-bot:
   each knight independently picked a target and a state. This led
   to spread-out, uncoordinated combat — no focus fire, no role
   positioning, no support patterns.

   This module runs once per frame per team, computes team-level
   intelligence, and stores it on m._teamAI = { [teamId]: { ... } }.
   The per-bot decideAIState then reads from this to pick targets
   and states that match the team's plan.

   Real-world references:
     - Focus fire (MOBAs / FPS): teams target one enemy at a time
       to remove threats from the fight. Lowest-HP enemy = lowest
       cost to kill = highest priority.
     - Frontline / backline (Overwatch / RTS): tanks engage close,
       ranged stays at distance. We use position archetype to
       assign frontline/backline per bot.
     - Outnumber detection (CS team play): if you have local
       numbers advantage at a corner, push it. If you're outnumbered,
       pull back toward your team.
     - Sticky targeting (combat games): commit to a target for a
       window of frames before re-evaluating, so bots don't ping-
       pong between half-dead enemies.
     - Cover use (any tactical shooter): when below-average HP,
       seek terrain that breaks line-of-sight from threats.
     - Healer escort (any support-role game): healers position
       within range of the most-wounded teammate, behind frontline.

   The output m._teamAI[teamId] is a snapshot:
     {
       focusTarget: <enemy bot> | null,        // who the team is collectively targeting
       frontline: [<bots>],                    // tanks/brawlers/strikers
       backline: [<bots>],                     // snipers/grenadiers/healers
       inTrouble: { [knightId]: true },        // teammates needing support
       teammatesByDistTo: (enemy) => [...],    // helper for flanking
       teamCenter: { x, y },                   // average position
       enemyCenter: { x, y },                  // average enemy position
       advanceLine: number                     // x-coord teammate front-line should hold
     }
   ============================================================ */
const TEAM_AI = (function() {

  function _isFrontline(p) {
    // Frontline positions: those that close to engage. Tank/Brawler always.
    // Striker/Speed are mid (frontline at full HP, backline if hurt).
    if (p.position === "tank" || p.position === "brawl") return true;
    if (p.position === "striker" || p.position === "speed") {
      return (p.hp / p.stats.hp) > 0.5;  // healthy strikers/speedsters push
    }
    return false;
  }

  function _isBackline(p) {
    if (p.position === "sniper" || p.position === "grenadier" || p.position === "healer") return true;
    if (p.position === "striker" || p.position === "speed") {
      return (p.hp / p.stats.hp) <= 0.5;  // wounded strikers/speedsters fall back
    }
    return false;
  }

  /* Score an enemy as a focus-fire target. Lower-HP, higher-streak,
     and isolated-from-allies enemies are higher priority. */
  function _scoreFocusTarget(enemy, alliesOfEnemy) {
    const hpFrac = enemy.hp / enemy.stats.hp;
    // Lower HP = higher score (steeper at low HP)
    let score = (1 - hpFrac) * 50;
    // On-streak enemies are extra-priority threats
    const streak = enemy.career.currentStreak || 0;
    if (streak >= 3) score += streak * 4;
    // Healers are high-value (kill the support first — RTS rule of thumb)
    if (enemy.position === "healer") score += 25;
    // Snipers are also high-priority (back-line damage source)
    if (enemy.position === "sniper") score += 12;
    // Isolated enemies are easier to kill (no nearby allies to interfere)
    let nearestAllyD = Infinity;
    for (const ally of alliesOfEnemy) {
      if (ally === enemy || !ally.alive) continue;
      const d = distance(enemy, ally);
      if (d < nearestAllyD) nearestAllyD = d;
    }
    if (nearestAllyD > 150) score += 15;
    else if (nearestAllyD > 80) score += 8;
    return score;
  }

  /* Public — called once per frame from tickMatch before per-bot AI. */
  function computeTeamState(m) {
    if (!m) return;
    if (!m._teamAI) m._teamAI = {};

    // Build per-team aggregates
    const aliveA = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => p.alive);
    const aliveB = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => p.alive);

    function buildFor(teamId, allies, enemies) {
      // Centroid
      let cx = 0, cy = 0;
      for (const p of allies) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
      const teamCenter = allies.length > 0
        ? { x: cx / allies.length, y: cy / allies.length }
        : { x: COMBAT.arenaW / 2, y: COMBAT.arenaH / 2 };
      let ecx = 0, ecy = 0;
      for (const e of enemies) { ecx += e.x; ecy += e.y; }
      const enemyCenter = enemies.length > 0
        ? { x: ecx / enemies.length, y: ecy / enemies.length }
        : { x: COMBAT.arenaW / 2, y: COMBAT.arenaH / 2 };

      // Frontline / backline classification
      const frontline = [], backline = [];
      for (const p of allies) {
        if (_isFrontline(p)) frontline.push(p);
        else if (_isBackline(p)) backline.push(p);
        else frontline.push(p);  // default
      }

      // Focus-fire target selection.
      // Stickiness: keep last frame's target if it's still a high-scoring
      // option (within 80% of best score), so bots don't switch every tick.
      const prev = m._teamAI[teamId];
      let focusTarget = null, bestScore = -Infinity;
      const scores = new Map();
      for (const e of enemies) {
        const s = _scoreFocusTarget(e, enemies);
        scores.set(e, s);
        if (s > bestScore) { bestScore = s; focusTarget = e; }
      }
      // Sticky: if previous target is alive and within 80% of best score, keep it.
      if (prev && prev.focusTarget && prev.focusTarget.alive) {
        const prevScore = scores.get(prev.focusTarget) ?? -Infinity;
        if (prevScore >= bestScore * 0.8 && prevScore > -Infinity) {
          focusTarget = prev.focusTarget;
        }
      }

      // In-trouble flags: bots with low HP OR locally outnumbered
      const inTrouble = Object.create(null);
      for (const p of allies) {
        const hpFrac = p.hp / p.stats.hp;
        let nearbyEnemies = 0, nearbyAllies = 0;
        for (const e of enemies) if (distance(p, e) < 200) nearbyEnemies++;
        for (const a of allies) if (a !== p && distance(p, a) < 200) nearbyAllies++;
        const outnumbered = nearbyEnemies > nearbyAllies + 1;
        if (hpFrac < 0.30 || outnumbered) {
          inTrouble[p.id] = true;
        }
      }

      // Advance line — x-coord that frontline should push to. We pull it
      // from "midfield + (enemy-center - midfield) * 0.5" so frontline
      // pushes halfway into enemy half rather than camping at midfield.
      const cxw = COMBAT.arenaW / 2;
      const advanceLine = cxw + (enemyCenter.x - cxw) * 0.5;

      // === v28.4: Last-stand classification ===
      // When teams thin out, behavior should shift:
      //   - lastStand: ≤2 bots alive AND outnumbered → aggressive + cover-using
      //     (bots NEVER rally/flee; clutch behavior, focus fire, prefer cover)
      //   - pursuing:  enemy is ≤2 AND we outnumber them → press the advantage
      //     (push forward, finish them, ignore loose drops)
      //   - normal:    everyone else → standard team coordination
      //
      // The flag is consumed in decideAIState's deathmatch branch where
      // RALLY/FLEE are ordinarily picked. lastStand suppresses both.
      const aliveCount = allies.length;
      const enemyCount = enemies.length;
      const lastStand = aliveCount <= 2 && aliveCount < enemyCount;
      const pursuing = enemyCount <= 2 && aliveCount > enemyCount;

      m._teamAI[teamId] = {
        focusTarget,
        frontline,
        backline,
        inTrouble,
        teamCenter,
        enemyCenter,
        advanceLine,
        lastStand,
        pursuing,
        aliveCount,
        enemyCount,
        // Helper: list of teammates sorted by distance to (x, y)
        teammatesByDistTo(x, y) {
          return [...allies].sort((a, b) => {
            const da = (a.x - x) ** 2 + (a.y - y) ** 2;
            const db = (b.x - x) ** 2 + (b.y - y) ** 2;
            return da - db;
          });
        },
      };
    }

    buildFor(m.teamAId, aliveA, aliveB);
    buildFor(m.teamBId, aliveB, aliveA);
  }

  /* Pick the best target for an individual bot, considering team focus
     fire and the bot's own role. Falls back to nearest if focus target
     is unreachable. */
  function pickTarget(p, enemyAlive, m) {
    if (enemyAlive.length === 0) return null;
    const teamState = m._teamAI && m._teamAI[p.teamId];
    if (!teamState) {
      // No team state — naive nearest
      let nearest = null, nd = Infinity;
      for (const e of enemyAlive) {
        const d = distance(p, e);
        if (d < nd) { nd = d; nearest = e; }
      }
      return nearest;
    }

    const wpn = p.weapon || {};
    const ranged = (wpn.range || 0) > 30;
    const myWpnRange = wpn.range || 220;

    // Focus target: prefer it if reachable. For ranged bots, "reachable"
    // means within 1.3× weapon range. For melee, within ~250px (close
    // enough to chase to in a few seconds).
    const focus = teamState.focusTarget;
    if (focus && focus.alive) {
      const d = distance(p, focus);
      const reachable = ranged ? (d <= myWpnRange * 1.3) : (d <= 250);
      if (reachable) return focus;
    }

    // Sniper-specific: prefer healers in range over team focus
    if (p.position === "sniper") {
      let bestHealer = null, bhd = Infinity;
      for (const e of enemyAlive) {
        if (e.position !== "healer") continue;
        const d = distance(p, e);
        if (d < myWpnRange && d < bhd) { bhd = d; bestHealer = e; }
      }
      if (bestHealer) return bestHealer;
    }

    // Otherwise: nearest enemy
    let nearest = null, nd = Infinity;
    for (const e of enemyAlive) {
      const d = distance(p, e);
      if (d < nd) { nd = d; nearest = e; }
    }
    return nearest;
  }

  /* Look for a "rally point" for an in-trouble bot — the position of the
     nearest healthy teammate (or healer) the bot can retreat toward.
     Returns { x, y } or null if no good option. */
  function findRallyPoint(p, m) {
    const teamState = m._teamAI && m._teamAI[p.teamId];
    if (!teamState) return null;
    const allies = (p.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers)
      .filter(a => a.alive && a !== p);
    // Prefer healers above 30% HP, then any teammate above 50% HP.
    let target = null, bestD = Infinity;
    for (const a of allies) {
      if (a.position !== "healer") continue;
      if ((a.hp / a.stats.hp) < 0.30) continue;
      const d = distance(p, a);
      if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; target = a; }
    }
    if (!target) {
      for (const a of allies) {
        if ((a.hp / a.stats.hp) < 0.50) continue;
        const d = distance(p, a);
        if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; target = a; }
      }
    }
    if (target) {
      // Rally point: position offset slightly toward the team center so we
      // don't crowd the teammate's own engagement.
      const tc = teamState.teamCenter;
      return {
        x: target.x * 0.7 + tc.x * 0.3,
        y: target.y * 0.7 + tc.y * 0.3,
      };
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* Should this bot retreat? Returns true if HP is low AND we have a
     viable rally point AND we're not already in melee. Used by decideAIState
     to substitute FLEE → RALLY (a directed retreat, not panic-flee).

     === v28.4: lastStand suppression ===
     When the team has ≤2 bots alive AND is outnumbered, NEVER rally —
     the team is in clutch territory and needs every bot fighting. */
  function shouldRally(p, nearestEnemyD, m) {
    const teamState = m._teamAI && m._teamAI[p.teamId];
    if (teamState && teamState.lastStand) return false;
    const hpFrac = p.hp / p.stats.hp;
    if (hpFrac >= 0.30) return false;       // not low enough yet
    if (nearestEnemyD < 30) return false;   // already pinned in melee
    const rally = findRallyPoint(p, m);
    return rally !== null;
  }

  return {
    computeTeamState,
    pickTarget,
    findRallyPoint,
    shouldRally,
  };
})();
window.TEAM_AI = TEAM_AI;


const MODE_AI = (function() {

  /* Helper: closest enemy (or any-team) bot to position. */
  function _closestTo(x, y, candidates, skipId) {
    let best = null, bestD = Infinity;
    for (const e of candidates) {
      if (!e.alive) continue;
      if (skipId !== undefined && e.id === skipId) continue;
      const dx = e.x - x, dy = e.y - y;
      const d = dx*dx + dy*dy;
      if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = e; }
    }
    return best;
  }

  /* Helper: angle blend (vector-mean rather than raw atan2 average,
     so 350° and 10° average to 0° not 180°). */
  function _blendAngles(a, b, t) {
    const ax = Math.cos(a), ay = Math.sin(a);
    const bx = Math.cos(b), by = Math.sin(b);
    return Math.atan2(ay * (1-t) + by * t, ax * (1-t) + bx * t);
  }

  /* ============================================================
     KOTH — basketball half-court possession model

     Once a bot picks up the crown, the team forms around it:
       - Closest 2 teammates become escorts (screen the nearest
         enemy threats — they position BETWEEN carrier and threat)
       - All other teammates pressure the furthest-from-carrier
         enemy cluster (force enemies to split attention)

     Enemy team:
       - Closest enemy attacks carrier
       - Other enemies converge but try to flank around escorts

     When crown is uncarried, the closest bot from each team races
     to it (positional lead-running — head to where the crown will
     be, not where it is).
     ============================================================ */
  function assignKothRoles(m) {
    if (!m.crown) return;
    const aliveA = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => p.alive);
    const aliveB = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => p.alive);
    const allAlive = [...aliveA, ...aliveB];
    const carrier = m.crown.carrierId
      ? allAlive.find(p => p.id === m.crown.carrierId)
      : null;

    if (carrier) {
      // === Carrier-team roles ===
      const carrierTeam = (carrier.teamId === m.teamAId) ? aliveA : aliveB;
      const enemyTeam   = (carrier.teamId === m.teamAId) ? aliveB : aliveA;
      // Carrier
      carrier._modeRole = "carrier";
      // Sort same-team teammates by distance to carrier (escorts: 2 closest)
      const teammates = carrierTeam.filter(p => p.id !== carrier.id);
      teammates.sort((a, b) => distance(a, carrier) - distance(b, carrier));
      for (let i = 0; i < teammates.length; i++) {
        teammates[i]._modeRole = (i < 2) ? "escort" : "pressure";
      }

      // === Enemy-team roles ===
      // Sort enemies by distance to carrier — closest is "attacker",
      // next two are "flankers", rest are "support".
      const enemyByDist = [...enemyTeam].sort((a, b) => distance(a, carrier) - distance(b, carrier));
      for (let i = 0; i < enemyByDist.length; i++) {
        if (i === 0) enemyByDist[i]._modeRole = "attacker";
        else if (i < 3) enemyByDist[i]._modeRole = "flanker";
        else enemyByDist[i]._modeRole = "support";
      }
    } else {
      // === Crown is loose ===
      // Each team's CLOSEST living bot becomes "chaser"; rest are "support".
      // Within "support", the closest 1 of each team also gets "interceptor"
      // — they head toward the crown's projected path to fight for it.
      for (const team of [aliveA, aliveB]) {
        const sorted = [...team].sort((a, b) => {
          const da = (a.x - m.crown.x)**2 + (a.y - m.crown.y)**2;
          const db = (b.x - m.crown.x)**2 + (b.y - m.crown.y)**2;
          return da - db;
        });
        for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length; i++) {
          sorted[i]._modeRole = (i === 0) ? "chaser"
                              : (i === 1) ? "interceptor"
                              : "support";
        }
      }
    }
  }

  function decideKoth(p, m) {
    if (!m.crown) return null;
    const role = p._modeRole || "support";
    const enemies = (p.teamId === m.teamAId) ? m.teamBplayers : m.teamAplayers;
    const allies  = (p.teamId === m.teamAId) ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers;
    const enemyAlive = enemies.filter(e => e.alive);

    if (role === "carrier") {
      // Carrier kites to safe space. Find direction with most distance to
      // ALL enemies (not just nearest) — vector sum away. Cap movement
      // toward the field's interior to avoid corner-trapping.
      const cxw = COMBAT.arenaW / 2, cyw = COMBAT.arenaH / 2;
      let avgX = 0, avgY = 0, count = 0;
      for (const e of enemyAlive) {
        const dx = p.x - e.x, dy = p.y - e.y;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
        // Inverse-distance weighted (closer enemies push harder)
        avgX += (dx / d) * (200 / Math.max(40, d));
        avgY += (dy / d) * (200 / Math.max(40, d));
        count++;
      }
      // Blend with toward-center bias proportional to wall proximity
      const wallProx = Math.max(
        (80 - p.x) / 80,
        (p.x - (COMBAT.arenaW - 80)) / 80,
        (60 - p.y) / 60,
        (p.y - (COMBAT.arenaH - 60)) / 60,
        0
      );
      const fleeAng = (count > 0) ? Math.atan2(avgY, avgX) : Math.atan2(cyw - p.y, cxw - p.x);
      const centerAng = Math.atan2(cyw - p.y, cxw - p.x);
      const blend = clamp(wallProx * 0.85, 0, 0.95);
      const moveAng = _blendAngles(fleeAng, centerAng, blend);
      // Set a virtual target ~120px in chosen direction
      p._koth_moveAng = moveAng;
      p.aiState = "KOTH_KITE";
      p._modeCarrier = true;
      return "KOTH_KITE";
    }

    if (role === "escort") {
      // Position between carrier and nearest threat to carrier.
      const carrier = (p.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers)
                        .find(t => t.id === m.crown.carrierId);
      if (!carrier) return null;
      const threat = _closestTo(carrier.x, carrier.y, enemyAlive);
      if (threat) {
        // Stand at midpoint between carrier and threat (slightly closer to threat)
        const tx = carrier.x + (threat.x - carrier.x) * 0.55;
        const ty = carrier.y + (threat.y - carrier.y) * 0.55;
        p._escort_x = tx; p._escort_y = ty;
        p.target = threat;  // attack the threat we're screening from
        p.aiState = "KOTH_ESCORT";
        return "KOTH_ESCORT";
      }
      // No threat — fall through to ENGAGE on nearest enemy
    }

    if (role === "pressure") {
      // Engage furthest-from-carrier enemy cluster — draw enemies away
      const carrier = (p.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers)
                        .find(t => t.id === m.crown.carrierId);
      if (carrier && enemyAlive.length > 0) {
        // Pick furthest-from-carrier enemy; if multiple, prefer closest-to-us
        let best = null, bestScore = -Infinity;
        for (const e of enemyAlive) {
          const distFromCarrier = distance(e, carrier);
          const distFromUs = distance(e, p);
          // Score: prefer far-from-carrier and close-to-us
          const score = distFromCarrier - distFromUs * 0.5;
          if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = e; }
        }
        if (best) {
          p.target = best;
          p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
          return "ENGAGE";
        }
      }
    }

    if (role === "attacker") {
      // Enemy attacker — directly engage carrier
      const carrier = allies.find(t => t.id === m.crown.carrierId);
      if (carrier) {
        p.target = carrier;
        p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
        return "ENGAGE";
      }
    }

    if (role === "flanker") {
      // Enemy flanker — try to reach carrier from a side angle.
      // Move to a point offset perpendicular to the carrier→nearest-enemy
      // line, then close in.
      const carrier = allies.find(t => t.id === m.crown.carrierId);
      if (carrier) {
        const ang = Math.atan2(carrier.y - p.y, carrier.x - p.x);
        // Pick a flank side based on which side we're on relative to the line
        const perpAng = ang + (Math.PI / 2);
        const flankX = carrier.x + Math.cos(perpAng) * 60;
        const flankY = carrier.y + Math.sin(perpAng) * 60;
        // If we're far, head to flank; if close, just attack
        const d = distance(p, carrier);
        if (d > 90) {
          p._escort_x = flankX; p._escort_y = flankY;
          p.target = carrier;
          p.aiState = "KOTH_FLANK";
          return "KOTH_FLANK";
        } else {
          p.target = carrier;
          p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
          return "ENGAGE";
        }
      }
    }

    if (role === "chaser") {
      // No carrier — race to crown. Use lead-running: head to (crown +
      // velocity * 4 frames) so we cut it off rather than chasing where
      // it currently is. Crown isn't moving when uncarried — but this is
      // future-proof if we ever animate dropped crowns.
      p._koth_moveAng = Math.atan2(m.crown.y - p.y, m.crown.x - p.x);
      p.aiState = "KOTH_RACE";
      return "KOTH_RACE";
    }

    if (role === "interceptor") {
      // No carrier, we're 2nd-closest. Anticipate: move to a point
      // ~40px past the crown on the line from us to crown — so when
      // a teammate grabs it, we're already in escort position.
      const dx = m.crown.x - p.x, dy = m.crown.y - p.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      p._koth_moveAng = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
      p.aiState = "KOTH_RACE";
      return "KOTH_RACE";
    }

    // Default — fall through to deathmatch logic
    return null;
  }

  /* ============================================================
     CTF — football handoff + ultimate frisbee zone defense

     Carrier-team roles:
       carrier     — runs toward own base, slows if escort is far
       escort_lead — sprints AHEAD of carrier toward base, picks off defenders
       escort_rear — trails carrier, intercepts pursuers
       support     — engages enemy defenders away from carrier line

     Enemy-team roles:
       chaser_a    — closest enemy, pursues carrier directly
       chaser_b    — second-closest, takes a flanking line
       zone_def    — closest 1-2 to OWN base, holds perimeter (camp goal-line)
       interceptor — predicts carrier's path, moves to block
       support     — engages other carrier-team players

     Uncarried flag:
       retriever_a — closest bot from each team races flag
       retriever_b — second-closest from each team takes lead-line
       (rest defend or hold)
     ============================================================ */
  function assignCtfRoles(m) {
    if (!m.flag || !m.basesById) return;
    const aliveA = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => p.alive);
    const aliveB = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => p.alive);
    const allAlive = [...aliveA, ...aliveB];
    const carrier = m.flag.carrierId
      ? allAlive.find(p => p.id === m.flag.carrierId)
      : null;

    if (carrier) {
      const carrierTeam = (carrier.teamId === m.teamAId) ? aliveA : aliveB;
      const enemyTeam   = (carrier.teamId === m.teamAId) ? aliveB : aliveA;
      const ownBase = m.basesById[carrier.teamId];

      carrier._modeRole = "carrier";

      // Carrier-team teammates: 1 escort_lead (closest to base), 1 escort_rear
      // (closest to carrier from behind), rest support.
      const teammates = carrierTeam.filter(p => p.id !== carrier.id);
      // Lead = teammate closest to own base AND ahead of carrier on path
      const baseDir = Math.atan2(ownBase.y - carrier.y, ownBase.x - carrier.x);
      const baseUx = Math.cos(baseDir), baseUy = Math.sin(baseDir);
      // For each teammate, project (teammate - carrier) onto baseDir
      // — positive = ahead of carrier, negative = behind.
      const projected = teammates.map(t => {
        const dx = t.x - carrier.x, dy = t.y - carrier.y;
        const proj = dx * baseUx + dy * baseUy;
        return { p: t, proj, distFromBase: distance(t, ownBase), distFromCarrier: distance(t, carrier) };
      });
      // escort_lead: most-ahead teammate (highest proj). Fallback: closest to base.
      let lead = null;
      let aheadSorted = projected.filter(o => o.proj > 0).sort((a, b) => b.proj - a.proj);
      if (aheadSorted.length > 0) lead = aheadSorted[0].p;
      else if (projected.length > 0) lead = [...projected].sort((a, b) => a.distFromBase - b.distFromBase)[0].p;
      if (lead) lead._modeRole = "escort_lead";
      // escort_rear: most-behind teammate (lowest proj). Fallback: closest to carrier.
      let rear = null;
      let behindSorted = projected.filter(o => o.p !== lead && o.proj <= 0).sort((a, b) => a.proj - b.proj);
      if (behindSorted.length > 0) rear = behindSorted[0].p;
      else {
        const remaining = projected.filter(o => o.p !== lead);
        if (remaining.length > 0) rear = [...remaining].sort((a, b) => a.distFromCarrier - b.distFromCarrier)[0].p;
      }
      if (rear) rear._modeRole = "escort_rear";
      // Rest: support
      for (const t of teammates) {
        if (t._modeRole !== "escort_lead" && t._modeRole !== "escort_rear") {
          t._modeRole = "support";
        }
      }

      // Enemy-team roles
      const enemyByDistToCarrier = [...enemyTeam].sort((a, b) => distance(a, carrier) - distance(b, carrier));
      for (let i = 0; i < enemyByDistToCarrier.length; i++) {
        const e = enemyByDistToCarrier[i];
        if (i === 0) e._modeRole = "chaser_a";
        else if (i === 1) e._modeRole = "chaser_b";
        else if (i === 2) e._modeRole = "interceptor";
        else e._modeRole = "support";
      }
      // Override one enemy to be zone_def: the one already closest to ENEMY's
      // own base (i.e. defending their goal-line stand) — unless they're chaser_a.
      const enemyOwnBase = m.basesById[enemyByDistToCarrier[0]?.teamId];
      if (enemyOwnBase) {
        const sortedByOwnBase = [...enemyTeam].sort((a, b) => distance(a, enemyOwnBase) - distance(b, enemyOwnBase));
        const candidate = sortedByOwnBase.find(e => e._modeRole !== "chaser_a");
        if (candidate) candidate._modeRole = "zone_def";
      }
    } else {
      // === Flag is loose ===
      // Each team: closest 2 to flag are retrievers, rest are zone_def or support
      for (const team of [aliveA, aliveB]) {
        const sorted = [...team].sort((a, b) => {
          const da = (a.x - m.flag.x)**2 + (a.y - m.flag.y)**2;
          const db = (b.x - m.flag.x)**2 + (b.y - m.flag.y)**2;
          return da - db;
        });
        for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length; i++) {
          if (i === 0) sorted[i]._modeRole = "retriever_a";
          else if (i === 1) sorted[i]._modeRole = "retriever_b";
          else if (i === 2 && m.basesById && m.basesById[sorted[i].teamId]) sorted[i]._modeRole = "zone_def";
          else sorted[i]._modeRole = "support";
        }
      }
    }
  }

  function decideCtf(p, m) {
    if (!m.flag) return null;
    const role = p._modeRole || "support";
    const enemies = (p.teamId === m.teamAId) ? m.teamBplayers : m.teamAplayers;
    const allies  = (p.teamId === m.teamAId) ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers;
    const enemyAlive = enemies.filter(e => e.alive);
    const ownBase = m.basesById && m.basesById[p.teamId];

    if (role === "carrier") {
      // Run toward own base. The CTF_RUSH state already handles base-rushing
      // movement at reduced speed. Just set state.
      p._modeCarrier = true;
      p.aiState = "CTF_RUSH";
      return "CTF_RUSH";
    }

    if (role === "escort_lead") {
      // Run AHEAD of carrier toward own base, picking off any enemies
      // along the path. If an enemy is between us and base, attack them.
      // If no enemy near path, sprint forward.
      const carrier = allies.find(a => a.id === m.flag.carrierId);
      if (!carrier || !ownBase) return null;
      // Find closest enemy on the carrier→base path
      const pathAng = Math.atan2(ownBase.y - carrier.y, ownBase.x - carrier.x);
      const ux = Math.cos(pathAng), uy = Math.sin(pathAng);
      let bestEnemy = null, bestProj = Infinity;
      for (const e of enemyAlive) {
        const dx = e.x - carrier.x, dy = e.y - carrier.y;
        const proj = dx * ux + dy * uy;
        // Perpendicular distance from path
        const perp = Math.abs(dx * (-uy) + dy * ux);
        // Pick enemies AHEAD of carrier and within 80px of the path
        if (proj > 0 && proj < distance(carrier, ownBase) && perp < 80) {
          if (proj < bestProj) { bestProj = proj; bestEnemy = e; }
        }
      }
      if (bestEnemy) {
        p.target = bestEnemy;
        p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
        return "ENGAGE";
      }
      // No path-blocker — head to a point ahead of carrier toward base
      const aheadX = carrier.x + Math.cos(pathAng) * 80;
      const aheadY = carrier.y + Math.sin(pathAng) * 80;
      p._escort_x = aheadX; p._escort_y = aheadY;
      p.aiState = "CTF_ESCORT_LEAD";
      return "CTF_ESCORT_LEAD";
    }

    if (role === "escort_rear") {
      // Trail carrier, attack pursuing chasers
      const carrier = allies.find(a => a.id === m.flag.carrierId);
      if (!carrier || !ownBase) return null;
      // Find any enemy within 120px of carrier (a pursuer). Engage if so.
      let pursuer = null, bestD = 120 * 120;
      for (const e of enemyAlive) {
        const dx = e.x - carrier.x, dy = e.y - carrier.y;
        const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
        if (d2 < bestD) { bestD = d2; pursuer = e; }
      }
      if (pursuer) {
        p.target = pursuer;
        p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
        return "ENGAGE";
      }
      // No pursuer — trail carrier from behind
      const baseDir = Math.atan2(ownBase.y - carrier.y, ownBase.x - carrier.x);
      const trailX = carrier.x - Math.cos(baseDir) * 50;
      const trailY = carrier.y - Math.sin(baseDir) * 50;
      p._escort_x = trailX; p._escort_y = trailY;
      p.aiState = "CTF_ESCORT_REAR";
      return "CTF_ESCORT_REAR";
    }

    if (role === "chaser_a") {
      // Direct chase — go straight at carrier
      const carrier = enemyAlive.find(e => e.id === m.flag.carrierId);
      if (carrier) {
        p.target = carrier;
        p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
        return "ENGAGE";
      }
    }

    if (role === "chaser_b") {
      // Flanking chase — head to a point ahead-of-carrier-toward-base
      // so we cut them off. Real-world: ultimate frisbee "lane defender".
      const carrier = enemyAlive.find(e => e.id === m.flag.carrierId);
      const enemyBase = m.basesById && m.basesById[carrier?.teamId];
      if (carrier && enemyBase) {
        const pathAng = Math.atan2(enemyBase.y - carrier.y, enemyBase.x - carrier.x);
        const cutX = carrier.x + Math.cos(pathAng) * 100;
        const cutY = carrier.y + Math.sin(pathAng) * 100;
        p._escort_x = cutX; p._escort_y = cutY;
        p.target = carrier;
        p.aiState = "CTF_FLANK";
        return "CTF_FLANK";
      }
    }

    if (role === "interceptor") {
      // Predict carrier's future position (carrier velocity * 30 frames)
      // and head there. If close enough, attack.
      const carrier = enemyAlive.find(e => e.id === m.flag.carrierId);
      if (carrier) {
        const fx = carrier.x + (carrier.vx || 0) * 30;
        const fy = carrier.y + (carrier.vy || 0) * 30;
        p._escort_x = fx; p._escort_y = fy;
        p.target = carrier;
        p.aiState = "CTF_FLANK";
        return "CTF_FLANK";
      }
    }

    if (role === "zone_def") {
      // Camp own base perimeter — football safety / zone defender.
      // Stay within ~80px of own base; engage anyone who comes within 120px.
      if (!ownBase) return null;
      const distToBase = distance(p, ownBase);
      // Find any enemy approaching base
      let approacher = null, bestD = 200 * 200;
      for (const e of enemyAlive) {
        const dx = e.x - ownBase.x, dy = e.y - ownBase.y;
        const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
        if (d2 < bestD) { bestD = d2; approacher = e; }
      }
      if (approacher && Math.sqrt(bestD) < 200) {
        p.target = approacher;
        p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
        return "ENGAGE";
      }
      // No threat — return to base perimeter
      if (distToBase > 80) {
        p._escort_x = ownBase.x; p._escort_y = ownBase.y;
        p.aiState = "CTF_HOLD";
        return "CTF_HOLD";
      }
      // At perimeter, no threat — fall through to engage nearest
    }

    if (role === "retriever_a" || role === "retriever_b") {
      // Race to flag. Lead-running for retriever_b (slight offset
      // toward expected pickup approach line).
      const dx = m.flag.x - p.x, dy = m.flag.y - p.y;
      let tx = m.flag.x, ty = m.flag.y;
      if (role === "retriever_b") {
        // Aim for a point slightly past the flag toward our own base
        if (ownBase) {
          const fb = Math.atan2(ownBase.y - m.flag.y, ownBase.x - m.flag.x);
          tx += Math.cos(fb) * 30;
          ty += Math.sin(fb) * 30;
        }
      }
      p._escort_x = tx; p._escort_y = ty;
      p.aiState = "CTF_RACE";
      return "CTF_RACE";
    }

    return null; // fall through to deathmatch
  }

  /* ============================================================
     TAG — predator-prey + freeze tag positional play

     It (predator):
       - Picks easiest target (closest, slowest, lowest HP, in our cone)
       - Uses predictive interception: aim where prey WILL be, not where they ARE
       - If multiple prey are clustered, breaks the herd by chasing one
         until it isolates, then commits

     Prey (free-for-all, no teams):
       - prey_flock: stay near OTHER prey (safety in numbers — predators
         hunt isolated prey in real ecosystems)
       - prey_break: when It is closing fast and we're near herd, scatter
         laterally so the herd doesn't all get caught
       - prey_shielded: when frozen prey or terrain is between us and It,
         stay behind it (cover-using)
     ============================================================ */
  function assignTagRoles(m) {
    if (!m.tag) return;
    const allAlive = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].filter(p => p.alive);
    const it = allAlive.find(p => p.id === m.tag.itId);

    if (!it) return;
    it._modeRole = "it_predator";

    const prey = allAlive.filter(p => p.id !== it.id);
    const isFrozen = (id) => !!(m.tag.tagFreezes && m.tag.tagFreezes[id]);

    for (const p of prey) {
      if (isFrozen(p.id)) {
        p._modeRole = "frozen";
        continue;
      }
      // Distance to It
      const dToIt = distance(p, it);
      // Distance to nearest non-frozen non-it prey peer (herd proximity)
      let nearestPeerD = Infinity;
      for (const peer of prey) {
        if (peer === p || isFrozen(peer.id)) continue;
        const d = distance(p, peer);
        if (d < nearestPeerD) nearestPeerD = d;
      }
      // Is there a frozen bot or another prey between us and It (a shield)?
      let hasShield = false;
      const itAng = Math.atan2(it.y - p.y, it.x - p.x);
      for (const other of allAlive) {
        if (other === p || other === it) continue;
        // Vector from us to other
        const ox = other.x - p.x, oy = other.y - p.y;
        const od = Math.hypot(ox, oy);
        if (od === 0 || od > dToIt) continue;
        const oAng = Math.atan2(oy, ox);
        let angDiff = Math.abs(oAng - itAng);
        if (angDiff > Math.PI) angDiff = 2*Math.PI - angDiff;
        if (angDiff < 0.35 && od > 12) {  // ~20° cone
          hasShield = true;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (hasShield && dToIt > 50) {
        p._modeRole = "prey_shielded";
      } else if (dToIt < 90 && nearestPeerD < 180) {
        // Predator is closing AND we're near a peer — break (scatter
        // away from peer so herd doesn't get caught together)
        p._modeRole = "prey_break";
      } else {
        // Default flock — stay near peers and away from It
        p._modeRole = "prey_flock";
      }
    }
  }

  function decideTag(p, m) {
    if (!m.tag) return null;
    const role = p._modeRole || "prey_flock";
    const allAlive = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].filter(e => e.alive);
    const it = allAlive.find(e => e.id === m.tag.itId);
    const isFrozen = (id) => !!(m.tag.tagFreezes && m.tag.tagFreezes[id]);

    if (role === "frozen") {
      // Frozen — can't act (stunned enforces no-action)
      return "FROZEN";
    }

    if (role === "it_predator") {
      // Pick easiest target via score: prefer close, slow, low-HP, in front of us
      // (in our motion cone). Real predators hunt the isolated/weakest.
      // Once committed to a target, stay committed for ≥60 frames before
      // re-evaluating — prevents ping-pong between similarly-scored prey.
      const candidates = allAlive.filter(e => e.id !== p.id && !isFrozen(e.id));
      if (candidates.length === 0) {
        // Only frozen prey left — pick nearest of those
        const frozenPrey = allAlive.filter(e => e.id !== p.id && isFrozen(e.id));
        if (frozenPrey.length > 0) {
          frozenPrey.sort((a, b) => distance(p, a) - distance(p, b));
          p.target = frozenPrey[0];
          p.aiState = "MELEE";
          return "MELEE";
        }
        return null;
      }
      // Commitment timer: if current target is still valid AND we picked
      // them less than 60 frames ago, stick with them.
      const committed =
        p.target && p.target.alive && !isFrozen(p.target.id) &&
        (m.roundFrame - (p._tag_targetSince || 0)) < 60;
      let bestTarget = null;
      if (committed) {
        bestTarget = p.target;
      } else {
        let bestScore = -Infinity;
        for (const e of candidates) {
          const d = distance(p, e);
          const hpScore = 1 - (e.hp / e.stats.hp);
          const speedScore = 1 - clamp((e.stats.spd || 2.5) / 5, 0, 1);
          // Isolation: score higher if no other prey is near them
          let nearestPeerD = Infinity;
          for (const peer of candidates) {
            if (peer === e) continue;
            const pd = distance(e, peer);
            if (pd < nearestPeerD) nearestPeerD = pd;
          }
          const isolationScore = clamp(nearestPeerD / 200, 0, 1);
          // Cone preference — bots in our forward arc are easier to chase
          const ang = Math.atan2(e.y - p.y, e.x - p.x);
          const motionAng = Math.atan2(p.vy || 0, p.vx || 1);
          let coneDiff = Math.abs(ang - motionAng);
          if (coneDiff > Math.PI) coneDiff = 2*Math.PI - coneDiff;
          const coneScore = 1 - clamp(coneDiff / Math.PI, 0, 1);
          // Composite: distance dominant, then HP, then isolation, then speed/cone.
          // Distance weight is heavy (-0.08) when few prey remain so we don't
          // chase far targets while close ones are reachable.
          const distWeight = candidates.length <= 2 ? -0.10 : -0.05;
          const score =
            d * distWeight +
            hpScore * 30 +
            isolationScore * 20 +
            speedScore * 10 +
            coneScore * 8;
          if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; bestTarget = e; }
        }
      }
      if (bestTarget) {
        if (p.target !== bestTarget) {
          p.target = bestTarget;
          p._tag_targetSince = m.roundFrame;
        }
        // Predictive interception: aim ahead based on target velocity.
        // Lookahead scales DOWN with distance — close targets need precise
        // aim, not guessed-ahead aim, or we overshoot into walls.
        const d = distance(p, bestTarget);
        const futureFrames = clamp(d / 30, 2, 14);
        const fx = bestTarget.x + (bestTarget.vx || 0) * futureFrames;
        const fy = bestTarget.y + (bestTarget.vy || 0) * futureFrames;
        p._tag_aim_x = fx;
        p._tag_aim_y = fy;
        p.attackedBy = null;
        if (d < (COMBAT.meleeSwitchRange || 60)) {
          p.aiState = "MELEE";
        } else {
          p.aiState = "TAG_HUNT";
        }
        return p.aiState;
      }
      return null;
    }

    // === Prey roles ===
    if (!it) return null;

    if (role === "prey_shielded") {
      // Stay BEHIND a shield (frozen bot or other prey relative to It).
      // Find the shield, then move to a point slightly behind it.
      const itAng = Math.atan2(it.y - p.y, it.x - p.x);
      let shield = null, shieldD = Infinity;
      for (const other of allAlive) {
        if (other === p || other === it) continue;
        const ox = other.x - p.x, oy = other.y - p.y;
        const od = Math.hypot(ox, oy);
        if (od === 0) continue;
        const oAng = Math.atan2(oy, ox);
        let angDiff = Math.abs(oAng - itAng);
        if (angDiff > Math.PI) angDiff = 2*Math.PI - angDiff;
        if (angDiff < 0.35 && od < shieldD) {
          shield = other; shieldD = od;
        }
      }
      if (shield) {
        // Hold position slightly behind shield (further from It)
        const awayAng = Math.atan2(p.y - it.y, p.x - it.x);
        const tx = shield.x + Math.cos(awayAng) * 25;
        const ty = shield.y + Math.sin(awayAng) * 25;
        p._tag_move_x = tx; p._tag_move_y = ty;
        p.target = it;
        p.aiState = "TAG_SHIELD";
        return "TAG_SHIELD";
      }
      // No shield — fall through to break/flock
    }

    if (role === "prey_break") {
      // It is close AND we're in a herd — scatter laterally away from the
      // herd centroid so the herd doesn't get caught together.
      const peers = allAlive.filter(e => e !== p && e !== it && !isFrozen(e.id));
      if (peers.length > 0) {
        let cx = 0, cy = 0;
        for (const peer of peers) { cx += peer.x; cy += peer.y; }
        cx /= peers.length; cy /= peers.length;
        // Direction AWAY from herd centroid AND away from It (vector sum)
        const fromHerd = Math.atan2(p.y - cy, p.x - cx);
        const fromIt = Math.atan2(p.y - it.y, p.x - it.x);
        const ang = _blendAngles(fromIt, fromHerd, 0.5);
        const tx = p.x + Math.cos(ang) * 100;
        const ty = p.y + Math.sin(ang) * 100;
        p._tag_move_x = tx; p._tag_move_y = ty;
        p.target = it;
        p.aiState = "TAG_BREAK";
        return "TAG_BREAK";
      }
    }

    if (role === "prey_flock") {
      // Flee from It while staying near peers (herd safety).
      const peers = allAlive.filter(e => e !== p && e !== it && !isFrozen(e.id));
      const fromIt = Math.atan2(p.y - it.y, p.x - it.x);
      let moveAng = fromIt;
      if (peers.length > 0) {
        let cx = 0, cy = 0;
        for (const peer of peers) { cx += peer.x; cy += peer.y; }
        cx /= peers.length; cy /= peers.length;
        const toHerd = Math.atan2(cy - p.y, cx - p.x);
        // Blend: 70% away from It, 30% toward herd
        moveAng = _blendAngles(fromIt, toHerd, 0.30);
      }
      // Wall-avoidance bias toward center
      const cxw = COMBAT.arenaW / 2, cyw = COMBAT.arenaH / 2;
      const wallProx = Math.max(
        (80 - p.x) / 80,
        (p.x - (COMBAT.arenaW - 80)) / 80,
        (60 - p.y) / 60,
        (p.y - (COMBAT.arenaH - 60)) / 60,
        0
      );
      if (wallProx > 0) {
        const centerAng = Math.atan2(cyw - p.y, cxw - p.x);
        moveAng = _blendAngles(moveAng, centerAng, clamp(wallProx * 0.7, 0, 0.85));
      }
      const tx = p.x + Math.cos(moveAng) * 100;
      const ty = p.y + Math.sin(moveAng) * 100;
      p._tag_move_x = tx; p._tag_move_y = ty;
      p.target = it;
      p.aiState = "TAG_FLEE";
      return "TAG_FLEE";
    }

    return null;
  }

  /* Public API */
  return {
    /* Called once per AI tick (per-team batch) — refreshes role
       assignments based on game state. Cheap; runs every frame so role
       changes are responsive. */
    assignRoles(m) {
      if (!m) return;
      const mode = m.gameMode;
      if (mode === "koth") assignKothRoles(m);
      else if (mode === "ctf") assignCtfRoles(m);
      else if (mode === "tag") assignTagRoles(m);
    },

    /* Per-bot decision — returns the new aiState if the mode took over,
       or null to fall through to deathmatch logic. */
    decide(p, m) {
      if (!m) return null;
      const mode = m.gameMode;
      if (mode === "koth") return decideKoth(p, m);
      if (mode === "ctf")  return decideCtf(p, m);
      if (mode === "tag")  return decideTag(p, m);
      return null;
    },
  };
})();
window.MODE_AI = MODE_AI;


function decideAIState(p, enemyAlive, m) {
  // Increment state-frame counter so other code can introspect duration.
  p.aiStateFrames = (p.aiStateFrames || 0) + 1;

  // Stunned bots can't act — keep current state but freeze the timer.
  if (p.stunned > 0) {
    p.aiStateFrames = 0;  // reset so post-stun re-evaluation is fresh
    return;
  }

  // === v28.1: Mode-specific AI dispatch ===
  // The MODE_AI module handles all role assignment + state selection
  // for KOTH / CTF / Tag. If it returns a non-null state, the bot is
  // fully controlled by mode logic this tick. Returning null means
  // either the mode doesn't apply (deathmatch) or the role's logic
  // wants to fall through to deathmatch decision-making.
  if (m.gameMode && m.gameMode !== "deathmatch") {
    // Frozen prey in Tag — can't act. Bail before any state logic.
    if (m.gameMode === "tag" && m.tag &&
        m.tag.tagFreezes && m.tag.tagFreezes[p.id]) {
      return;
    }
    // Carrier flag tracking — used by execute layer to suppress shooting.
    p._modeCarrier =
      (m.gameMode === "koth" && m.crown && m.crown.carrierId === p.id) ||
      (m.gameMode === "ctf"  && m.flag  && m.flag.carrierId  === p.id);
    const modeState = MODE_AI.decide(p, m);
    if (modeState) return;  // mode took full control this tick
  } else {
    p._modeCarrier = false;
  }
  // === end mode dispatch — fall through to deathmatch logic ===

  /* ============================================================
     v28.2 DEATHMATCH AI — full rework

     Decision priority (each step returns if the state is set;
     otherwise falls through):

       1. Stunned/healer/grenadier — early exit (handled elsewhere)
       2. No enemies → IDLE (drift to center)
       3. Critically low HP + rally point available → RALLY
          (directed retreat to nearest healthy teammate, NOT
          panic-flee toward arena edges)
       4. Mercy / FINISH_OFF (someone's shooting us)
       5. Wounded + rally available → RALLY (preventive retreat)
       6. MELEE override (ranged bot at point-blank)
       7. Sniper backline behavior (kite to keep distance)
       8. SEEK_ITEM (high-value drop nearby)
       9. SEEK_CORE (power core eligible)
      10. Default ENGAGE on TEAM_AI focus target (or fallback nearest)

     Sticky targeting: a target picked this frame holds for ≥30
     frames before re-evaluating, unless the target dies. The
     TEAM_AI focus shifts as enemies die, but per-bot commitment
     prevents target ping-ponging within the team's plan.
     ============================================================ */
  const hpFrac = p.hp / p.stats.hp;
  const myWpn = p.weapon || {};
  const ranged = (myWpn.range || 0) > 30;

  // Healer/Grenadier specialized behaviors live in executeAIState
  if (p.position === "healer" || p.position === "grenadier") {
    p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
    return;
  }

  // Use TEAM_AI to pick a target (focus fire when reachable, else nearest)
  const focusedTarget = TEAM_AI.pickTarget(p, enemyAlive, m);
  let nearest = null, nearestD = Infinity;
  for (const e of enemyAlive) {
    const d = distance(p, e);
    if (d < nearestD) { nearestD = d; nearest = e; }
  }

  // No enemies → IDLE
  if (!nearest) {
    p.aiState = "IDLE";
    p.target = null;
    return;
  }

  // === Mercy: if someone's hitting us hard, pivot to them ===
  // Higher priority than rally so we don't ignore the bot in our face.
  if (p.attackedBy) {
    const attacker = enemyAlive.find(e => e.id === p.attackedBy);
    if (attacker && distance(p, attacker) < (ranged ? myWpn.range : 220)) {
      p.aiState = "FINISH_OFF";
      p.target = attacker;
      return;
    }
  }

  // === RALLY — directed retreat, not panic flee ===
  // Critically low HP + rally point available → fall back toward team.
  // This replaces v28's center-biased FLEE for the deathmatch path
  // because rallying to a healer or healthy teammate is strictly
  // better tactics than running toward map center alone.
  const wasFleeing = (p.aiState === "FLEE" || p.aiState === "RALLY");
  if (TEAM_AI.shouldRally(p, nearestD, m)) {
    if (wasFleeing && p.aiStateFrames > 90) {
      // Time-cap escape — back to action even at low HP
      p.aiState = "IDLE";
      p.aiStateFrames = 0;
      // fall through — RALLY won't be picked again until aiStateFrames resets
    } else {
      if (!wasFleeing) p.aiStateFrames = 0;
      p.aiState = "RALLY";
      p.target = nearest;  // remember the threat so executeAIState can flee from it
      const rallyPt = TEAM_AI.findRallyPoint(p, m);
      if (rallyPt) {
        p._rally_x = rallyPt.x;
        p._rally_y = rallyPt.y;
      }
      return;
    }
  }
  // Legacy FLEE fallback when there's no rally point but HP is critical.
  // === v28.4: also suppressed in lastStand mode (≤2 bots outnumbered) —
  // those bots fight to the finish, no panic-flee. ===
  const teamState = m._teamAI && m._teamAI[p.teamId];
  const inLastStand = teamState && teamState.lastStand;
  if (!inLastStand && hpFrac < 0.20 && nearestD > 40) {
    if (wasFleeing && p.aiStateFrames > 90) {
      p.aiState = "IDLE";
      p.aiStateFrames = 0;
    } else {
      if (!wasFleeing) p.aiStateFrames = 0;
      p.aiState = "FLEE";
      p.target = nearest;
      return;
    }
  }

  // === MELEE override — point-blank ranged bot swap to fallback ===
  if (ranged && nearestD < (COMBAT.meleeSwitchRange || 60)) {
    p.aiState = "MELEE";
    p.target = nearest;
    return;
  }

  // === SNIPER backline — actively kite to maintain distance ===
  // Real-world: a sniper without distance is a dead sniper. If the
  // closest enemy is well inside the sniper's preferred range
  // (~75% of weapon range), pivot to a SNIPER_HOLD that backs off
  // before firing. This pulls them toward the team's backline
  // position and away from incoming threats.
  // === v28.13: hysteresis — enter at 0.40, exit only at 0.55. ===
  if (p.position === "sniper" && hpFrac > 0.20) {
    const sniperHoldRange = myWpn.range * 0.40;
    const sniperReleaseRange = myWpn.range * 0.55;
    const wasSniperHold = (p.aiState === "SNIPER_HOLD");
    if (nearestD < sniperHoldRange || (wasSniperHold && nearestD < sniperReleaseRange)) {
      p.aiState = "SNIPER_HOLD";
      p.target = focusedTarget || nearest;
      return;
    }
  }

  // === SEEK_ITEM — useful drop nearby ===
  const wantedDrop = findBestDropFor(p, m);
  if (wantedDrop) {
    const isUrgent =
      (wantedDrop.slot === "health" && hpFrac < 0.5) ||
      (wantedDrop.slot === "armor" && (p.armor?.defMod || 0) < 5);
    const safeFromEnemy = !nearest || nearestD > 55;
    if (isUrgent || safeFromEnemy) {
      p.aiState = "SEEK_ITEM";
      p.itemTarget = wantedDrop;
      return;
    }
  }

  // === SEEK_CORE (Power Core) ===
  if (m.powerCore && m.powerCore.state === "active") {
    const dx = p.x - m.powerCore.x, dy = p.y - m.powerCore.y;
    const distToCore = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
    const teamHasBuff = (p.boostMods && p.boostMods.dmgMult >= 1.25);
    const myTeamPostCaptureCooldown = (m.powerCore.lastCapturedTeam === p.teamId)
      && (m.roundFrame - (m.powerCore.lastCapturedFrame || 0)) < 120;
    if (!teamHasBuff && !myTeamPostCaptureCooldown && distToCore < 180) {
      const enemyClear = !nearest || nearestD > 110;
      if (enemyClear) {
        const teammates = (p.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers);
        let isClosest = true;
        for (const mate of teammates) {
          if (!mate.alive || mate === p) continue;
          const mdx = mate.x - m.powerCore.x, mdy = mate.y - m.powerCore.y;
          const mateDist = Math.sqrt(mdx*mdx + mdy*mdy);
          if (mateDist < distToCore) { isClosest = false; break; }
        }
        if (isClosest) {
          p.aiState = "SEEK_CORE";
          return;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // === Sticky targeting — commit for 30 frames ===
  // Once we've picked a target, hold it for 30 frames before reconsidering
  // (unless target dies). Combined with TEAM_AI's frame-by-frame focus
  // calculation, this means the team's plan stays coherent even though
  // each bot updates independently.
  const targetExpired = !p.target || !p.target.alive ||
    (m.roundFrame - (p._targetCommitFrame || 0)) > 30;
  if (targetExpired) {
    p.target = focusedTarget || nearest;
    p._targetCommitFrame = m.roundFrame;
  }
  // Honor the focus target if the current target has lower priority
  // and the focus is reasonably close — but only at retarget windows.

  if (p.aiState !== "ENGAGE") p.aiStateFrames = 0;
  p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
}

function findBestDropFor(p, m) {
  if (!m.drops.length) return null;
  let tx = 0, ty = 0, hasDir = false;
  if (p.target && p.target.alive) {
    const dx = p.target.x - p.x, dy = p.target.y - p.y;
    const len = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy) || 1;
    tx = dx / len; ty = dy / len; hasDir = true;
  }
  let best = null, bestScore = 0;
  for (const d of m.drops) {
    const dx = d.x - p.x, dy = d.y - p.y;
    const dist2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
    // Wider detection radius — was 220 (about 1/4 arena width), now 340
    // so AI sees pickups across most of their half of the arena.
    if (dist2 > 340*340) continue;
    const dist = Math.sqrt(dist2);

    let upgrade;
    if (d.slot === "health") {
      if (p.hp >= p.stats.hp) continue;
      const hpFrac = p.hp / p.stats.hp;
      upgrade = 6 + (1 - hpFrac) * 22; // 6 at full → 28 at near-dead (more weight)
    } else {
      const cur = gearScore(d.slot, p[d.slot], p.position);
      const newScore = gearScore(d.slot, d.gear, p.position);
      upgrade = newScore - cur;
      // Lower threshold (1.5 → 1.0) so smaller upgrades still register.
      // Armor specifically gets +5 utility floor — even small armor
      // upgrades feel attractive when knights are taking real damage.
      if (d.slot === "armor" && upgrade > 0) upgrade += 4;
      if (upgrade <= 1.0) continue;
    }

    let alignment = 0;
    if (hasDir) {
      const ux = dx / dist, uy = dy / dist;
      alignment = Math.max(0, ux * tx + uy * ty); // 0..1
    }
    // Distance penalty reduced (0.025 → 0.018) so AI is more willing to
    // path further to grab valuable pickups.
    const utility = upgrade + alignment * 6 - dist * 0.018;
    if (utility > bestScore) { bestScore = utility; best = d; }
  }
  return best;
}

/* ============================================================
   PROJECTILE DODGING — bots evade incoming shots they can see.

   For each incoming projectile, predict its trajectory and check
   if the bot intersects within a short time window. If so, sidestep
   perpendicular to the projectile's flight direction. Reaction time
   and dodge speed scale with the bot's stat.spd so Speedsters
   visibly weave while Tanks barely flinch.

   The dodge check runs BEFORE state-driven movement so a
   successful dodge replaces this frame's intended motion. Returns
   true if the bot dodged (caller should skip its own movement).
   ============================================================ */
function tryDodgeProjectile(p, m, effSpd) {
  if (!m.projectiles || !m.projectiles.length) return false;
  if (p.stunned > 0) return false;

  // === DODGE COOLDOWN ===
  // After a successful dodge, the bot needs to recover before dodging again.
  // Speedsters recover faster (8 frames), Tanks slower (24).
  if (p._dodgeCdUntil && m.frame < p._dodgeCdUntil) return false;

  // === POSITION-SPECIFIC DODGE COMPETENCE ===
  // Tanks DO NOT dodge — they're armored to take hits. (Hard 0%.)
  // Snipers dodge LESS than their SPD would imply — they're already strong
  // and giving them dodge would tip the balance.
  // Healers + grenade-throwers dodge slightly less than a striker baseline.
  if (p.position === "tank") return false;

  // Reaction-time scaling: faster bots see threats EARLIER.
  const reactionFrames = Math.max(6, Math.round(p.stats.spd * 5 + 4));

  // === v28.4 dodge rebalance ===
  // User reported dodging was underutilized. Three tweaks:
  //   1. Base skill bumped: 0.15+spd*0.09 (cap 0.45) → 0.25+spd*0.12 (cap 0.65).
  //      A typical SPD-2.5 bot now dodges 55% (was 37%).
  //   2. Detection radius HIT_RADIUS+4 → HIT_RADIUS+8 (24px vs 18) so
  //      "near miss" projectiles that the bot SHOULD react to actually
  //      register as threats.
  //   3. Cooldown shortened: max(8, 28-spd*5) → max(5, 22-spd*5).
  //      Speedsters can now dodge ~every 7 frames vs ~13 before.
  let dodgeSkill = clamp(0.25 + p.stats.spd * 0.12, 0.25, 0.65);
  if (p.position === "sniper") dodgeSkill *= 0.55; // was 0.45 — sniper still dodges less but more than v28.3
  if (p.position === "healer" || p.position === "grenadier") dodgeSkill *= 0.90;

  const HIT_RADIUS = 14;
  let bestThreat = null, bestEta = Infinity;

  for (const pr of m.projectiles) {
    if (pr.teamId === p.teamId) continue;
    const sp = Math.hypot(pr.vx, pr.vy);
    if (sp < 0.1) continue;
    const dx = p.x - pr.x, dy = p.y - pr.y;
    const t = (dx * pr.vx + dy * pr.vy) / (sp * sp);
    if (t < 0 || t > reactionFrames + 18) continue;
    const cx = pr.x + pr.vx * t;
    const cy = pr.y + pr.vy * t;
    const closest = Math.hypot(p.x - cx, p.y - cy);
    if (closest > HIT_RADIUS + 8) continue;  // wider detection envelope
    if (t < bestEta) { bestEta = t; bestThreat = pr; }
  }

  if (!bestThreat) return false;
  if (SIM_RNG.next() > dodgeSkill) return false;

  // === BACKWARDS DISENGAGE DODGE ===
  // If we're in melee range of an enemy AND our HP is low enough that we'd
  // benefit from breaking out of the scrum, dodge AWAY from the nearest
  // melee threat instead of perpendicular to the projectile. This gives
  // bots a way to escape close-quarters punishment without needing the
  // FLEE state to take over.
  let useBackwardsDisengage = false;
  let backDirX = 0, backDirY = 0;
  if (p.target && p.target.alive) {
    const distToTarget = distance(p, p.target);
    const hpFrac = p.hp / p.stats.hp;
    // Trigger backwards dodge: enemy in melee range + meaningfully wounded
    if (distToTarget < 35 && hpFrac < 0.5) {
      useBackwardsDisengage = true;
      const dx = p.x - p.target.x, dy = p.y - p.target.y;
      const dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      backDirX = dx / dl; backDirY = dy / dl;
    }
  }

  if (useBackwardsDisengage) {
    // Move away from melee threat — slightly larger magnitude (it's an
    // escape maneuver, not a weave) and a "↩" tell instead of "↯"
    p._dodgeNudge = {
      x: backDirX * effSpd * 0.55,
      y: backDirY * effSpd * 0.55,
    };
    if (!p._lastDodgeFrame || (m.frame - p._lastDodgeFrame) > 30) {
      m.floats.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y - 16, text: "↩", life: 14, color: "#f7c948" });
      p._lastDodgeFrame = m.frame;
    }
  } else {
    // Perpendicular sidestep — the standard projectile dodge
    const sp = Math.hypot(bestThreat.vx, bestThreat.vy);
    const px = -bestThreat.vy / sp;
    const py =  bestThreat.vx / sp;
    const cross = (p.x - bestThreat.x) * bestThreat.vy - (p.y - bestThreat.y) * bestThreat.vx;
    const side = cross >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
    p._dodgeNudge = {
      x: px * side * effSpd * 0.30,
      y: py * side * effSpd * 0.30,
    };
    if (!p._lastDodgeFrame || (m.frame - p._lastDodgeFrame) > 30) {
      m.floats.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y - 16, text: "↯", life: 12, color: "#88c0d0" });
      p._lastDodgeFrame = m.frame;
    }
  }

  // Set cooldown — shortened in v28.4 from max(8, 28-spd*5) to make
  // dodges feel more responsive. Speedsters now dodge ~every 7 frames.
  const cd = Math.max(5, Math.round(22 - p.stats.spd * 5));
  p._dodgeCdUntil = m.frame + cd;
  return true;
}

/* Apply any dodge nudge accumulated this frame, AFTER state-driven movement. */
function applyDodgeNudge(p, m) {
  if (!p._dodgeNudge) return;
  const n = p._dodgeNudge;
  const ang = Math.atan2(n.y, n.x);
  const mag = Math.hypot(n.x, n.y);
  moveAlong(p, ang, mag);
  p._dodgeNudge = null;
}

/* ============================================================
   MELEE-DISENGAGE DODGE — fires when a bot is in melee scrum
   and either taking heavy hits, on low HP, or wields a primarily
   ranged weapon (sniper/laser). The bot leaps backward away
   from the closest enemy to break the scrum.
   ============================================================ */
function tryDisengageDodge(p, m, effSpd, nearestEnemy, nearestD) {
  if (p.stunned > 0) return false;
  if (p.position === "tank") return false; // tanks don't disengage
  if (p._disengageCdUntil && m.frame < p._disengageCdUntil) return false;
  if (!nearestEnemy || nearestD > 30) return false; // not in melee range

  const wpn = p.weapon || {};
  const isRanged = (wpn.range || 0) > 30;
  const hpFrac = p.hp / p.stats.hp;

  // Trigger conditions:
  //   - ranged-weapon bot stuck in melee (always tries to disengage)
  //   - any non-tank below 35% HP
  //   - sniper/healer/grenadier (always prefer to keep distance)
  const wantsOut =
    isRanged ||
    hpFrac < 0.35 ||
    p.position === "sniper" || p.position === "healer" || p.position === "grenadier";
  if (!wantsOut) return false;

  // Roll once per opportunity — speedsters succeed often, others sometimes
  const skill = clamp(0.25 + p.stats.spd * 0.15, 0.25, 0.7);
  if (SIM_RNG.next() > skill) return false;

  // Backward leap = away from the nearest enemy
  const ang = Math.atan2(p.y - nearestEnemy.y, p.x - nearestEnemy.x);
  // Larger displacement than projectile dodge — this is a real disengage
  moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd * 1.05);

  // Cooldown — about a second
  p._disengageCdUntil = m.frame + 22;

  // Visual tell: backward arrow float
  m.floats.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y - 18, text: "←DASH", life: 16, color: "#88c0d0" });
  return true;
}

/* ============================================================
   HEALER BEHAVIOR — passively heals teammates over time.
   Picks a "patient" (lowest HP teammate, or self if no one needs
   it more) and stays in heal-beam range (~70-90px). Re-evaluates
   patient every ~60 frames. When a beam connects, patient gains
   ~3 HP/sec until full.

   Snipers prefer to target healers (handled in target selection).
   ============================================================ */
function tickHealerBehavior(p, m, effSpd) {
  // Select / re-select patient periodically
  if (!p._patientCheckUntil || m.frame >= p._patientCheckUntil) {
    p._patientCheckUntil = m.frame + 60; // re-evaluate every ~3 sec
    const team = p.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers;
    let lowest = null, lowestFrac = Infinity;
    for (const ally of team) {
      if (!ally.alive) continue;
      const frac = ally.hp / ally.stats.hp;
      // Prefer non-self patients unless self is critical
      const selfPenalty = (ally === p) ? 0.15 : 0;
      const adjusted = frac + selfPenalty;
      if (adjusted < lowestFrac) { lowestFrac = adjusted; lowest = ally; }
    }
    p._patient = lowest;
  }

  const patient = p._patient;
  const wpn = p.weapon || {};
  const HEAL_RANGE = wpn.range || 90;
  const MIN_RANGE = 25; // stay BEHIND patient, not on top of them
  if (!patient || !patient.alive) return;

  const d = distance(p, patient);
  // Move to maintain range — between MIN_RANGE and HEAL_RANGE
  if (d > HEAL_RANGE - 10) {
    // Too far — close in
    const ang = Math.atan2(patient.y - p.y, patient.x - p.x);
    moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd);
  } else if (d < MIN_RANGE) {
    // Too close — back off (this naturally leaves the healer "behind"
    // whoever they're following since allies move forward to engage).
    const ang = Math.atan2(p.y - patient.y, p.x - patient.x);
    moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd * 0.7);
  }
  // Otherwise hold position

  // Heal beam: only fires if in range + LOS clear + patient missing HP
  if (d <= HEAL_RANGE && patient.hp < patient.stats.hp && p.stunned <= 0) {
    if (hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, patient.x, patient.y, m.terrain)) {
      // ~3 HP per second. tickMatch is 20 fps → 0.15 hp/frame.
      const healAmount = 0.15 + (p.stats.dmg || 0) * 0.005;
      patient.hp = Math.min(patient.stats.hp, patient.hp + healAmount);
      // Visual: heal-beam particle along the line, sparingly
      if (m.frame % 6 === 0) {
        const midX = (p.x + patient.x) / 2 + R(-2, 2);
        const midY = (p.y + patient.y) / 2 + R(-2, 2);
        m.floats.push({ x: midX, y: midY, text: "✚", life: 8, color: "#7ed884" });
      }
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   GRENADIER BEHAVIOR — throws ONE grenade per match (the cooldown
   is set so high that effectively only one full grenade lands per
   match unless rounds run very long), then degenerates to weak
   melee. The grenade is a slow projectile with AoE on impact.
   ============================================================ */
function tickGrenadierBehavior(p, m, effSpd, nearestEnemy, nearestD) {
  if (!nearestEnemy) return;

  const wpn = p.weapon || {};
  const GRENADE_RANGE = wpn.range || 220;
  const isLauncher = (wpn.id === "launcher");

  // Position: try to keep ~120-180px from nearest enemy. Close to throw
  // grenades effectively, far enough to not get melee'd.
  const idealDist = isLauncher ? 150 : 30;
  if (nearestD > idealDist + 30) {
    const ang = Math.atan2(nearestEnemy.y - p.y, nearestEnemy.x - p.x);
    moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd);
  } else if (nearestD < idealDist - 30 && isLauncher) {
    // Too close — back off if we still have a grenade ready
    const ang = Math.atan2(p.y - nearestEnemy.y, p.x - nearestEnemy.x);
    moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd * 0.7);
  }

  // Fire (grenade or weak melee)
  p.target = nearestEnemy;
  if (p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0) {
    if (isLauncher && nearestD <= GRENADE_RANGE && nearestD > 30) {
      // Throw grenade
      if (hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, nearestEnemy.x, nearestEnemy.y, m.terrain)) {
        fireGrenade(p, nearestEnemy, m);
        // === v28.5: Cooldown reduced 4× → 2.5× of baseline so grenadiers
        // throw repeatedly. At 60fps base ~0.4s cooldown × 2.5 = ~1s
        // between throws, scaled by speed. User reported only seeing the
        // initial throw. ===
        p.attackCooldown = Math.round(computeCooldown(p, effSpd) * 2.5);
      }
    } else if (nearestD <= COMBAT.hitRange) {
      // Fall back to melee — punching, basically
      fireAttack(p, nearestEnemy, m);
      p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   GRENADE PROJECTILE — slow arcing projectile with AoE on impact.
   Spawned by Grenade Launchers. Damages all enemies within radius.
   ============================================================ */
function fireGrenade(attacker, target, m) {
  const wpn = attacker.weapon;
