  const dx = target.x - attacker.x;
  const dy = target.y - attacker.y;
  const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  const sp = wpn.projSpeed || 6;
  // Aim where target will be in (dist/sp) frames
  const tFrames = dist / sp;
  const aimX = target.x + (target.vx || 0) * tFrames;
  const aimY = target.y + (target.vy || 0) * tFrames;
  const aimDx = aimX - attacker.x, aimDy = aimY - attacker.y;
  const aimDist = Math.hypot(aimDx, aimDy) || 1;
  const ux = aimDx / aimDist, uy = aimDy / aimDist;
  // Slight random spread
  const spreadAng = (SIM_RNG.next() - 0.5) * (wpn.inacc || 0.05);
  const cs = Math.cos(spreadAng), sn = Math.sin(spreadAng);
  const fx = ux * cs - uy * sn;
  const fy = ux * sn + uy * cs;
  m.projectiles = m.projectiles || [];
  m.projectiles.push({
    x: attacker.x, y: attacker.y,
    vx: fx * sp, vy: fy * sp,
    teamId: attacker.teamId,
    ttl: Math.ceil(aimDist / sp) + 4, // explode near target
    dmg: 0, // damage handled at AoE explosion time
    isCrit: false,
    wpn: wpn,
    isGrenade: true,
    explodeAtX: aimX, explodeAtY: aimY,
    blastRadius: 60,
    blastDamage: clamp((attacker.stats.dmg || 10) + 6, 12, 35),
  });
  m.floats.push({ x: attacker.x, y: attacker.y - 14, text: "💣", life: 18, color: "#f7c948" });
}

/* Detonate a grenade projectile — AoE damage + knockback to enemies in radius. */
function detonateGrenade(pr, m) {
  const enemies = pr.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamBplayers : m.teamAplayers;
  for (const e of enemies) {
    if (!e.alive) continue;
    const ddx = e.x - pr.x, ddy = e.y - pr.y;
    const d = Math.hypot(ddx, ddy);
    if (d > pr.blastRadius) continue;
    // Falloff with distance
    const falloff = 1 - (d / pr.blastRadius);
    const dmg = Math.max(2, Math.round(pr.blastDamage * falloff));
    // Find a representative attacker (nearest teammate) for kill credit
    const teamList = pr.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers;
    const attacker = teamList.find(t => t.alive && t.position === "grenadier") || teamList[0];
    if (!attacker) continue;
    e.attackedBy = attacker.id;
    e.attackedByExpires = 90;
    applyDamage(attacker, e, dmg, false, m);
    // === v28.5 knockback ===
    // Push the enemy away from the blast center. Falloff-scaled so a
    // direct hit shoves hard, edge of blast just nudges. We write into
    // _knockbackVx/_knockbackVy which moveAlong reads and decays.
    if (e.alive) {
      const dl = Math.max(1, d);
      const ux = ddx / dl, uy = ddy / dl;
      const power = 6 + 8 * falloff;  // 6..14 px/frame initial push
      e._knockbackVx = (e._knockbackVx || 0) + ux * power;
      e._knockbackVy = (e._knockbackVy || 0) + uy * power;
      e._knockbackTtl = Math.max(e._knockbackTtl || 0, 12);  // ~0.2s
    }
  }
  // === v28.5 lasting explosion visual ===
  // Push an entry onto m.explosions for the renderer to animate
  // (expanding rings, smoke billows). Replaces the spammy emoji
  // floats from v28.4 with one coherent effect.
  m.explosions = m.explosions || [];
  m.explosions.push({
    x: pr.x, y: pr.y,
    radius: pr.blastRadius,
    frame: m.frame || 0,
    maxFrames: 28,
    teamColor: (TEAM_BY_ID[pr.teamId] && TEAM_BY_ID[pr.teamId].color) || "#f7c948",
  });
  // Single emoji float for kill-feed-style readability
  m.floats.push({ x: pr.x, y: pr.y - 8, text: "💥 BOOM", life: 22, color: "#f7c948" });
  if (typeof triggerShake === "function") triggerShake(12);
  // Trigger crowd cheer at the explosion site too
  m._crowdCheer = {
    x: pr.x, y: pr.y,
    frame: m.frame || 0,
    radius: 240,
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   AI STATE EXECUTION
   ============================================================ */
function executeAIState(p, enemyAlive, m) {
  // Base effective speed (stat × 1.4 baseline scale + booster)
  let effSpd = (p.stats.spd + (p.boostMods?.spd || 0)) * 1.4;

  // === v28 Tag mode: speed asymmetry between It and prey ===
  // Without this, Tag is a stalemate — fleeing bots and the It bot have
  // identical speed and "It" can never make contact. Give It a chase
  // bonus and prey a small speed reduction so each successful tag is
  // actually achievable in a few seconds of chase. End-game (≤3 prey)
  // boosts It speed further so the last 1-2 prey can't perma-loop.
  if (m.gameMode === "tag" && m.tag) {
    if (m.tag.itId === p.id) {
      const allAlive = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].filter(e => e.alive);
      const livingPrey = allAlive.filter(e => e.id !== p.id &&
        !(m.tag.tagFreezes && m.tag.tagFreezes[e.id])).length;
      // Scale: 14 prey → 1.55×, 3 prey → 1.75×, 1 prey → 1.95×
      const boost = livingPrey <= 1 ? 1.95
                  : livingPrey <= 3 ? 1.75
                  : 1.55;
      effSpd *= boost;
    } else if (!(m.tag.tagFreezes && m.tag.tagFreezes[p.id])) {
      effSpd *= 0.92;  // prey slightly slower
    }
  }

  const wpn = p.weapon || {};
  const ranged = (wpn.range || 0) > 30;

  // Find nearest enemy for dodge + state purposes
  let nearestEnemy = null, nearestD = Infinity;
  for (const e of enemyAlive) {
    const d = distance(p, e);
    if (d < nearestD) { nearestD = d; nearestEnemy = e; }
  }

  // === SPECIALIZED POSITION TICKS ===
  // Healer and Grenadier override the standard ENGAGE/SEEK_*/FLEE flow.
  // They still benefit from dodging (called below) but have their own
  // movement + attack behavior. They never enter SEEK_CORE since they
  // shouldn't break formation for it.
  if (p.position === "healer") {
    tryDodgeProjectile(p, m, effSpd);
    if (!p._dodgeNudge) tryDisengageDodge(p, m, effSpd, nearestEnemy, nearestD);
    tickHealerBehavior(p, m, effSpd);
    applyDodgeNudge(p, m);
    return;
  }
  if (p.position === "grenadier") {
    tryDodgeProjectile(p, m, effSpd);
    if (!p._dodgeNudge) tryDisengageDodge(p, m, effSpd, nearestEnemy, nearestD);
    tickGrenadierBehavior(p, m, effSpd, nearestEnemy, nearestD);
    applyDodgeNudge(p, m);
    return;
  }

  // DODGE — fires before state-driven movement.
  tryDodgeProjectile(p, m, effSpd);
  if (!p._dodgeNudge) {
    tryDisengageDodge(p, m, effSpd, nearestEnemy, nearestD);
  }

  switch (p.aiState) {
    case "CTF_RUSH": {
      // Flag carrier in CTF mode — rush toward own base. Cannot fire.
      // Reduced speed (carrierSpeedMult applied via boostMods-like mult below).
      const myBase = m.basesById && m.basesById[p.teamId];
      if (!myBase) { p.aiState = "IDLE"; break; }
      const ang = Math.atan2(myBase.y - p.y, myBase.x - p.x);
      const cfg = (LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig || {}).ctf || {};
      const speedMult = cfg.carrierSpeedMult || 0.80;
      moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd * speedMult);
      break;
    }

    /* ============================================================
       MODE-SPECIFIC EXECUTION STATES (v28.1)
       Most just convert a stored target point or angle into movement.
       ENGAGE-style states also fire if in range.
       ============================================================ */
    case "KOTH_KITE": {
      // Crown carrier kiting. Move along p._koth_moveAng. NO firing.
      if (typeof p._koth_moveAng === "number") {
        moveAlong(p, p._koth_moveAng, effSpd * 1.05);
      }
      break;
    }
    case "KOTH_ESCORT":
    case "CTF_ESCORT_LEAD":
    case "CTF_ESCORT_REAR":
    case "CTF_HOLD": {
      // Move toward a stored escort point (p._escort_x, _escort_y).
      // Once near it, hold position and engage if a target is set.
      if (typeof p._escort_x === "number") {
        const dx = p._escort_x - p.x, dy = p._escort_y - p.y;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (d > 18) moveAlong(p, Math.atan2(dy, dx), effSpd);
      }
      // Fire opportunistically at the escort target if in range
      if (p.target && p.target.alive && p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0) {
        const td = distance(p, p.target);
        const inRange = ranged
          ? (td <= wpn.range && hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, p.target.x, p.target.y, m.terrain))
          : td <= COMBAT.hitRange;
        if (inRange) {
          fireAttack(p, p.target, m);
          p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    case "KOTH_FLANK":
    case "CTF_FLANK": {
      // Move to flank point, then close on target
      if (typeof p._escort_x === "number") {
        const dx = p._escort_x - p.x, dy = p._escort_y - p.y;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (d > 30) {
          moveAlong(p, Math.atan2(dy, dx), effSpd);
        } else if (p.target && p.target.alive) {
          // Reached flank; close on target
          const tang = Math.atan2(p.target.y - p.y, p.target.x - p.x);
          moveAlong(p, tang, effSpd);
        }
      }
      // Fire if in range
      if (p.target && p.target.alive && p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0) {
        const td = distance(p, p.target);
        const inRange = ranged
          ? (td <= wpn.range && hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, p.target.x, p.target.y, m.terrain))
          : td <= COMBAT.hitRange;
        if (inRange) {
          fireAttack(p, p.target, m);
          p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    case "KOTH_RACE":
    case "CTF_RACE": {
      // Race for crown / flag. Pure movement at full sprint, no firing.
      let ang;
      if (p.aiState === "KOTH_RACE" && typeof p._koth_moveAng === "number") {
        ang = p._koth_moveAng;
      } else if (typeof p._escort_x === "number") {
        ang = Math.atan2(p._escort_y - p.y, p._escort_x - p.x);
      } else break;
      moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd * 1.10);
      break;
    }
    case "TAG_HUNT": {
      // It pursuing prey with predictive aim-ahead. p._tag_aim_x/y is
      // already lead-corrected; move toward it.
      if (typeof p._tag_aim_x === "number" && p.target && p.target.alive) {
        const dx = p._tag_aim_x - p.x, dy = p._tag_aim_y - p.y;
        moveAlong(p, Math.atan2(dy, dx), effSpd * 1.10);
        // If we're within tag-strike range, fire melee
        const td = distance(p, p.target);
        if (td <= COMBAT.hitRange + 12 && p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0) {
          const realWpn = p.weapon;
          const fb = p.meleeFallback || pickMeleeFallback(p);
          p.weapon = fb;
          resolveAttack(p, p.target, m);
          p.weapon = realWpn;
          p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    case "TAG_SHIELD":
    case "TAG_BREAK":
    case "TAG_FLEE": {
      // Prey movement — head to stored move target (always exists)
      if (typeof p._tag_move_x === "number") {
        const dx = p._tag_move_x - p.x, dy = p._tag_move_y - p.y;
        moveAlong(p, Math.atan2(dy, dx), effSpd * 1.10);
      }
      // No firing — Tag is melee-only and prey have no business fighting back
      break;
    }
    case "FROZEN": {
      // Frozen — do nothing. Stunned timer enforces no movement anyway.
      break;
    }

    case "IDLE": {
      // === v28.13: GUARD-style hold instead of drift-to-center ===
      // The old IDLE drifted bots toward arena center every tick. With
      // many bots simultaneously, this caused visible jiggle as boids
      // separation pushed them apart while center-attraction pulled
      // them back. The new IDLE picks a guard position once and holds
      // it: typically near a healthy ally or the team center, slightly
      // forward of own spawn. Re-evaluates every 60 frames or when
      // displaced significantly.
      const cxw = COMBAT.arenaW / 2, cyw = COMBAT.arenaH / 2;
      const recompute =
        (p._guardCommitTtl || 0) <= 0 ||
        typeof p._guardX !== "number" ||
        Math.hypot(p.x - p._guardX, p.y - p._guardY) > 240;
      if (recompute) {
        // Pick a guard point: 60% toward team centroid, 40% toward arena center
        const teamState = m._teamAI && m._teamAI[p.teamId];
        let gx, gy;
        if (teamState && teamState.teamCenter) {
          gx = teamState.teamCenter.x * 0.6 + cxw * 0.4;
          gy = teamState.teamCenter.y * 0.6 + cyw * 0.4;
        } else {
          gx = cxw; gy = cyw;
        }
        // Add small per-bot offset so 7 bots don't all stand on one point
        // (use bot id for stable per-bot spacing — not random per frame)
        const offset = (p.id * 0.61803) % 1;        // golden-ratio scrambled
        const offAng = offset * Math.PI * 2;
        const offR = 60 + (offset * 30);
        gx += Math.cos(offAng) * offR;
        gy += Math.sin(offAng) * offR;
        // Clamp to playable area
        p._guardX = clamp(gx, 60, COMBAT.arenaW - 60);
        p._guardY = clamp(gy, 60, COMBAT.arenaH - 60);
        p._guardCommitTtl = 60;
      }
      p._guardCommitTtl--;
      // Move to the guard point only if significantly displaced (>40px).
      // Below 40px, hold in place to avoid jiggle.
      const dx = p._guardX - p.x;
      const dy = p._guardY - p.y;
      const distToGuard = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (distToGuard > 40) {
        const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
        moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd * 0.55);
      }
      break;
    }
    case "RALLY": {
      // === v28.2: Directed retreat toward a healthy teammate ===
      // Move toward the rally point set in decideAIState (the location
      // of the nearest healthy teammate, biased toward team center).
      // While retreating, fire over our shoulder if the threat is in
      // range and we have line-of-sight — small "covering fire" effect.
      const rx = p._rally_x;
      const ry = p._rally_y;
      if (typeof rx === "number" && typeof ry === "number") {
        const dx = rx - p.x, dy = ry - p.y;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (d > 30) {
          moveAlong(p, Math.atan2(dy, dx), effSpd * 1.10);  // 10% sprint bonus
        }
      }
      // === v28.4: Fire desperate covering shots while retreating ===
      // Previously gated on `td <= wpn.range`, which prevented firing
      // once the bot exceeded weapon range. Now we drop that check
      // (the projectile may miss at long range — that's fine, it still
      // shows the bot fighting back). Range gating happens elsewhere
      // (in fireAttack's miss-roll). LOS check stays so we don't shoot
      // through walls.
      if (ranged && p.target && p.target.alive &&
          p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0 &&
          hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, p.target.x, p.target.y, m.terrain)) {
        fireAttack(p, p.target, m);
        p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
      }
      break;
    }
    case "SNIPER_HOLD": {
      // === v28.2: Sniper kite — back off to maintain optimal range ===
      // If the closest enemy is within ~40% of weapon range, retreat
      // toward team backline (or just away from threat) until at ~70%
      // weapon range. Fires at the team focus target while moving.
      if (!p.target || !p.target.alive) {
        p.aiState = "IDLE";
        p.aiStateFrames = 0;
        break;
      }
      // Find immediate threat (nearest enemy of any team-aware list)
      const enemies = (p.teamId === m.teamAId) ? m.teamBplayers : m.teamAplayers;
      let threat = null, threatD = Infinity;
      for (const e of enemies) {
        if (!e.alive) continue;
        const d = distance(p, e);
        if (d < threatD) { threatD = d; threat = e; }
      }
      // Back away from immediate threat
      const optimalRange = wpn.range * 0.70;
      if (threat && threatD < optimalRange) {
        let backAng = Math.atan2(p.y - threat.y, p.x - threat.x);
        // Bias toward team center if we're near a wall
        const teamState = m._teamAI && m._teamAI[p.teamId];
        if (teamState) {
          const tc = teamState.teamCenter;
          const tcAng = Math.atan2(tc.y - p.y, tc.x - p.x);
          // Mild blend so we stay near team but still back away
          const ax = Math.cos(backAng) * 0.7 + Math.cos(tcAng) * 0.3;
          const ay = Math.sin(backAng) * 0.7 + Math.sin(tcAng) * 0.3;
          backAng = Math.atan2(ay, ax);
        }
        moveAlong(p, backAng, effSpd);
      }
      // Fire at team focus / our current target
      if (p.target && p.target.alive && p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0) {
        const td = distance(p, p.target);
        if (td <= wpn.range && hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, p.target.x, p.target.y, m.terrain)) {
          fireAttack(p, p.target, m);
          p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    case "FLEE": {
      // First try to flee TOWARD a health pickup — far better than just running
      let bestHP = null, bestHPd = Infinity;
      for (const d of m.drops) {
        if (d.slot !== "health") continue;
        const dx = p.x - d.x, dy = p.y - d.y;
        const dist2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
        if (dist2 < 320*320 && dist2 < bestHPd) { bestHPd = dist2; bestHP = d; }
      }
      if (bestHP) {
        const ang = Math.atan2(bestHP.y - p.y, bestHP.x - p.x);
        moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd * 1.15); // adrenaline + medkit hope
      } else if (p.target && p.target.alive) {
        // === v28 fix: bias the flee direction toward the field center,
        // weighted by how close the bot is to a wall. The pure
        // "atan2(p.y - target.y, p.x - target.x)" angle frequently
        // points at a corner; we mix in an angle-toward-center to keep
        // the bot in playable space. ===
        let awayAng = Math.atan2(p.y - p.target.y, p.x - p.target.x);
        const cxw = COMBAT.arenaW / 2, cyw = COMBAT.arenaH / 2;
        const wallProximity = Math.max(
          (80 - p.x) / 80,
          (p.x - (COMBAT.arenaW - 80)) / 80,
          (60 - p.y) / 60,
          (p.y - (COMBAT.arenaH - 60)) / 60,
          0
        );
        if (wallProximity > 0) {
          // Heavy blend when near a wall — at the very edge, flee
          // toward center entirely (better than backing into a corner).
          const angToCenter = Math.atan2(cyw - p.y, cxw - p.x);
          const blend = clamp(wallProximity * 0.85, 0, 0.95);
          // Angle blending uses sin/cos to avoid the discontinuity at ±π.
          const ax = Math.cos(awayAng) * (1 - blend) + Math.cos(angToCenter) * blend;
          const ay = Math.sin(awayAng) * (1 - blend) + Math.sin(angToCenter) * blend;
          awayAng = Math.atan2(ay, ax);
        }
        moveAlong(p, awayAng, effSpd * 1.1);
      } else {
        p.aiState = "IDLE";
        p.aiStateFrames = 0;
        break;
      }
      // Maybe fire a desperate parting shot (suppressed for mode carriers)
      if (!p._modeCarrier && ranged && p.target && p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0
          && hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, p.target.x, p.target.y, m.terrain)) {
        fireAttack(p, p.target, m);
        p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
      }
      break;
    }
    case "MELEE": {
      // Close-quarters override for ranged-weapon bots. We don't actually
      // swap weapons — we use the cached meleeFallback profile in place
      // of p.weapon for the resolveAttack call. Once the enemy is out of
      // melee switch range again, decideAIState transitions back to ENGAGE.
      if (!p.target || !p.target.alive) { p.aiState = "IDLE"; p.aiStateFrames = 0; break; }
      const d = distance(p, p.target);
      // Tag mode: It bot gets a 35% speed boost when chasing (otherwise
      // they can never catch a fleeing bot of similar speed). Also use
      // a wider effective hit range for tagging since the goal is to
      // make contact, not strictly land a melee weapon.
      const isTagIt = (m.gameMode === "tag" && m.tag && p.id === m.tag.itId);
      const chaseSpeed = effSpd * (isTagIt ? 1.40 : 1.05);
      const tagRange = isTagIt ? COMBAT.hitRange + 12 : COMBAT.hitRange + 6;
      // Close to melee distance
      if (d > tagRange) {
        const ang = Math.atan2(p.target.y - p.y, p.target.x - p.x);
        moveAlong(p, ang, chaseSpeed);
      }
      // Fire when in actual melee range
      if (d <= tagRange && p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0) {
        // Temporarily swap weapon profile for the attack resolution
        const realWpn = p.weapon;
        const fb = p.meleeFallback || pickMeleeFallback(p);
        p.weapon = fb;
        resolveAttack(p, p.target, m);
        p.weapon = realWpn;
        p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
      }
      break;
    }
    case "SEEK_ITEM": {
      const drop = p.itemTarget;
      // === v28 fix: abort SEEK_ITEM if a threat enters close range
      // (was only checked at decideAIState cadence; now also in execute).
      if (!drop || !m.drops.includes(drop)) {
        p.aiState = "IDLE";
        p.aiStateFrames = 0;
        break;
      }
      // Recompute nearest threat at execution time
      let threat = null, threatD = Infinity;
      for (const e of enemyAlive) {
        const dd = distance(p, e);
        if (dd < threatD) { threatD = dd; threat = e; }
      }
      if (threat && threatD < 60) {
        // Abandon item, pivot to engagement
        p.target = threat;
        p.aiState = "ENGAGE";
        p.aiStateFrames = 0;
        break;
      }
      const ang = Math.atan2(drop.y - p.y, drop.x - p.x);
      moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd);
      break;
    }
    case "SEEK_CORE": {
      if (!m.powerCore || m.powerCore.state !== "active") {
        p.aiState = "IDLE";
        p.aiStateFrames = 0;
        break;
      }
      const ang = Math.atan2(m.powerCore.y - p.y, m.powerCore.x - p.x);
      moveAlong(p, ang, effSpd * 1.05);
      // Opportunistic shooting while running
      if (ranged && p.target && p.target.alive && p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0) {
        const d = distance(p, p.target);
        if (d <= wpn.range && hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, p.target.x, p.target.y, m.terrain)) {
          fireAttack(p, p.target, m);
          p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    case "FINISH_OFF":
    case "ENGAGE":
    default: {
      if (!p.target || !p.target.alive) {
        let nearest = null, nearestD = Infinity;
        for (const e of enemyAlive) {
          const d = distance(p, e);
          if (d < nearestD) { nearestD = d; nearest = e; }
        }
        p.target = nearest;
      }
      // === v28 fix: if STILL no target after re-search, drop to IDLE
      // so we drift toward center instead of freezing in place.
      if (!p.target) { p.aiState = "IDLE"; p.aiStateFrames = 0; break; }

      const d = distance(p, p.target);
      const losClear = !ranged || hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, p.target.x, p.target.y, m.terrain);
      const isSniper = p.position === "sniper";
      const idealDist = ranged
        ? (isSniper ? Math.min(wpn.range * 0.5, 200) : Math.min(wpn.range * 0.6, 220))
        : 18;
      const myHalf = p.x < COMBAT.arenaW / 2 ? "left" : "right";
      const onBackEdge = (myHalf === "left" && p.x < 80) || (myHalf === "right" && p.x > COMBAT.arenaW - 80);
      // === v28.13: HYSTERESIS on tooClose to prevent threshold ping-pong ===
      // Enter tooClose mode at d < 0.5*idealDist; exit only when d > 0.65*idealDist.
      // The state is held in p._tooCloseMode (boolean).
      const enterTooClose = ranged && d < idealDist * 0.50 && losClear && !onBackEdge;
      const exitTooClose  = !ranged || d > idealDist * 0.65 || !losClear || onBackEdge;
      if (enterTooClose) p._tooCloseMode = true;
      else if (exitTooClose) p._tooCloseMode = false;
      const tooClose = !!p._tooCloseMode;

      // === v28.13: HEADING COMMITMENT to prevent per-tick angle pivots ===
      // Compute the desired heading once, commit for HEADING_COMMIT_FRAMES.
      // Re-evaluate when: target died, target swapped, distance bracket changed
      // (tooClose/in-range/far), navAvoidance triggered, or commit expired.
      const HEADING_COMMIT_FRAMES = 8;
      const distBracket = tooClose ? "back" : (d > idealDist || !losClear) ? "close" : "hold";
      const bracketChanged = p._headingBracket !== distBracket;
      const targetChanged = p._headingTargetId !== p.target.id;
      const commitExpired = (p._headingCommitTtl || 0) <= 0;
      if (bracketChanged || targetChanged || commitExpired) {
        // (Re)compute heading
        if (distBracket === "back") {
          let backAng = Math.atan2(p.y - p.target.y, p.x - p.target.x);
          const centerX = COMBAT.arenaW / 2, centerY = COMBAT.arenaH / 2;
          const distFromCenter = Math.hypot(p.x - centerX, p.y - centerY);
          if (distFromCenter > 220) {
            const angToCenter = Math.atan2(centerY - p.y, centerX - p.x);
            const wallProximity = Math.max(
              (80 - p.x) / 80,
              (p.x - (COMBAT.arenaW - 80)) / 80,
              (60 - p.y) / 60,
              (p.y - (COMBAT.arenaH - 60)) / 60
            );
            if (wallProximity > 0) {
              const blend = clamp(wallProximity * 0.7, 0, 0.8);
              backAng = backAng * (1 - blend) + angToCenter * blend;
            }
          }
          p._headingAng = backAng;
          p._headingSpdMult = 0.7;
        } else if (distBracket === "close") {
          p._headingAng = Math.atan2(p.target.y - p.y, p.target.x - p.x);
          p._headingSpdMult = 1.0;
        } else { // "hold"
          p._headingAng = null;  // no movement; just shoot
          p._headingSpdMult = 0;
        }
        p._headingBracket = distBracket;
        p._headingTargetId = p.target.id;
        p._headingCommitTtl = HEADING_COMMIT_FRAMES;
      } else {
        p._headingCommitTtl--;
      }

      // Apply committed heading
      if (p._headingAng !== null) {
        const moved = moveAlong(p, p._headingAng, effSpd * p._headingSpdMult);
        if (!moved && distBracket === "close" && m.terrain) {
          if (!p.navAvoidanceFrames || p.navAvoidanceFrames <= 0) {
            p.navAvoidanceFrames = 18;
            p.navAvoidanceDir = SIM_RNG.next() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
            p._headingCommitTtl = 0;  // force recompute next frame
          }
        }
      }
      if (p.navAvoidanceFrames > 0) {
        const baseAng = Math.atan2(p.target.y - p.y, p.target.x - p.x);
        const perpAng = baseAng + (Math.PI / 2) * p.navAvoidanceDir;
        const nudged = moveAlong(p, perpAng, effSpd);
        if (!nudged) {
          // Perpendicular also blocked — flip direction and try the other side
          p.navAvoidanceDir = -p.navAvoidanceDir;
          p.navAvoidanceFrames = Math.max(p.navAvoidanceFrames - 1, 6);
        }
        p.navAvoidanceFrames--;
      }

      // Attempt attack if in range AND we have line of sight
      const inRange = ranged
        ? (d <= wpn.range && hasLineOfSight(p.x, p.y, p.target.x, p.target.y, m.terrain))
        : d <= COMBAT.hitRange;
      if (inRange && p.attackCooldown <= 0 && p.stunned <= 0) {
        fireAttack(p, p.target, m);
        p.attackCooldown = computeCooldown(p, effSpd);
      }
      break;
    }
  }
  if (p.attackCooldown > 0) p.attackCooldown--;

  // Auto-pickup if walking over a drop (any slot, any score — if you're on
  // top of it, take it and worry about the swap later)
  attemptScavenge(p, m);
}

function moveAlong(p, ang, speed) {
  const startX = p.x, startY = p.y;
  let nx = p.x + Math.cos(ang) * speed;
  let ny = p.y + Math.sin(ang) * speed;

  // === v28.5 grenade knockback ===
  // If a grenade pushed us this round, apply the residual velocity on
  // top of intended movement and decay it. Decay 0.82× per frame ≈
  // ~12 frames of visible drift before settling. TTL counts down too
  // so it doesn't linger forever even if velocity stays large.
  if (p._knockbackTtl && p._knockbackTtl > 0) {
    nx += (p._knockbackVx || 0);
    ny += (p._knockbackVy || 0);
    p._knockbackVx = (p._knockbackVx || 0) * 0.82;
    p._knockbackVy = (p._knockbackVy || 0) * 0.82;
    p._knockbackTtl -= 1;
    if (p._knockbackTtl <= 0) {
      p._knockbackVx = 0; p._knockbackVy = 0;
    }
  }

  // Apply separation push from nearby allies + enemies (boids-style)
  // === v28.13: relax separation between same-team allies in active pursuit ===
  // When both bots are heading toward the same focus target, full separation
  // creates visible weaving as each pushes the other off-line. We softly relax
  // separation when the nearby ally is also in an engage-style state.
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (m) {
    const all = m.teamAplayers.concat(m.teamBplayers);
    const inActiveEngage = (s) => s === "ENGAGE" || s === "MELEE" || s === "FINISH_OFF" || s === "SEEK_CORE" || s === "CTF_RUSH";
    const myEngaged = inActiveEngage(p.aiState);
    let pushX = 0, pushY = 0;
    for (const o of all) {
      if (o === p || !o.alive) continue;
      // Same-team and both engaged → use a reduced radius (less weaving).
      let sepRadius = 22;
      let force = 1.0;
      if (o.teamId === p.teamId && myEngaged && inActiveEngage(o.aiState)) {
        sepRadius = 18;
        force = 0.5;
      }
      const dx = nx - o.x, dy = ny - o.y;
      const d2 = dx*dx + dy*dy;
      if (d2 < sepRadius * sepRadius && d2 > 0.01) {
        const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
        const f = (sepRadius - d) / sepRadius * force;
        pushX += (dx / d) * f * speed * 0.6;
        pushY += (dy / d) * f * speed * 0.6;
      }
    }
    nx += pushX;
    ny += pushY;
  }

  // Terrain collision — wall-slide if blocked
  if (m && m.terrain) {
    if (collidesTerrain(nx, ny, m.terrain)) {
      // Try X-only
      if (!collidesTerrain(nx, p.y, m.terrain)) {
        ny = p.y;
      } else if (!collidesTerrain(p.x, ny, m.terrain)) {
        nx = p.x;
      } else {
        nx = p.x; ny = p.y;
      }
    }
  }

  p.vx = nx - p.x;
  p.vy = ny - p.y;
  p.x = clamp(nx, 12, COMBAT.arenaW - 12);
  p.y = clamp(ny, 12, COMBAT.arenaH - 12);
  p.facing = p.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1;

  // Did we actually move? (Returns false if fully stuck — caller can fall
  // back to perpendicular strafe to round corners.)
  return Math.abs(p.x - startX) > 0.1 || Math.abs(p.y - startY) > 0.1;
}

// Test whether a point (with player half-size ~10px) collides with any
// terrain block. Terrain blocks are squares with x,y as top-left + size.
function collidesTerrain(x, y, terrain) {
  const PAD = 10;
  for (const t of terrain) {
    if (x + PAD > t.x && x - PAD < t.x + t.size &&
        y + PAD > t.y && y - PAD < t.y + t.size) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

// Liang-Barsky segment-vs-AABB test. Returns true if the segment from
// (x0,y0) to (x1,y1) intersects the axis-aligned rect at (rx,ry,rw,rh).
// Used for swept projectile collision and line-of-sight checks.
function segmentHitsRect(x0, y0, x1, y1, rx, ry, rw, rh) {
  const dx = x1 - x0, dy = y1 - y0;
  let tEnter = 0, tExit = 1;
  const tests = [[-dx, x0 - rx], [dx, rx + rw - x0], [-dy, y0 - ry], [dy, ry + rh - y0]];
  for (const [p, q] of tests) {
    if (p === 0) {
      if (q < 0) return false;
    } else {
      const t = q / p;
      if (p < 0) {
        if (t > tExit) return false;
        if (t > tEnter) tEnter = t;
      } else {
        if (t < tEnter) return false;
        if (t < tExit) tExit = t;
      }
    }
  }
  return tEnter <= tExit;
}

// Does the line from a→b cross any terrain block?
function hasLineOfSight(ax, ay, bx, by, terrain) {
  if (!terrain || !terrain.length) return true;
  for (const t of terrain) {
    if (segmentHitsRect(ax, ay, bx, by, t.x, t.y, t.size, t.size)) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function computeCooldown(p, effSpd) {
  const baseCd = Math.max(6, Math.round(18 / Math.max(0.6, effSpd / 1.4)));
  const cdScale = 1 + (p.weapon?.cdMod || 0);
  const globalScale = COMBAT.cdMult || 1;
  return Math.max(4, Math.round(baseCd * cdScale * globalScale));
}

/* ============================================================
   FIRE ATTACK — branches melee vs projectile
   ============================================================ */
function fireAttack(attacker, target, m) {
  const wpn = attacker.weapon || {};
  const range = wpn.range || 0;

  if (range <= 30) {
    // Melee — instant resolve as before
    resolveAttack(attacker, target, m);
    return;
  }

  // Ranged — spawn a projectile with accuracy spread
  const dx = target.x - attacker.x, dy = target.y - attacker.y;
  const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
  const baseAng = Math.atan2(dy, dx);

  // === v28: Pre-roll a "miss" — separate from spread cone. Probability
  // scales with distance from 0 (point-blank) to 1 (max range), with a
  // floor (rangedMissAtZeroRange) and ceiling (rangedMissAtMaxRange).
  // A pre-rolled miss yanks the aim angle hard outside the spread cone
  // so the shot visually goes wide and almost never connects. ===
  const rangeFrac = clamp(dist / Math.max(1, wpn.range), 0, 1);
  const missChance = COMBAT.rangedMissAtZeroRange +
                     (COMBAT.rangedMissAtMaxRange - COMBAT.rangedMissAtZeroRange) * rangeFrac;
  const isMiss = SIM_RNG.next() < missChance;

  // Inaccuracy scales with: weapon baseline, distance, target velocity
  const targetSpeed = Math.sqrt(target.vx*target.vx + target.vy*target.vy);
  const distFactor = (dist / wpn.range) * 0.18;             // out to weapon range adds spread
  const velFactor = Math.min(targetSpeed * 0.04, 0.15);     // fast targets harder to track
  let spread = (wpn.inacc || 0.05) + distFactor + velFactor;
  if (isMiss) {
    // Force a wide shot — angle is pulled OUTSIDE the spread cone in a
    // random direction. Looks like the bullet whiffs past the target.
    spread = Math.max(spread * 3, 0.18);
  }
  const aimAng = baseAng + (SIM_RNG.next() - 0.5) * 2 * spread;

  // === v28.5 Scattergun pellet branch ===
  // Fire wpn.pellets shots in a single attack, each with its own spread
  // and its own miss roll. Per-pellet damage is reduced (33% of base)
  // so a full point-blank cluster does ~1.65× base damage but at long
  // range you typically only catch 1 pellet, dealing 0.33× — matching
  // the short-range-shotgun identity.
  if (wpn.pellets && wpn.pellets > 1) {
    const pellets = wpn.pellets;
    const speed = wpn.projSpeed || 12;
    for (let i = 0; i < pellets; i++) {
      // Per-pellet spread is wider — pellets fan out
      const pelletSpread = (wpn.inacc || 0.20) * 1.5;
      const pelletMissChance = missChance * 0.7;  // each pellet rolls independently
      const pMiss = SIM_RNG.next() < pelletMissChance;
      let pSpread = pelletSpread + (dist / wpn.range) * 0.30;
      if (pMiss) pSpread = Math.max(pSpread * 2, 0.30);
      const pAng = baseAng + (SIM_RNG.next() - 0.5) * 2 * pSpread;
      m.projectiles.push({
        x: attacker.x + Math.cos(baseAng) * 8,
        y: attacker.y + Math.sin(baseAng) * 8 - 4,
        vx: Math.cos(pAng) * speed,
        vy: Math.sin(pAng) * speed,
        dmg: Math.round((attacker.stats.dmg + (wpn.dmgMod || 0)) * 0.33),
        fromId: attacker.id,
        teamId: attacker.teamId,
        ttl: Math.ceil(wpn.range / speed) + 4,
        wpn,
        isCrit: SIM_RNG.next() < clamp(attacker.stats.crit + (wpn.critMod || 0) + (attacker.armor?.critMod || 0), 0.02, 0.5),
      });
    }
    // Muzzle flash — bigger for the scattergun
    m.floats.push({ x: attacker.x, y: attacker.y - 2, text: "✦", life: 8, color: "#ffd96b" });
    return;
  }

  const speed = wpn.projSpeed || 12;
  m.projectiles.push({
    x: attacker.x + Math.cos(baseAng) * 8,
    y: attacker.y + Math.sin(baseAng) * 8 - 4,
    vx: Math.cos(aimAng) * speed,
    vy: Math.sin(aimAng) * speed,
    dmg: attacker.stats.dmg + (wpn.dmgMod || 0),
    fromId: attacker.id,
    teamId: attacker.teamId,
    ttl: Math.ceil(wpn.range / speed) + 4,
    wpn,
    isCrit: SIM_RNG.next() < clamp(attacker.stats.crit + (wpn.critMod || 0) + (attacker.armor?.critMod || 0) + ((attacker.item?.id === "visor" || attacker.item?.name === "Targeting Visor") ? 0.05 : 0), 0.02, 0.5),
  });
  // Muzzle flash
  m.floats.push({ x: attacker.x, y: attacker.y - 2, text: "•", life: 6, color: "#ffd96b" });
}

/* ============================================================
   PROJECTILE PHYSICS — fly along path, collide with first enemy
   ============================================================ */
function tickProjectiles(m) {
  if (!m.projectiles || !m.projectiles.length) return;
  const enemies = (teamId) => teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamBplayers : m.teamAplayers;

  for (let i = m.projectiles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const pr = m.projectiles[i];
    const oldX = pr.x, oldY = pr.y;
    pr.x += pr.vx;
    pr.y += pr.vy;
    pr.ttl--;

    // Off-arena or expired
    if (pr.ttl <= 0 || pr.x < 0 || pr.x > COMBAT.arenaW || pr.y < 0 || pr.y > COMBAT.arenaH) {
      // Grenade reaching its target zone — detonate
      if (pr.isGrenade) detonateGrenade(pr, m);
      m.projectiles.splice(i, 1);
      continue;
    }

    // Grenades also detonate when they reach their target point
    if (pr.isGrenade) {
      const dToTarget = Math.hypot(pr.x - pr.explodeAtX, pr.y - pr.explodeAtY);
      if (dToTarget < 8) {
        detonateGrenade(pr, m);
        m.projectiles.splice(i, 1);
        continue;
      }
    }

    // Terrain — SWEPT check against the segment
    if (m.terrain) {
      let blocked = null;
      for (const t of m.terrain) {
        if (segmentHitsRect(oldX, oldY, pr.x, pr.y, t.x, t.y, t.size, t.size)) {
          blocked = t; break;
        }
      }
      if (blocked) {
        if (pr.isGrenade) {
          // Grenade hits a wall — explode there
          detonateGrenade(pr, m);
        } else {
          m.floats.push({ x: pr.x, y: pr.y, text: "✦", life: 8, color: "#f7c948" });
        }
        m.projectiles.splice(i, 1);
        continue;
      }
    }

    // Direct hit (only for non-grenade projectiles — grenades are AoE-only)
    if (!pr.isGrenade) {
      let hit = null;
      for (const e of enemies(pr.teamId)) {
        if (!e.alive) continue;
        const dx = e.x - pr.x, dy = e.y - pr.y;
        if (dx*dx + dy*dy < 11*11) { hit = e; break; }
      }
      if (hit) {
        const attacker = (m.teamAplayers.concat(m.teamBplayers)).find(x => x.id === pr.fromId);
        if (attacker) applyProjectileHit(attacker, hit, pr, m);
        m.projectiles.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
  }
}

function applyProjectileHit(attacker, defender, pr, m) {
  // Defense: scale damage down by def/(def+50)
  const arm = defender.armor || {};
  const effDef = clamp(defender.stats.def + (arm.defMod || 0) + (defender.weapon?.defMod || 0), 0, CAPS.def + 20);
  const dmgMult = attacker.boostMods?.dmgMult || 1;
  const rng = R(1 - COMBAT.rngSpread, 1 + COMBAT.rngSpread);
  const raw = pr.dmg * dmgMult * rng * (pr.isCrit ? COMBAT.critMult : 1.0);
  const reduction = effDef / (effDef + COMBAT.defDenom);
  const dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(raw * (1 - reduction)));

  // Track attacker-of-record on defender (for mercy state)
  defender.attackedBy = attacker.id;
  defender.attackedByExpires = 90;

  applyDamage(attacker, defender, dmg, pr.isCrit, m);
}

/* Shared damage / death pipeline for both melee and projectile hits */
function applyDamage(attacker, defender, dmg, isCrit, m) {
  const hpFracBefore = defender.hp / defender.stats.hp;
  defender.hp -= dmg;
  const hpFracAfter = defender.hp / defender.stats.hp;
  // Balloon pop thresholds at 2/3 and 1/3
  for (const t of [2/3, 1/3]) {
    if (hpFracBefore > t && hpFracAfter <= t && defender.hp > 0) {
      defender.balloonsPopped = (defender.balloonsPopped || 0) + 1;
      m.floats.push({ x: defender.x, y: defender.y - 18, text: "POP!", life: 22, color: "#fff" });
    }
  }
  m.floats.push({
    x: defender.x, y: defender.y - 10,
    text: isCrit ? `${dmg}!` : String(dmg),
    life: 20,
    color: isCrit ? "#f7c948" : "#ef4d4d",
  });
  for (let i = 0; i < (isCrit ? 6 : 3); i++) {
    m.blood.push({
      x: defender.x + R(-6, 6), y: defender.y + R(-6, 6),
      life: 60 + RI(0, 30),
      color: pick(["#a01818", "#831010", "#7a0c0c", "#bf2020"]),
    });
  }
  // Stun chance from attacker's weapon. ARMOR REDUCES stun probability:
  // each point of total defense (stat + armor + weapon) reduces the
  // effective stun chance by 1.5%. A heavy-plate Tank with def=40 and
  // a +10 armor (50 total) gets a 75% stun-resistance vs raw stun rolls.
  const wpn = attacker.weapon || {};
  if ((wpn.stun || 0) > 0) {
    const defArm = defender.armor || {};
    const totalDef = clamp(defender.stats.def + (defArm.defMod || 0) + (defender.weapon?.defMod || 0), 0, CAPS.def + 20);
    const stunResist = clamp(totalDef * 0.015, 0, 0.85); // up to 85% resist
    const effectiveStun = wpn.stun * (1 - stunResist);
    if (SIM_RNG.next() < effectiveStun) {
      defender.stunned = 30;
      m.floats.push({ x: defender.x, y: defender.y - 14, text: "STUN", life: 18, color: "#88c0d0" });
    } else if (totalDef > 30) {
      // Visible "shrugged off" tell when heavy armor resists
      m.floats.push({ x: defender.x, y: defender.y - 14, text: "🛡", life: 14, color: "#88c0d0" });
    }
  }

  // KNOCKBACK — every hit pushes the defender slightly away from attacker.
  // Damage scales the push, but heavier armor (defMod) reduces it. This
  // is purely visual+positional (doesn't break terrain collision; uses
  // moveAlong's wall-slide), but means heavy attacks visibly displace
  // light targets while plate-armored Tanks barely flinch.
  {
    const defArm = defender.armor || {};
    const totalDef = clamp(defender.stats.def + (defArm.defMod || 0), 0, CAPS.def + 20);
    const kbResist = clamp(totalDef * 0.015, 0, 0.8);
    const kbStrength = Math.min(8, dmg / 8) * (1 - kbResist);
    if (kbStrength > 0.5) {
      const ang = Math.atan2(defender.y - attacker.y, defender.x - attacker.x);
      // Apply knockback as a velocity nudge — clamped so it doesn't yeet
      const nx = defender.x + Math.cos(ang) * kbStrength;
      const ny = defender.y + Math.sin(ang) * kbStrength;
      // Respect terrain
      if (!m.terrain || !collidesTerrain(nx, ny, m.terrain)) {
        defender.x = clamp(nx, 12, COMBAT.arenaW - 12);
        defender.y = clamp(ny, 12, COMBAT.arenaH - 12);
      }
    }
  }
  if (isCrit) feed("kill", phrasePick(PHRASE_CRIT, { a: attacker.name, b: defender.name }));

  // Visual punch — camera shake on crits and on knockout
  if (typeof triggerShake === "function") {
    if (isCrit) triggerShake(5);
    if (defender.hp <= 0) triggerShake(7);
  }

  if (defender.hp <= 0) handleKO(attacker, defender, m);
}

/* Streak tracking — increments attacker's currentStreak, breaks defender's,
   updates longestStreak. Tracked across matches; the streak ends only when
   the knight is KO'd (whether they keep their life or not). */
function updateKillStreak(attacker, defender, m) {
  // Capture first-kill team for the firstkill bet market (once per match)
  const wasFirstKill = m && !m.firstKillTeam;
  if (wasFirstKill) {
    m.firstKillTeam = attacker.teamId;
    // First-kill commentary
    const at = TEAM_BY_ID[attacker.teamId], dt = TEAM_BY_ID[defender.teamId];
    commentate("play", ccPick(COMM_FIRST_KILL, {
      a: at.emoji + " " + escapeHTML(attacker.name),
      b: dt.emoji + " " + escapeHTML(defender.name),
    }), { flavor: "hype", aiContext: { type: "firstKill", attacker, defender } });
  }
  attacker.career.currentStreak = (attacker.career.currentStreak || 0) + 1;
  if (attacker.career.currentStreak > (attacker.career.longestStreak || 0)) {
    attacker.career.longestStreak = attacker.career.currentStreak;
  }
  // Toast + in-arena taunt for milestone streaks
  if (attacker.career.currentStreak === 5 || attacker.career.currentStreak === 10 ||
      attacker.career.currentStreak === 15 || attacker.career.currentStreak === 25) {
    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast) {
      window.toast(`🔥 <b>${attacker.name}</b> is on a ${attacker.career.currentStreak}-kill streak!`, "info", { ico: "🔥", ttl: 7000 });
    }
    feed("kill", `🔥 ${attacker.name} on a ${attacker.career.currentStreak}-KILL STREAK!`);
    // Sportscaster commentary on big streaks
    commentate("play", ccPick(COMM_STREAK_BIG, {
      p: escapeHTML(attacker.name),
      n: attacker.career.currentStreak,
    }), { flavor: "hype" });
    // Taunt — pick from the matching pool
    const pool = attacker.career.currentStreak === 5  ? TAUNTS_STREAK_5
              : attacker.career.currentStreak === 10 ? TAUNTS_STREAK_10
              : attacker.career.currentStreak === 15 ? TAUNTS_STREAK_15
              :                                          TAUNTS_STREAK_25;
    taunt(pick(pool), attacker, m);
  }
  // Defender's streak ends here
  defender.career.deaths = (defender.career.deaths || 0) + 1;
  if (defender.career.currentStreak >= 5) {
    feed("kill", `💔 ${attacker.name} ENDS ${defender.name}'s ${defender.career.currentStreak}-kill streak.`);
    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast) {
      window.toast(`<b>${attacker.name}</b> ends ${defender.name}'s ${defender.career.currentStreak}-streak`, "warn", { ico: "💔", ttl: 7000 });
    }
  }
  defender.career.currentStreak = 0;
}

function handleKO(attacker, defender, m) {
  // Capture context BEFORE we mutate anything, for death-cry tone selection
  const wasOnStreak = (defender.career.currentStreak || 0) >= 3;
  const wasStunned = (defender.stunned || 0) > 0;
  const isSelfDestruct = attacker.id === defender.id;

  defender.alive = false;
  defender.hp = 0;
  defender.balloonsPopped = 3;
  defender.lives = Math.max(0, (defender.lives || 0) - 1);
  updateKillStreak(attacker, defender, m);
  attacker.career.kills++;
  m.killsByTeam[attacker.teamId]++;

  // === v28.4: trigger stadium crowd cheer ===
  // Sets a transient flag the renderer reads to bounce fans near the
  // kill location for ~30 frames. Honored regardless of disableCrowd
  // (the setter is cheap; the renderer checks the toggle).
  m._crowdCheer = {
    x: defender.x,
    y: defender.y,
    frame: m.frame || 0,
    radius: 200,
  };

  const isPermadeath = defender.lives <= 0;
  // Pull the most recent damage event's crit flag — applyDamage stamped it
  const wasCrit = (m.floats || []).slice(-3).some(f => /!$/.test(f.text || ""));

  // DEATH CRY — Worms-style last words. Picked by tonal context.
  const cry = deathCryFor(defender, attacker, {
    isCrit: wasCrit,
    isPermadeath,
    wasOnStreak,
    wasStunned,
    isSelfDestruct,
  });
  if (cry) {
    // Float the cry above the corpse
    m.floats.push({
      x: defender.x, y: defender.y - 22,
      text: '"' + cry + '"',
      life: 55,
      color: isPermadeath ? "#b46df0" : "#cdd2d6",
    });
    // Push to feed so it rolls in the ticker
    feed("kill", `💬 ${defender.name}: "${cry}"`);
  }

  if (defender.lives > 0) {
    feed("kill", `💢 ${attacker.name} KOs ${defender.name}! (${defender.lives} ${defender.lives === 1 ? "life" : "lives"} left)`);
    m.floats.push({ x: defender.x, y: defender.y - 14, text: "DOWN", life: 24, color: "#ef4d4d" });
  } else {
    defender.deceased = true;
    defender.deceasedSeason = LEAGUE.season;
    defender.deceasedBy = attacker.name;
    LEAGUE.graveyard.push({
      name: defender.name, teamId: defender.teamId, position: defender.position,
      season: LEAGUE.season, killer: attacker.name, killerTeam: attacker.teamId,
      slot: m.slot, round: m.currentRoundIndex,
    });
    m.corpses.push({ x: defender.x, y: defender.y, color: TEAM_BY_ID[defender.teamId].color });
    dropGear(defender, m);
    feed("kill", phrasePick(PHRASE_KILL, { a: attacker.name, b: defender.name }));
  }

  // LAST KNIGHT STANDING — if defender's team just got reduced to a single
  // alive knight on the field, that survivor shouts a defiant taunt. We
  // dedupe per-round via _tauntedLastKnightThisRound so it only fires once.
  const defendersTeam = defender.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers;
  const stillAlive = defendersTeam.filter(p => p.alive);
  if (stillAlive.length === 1 && !stillAlive[0]._tauntedLastKnightThisRound) {
    const lone = stillAlive[0];
    lone._tauntedLastKnightThisRound = true;
    taunt(pick(TAUNTS_LAST_KNIGHT), lone, m);
    // Sportscaster — dramatic call-out
    const tm = TEAM_BY_ID[lone.teamId];
    commentate("play", ccPick(COMM_LAST_KNIGHT, {
      p: escapeHTML(lone.name),
      tm: tm.short,
    }), { flavor: "hype", aiContext: { type: "lastKnight", knight: lone, team: tm } });
  }

  // === SLOW-MO ON MATCH-DECIDING KO ===
  // If this KO leaves the defender's team with 0 alive AND the attacker
  // already had `roundsToWin - 1` round wins, this round will end the match.
  // Trigger a brief slow-mo period so the broadcast lingers on the moment.
  if (stillAlive.length === 0 && !m.slowMoTriggered) {
    const attackerScore = attacker.teamId === m.teamAId ? m.roundsWonA : m.roundsWonB;
    const roundsToWin = Math.floor(m.bestOf / 2) + 1;
    if (attackerScore === roundsToWin - 1) {
      // Settings toggle — disable field FX skips slow-mo too
      if (!LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableFieldFx) {
        m.slowMoFrames = 30;
      }
      m.slowMoTriggered = true;
      // Big shake to punctuate (also gated by triggerShake's own toggle)
      if (typeof triggerShake === "function") triggerShake(9);
    }
  }
}

/* Melee instant-hit path — kept for backwards-compat with applyItemTrigger
   (grenade) and for melee weapons. v28: now has a miss chance based on
   defender_speed - attacker_speed (faster defenders dodge melee more
   often) plus a baseline meleeMissBase. */
function resolveAttack(attacker, defender, m) {
  // === v28: Tag mode special-case ===
  // If attacker is currently "It" in Tag mode, apply tag instead of
  // normal melee damage. The tag freezes the target AND passes "It"
  // to them, AND deals heavy damage so the round resolves quickly.
  if (m && m.gameMode === "tag" && m.tag && attacker.id === m.tag.itId) {
    if (typeof applyTagHit === "function" && applyTagHit(attacker, defender, m)) {
      return;
    }
  }
  const wpn = attacker.weapon || {};
  const arm = defender.armor || {};
  const visorBonus = (attacker.item && (attacker.item.id === "visor" || attacker.item.name === "Targeting Visor")) ? 0.05 : 0;
  const dmgMult = attacker.boostMods?.dmgMult || 1;
  const effDmg  = clamp(attacker.stats.dmg + (wpn.dmgMod || 0), 1, CAPS.dmg + 20) * dmgMult;
  const effDef  = clamp(defender.stats.def + (arm.defMod || 0) + (defender.weapon?.defMod || 0), 0, CAPS.def + 20);
  const effCrit = clamp(attacker.stats.crit + (wpn.critMod || 0) + (arm.critMod || 0) + visorBonus,
                        COMBAT.critClampMin, COMBAT.critClampMax);

  // === v28: Melee miss chance ===
  // Baseline + scaling with defender's speed advantage. Stunned/critical
  // defenders auto-fail dodges (miss chance forced to 0).
  let missChance = 0;
  if (defender.stunned > 0) {
    missChance = 0;  // stunned can't dodge
  } else {
    const spdDelta = Math.max(0, (defender.stats.spd || 0) - (attacker.stats.spd || 0));
    missChance = COMBAT.meleeMissBase + spdDelta * COMBAT.meleeMissPerSpdDelta;
    missChance = clamp(missChance, 0, 0.35);
  }
  if (SIM_RNG.next() < missChance) {
    // Miss — log a small float, no damage. Track attacker-of-record so
    // mercy state still considers them engaged.
    if (m && m.floats) {
      m.floats.push({
        x: defender.x, y: defender.y - 8,
        text: "MISS", life: 24, color: "#88c0d0",
      });
    }
    defender.attackedBy = attacker.id;
    defender.attackedByExpires = 90;
    return;
  }

  const rng = R(1 - COMBAT.rngSpread, 1 + COMBAT.rngSpread);
  const isCrit = SIM_RNG.next() < effCrit;
  const raw = effDmg * rng * (isCrit ? COMBAT.critMult : 1.0);
  const reduction = effDef / (effDef + COMBAT.defDenom);
  const dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(raw * (1 - reduction)));

  // Track attacker-of-record (mercy state)
  defender.attackedBy = attacker.id;
  defender.attackedByExpires = 90;

  // === v28.5 Slugger Bat knockback ===
  // The bat shoves the defender backwards on hit. Lighter than grenade
  // knockback (no falloff, smaller magnitude) but enough to break melee
  // engagement and reposition the target for the next swing.
  if (wpn.id === "slugger" && defender.alive) {
    const ddx = defender.x - attacker.x, ddy = defender.y - attacker.y;
    const dl = Math.max(1, Math.hypot(ddx, ddy));
    const ux = ddx / dl, uy = ddy / dl;
    const power = isCrit ? 8 : 5;
    defender._knockbackVx = (defender._knockbackVx || 0) + ux * power;
    defender._knockbackVy = (defender._knockbackVy || 0) + uy * power;
    defender._knockbackTtl = Math.max(defender._knockbackTtl || 0, 8);
  }

  applyDamage(attacker, defender, dmg, isCrit, m);
}

/* ============================================================
   GEAR DROPS & SCAVENGING
   When a knight falls, their loadout (weapon/armor/item) drops
   at the corpse. Living players who walk within range auto-pickup
   any upgrade. Picked-up gear is renamed
     "<owner>'s <gear> (scavenged)"
   and re-drops with that scavenged name if its new owner dies.
   ============================================================ */
function dropGear(deadPlayer, m) {
  const owner = deadPlayer.name;
  // Drop weapon
  if (deadPlayer.weapon) {
    m.drops.push({
      x: deadPlayer.x + R(-6, 6),
      y: deadPlayer.y + R(-6, 6),
      slot: "weapon",
      gear: { ...deadPlayer.weapon, displayName: scavengedName(deadPlayer.weapon, owner), originalOwner: deadPlayer.weapon.originalOwner || owner },
      fromName: owner,
    });
  }
  // Drop armor
  if (deadPlayer.armor) {
    m.drops.push({
      x: deadPlayer.x + R(-6, 6),
      y: deadPlayer.y + R(-6, 6),
      slot: "armor",
      gear: { ...deadPlayer.armor, displayName: scavengedName(deadPlayer.armor, owner), originalOwner: deadPlayer.armor.originalOwner || owner },
      fromName: owner,
    });
  }
  // Drop item only if not yet used (a used consumable is gone)
  if (deadPlayer.item && !deadPlayer.itemUsed) {
    m.drops.push({
      x: deadPlayer.x + R(-6, 6),
      y: deadPlayer.y + R(-6, 6),
      slot: "item",
      gear: { ...deadPlayer.item, displayName: scavengedName(deadPlayer.item, owner), originalOwner: deadPlayer.item.originalOwner || owner },
      fromName: owner,
    });
  }
}

function scavengedName(gear, owner) {
  // Strip any existing possessive prefix so we don't get "X's Y's Z's ... (scavenged)"
  const base = gear.name; // canonical name from catalog (never mutated)
  return `${owner}'s ${base} (scavenged)`;
}

function attemptScavenge(p, m) {
  if (!m.drops.length) return;
  const RANGE = 22;
  for (let i = 0; i < m.drops.length; i++) {
    const d = m.drops[i];
    const dx = p.x - d.x, dy = p.y - d.y;
    if (dx*dx + dy*dy > RANGE*RANGE) continue;

    // Health Pack — instant heal, no slot swap
    if (d.slot === "health") {
      // Skip if already at full HP
      if (p.hp >= p.stats.hp) continue;
      const heal = d.gear.heal || 30;
      const before = Math.round(p.hp);  // round any floating-point residue from bleed/dpsTick
      p.hp = Math.min(p.stats.hp, before + heal);
      const actual = p.hp - before;
      feed("item", `❤ ${p.name} grabs a Health Pack (+${actual} HP → ${p.hp}/${p.stats.hp})`);
      m.floats.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y - 16, text: `+${actual}♥`, life: 22, color: "#ef4d4d" });
      m.drops.splice(i, 1);
      return;
    }

    // Compare scores using THIS player's archetype preference
    const slot = d.slot;
    const currentScore = gearScore(slot, p[slot], p.position);
    const newScore     = gearScore(slot, d.gear,   p.position);

    // Pick up if it's an upgrade for THIS player. Items always swap.
    const shouldPickUp = (slot === "item" && !p.itemUsed) ? newScore >= currentScore
                                                          : newScore > currentScore + 0.5;
    if (!shouldPickUp) continue;

    // Pick it up — replace gear, mark scavenge stat
    d.gear.scavengedCount = (d.gear.scavengedCount || 0) + 1;
    // === ROADMAP 2.4: track per-knight gear history for memorials ===
    // Record what we WERE carrying before the swap (so the memorial can
    // show the full lineage). Only record on actual changes.
    if (!p.career.gearLog) p.career.gearLog = [];
    const prev = p[slot];
    if (prev && (prev.id !== d.gear.id || prev.displayName !== d.gear.displayName)) {
      p.career.gearLog.push({
        season: LEAGUE.season,
        slot,
        from: prev.displayName || prev.name,
        to: d.gear.displayName || d.gear.name,
        scavenged: d.gear.scavengedCount,
        ico: d.gear.ico,
      });
    }
    p[slot] = d.gear;
    if (slot === "item") p.itemUsed = false;
    p.career.scavenged = (p.career.scavenged || 0) + 1;
    const legendary = d.gear.scavengedCount >= 3 ? " ⭐LEGENDARY⭐" : "";
    feed("item", `🎒 ${p.name} scavenges ${gearDisplayName(d.gear)}!${legendary}`);
    m.floats.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y - 16, text: "+gear", life: 22, color: "#4dd58a" });
    m.drops.splice(i, 1);
    return;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   ROUND FINALIZATION
   Determines round winner (the team with at least one alive player).
   Increments round score. Checks for match end (best-of-N reached
   OR a team has no fielded players for the next round). Otherwise
   transitions to "betweenRounds" so the user can place bets / shop.
   ============================================================ */
function finalizeRound(forfeit) {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;

  const aAlive = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => p.alive).length;
  const bAlive = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => p.alive).length;
  let roundWinnerId = null, roundLoserId = null, isDraw = false;
  if (forfeit) {
    const eligibleA = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => !p.deceased && p.lives > 0);
    const eligibleB = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => !p.deceased && p.lives > 0);
    roundWinnerId = eligibleA.length ? m.teamAId : m.teamBId;
    roundLoserId  = eligibleA.length ? m.teamBId : m.teamAId;
    feed("match", `⚐ ${TEAM_BY_ID[roundLoserId].short} cannot field a roster — ${TEAM_BY_ID[roundWinnerId].short} wins by forfeit!`);
  } else if (aAlive > bAlive) {
    roundWinnerId = m.teamAId; roundLoserId = m.teamBId;
  } else if (bAlive > aAlive) {
    roundWinnerId = m.teamBId; roundLoserId = m.teamAId;
  } else if (aAlive > 0 && bAlive > 0) {
    // Both teams have living players AND equal counts (timeout). Real draw.
    isDraw = true;
  } else {
    // Mutual wipe (0-0) — coin flip
    if (SIM_RNG.next() < 0.5) { roundWinnerId = m.teamAId; roundLoserId = m.teamBId; }
    else { roundWinnerId = m.teamBId; roundLoserId = m.teamAId; }
  }

  if (isDraw) {
    feed("match", `🤝 Round ${m.currentRoundIndex} ends in a DRAW (${aAlive}–${bAlive}, neither team wiped). No round point awarded.`);
    m.rounds.push({
      index: m.currentRoundIndex,
      winner: null,
      isDraw: true,
      aAlive, bAlive,
    });
  } else {
    if (roundWinnerId === m.teamAId) m.roundsWonA++;
    else m.roundsWonB++;

    settleRoundBets(roundWinnerId);

    m.rounds.push({
      index: m.currentRoundIndex,
      winner: roundWinnerId,
      aAlive, bAlive,
    });

    if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
  feed("match", `🏁 Round ${m.currentRoundIndex} won by ${TEAM_BY_ID[roundWinnerId].emoji} ${TEAM_BY_ID[roundWinnerId].short} (${m.roundsWonA}–${m.roundsWonB} match score).`);
  }

  // Check for match end. Match ends if a team hits roundsToWin OR forfeits
  // OR we've played all bestOf rounds and there's still a leader (a draw at
  // bestOf may need a sudden-death extra round).
  const matchOver =
    m.roundsWonA >= m.roundsToWin ||
    m.roundsWonB >= m.roundsToWin ||
    forfeit ||
    (m.currentRoundIndex >= m.bestOf && m.roundsWonA !== m.roundsWonB);
  if (matchOver) {
    finalizeMatch();
    return;
  }
  // If we've played all rounds and they're tied, the match itself is a draw
  if (m.currentRoundIndex >= m.bestOf && m.roundsWonA === m.roundsWonB) {
    finalizeMatch(/*matchDraw*/ true);
    return;
  }

  m.phase = "betweenRounds";
  if (typeof showRoundIntermission === "function") showRoundIntermission();
}

function continueAfterRound() {
  // Called from the round-intermission "Begin Next Round" button.
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;
  startRound();
}

/* ============================================================
   MATCH FINALIZATION
   Reached when one team wins `roundsToWin` rounds OR by forfeit.
   ============================================================ */
function finalizeMatch(matchDraw) {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;

  m.phase = "post";

  if (matchDraw) {
    // True match-level draw — bestOf rounds played, score tied
    m.winnerId = null;
    for (const p of [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers]) p.career.matchesPlayed++;
    LEAGUE.records[m.teamAId].draws = (LEAGUE.records[m.teamAId].draws || 0) + 1;
    LEAGUE.records[m.teamBId].draws = (LEAGUE.records[m.teamBId].draws || 0) + 1;

    // Bracket can't have a draw advance — coin flip determines who advances
    // (the league must have a champion). Tracked separately in records.
    const advancingId = SIM_RNG.next() < 0.5 ? m.teamAId : m.teamBId;
    LEAGUE.bracket[m.slot].winner = advancingId;
    LEAGUE.bracket[m.slot].casualties = LEAGUE.graveyard
      .filter(g => g.season === LEAGUE.season && g.slot === m.slot);

    // Refund all match-level bets (no clear winner)
    settleBetsAsRefund();

    if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
    feed("match", `🤝 Match ends in a DRAW (${m.roundsWonA}–${m.roundsWonB}). Coin flip → ${TEAM_BY_ID[advancingId].emoji} ${TEAM_BY_ID[advancingId].short} advances.`);
    // Sportscaster — DRAW commentary
    if (typeof commentate === "function") {
      commentate("play", ccPick(COMM_MATCH_DRAW, {
        a: TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId].emoji + " " + TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId].short,
        b: TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId].emoji + " " + TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId].short,
        sa: m.roundsWonA,
        sb: m.roundsWonB,
      }), { flavor: "sad" });
    }
    if (typeof showIntermission === "function") showIntermission();
    return;
  }

  const winnerId = m.roundsWonA > m.roundsWonB ? m.teamAId : m.teamBId;
  const loserId = winnerId === m.teamAId ? m.teamBId : m.teamAId;
  m.winnerId = winnerId;

  for (const p of [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers]) {
    p.career.matchesPlayed++;
  }
  for (const p of (winnerId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers)) {
    if (!p.deceased) p.career.matchesWon++;
  }

  LEAGUE.records[winnerId].wins++;
  LEAGUE.records[loserId].losses++;

  LEAGUE.bracket[m.slot].winner = winnerId;
  LEAGUE.bracket[m.slot].casualties = LEAGUE.graveyard
    .filter(g => g.season === LEAGUE.season && g.slot === m.slot);

  // Scan the field for "Legendary" items that no one picked up — these
  // are LOST FOREVER. Display in the About-tab roadmap as a memorial.
  if (!LEAGUE.lostItems) LEAGUE.lostItems = [];
  for (const d of m.drops) {
    if ((d.gear.scavengedCount || 0) >= 2) {
      LEAGUE.lostItems.push({
        season: LEAGUE.season,
        slot: m.slot,
        slotType: d.slot,
        name: d.gear.displayName || d.gear.name,
        baseName: d.gear.name,
        scavengedCount: d.gear.scavengedCount,
        ico: d.gear.ico,
        lostBy: d.fromName || "the void",
      });
      feed("item", `📉 ${d.gear.displayName || d.gear.name} (${d.gear.scavengedCount}× scavenged) was LOST on the field.`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast) {
        window.toast(`<b>${d.gear.displayName || d.gear.name}</b> lost on the field after ${d.gear.scavengedCount} scavenges`, "warn", { ico: "📉", ttl: 7000 });
      }
    }
  }

  // Record this match's seed + outcome for replay/audit. The seed alone is
  // enough to reproduce the match exactly (assuming the league state going
  // into it is the same).
  if (!LEAGUE.matchHistory) LEAGUE.matchHistory = [];
  LEAGUE.matchHistory.push({
    season: LEAGUE.season,
    slot: m.slot,
    seed: computeMatchSeed(LEAGUE.season, m.slot),
    teamA: m.teamAId, teamB: m.teamBId,
    winner: winnerId,
    score: `${m.roundsWonA}-${m.roundsWonB}`,
    casualties: LEAGUE.graveyard
      .filter(g => g.season === LEAGUE.season && g.slot === m.slot)
      .map(g => g.name),
  });

  settleBets(winnerId);

  if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
  feed("match", `🏆 ${TEAM_BY_ID[winnerId].emoji} ${TEAM_BY_ID[winnerId].name} WIN the match ${m.roundsWonA}–${m.roundsWonB}!`);

  // Sportscaster — pick dominant vs close pool by margin
  if (typeof commentate === "function") {
    const margin = Math.abs(m.roundsWonA - m.roundsWonB);
    const pool = margin >= 3 ? COMM_MATCH_WIN_DOM : COMM_MATCH_WIN_CLOSE;
    const w = TEAM_BY_ID[winnerId], l = TEAM_BY_ID[loserId];
    commentate("play", ccPick(pool, {
      w: w.emoji + " " + w.short,
      l: l.emoji + " " + l.short,
      sa: m.roundsWonA,
      sb: m.roundsWonB,
    }), { flavor: margin >= 3 ? "deadpan" : "hype",
        aiContext: { type: "matchEnd", winner: w, loser: l, score: [m.roundsWonA, m.roundsWonB] } });
  }

  if (typeof showIntermission === "function") showIntermission();
}

// Refund all match-scoped bets (used on draws — no winner exists)
function settleBetsAsRefund() {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;
  const remaining = [];
  for (const bet of LEAGUE.bets) {
    if (bet.slot !== m.slot) { remaining.push(bet); continue; }
    LEAGUE.bits += bet.amount;
    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
      window.toast(`Match drew — bet refunded: <b>+${bet.amount}</b> bits`, "warn");
  }
  LEAGUE.bets = remaining;
}

function continueAfterMatch() {
  // Called from the intermission "Continue" button.
  const wasFinal = LEAGUE.currentSlot === "final";
  advanceBracket();

  if (wasFinal || !LEAGUE.currentSlot) {
    // Season complete
    finalizeSeason();
    return;
  }

  // Reap deceased and build the next match
  const callups = reapDeceased();
  for (const c of callups) {
    feed("spawn", `📣 Rookie call-up: ${c.rookie.name} replaces ${c.replaced.name} (${TEAM_BY_ID[c.team].name}).`);
  }
  LEAGUE.match = null;
  startMatch();
}

/* ============================================================
   SEASON FINALIZATION
   - Champion crowned
   - History recorded
   - All deceased reaped, rookies called up
   - New bracket built for next season
   ============================================================ */
function finalizeSeason() {
  const finalSlot = LEAGUE.bracket.final || {};
  const champion = finalSlot.winner;
  const runnerup = champion === finalSlot.teamA ? finalSlot.teamB : finalSlot.teamA;
  if (champion) {
    LEAGUE.records[champion].championships++;
    feed("season",
      `🏆 SEASON ${LEAGUE.season} CHAMPION: ${TEAM_BY_ID[champion].emoji} ${TEAM_BY_ID[champion].name}!`);
  }

  const seasonCasualties = LEAGUE.graveyard.filter(g => g.season === LEAGUE.season);
  // Save bracket results for season history (all slots)
  const bracketResults = {};
  for (const slot of (LEAGUE.bracket._order || [])) {
    bracketResults[slot] = LEAGUE.bracket[slot]?.winner || null;
  }
  LEAGUE.history.push({
    season: LEAGUE.season,
    champion, runnerup,
    bracketResults,
    casualties: seasonCasualties.length,
  });

  // === ROADMAP 2.6: Snapshot the current ladder for next-season delta ===
  // computeLadderRanking is defined in render-side; we replicate the
  // formula here so the snapshot is sim-side and survives saves.
  if (typeof computeLadderRanking === "function") {
    const ladder = computeLadderRanking();
    LEAGUE.previousLadder = {};
    for (const entry of ladder) {
      LEAGUE.previousLadder[entry.team.id] = entry.rank;
    }
  }

  if (typeof showSeasonEnd === "function") showSeasonEnd(champion, runnerup, seasonCasualties.length);
}

function beginNextSeason() {
  // Reap and replace
  const callups = reapDeceased();
  for (const p of Object.values(LEAGUE.rosters).flat()) {
    p.career.seasonsPlayed++;
  }
  if (callups.length) {
    feed("season", `📣 Off-season call-ups: ${callups.length} rookies join the league.`);
  }
  LEAGUE.season++;
  LEAGUE.match = null;
  buildBracket();
  startMatch();
}

/* ============================================================
   BETTING — both team-level and per-player (last-standing)
   Odds are computed from rough roster strength.
   ============================================================ */
function teamStrength(teamId) {
  // If a match is in progress AND this team is one of the two participants,
  // use LIVE state — current lives, current HP, gear-modified stats. This
  // makes round-bet odds shift between rounds based on actual position.
  // Outside a match (or for non-participating teams), use roster baseline.
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const inMatch = m && m.phase !== "post" && (teamId === m.teamAId || teamId === m.teamBId);

  if (inMatch) {
    const players = teamId === m.teamAId ? m.teamAplayers : m.teamBplayers;
    let s = 0;
    for (const p of players) {
      if (p.deceased) continue;
      const livesLeft = Math.max(0, p.lives || 0);
      // A knight with 0 lives contributes nothing; one with full lives contributes
      // their gear-modified stats; one with partial HP contributes proportionally.
      if (livesLeft === 0) continue;
      const hpFrac = p.alive ? clamp(p.hp / p.stats.hp, 0.1, 1) : 0.5;
      const wDmg = p.stats.dmg + (p.weapon?.dmgMod || 0);
      const wDef = p.stats.def + (p.armor?.defMod || 0) + (p.weapon?.defMod || 0);
      const wSpd = p.stats.spd + (p.armor?.spdMod || 0);
      // Lives multiplier: 1.0 / 0.85 / 0.7 / 0.55 ... so a 3-lives knight is
      // worth meaningfully more than a 1-life knight even at full HP.
      const livesMult = 0.55 + 0.15 * livesLeft;
      s += (p.stats.hp * hpFrac + wDmg * 4 + wDef + wSpd * 8) * livesMult;
    }
    return Math.max(1, s);
  }

  const r = (LEAGUE.rosters[teamId] || []).filter(p => !p.deceased);
  let s = 0;
  for (const p of r) {
    s += p.stats.hp + p.stats.dmg * 4 + p.stats.def + p.stats.spd * 8;
  }
  return Math.max(1, s);
}

function teamOdds(teamA, teamB) {
  const sA = teamStrength(teamA), sB = teamStrength(teamB);
  const total = sA + sB;
  const probA = sA / total, probB = sB / total;
  // Convert to payout multiplier (with a small house edge of 5%)
  const margin = 0.95;
  return {
    [teamA]: +(margin / probA).toFixed(2),
    [teamB]: +(margin / probB).toFixed(2),
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   LIVE MATCH ODDS (ROADMAP 2.5)

   Static teamOdds() above is computed at match start from team
   strength only. During a live match, the odds should update as
   rounds resolve and knights die — a team down 0-2 with 2 dead
   knights is genuinely longer odds than they were at coin flip.

   liveMatchOdds(m) takes the active match snapshot and returns
   updated payout multipliers reflecting:
     - Round score (each round won is meaningful progress toward
       best-of-N — this is the dominant signal)
     - Remaining lives across the active roster
     - Total remaining HP fraction
     - Original team strength (still matters if both teams are even)

   The returned object has the same shape as teamOdds() so the bet
   UI doesn't need to special-case it.
   ============================================================ */
function liveMatchOdds(m) {
  if (!m) return null;
  const teamA = m.teamAId, teamB = m.teamBId;
  // === Component 1: Round score ===
  // Best-of-5 means 3 wins to take the match. A team at 2-0 has won
  // ~80% of equivalent matches (extrapolation). A team at 2-1 is ~67%.
  const sA = m.roundsWonA || 0;
  const sB = m.roundsWonB || 0;
  const winsNeeded = Math.ceil((m.bestOf || 5) / 2);  // 3 for bestOf 5
  const remA = winsNeeded - sA, remB = winsNeeded - sB;
  // If a team has already won enough, return certain odds
  if (remA <= 0) return { [teamA]: 1.01, [teamB]: 99 };
  if (remB <= 0) return { [teamA]: 99, [teamB]: 1.01 };
  // Naive Markov chain for remaining rounds: assume per-round 50/50
  // (we'll mix in strength next). Probability of winning the match
  // given (remA, remB) rounds-needed: p = sum of negative binomial.
  function winProb(needA, needB) {
    // p(A wins match) given needA, needB rounds remaining each.
    // Each round: 50% A wins (will tune below).
    // Closed form: P = sum_{k=0..needB-1} C(needA-1+k, k) * 0.5^(needA+k)
    let p = 0;
    let comb = 1;  // C(needA-1, 0)
    for (let k = 0; k < needB; k++) {
      p += comb * Math.pow(0.5, needA + k);
      comb = comb * (needA - 1 + k + 1) / (k + 1);  // C(needA-1+k+1, k+1)
    }
    return p;
  }
  let pAroundsOnly = winProb(remA, remB);

  // === Component 2: Original team strength (mix in) ===
  // If team strengths differ, shift the per-round 50/50 baseline.
  const strA = teamStrength(teamA), strB = teamStrength(teamB);
  const strFrac = strA / (strA + strB);  // 0.5 = even, > 0.5 favors A
  // Re-run winProb with the strength-shifted per-round prob.
  // (Approximate: for asymmetric per-round prob, we rebuild winProb.)
  function winProbShifted(needA, needB, pA) {
    const pB = 1 - pA;
    let p = 0;
    let comb = 1;
    for (let k = 0; k < needB; k++) {
      p += comb * Math.pow(pA, needA) * Math.pow(pB, k);
      comb = comb * (needA - 1 + k + 1) / (k + 1);
    }
    return p;
  }
  pAroundsOnly = winProbShifted(remA, remB, strFrac);

  // === Component 3: Remaining-lives shift ===
  // If a team has fewer surviving knights, their per-round prob drops.
  // We re-do the calculation with per-round prob = strFrac shifted by
  // the lives delta. ~3% per missing knight is a moderate signal.
  let liveA = 5, liveB = 5;
  if (m.teamAplayers) liveA = m.teamAplayers.filter(p => p.alive || p.lives > 0).length;
  if (m.teamBplayers) liveB = m.teamBplayers.filter(p => p.alive || p.lives > 0).length;
  const livesDelta = (liveA - liveB) * 0.03;
  const adjustedPA = clamp(strFrac + livesDelta, 0.02, 0.98);

  let pA = winProbShifted(remA, remB, adjustedPA);
  pA = clamp(pA, 0.01, 0.99);
  const pB = 1 - pA;

  const margin = 0.95;
  return {
    [teamA]: +(margin / pA).toFixed(2),
    [teamB]: +(margin / pB).toFixed(2),
  };
}
window.liveMatchOdds = liveMatchOdds;

function playerOdds(teamA, teamB) {
  // Probability of "last standing" (or last-killed) for each player.
  // Crude: stat strength relative to the entire match pool, weighted toward survivors.
  const all = [
    ...(LEAGUE.rosters[teamA] || []).filter(p => !p.deceased),
    ...(LEAGUE.rosters[teamB] || []).filter(p => !p.deceased),
  ];
  const strengths = all.map(p =>
    p.stats.hp / 100 + p.stats.dmg / 8 + p.stats.def / 20 + p.stats.spd / 1.5);
  const total = strengths.reduce((a,b)=>a+b, 0);
  const odds = {};
  for (let i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
    const prob = strengths[i] / total;
    odds[all[i].id] = +(0.85 / Math.max(0.01, prob)).toFixed(2);
  }
  return odds;
}

function placeBet(kind, target, amount) {
  // kind: "team"      → target is teamId, resolves at MATCH end
  //       "round"     → target is teamId, resolves at end of NEXT round
  //       "player"    → target is playerId, resolves at MATCH end
  //       "firstkill" → target is teamId, resolves on first kill of the match
  //       "totalcas"  → target is "over"/"under", resolves at MATCH end (5.5 line)
  //       "rounds"    → target is "over"/"under", resolves at MATCH end (3.5 line)

  // === v28.5: Lock betting during live matches ===
  // Previously allowed bets at any time including mid-match, which lets
  // a viewer wait until the outcome is visually obvious (~10s in, when
  // one team has clearly broken serve) and then place a "guaranteed"
  // wager. Now bets are only accepted before the round starts (preRound),
  // between rounds (betweenRounds), after the match concludes (post),
  // or before any match has begun for the slot.
  //
  // "round" bets DO get a per-round window (between rounds) — so you
  // can bet on the next round's winner during intermissions but not
  // while the round is being fought.
  const liveMatch = LEAGUE.match;
  if (liveMatch && liveMatch.phase === "round") {
    feed("item", "🔒 Betting locked — wait for the round to end.");
    return false;
  }

  if (amount <= 0 || amount > LEAGUE.bits) return false;
  if (!LEAGUE.currentSlot) return false;
  const slotData = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
  if (!slotData || !slotData.teamA || !slotData.teamB) return false;

  let payoutMult = 1;
  if (kind === "team" || kind === "round") {
    const o = teamOdds(slotData.teamA, slotData.teamB);
    payoutMult = o[target] || 0;
    if (!payoutMult) return false;
    if (kind === "round") payoutMult = +(payoutMult * 0.85).toFixed(2);
  } else if (kind === "player") {
    const o = playerOdds(slotData.teamA, slotData.teamB);
    payoutMult = o[target] || 0;
    if (!payoutMult) return false;
  } else if (kind === "firstkill") {
    // Lock the market once the first kill of the match has been scored.
    // The UI already hides the section, but the backend must enforce too.
    const m = LEAGUE.match;
    if (m && m.firstKillTeam) return false;
    // Both teams roughly equal probability — payouts mirror match odds × 0.95
    const o = teamOdds(slotData.teamA, slotData.teamB);
    payoutMult = +((o[target] || 1.9) * 0.95).toFixed(2);
    if (!payoutMult) return false;
  } else if (kind === "totalcas") {
    // Over/under 5.5 casualties this match. Roughly even-money bet.
    if (target !== "over" && target !== "under") return false;
    payoutMult = 1.85;
  } else if (kind === "rounds") {
    // Over/under 3.5 rounds played in the match. Most matches are 3-0 sweeps;
    // "over" is moderately less likely so it pays slightly more.
    if (target !== "over" && target !== "under") return false;
    payoutMult = target === "over" ? 2.10 : 1.65;
  } else return false;

  LEAGUE.bits -= amount;
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const roundIndex = (kind === "round" && m) ? (m.currentRoundIndex + (m.phase === "betweenRounds" || m.phase === "preRound" ? 1 : 0)) : null;
  LEAGUE.bets.push({ kind, target, amount, payoutMult, slot: LEAGUE.currentSlot, roundIndex });
  if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
  const labelMap = {
    team: "match winner", round: "round winner", player: "last standing",
    firstkill: "first kill", totalcas: "total casualties", rounds: "rounds played",
  };
  feed("item", `💰 Bet placed: ${amount} bits on ${labelMap[kind] || kind} = ${target} @ ${payoutMult}×`);
  return true;
}

// Resolves "round winner" bets after each round.
function settleRoundBets(roundWinnerTeamId) {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;
  const remaining = [];
  for (const bet of LEAGUE.bets) {
    if (bet.kind !== "round" || bet.slot !== m.slot) {
      remaining.push(bet);
      continue;
    }
    if (bet.roundIndex !== m.currentRoundIndex) {
      remaining.push(bet);
      continue;
    }
    const tName = TEAM_BY_ID[bet.target]?.short || "?";
    if (bet.target === roundWinnerTeamId) {
      const payout = Math.round(bet.amount * bet.payoutMult);
      LEAGUE.bits += payout;
      feed("item", `💸 Round bet won! +${payout} bits (round ${m.currentRoundIndex})`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
        window.toast(`Round ${m.currentRoundIndex} bet WON: <b>+${payout}</b> bits on ${tName}`, "win");
    } else {
      feed("item", `💔 Round bet lost: ${bet.amount} bits.`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
        window.toast(`Round ${m.currentRoundIndex} bet lost: <b>-${bet.amount}</b> bits on ${tName}`, "lose");
    }
  }
  LEAGUE.bets = remaining;
  if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
}

// Resolves match-level bets ("team" + "player") after a match concludes.
function settleBets(winnerTeamId) {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;
  const lastStanding = (() => {
    const all = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers]
      .filter(p => !p.deceased && p.lives > 0);
