    if (!all.length) return null;
    all.sort((a,b) => (b.lives - a.lives) || (b.career.kills - a.career.kills));
    return all[0];
  })();

  const remaining = [];
  for (const bet of LEAGUE.bets) {
    if (bet.slot !== m.slot) {
      remaining.push(bet);
      continue;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "round") {
      LEAGUE.bits += bet.amount;
      feed("item", `↩️ Unsettled round bet refunded: ${bet.amount} bits.`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
        window.toast(`Round bet refunded: <b>+${bet.amount}</b> bits (match ended early)`, "warn");
      continue;
    }
    let win = false;
    let label = "";
    if (bet.kind === "team") {
      label = TEAM_BY_ID[bet.target]?.short || "?";
      if (bet.target === winnerTeamId) win = true;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "player") {
      const p = Object.values(LEAGUE.rosters).flat().find(x => x.id === bet.target);
      label = p ? p.name : "?";
      if (lastStanding && bet.target === lastStanding.id) win = true;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "firstkill") {
      label = (TEAM_BY_ID[bet.target]?.short || "?") + " first kill";
      if (m.firstKillTeam && bet.target === m.firstKillTeam) win = true;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "totalcas") {
      // Casualty count = unique deceased knights this match
      const matchDeaths = LEAGUE.graveyard.filter(g => g.season === LEAGUE.season && g.slot === m.slot).length;
      const line = 5.5;
      label = bet.target === "over" ? `over ${line} deaths` : `under ${line} deaths`;
      if ((bet.target === "over" && matchDeaths > line) || (bet.target === "under" && matchDeaths < line)) win = true;
    }
    if (bet.kind === "rounds") {
      const totalRounds = m.rounds.length;
      const line = 3.5;
      label = bet.target === "over" ? `over ${line} rounds` : `under ${line} rounds`;
      if ((bet.target === "over" && totalRounds > line) || (bet.target === "under" && totalRounds < line)) win = true;
    }
    if (win) {
      const payout = Math.round(bet.amount * bet.payoutMult);
      LEAGUE.bits += payout;
      feed("item", `💸 Bet won! +${payout} bits (${bet.kind})`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
        window.toast(`Match bet WON: <b>+${payout}</b> bits on ${label}`, "win");
    } else {
      feed("item", `💔 Bet lost: ${bet.amount} bits on ${bet.kind}.`);
      if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.toast)
        window.toast(`Match bet lost: <b>-${bet.amount}</b> bits on ${label}`, "lose");
    }
  }
  LEAGUE.bets = remaining;

  // Always award some bits for watching
  const watchReward = 5 + RI(0, 5);
  LEAGUE.bits += watchReward;
  feed("item", `🪙 +${watchReward} bits (broadcast tip)`);
  if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
}

/* ============================================================
   SHOP
   ============================================================ */
function buyItem(itemId, teamId) {
  const item = SHOP_BY_ID[itemId];
  if (!item) return false;
  if (LEAGUE.bits < item.cost) return false;
  if (LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.phase === "round") return false; // can't buy during a live round
  // Specific-player targeted items must use buyTargetedItem instead.
  if (item.target === "specific_player") return false;

  // Saboteur targets the OPPONENT in the next active match
  let appliedToTeam = teamId;
  if (item.target === "opp") {
    if (!LEAGUE.currentSlot) return false;
    const m = LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot];
    appliedToTeam = (teamId === m.teamA) ? m.teamB : m.teamA;
  }

  const boost = LEAGUE.pendingBoosts[appliedToTeam] || (LEAGUE.pendingBoosts[appliedToTeam] = {});
  item.apply(boost);
  LEAGUE.bits -= item.cost;
  feed("item", `🛒 Purchased ${item.nm} for ${TEAM_BY_ID[appliedToTeam].name}.`);
  if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
  return true;
}

/* === v28.1: Targeted shop items (e.g. Personal Sandbag) ===
   These need a specific knight as target. Caller provides both itemId
   and the knight ID; we look up the knight, charge the cost, and store
   the boost in pendingTargetedBoosts under the knight's team. */
function buyTargetedItem(itemId, knightId) {
  const item = SHOP_BY_ID[itemId];
  if (!item) return false;
  if (item.target !== "specific_player") return false;
  if (LEAGUE.bits < item.cost) return false;
  if (LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.phase === "round") return false;
  // Find the knight + their team
  let knight = null, knightTeamId = null;
  for (const tid in LEAGUE.rosters) {
    const found = LEAGUE.rosters[tid].find(p => p.id === knightId);
    if (found) { knight = found; knightTeamId = tid; break; }
  }
  if (!knight || knight.deceased) return false;
  // Initialize pending targeted boost for this knight
  if (!LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts) LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts = {};
  if (!LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts[knightTeamId]) LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts[knightTeamId] = {};
  const slot = LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts[knightTeamId][knight.id]
            || (LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts[knightTeamId][knight.id] = {});
  item.apply(slot);
  LEAGUE.bits -= item.cost;
  feed("item", `⚓ Personal sandbag placed on ${knight.name} (${TEAM_BY_ID[knightTeamId].short}).`);
  if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
  return true;
}
window.buyTargetedItem = buyTargetedItem;

</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     UI — Part A
     Canvas pixel-art renderer + DOM render functions
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
'use strict';

/* ============================================================
   ELEMENT REFS
   ============================================================ */
const $ = (sel) => document.querySelector(sel);
const $$ = (sel) => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(sel));
const arenaCanvas = $("#arena");

// Camera shake — set by triggerShake(magnitude); decays each frame.
// Drawn as a CSS transform on the canvas wrapper so it doesn't fight
// the canvas's own coordinate system.
let _shakeAmp = 0;
function triggerShake(amp) {
  // Settings toggle — skip if user has disabled camera shake
  if (typeof LEAGUE_CONFIG !== "undefined" && LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableShake) return;
  _shakeAmp = Math.max(_shakeAmp, amp);
}
function applyShake() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  const wrap = document.querySelector(".arena-wrap");
  if (!wrap) return;
  if (_shakeAmp <= 0.05) {
    wrap.style.transform = "";
    _shakeAmp = 0;
    return;
  }
  const t = (typeof performance !== "undefined" ? performance.now() : Date.now());
  // Smooth high-frequency jitter
  const dx = Math.sin(t * 0.07) * _shakeAmp;
  const dy = Math.cos(t * 0.09) * _shakeAmp * 0.7;
  wrap.style.transform = `translate(${dx.toFixed(2)}px, ${dy.toFixed(2)}px)`;
  _shakeAmp *= 0.78; // exponential decay
}
/* `ctx` is the global drawing context — bound to the main arena canvas
   by default. It's declared with `let` (not `const`) so the biome-preview
   helper can temporarily redirect drawing to a thumbnail canvas, run the
   existing scatter+court rendering routines (which all reference this
   global), and then restore it. The redirect happens synchronously
   within drawBiomePreview() so no other render call can interleave. */
let ctx = arenaCanvas.getContext("2d");
ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;

/* ============================================================
   CAMERA — broadcast tracking shot.

   The arena is a fixed logical 960x540 box; the canvas is the
   render surface. When TV mode is on, we apply a virtual camera
   that frames the action like an NFL/NBA tracking shot — pans
   and zooms so the active players and objectives stay in view
   while padding is dropped from empty parts of the field.

   Each frame:
     1. Compute the AABB of all alive players + objectives
        (drops, power-core, crown, flag, bases) with margin
     2. Translate that AABB into a desired camera (cx, cy, zoom)
     3. Smoothly lerp the live camera toward the target
     4. drawArena applies translate+scale before drawing world
        contents, then restores

   World-space drawing happens *inside* the camera transform.
   HUD/overlays (TV scoreboard, kill feed, vignette) draw
   *outside* it so they stay viewport-locked.
   ============================================================ */
const CAMERA = {
  x: 640,        // current center X in world coords (arenaW=1280 / 2)
  y: 360,        // current center Y in world coords (arenaH=720 / 2)
  zoom: 1.0,     // current zoom (1 = fit field exactly)
  targetX: 640,
  targetY: 360,
  targetZoom: 1.0,
  enabled: false,  // toggled by TV mode + opt-in setting
  // Tunables — v28.13: re-tuned for less aggressive zoom + smoother motion
  // per user feedback ("zoom too aggressive, user never sees the stadium;
  // camera snap too jerky"). The dynamic camera is now cinematic, not
  // nauseating: it tracks the action subtly and prefers wide framing.
  PAD_TV: 90,     // padding around tracked AABB in TV mode
  PAD_NORMAL: 200, // (was 80) much more padding so small alive-player
                   // clusters don't make the AABB tiny → camera no longer
                   // tightens on a 2-player engagement
  MIN_ZOOM: 0.78, // (was 0.85) — allow pulling back further to show
                  // stadium edges when action spreads to corners
  MAX_ZOOM: 1.10, // (was 1.6) — barely zoom in past fit-the-field. The
                  // stadium stays visible at all times. Cinematic, not
                  // claustrophobic.
  LERP: 0.04,     // (was 0.07) per-frame movement toward target — slower
                  // glide eliminates the "snap" feel
  ZOOM_LERP: 0.025, // (was 0.05) zoom changes are now barely perceptible
                    // frame-to-frame; total zoom transitions still complete
                    // in well under a second of real time
};

/* Compute the desired camera target from the current match state.
   Considers: alive players + projectiles' targets + drops + objectives. */
function _computeCameraTarget(m) {
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW || 1280, H = COMBAT.arenaH || 720;
  if (!m) return { cx: W/2, cy: H/2, zoom: 1.0 };

  const pad = CAMERA.PAD_NORMAL;

  let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  let count = 0;

  // Alive players
  const all = [...(m.teamAplayers || []), ...(m.teamBplayers || [])];
  for (const p of all) {
    if (!p.alive) continue;
    if (p.x < minX) minX = p.x;
    if (p.y < minY) minY = p.y;
    if (p.x > maxX) maxX = p.x;
    if (p.y > maxY) maxY = p.y;
    count++;
  }

  // Mode objectives — crown, flag, bases — when present
  if (m.crown) {
    if (m.crown.x < minX) minX = m.crown.x;
    if (m.crown.y < minY) minY = m.crown.y;
    if (m.crown.x > maxX) maxX = m.crown.x;
    if (m.crown.y > maxY) maxY = m.crown.y;
    count++;
  }
  if (m.flag) {
    if (m.flag.x < minX) minX = m.flag.x;
    if (m.flag.y < minY) minY = m.flag.y;
    if (m.flag.x > maxX) maxX = m.flag.x;
    if (m.flag.y > maxY) maxY = m.flag.y;
    count++;
  }

  // Power core (deathmatch focal point)
  if (m.powerCore && m.powerCore.state === "active") {
    if (m.powerCore.x < minX) minX = m.powerCore.x;
    if (m.powerCore.y < minY) minY = m.powerCore.y;
    if (m.powerCore.x > maxX) maxX = m.powerCore.x;
    if (m.powerCore.y > maxY) maxY = m.powerCore.y;
    count++;
  }

  // Dropped items — only consider drops within reach of any alive player
  // so the camera doesn't over-fit on stale far-corner drops
  if (m.drops && count > 0) {
    for (const d of m.drops) {
      // Is any alive player within 200px of this drop?
      let near = false;
      for (const p of all) {
        if (!p.alive) continue;
        const dx = p.x - d.x, dy = p.y - d.y;
        if (dx*dx + dy*dy < 200*200) { near = true; break; }
      }
      if (near) {
        if (d.x < minX) minX = d.x;
        if (d.y < minY) minY = d.y;
        if (d.x > maxX) maxX = d.x;
        if (d.y > maxY) maxY = d.y;
      }
    }
  }

  // Empty match (between rounds) — frame the whole field PLUS the
  // surrounding crowd band so the inter-round shot shows the stadium,
  // not just the empty pitch. zoom 0.92 × canvasFillScale (1.5 in
  // fullscreen) = finalScale 1.38, which makes the visible world
  // ~1391×783 — wider than the 1280×720 world by exactly the
  // CROWD_DEPTH (32) on each side. center-clamp logic below detects
  // halfW_world > W/2 and snaps cx to W/2, so the world stays
  // perfectly centered with crowd visible on all four edges.
  if (count === 0) {
    return { cx: W/2, cy: H/2, zoom: 0.92 };
  }

  // Pad the AABB
  minX = Math.max(0, minX - pad);
  minY = Math.max(0, minY - pad);
  maxX = Math.min(W, maxX + pad);
  maxY = Math.min(H, maxY + pad);

  const bw = maxX - minX;
  const bh = maxY - minY;
  let cx = (minX + maxX) / 2;
  let cy = (minY + maxY) / 2;

  // Zoom = how much we'd need to scale to fill the canvas with this AABB.
  // Take the smaller axis to ensure the entire AABB fits.
  const zx = W / Math.max(1, bw);
  const zy = H / Math.max(1, bh);
  let zoom = Math.min(zx, zy);
  zoom = clamp(zoom, CAMERA.MIN_ZOOM, CAMERA.MAX_ZOOM);

  // === v28.4 fix #1: enforce minimum zoom such that the world fills
  // the canvas in TV mode. The render path multiplies CAMERA.zoom by
  // canvasFillScale = max(canvasW/worldW, canvasH/worldH). For the
  // world to actually FILL the canvas (no black bars from undershoot),
  // we need finalScale = CAMERA.zoom * canvasFillScale ≥ 1.0 in canvas
  // pixel terms — i.e. CAMERA.zoom must be ≥ 1.0 / canvasFillScale.
  //
  // Read canvas dims through arenaCanvas (lives at module scope). In
  // non-TV mode the canvas matches the world, canvasFillScale = 1.0,
  // and this floor evaluates to 1.0 — equal to the world-fits-canvas
  // baseline, so behavior is unchanged. ===
  if (typeof arenaCanvas !== "undefined" && arenaCanvas) {
    const cW = arenaCanvas.width || W;
    const cH = arenaCanvas.height || H;
    // Match the FILL/FIT logic used in _applyCameraTransform so the
    // floor matches what's actually used at draw time. (See v28.7
    // portrait-mismatch comment in _applyCameraTransform.)
    const canvasAspect = cW / cH;
    const worldAspect = W / H;
    const isPortraitMismatch = canvasAspect < worldAspect * 0.85;
    const canvasFillScale = isPortraitMismatch
      ? Math.min(cW / W, cH / H)
      : Math.max(cW / W, cH / H);
    if (canvasFillScale > 0) {
      const minZoomForFill = 1.0 / canvasFillScale;
      if (zoom < minZoomForFill) zoom = minZoomForFill;
    }
  }

  // === v28.4 fix #2: clamp camera center so the visible viewport
  // doesn't extend past the world bounds. Otherwise when a bot is near
  // a field edge, the camera centers on them and the off-field side of
  // the canvas shows black void.
  //
  // Visible viewport in world coords (after canvas-fill scaling):
  //   halfW_world = (canvasW / 2) / (zoom * canvasFillScale)
  //   halfH_world = (canvasH / 2) / (zoom * canvasFillScale)
  // For the camera not to show outside the world:
  //   halfW_world ≤ cx ≤ W - halfW_world
  //   halfH_world ≤ cy ≤ H - halfH_world
  //
  // If the visible half-extent exceeds half the world (which can happen
  // if MIN_ZOOM forces zoom small enough that the world is smaller than
  // the canvas in world units), we just center on the world midpoint. ===
  if (typeof arenaCanvas !== "undefined" && arenaCanvas) {
    const cW = arenaCanvas.width || W;
    const cH = arenaCanvas.height || H;
    const canvasAspect = cW / cH;
    const worldAspect = W / H;
    const isPortraitMismatch = canvasAspect < worldAspect * 0.85;
    const canvasFillScale = isPortraitMismatch
      ? Math.min(cW / W, cH / H)
      : Math.max(cW / W, cH / H);
    const finalScale = zoom * canvasFillScale;
    const halfW_world = (cW / 2) / finalScale;
    const halfH_world = (cH / 2) / finalScale;
    if (halfW_world * 2 >= W) {
      cx = W / 2;
    } else {
      cx = clamp(cx, halfW_world, W - halfW_world);
    }
    if (halfH_world * 2 >= H) {
      cy = H / 2;
    } else {
      cy = clamp(cy, halfH_world, H - halfH_world);
    }
  }

  return { cx, cy, zoom };
}

/* Update camera target + lerp current toward it. Called once per
   frame from drawArena before any world-space drawing. */
function _updateCamera(m) {
  // Camera is always enabled (subtle pan even in standard mode), but uses
  // tighter framing in TV mode for a more cinematic broadcast feel.
  CAMERA.enabled = true;

  const tgt = _computeCameraTarget(m);
  CAMERA.targetX = tgt.cx;
  CAMERA.targetY = tgt.cy;
  CAMERA.targetZoom = tgt.zoom;

  // Smooth lerp — separate factors for position and zoom so zoom
  // changes don't cause motion sickness.
  CAMERA.x = CAMERA.x + (CAMERA.targetX - CAMERA.x) * CAMERA.LERP;
  CAMERA.y = CAMERA.y + (CAMERA.targetY - CAMERA.y) * CAMERA.LERP;
  CAMERA.zoom = CAMERA.zoom + (CAMERA.targetZoom - CAMERA.zoom) * CAMERA.ZOOM_LERP;
}

/* Apply the current camera transform to ctx. Caller must save+restore
   around their world-space drawing block.

   v28.3.3 fullscreen-fill fix: in TV mode, _resizeCanvasForTVMode sets
   the canvas internal resolution to the viewport (e.g. 1920×1080), but
   the world is fixed at COMBAT.arenaW × arenaH (1280×720). With only
   `ctx.scale(CAMERA.zoom, CAMERA.zoom)`, world coordinates map 1:1 to
   canvas pixels — so a 1280-wide world drawn into a 1920-wide canvas
   leaves 320px of black on each side. The world is letterboxed inside
   the canvas.

   Fix: compute a `canvasFillScale` = max(canvasW/worldW, canvasH/worldH)
   and apply it on top of CAMERA.zoom. With matching aspect ratios
   (16:9 fullscreen on 16:9 world), canvasFillScale = 1.5 and the world
   fills the canvas. With mismatched aspect (e.g. 16:10 monitor), the
   larger axis fills and the smaller axis crops slightly — preferable
   to black bars for a "fullscreen broadcast" feel.

   In non-TV mode, the canvas internal resolution matches the world
   (1280×720), so canvasFillScale = 1.0 and behavior is unchanged. */
function _applyCameraTransform() {
  const W = arenaCanvas.width, H = arenaCanvas.height;
  const worldW = COMBAT.arenaW || 1280;
  const worldH = COMBAT.arenaH || 720;
  // === v28.7 mobile portrait support ===
  // The world is 16:9 horizontal (1280×720, ratio 1.78). Most desktop
  // and landscape mobile viewports are similar — ~16:9 to ~16:10 — and
  // benefit from the original FILL behavior (max(scaleX, scaleY)) which
  // crops slightly to eliminate letterbox bars.
  //
  // Phone portrait (~9:19, ratio 0.47) is the opposite — far taller
  // than wide. FILL on portrait gives canvasFillScale = scaleY, which
  // makes the world ~1.1× viewport tall but ~3× viewport wide. Most
  // of the field falls offscreen — the user only sees a narrow vertical
  // slice of action.
  //
  // Detect severe portrait mismatch and switch to FIT (min) instead.
  // FIT lets the entire 16:9 world fit inside the portrait viewport
  // with black bars top/bottom. The user sees ALL the action.
  // The threshold is canvas-aspect < 0.85 × world-aspect, which
  // catches phone portrait while preserving landscape FILL.
  const canvasAspect = W / H;
  const worldAspect = worldW / worldH;
  const isPortraitMismatch = canvasAspect < worldAspect * 0.85;
  const canvasFillScale = isPortraitMismatch
    ? Math.min(W / worldW, H / worldH)   // FIT: world fits inside, black bars
    : Math.max(W / worldW, H / worldH);  // FILL: world covers canvas, slight crop
  const finalScale = CAMERA.zoom * canvasFillScale;
  // Translate canvas center to world camera position, scaled by zoom.
  // World point (x, y) → screen point
  //   ((x - CAMERA.x) * finalScale + W/2, (y - CAMERA.y) * finalScale + H/2)
  ctx.translate(W/2, H/2);
  ctx.scale(finalScale, finalScale);
  ctx.translate(-CAMERA.x, -CAMERA.y);
}


/* Decorative scenery — fixed positions for 16:9 arena (960×540) */
const SCENERY = [
  { type: "pillar", x: 200, y: 130 },
  { type: "pillar", x: 760, y: 130 },
  { type: "pillar", x: 200, y: 410 },
  { type: "pillar", x: 760, y: 410 },
  { type: "banner", x: 480, y:  60 },
  { type: "banner", x: 480, y: 480 },
  { type: "rubble", x: 340, y: 280 },
  { type: "rubble", x: 620, y: 280 },
  { type: "rubble", x: 480, y: 200 },
  { type: "rubble", x: 480, y: 340 },
];

// Convert "#RRGGBB" to "rgba(r,g,b,a)" for compositing
function _hexA(hex, a) {
  const c = (hex || "#888").replace("#", "");
  const r = parseInt(c.substring(0,2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(c.substring(2,4), 16);
  const b = parseInt(c.substring(4,6), 16);
  return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${a})`;
}

/* Blend two "#RRGGBB" colors at the given ratio (0=a, 1=b). Used to
   produce a smooth midpoint for the floor gradient. */
function _blend(hexA, hexB, t) {
  const ca = (hexA || "#888").replace("#", "");
  const cb = (hexB || "#888").replace("#", "");
  const ar = parseInt(ca.substring(0,2), 16), ag = parseInt(ca.substring(2,4), 16), ab = parseInt(ca.substring(4,6), 16);
  const br = parseInt(cb.substring(0,2), 16), bg = parseInt(cb.substring(2,4), 16), bb = parseInt(cb.substring(4,6), 16);
  const r = Math.round(ar + (br - ar) * t);
  const g = Math.round(ag + (bg - ag) * t);
  const b = Math.round(ab + (bb - ab) * t);
  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
}

/* ============================================================
   BIOME SCATTER RENDER — draws the per-match decorative sprites
   (grass tufts, dandelions, pebbles, ice cracks, embers, petals)
   directly to the floor canvas. No filtering or culling — the count
   is bounded (<300 sprites typically) so blasting through is fast.
   ============================================================ */
function drawBiomeScatter(scatter) {
  for (const s of scatter) {
    switch (s.type) {
      case "grassTuft": {
        // Three little vertical blades fanning out
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 4 - s.r);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 2, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 3, s.y - 2 - s.r);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x + 2, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 3, s.y - 2 - s.r);
        ctx.stroke();
        // Highlight on the center blade
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 1);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 3 - s.r);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "dandelion": {
        // Tiny puff: a yellow-ish circle + small white dots around it
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
          const a = i * Math.PI / 2 + s.rot;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(s.x + Math.cos(a) * 2.2, s.y + Math.sin(a) * 2.2, 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        break;
      }
      case "pebble": {
        // Small dark ellipse with highlight
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 1.5 + s.r * 0.5, 1 + s.r * 0.4, s.rot, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y - 0.5, 1, 0.5);
        break;
      }
      case "cactus": {
        // Vertical green oval w/ ridge
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 2 + s.r, 5 + s.r * 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 5 - s.r);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y + 5 + s.r);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "sandTuft": {
        // Tiny windswept marks
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 2, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 2, s.y - 1);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "iceCrack": {
        // Jagged crack lines
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 6, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 1, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 3, s.y - 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 7, s.y + 1);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 1, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 1, s.y + 5);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "snowTuft": {
        // Tiny white dot
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 0.8 + s.r * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "ember": {
        // Glowing dot with halo
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(s.c1.replace(/^#/, "#") || "#ff8030", 0.18);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "crack": {
        // Glowing volcanic crack
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.2;
        ctx.lineCap = "round";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 8, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 6, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 10, s.y - 1);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.8;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 8, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 6, s.y + 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.lineCap = "butt";
        break;
      }
      case "petal": {
        // Soft pink ellipse
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.rotate(s.rot);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 2.8, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(-0.4, -0.4, 1.4, 0.7, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
        break;
      }
      case "crystal": {
        // Diamond / triangle gem shape — pointy with bright highlight
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.rotate(s.rot);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, -3 - s.r);
        ctx.lineTo(2.5, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 3 + s.r * 0.6);
        ctx.lineTo(-2.5, 0);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, -3 - s.r);
        ctx.lineTo(1, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 0);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
        break;
      }
      case "stalagmite": {
        // Pointy upward cone
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 4, s.y + 4);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 6 - s.r);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 4, s.y + 4);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y - 5 - s.r, 1, 4);
        break;
      }
      case "seaweed": {
        // Wavy vertical strand
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 6);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(s.x - 3, s.y + 2, s.x + 3, s.y - 2, s.x, s.y - 8 - s.r * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 4);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(s.x - 2, s.y + 1, s.x + 2, s.y - 1, s.x, s.y - 6 - s.r * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "coral": {
        // Tree-branch shape with multiple prongs
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 3 + s.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let a = -Math.PI / 2; a < Math.PI / 2; a += Math.PI / 5) {
          ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y);
          ctx.lineTo(s.x + Math.cos(a) * 5, s.y + Math.sin(a) * 5);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "manhole": {
        // Round dark circle with cross pattern
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 7.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 6, s.y); ctx.lineTo(s.x + 6, s.y);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 6); ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y + 6);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "oilStain": {
        // Irregular dark blob
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 5 + s.r, 3 + s.r * 0.6, s.rot, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x - 1, s.y - 0.5, 2, 1.2, s.rot, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "gum": {
        // Tiny squashed circle
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 1.8, 1.2, s.rot, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "leaf": {
        // Pointed oval, rotated
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.rotate(s.rot);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 3.5 + s.r * 0.4, 1.6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-3, 0); ctx.lineTo(3, 0);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
        break;
      }
      case "vine": {
        // Curving thin line
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 8, s.y);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(s.x - 2, s.y - 4, s.x + 2, s.y + 4, s.x + 8, s.y);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 1);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 3, s.y - 4);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "gridDot": {
        // Bright glowing dot — neon biomes
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(s.c1.startsWith("#") ? s.c1 : "#ff40c8", 0.20);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "neonStripe": {
        // Short bright horizontal line with glow
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(s.x, s.y);
        ctx.rotate(s.rot);
        ctx.strokeStyle = _hexA(s.c1.startsWith("#") ? s.c1 : "#ff40c8", 0.30);
        ctx.lineWidth = 4;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-10, 0); ctx.lineTo(10, 0);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-10, 0); ctx.lineTo(10, 0);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
        break;
      }
      case "glowMushroom": {
        // Small mushroom with bright cap
        ctx.fillStyle = "#3a3028";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.7, s.y - 1, 1.4, 4);
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(s.c1.startsWith("#") ? s.c1 : "#88e0c8", 0.4);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y - 1, 4, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y - 1, 2.5, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y - 1.5, 0.7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "evergreen": {
        // Triangular pine tree silhouette
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.30)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x + 2, s.y + 8, 8, 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 6, s.y + 6);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 12);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 6, s.y + 6);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 4, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 8);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 4, s.y + 2);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = "#3d2515";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y + 6, 2, 2);
        break;
      }
      case "flowerPink":
      case "flowerYellow": {
        // 5-petal flower with center
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
          const a = i * (Math.PI * 2 / 5) + s.rot;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(s.x + Math.cos(a) * 1.8, s.y + Math.sin(a) * 1.8, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "gift": {
        // Wrapped gift box — small square with a ribbon
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 4, s.y - 4, 8, 8);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 4, s.y - 0.5, 8, 1);
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.5, s.y - 4, 1, 8);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case "ornament": {
        // Christmas ornament — gold/silver bauble with a tiny cap
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(s.c1.startsWith("#") ? s.c1 : "#ffc848", 0.35);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 4.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x - 0.8, s.y - 0.8, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = "#5a4830";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.5, s.y - 4, 1, 1.5);
        break;
      }
      case "pumpkin": {
        // Orange ridged pumpkin
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.30)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x + 1, s.y + 4, 5, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 5, 4, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 2, s.y - 3); ctx.lineTo(s.x - 2, s.y + 3);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 4);     ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y + 4);
        ctx.moveTo(s.x + 2, s.y - 3); ctx.lineTo(s.x + 2, s.y + 3);
        ctx.stroke();
        // Stem
        ctx.fillStyle = "#3a4818";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.5, s.y - 5, 1, 2);
        break;
      }
      case "gravestone": {
        // Rounded-top tombstone
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.40)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x + 1, s.y + 7, 6, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 4, s.y + 6);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 4, s.y - 3);
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y - 3, 4, Math.PI, 0, false);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 4, s.y + 6);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 2.5, s.y - 1, 5, 0.6);
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 2,   s.y + 1, 4, 0.6);
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 2.5, s.y + 3, 5, 0.6);
        break;
      }
      // === v28.10/v28.11: scatter handlers for new effect overlays ===
      case "lavaCrack": {
        // Glowing zigzag in the floor — bright orange under thin dark crust
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 5, s.y - 1);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 1, s.y + 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 2, s.y - 1);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 5, s.y + 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.7;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 4, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y + 1);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 4, s.y);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "ash": {
        // Small grey smudge — soft puff
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 2 + s.r * 0.6, 1, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 0.5, s.y - 0.5, 1, 1);
        break;
      }
      case "mushroom": {
        // Cap + stem — cap colored, stem off-white
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;  // stem
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y, 2, 3);
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;  // cap
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, 2.5 + s.r * 0.4, Math.PI, 0, false);
        ctx.fill();
        // Spots
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.8)";
        ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y - 1, 0.7, 0.7);
        ctx.fillRect(s.x + 0.5, s.y - 1.4, 0.6, 0.6);
        break;
      }
      case "pinecone": {
        // Brown teardrop with hatched scales
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 2, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(s.x - 1.5, s.y - 1.5 + i * 1.2);
          ctx.lineTo(s.x + 1.5, s.y - 1.5 + i * 1.2);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
        break;
      }
      case "cherryLeaf": {
        // Pointed oval leaf, slight stem
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(s.x, s.y, 1.8, 3, Math.PI / 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 1.5, s.y + 1.5);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 1.5, s.y - 1.5);
        ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case "wheatStalk": {
        // Vertical stem with bristled head
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y + 4);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y - 4);
        ctx.stroke();
        // Bristles
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
        for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 2 - i);
          ctx.lineTo(s.x - 2, s.y - 3 - i);
          ctx.moveTo(s.x, s.y - 2 - i);
          ctx.lineTo(s.x + 2, s.y - 3 - i);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
        break;
      }
      case "auroraWisp": {
        // Soft glowing curve — blurred trail
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(s.x - 6, s.y, s.x + 6, s.y);
        grad.addColorStop(0,    "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
        grad.addColorStop(0.4,  s.c1);
        grad.addColorStop(0.6,  s.c2);
        grad.addColorStop(1,    "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
        ctx.strokeStyle = grad;
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 6, s.y);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(s.x, s.y - 4, s.x + 6, s.y);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
        break;
      }
      case "circuitArc": {
        // Tiny zig-zag in bright neon — like a printed-circuit trace
        ctx.strokeStyle = s.c1;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(s.x - 4, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 2, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x - 2, s.y - 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 2, s.y - 2);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 2, s.y);
        ctx.lineTo(s.x + 4, s.y);
        ctx.stroke();
        // Glow dot at one end
        ctx.fillStyle = s.c2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(s.x + 4, s.y, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   COURT-SPECIFIC FIELD MARKINGS — for tennis/badminton/basketball/
   volleyball biomes. Drawn in place of the generic grid + center
   circle when biome.courtType is set.

   The court runs the full arena, scaled to the available space.
   Lines color comes from biome.accents.lineColor (typically white).
   ============================================================ */
function drawCourtMarkings(W, H, courtType, lineColor) {
  ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  // Outer boundary — common to all courts
  ctx.strokeRect(40, 40, W - 80, H - 80);
  // Center net line (vertical)
  ctx.lineWidth = 4;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(W / 2, 30); ctx.lineTo(W / 2, H - 30);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;

  if (courtType === "tennis") {
    // Service boxes
    const sx = W / 2 - 200, ex = W / 2 + 200;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(sx, 40);   ctx.lineTo(sx, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(ex, 40);   ctx.lineTo(ex, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2, H / 2 - 0); ctx.lineTo(W / 2, H / 2 - 0);
    ctx.moveTo(sx, H / 2);  ctx.lineTo(ex, H / 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Singles sidelines
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(80, 40);    ctx.lineTo(80, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W - 80, 40); ctx.lineTo(W - 80, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
  } else if (courtType === "basketball") {
    // Three-point arc on each side + key (free-throw rectangle)
    const arcR = Math.min(W, H) * 0.32;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(40, H / 2, arcR, -Math.PI / 2.6, Math.PI / 2.6);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W - 40, H / 2, arcR, Math.PI - Math.PI / 2.6, Math.PI + Math.PI / 2.6);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Key boxes
    ctx.strokeRect(40, H / 2 - 80, 200, 160);
    ctx.strokeRect(W - 240, H / 2 - 80, 200, 160);
    // Free throw circles
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(240, H / 2, 60, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W - 240, H / 2, 60, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Center circle
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W / 2, H / 2, 60, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  } else if (courtType === "volleyball") {
    // Attack lines 3m from net
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 - 180, 40);   ctx.lineTo(W / 2 - 180, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 + 180, 40);   ctx.lineTo(W / 2 + 180, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Net dotted line down center
    ctx.setLineDash([10, 6]);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2, 30); ctx.lineTo(W / 2, H - 30);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
  } else if (courtType === "badminton") {
    // Singles sidelines + service lines
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(70, 40);    ctx.lineTo(70, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W - 70, 40); ctx.lineTo(W - 70, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Short service lines (close to net)
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 - 100, 40);   ctx.lineTo(W / 2 - 100, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 + 100, 40);   ctx.lineTo(W / 2 + 100, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Center service line
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 - 100, H / 2); ctx.lineTo(W / 2 + 100, H / 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  } else if (courtType === "football") {
    // === v28.8: American football gridiron ===
    // Yard lines every 80px (10 yards). End zones at far ends.
    // Hash marks at 1/3 height and 2/3 height.
    const yardSpacing = 80;
    const endZoneW = 120;
    // End zones — slight tint to differentiate
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)";
    ctx.fillRect(40, 40, endZoneW, H - 80);
    ctx.fillRect(W - 40 - endZoneW, 40, endZoneW, H - 80);
    // Goal lines (boundary of end zones)
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(40 + endZoneW, 40); ctx.lineTo(40 + endZoneW, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W - 40 - endZoneW, 40); ctx.lineTo(W - 40 - endZoneW, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    // Yard lines across the playing field
    for (let x = 40 + endZoneW + yardSpacing; x < W - 40 - endZoneW; x += yardSpacing) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x, 40); ctx.lineTo(x, H - 40);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Hash marks at top/bottom thirds (NFL style)
      const hashY1 = 40 + (H - 80) / 3;
      const hashY2 = 40 + 2 * (H - 80) / 3;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x - 10, hashY1); ctx.lineTo(x + 10, hashY1);
      ctx.moveTo(x - 10, hashY2); ctx.lineTo(x + 10, hashY2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  } else if (courtType === "soccer") {
    // === v28.8: Soccer pitch ===
    // Penalty boxes (18-yard) + goal boxes (6-yard) + penalty arcs + center circle
    const pBoxW = 180, pBoxH = 380;
    const gBoxW = 60, gBoxH = 200;
    const pBoxYTop = (H - pBoxH) / 2;
    const gBoxYTop = (H - gBoxH) / 2;
    // Penalty boxes
    ctx.strokeRect(40, pBoxYTop, pBoxW, pBoxH);
    ctx.strokeRect(W - 40 - pBoxW, pBoxYTop, pBoxW, pBoxH);
    // Goal boxes
    ctx.strokeRect(40, gBoxYTop, gBoxW, gBoxH);
    ctx.strokeRect(W - 40 - gBoxW, gBoxYTop, gBoxW, gBoxH);
    // Penalty arcs — partial circle on the field-side of each penalty box
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(40 + 130, H / 2, 50, -Math.PI / 3, Math.PI / 3);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W - 40 - 130, H / 2, 50, Math.PI - Math.PI / 3, Math.PI + Math.PI / 3);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Center circle
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W / 2, H / 2, 80, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W / 2, H / 2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    // Corner arcs
    [[40, 40], [W - 40, 40], [40, H - 40], [W - 40, H - 40]].forEach(([cx, cy]) => {
      const dx = cx === 40 ? 1 : -1;
      const dy = cy === 40 ? 1 : -1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, 14, dx > 0 ? (dy > 0 ? 0 : 1.5 * Math.PI) : (dy > 0 ? 0.5 * Math.PI : Math.PI), dx > 0 ? (dy > 0 ? 0.5 * Math.PI : 0) : (dy > 0 ? Math.PI : 1.5 * Math.PI));
      ctx.stroke();
    });
  } else if (courtType === "hockey") {
    // === v28.8: Ice hockey rink ===
    // Two blue lines, red center, face-off circles, goalie creases
    // Center red line with dotted pattern
    ctx.setLineDash([10, 6]);
    ctx.lineWidth = 4;
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(220, 70, 70, 0.85)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2, 40); ctx.lineTo(W / 2, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.setLineDash([]);
    ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
    // Blue lines (1/3 from each end)
    ctx.lineWidth = 4;
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(80, 130, 220, 0.75)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 3, 40); ctx.lineTo(W / 3, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(2 * W / 3, 40); ctx.lineTo(2 * W / 3, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    // Center face-off circle
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W / 2, H / 2, 50, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
    // Four end-zone face-off circles
    [[180, H * 0.3], [180, H * 0.7], [W - 180, H * 0.3], [W - 180, H * 0.7]].forEach(([cx, cy]) => {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, 50, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    });
    // Goalie creases — half-circles at each end
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(80, 130, 220, 0.20)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(70, H / 2, 40, -Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W - 70, H / 2, 40, Math.PI / 2, 1.5 * Math.PI);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.stroke();
  } else if (courtType === "curling") {
    // === v28.8: Curling sheet ===
    // Two houses (concentric target circles) + hog lines + center line
    // Center line
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2, 40); ctx.lineTo(W / 2, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
    // House at each end — 4 concentric rings
    const houseRadii = [60, 45, 28, 12];
    const houseColors = ["rgba(220, 70, 70, 0.40)", "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)", "rgba(80, 130, 220, 0.40)", "rgba(255,255,255,0.20)"];
    [180, W - 180].forEach((hx) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < houseRadii.length; i++) {
        ctx.fillStyle = houseColors[i];
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(hx, H / 2, houseRadii[i], 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      // Tee line through center of each house
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(hx, 40); ctx.lineTo(hx, H - 40);
      ctx.stroke();
    });
    // Hog lines (where stones must cross)
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 - 280, 40); ctx.lineTo(W / 2 - 280, H - 40);
    ctx.moveTo(W / 2 + 280, 40); ctx.lineTo(W / 2 + 280, H - 40);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   CLOUD SHADOWS — soft semi-transparent dark patches that drift
   diagonally across the field. Drawn beneath the vignette so they
   read as actual cloud cover rather than a UI element.
   ============================================================ */
function drawCloudShadows(clouds) {
  for (const c of clouds) {
    const grd = ctx.createRadialGradient(
      c.x + c.w / 2, c.y + c.h / 2, 0,
      c.x + c.w / 2, c.y + c.h / 2, Math.max(c.w, c.h) / 2
    );
    grd.addColorStop(0, `rgba(0,0,0,${c.alpha})`);
    grd.addColorStop(0.7, `rgba(0,0,0,${c.alpha * 0.5})`);
    grd.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
    ctx.fillStyle = grd;
    ctx.fillRect(c.x, c.y, c.w, c.h);
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   STADIUM PITCH LIGHTS — soft warm flares in the four corners.
   Sells the broadcast-stadium fiction. Drawn AFTER the vignette
   so the lights cut through the dark corners. Each flare is a
   radial gradient from warm white at the corner edge to fully
   transparent at ~25% of the diagonal. Subtle but present.
   ============================================================ */
function drawPitchLights(W, H) {
  const flareR = Math.min(W, H) * 0.42;
  const corners = [
    [0,     0    ],   // top-left
    [W,     0    ],   // top-right
    [0,     H    ],   // bottom-left
    [W,     H    ],   // bottom-right
  ];
  for (const [cx, cy] of corners) {
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, flareR);
    g.addColorStop(0,   "rgba(255,240,210,0.18)");
    g.addColorStop(0.4, "rgba(255,235,200,0.06)");
    g.addColorStop(1,   "rgba(255,235,200,0)");
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  }
  // Tiny "lamp" pips in each corner so the lights have a visible source
  for (const [cx, cy] of corners) {
    const px = cx === 0 ? 8 : W - 8;
    const py = cy === 0 ? 8 : H - 8;
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,250,230,0.6)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(px, py, 2.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,1)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(px, py, 1.0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   AMBIENT PARTICLES — biome-driven atmospheric layer drawn
   between cloud shadows and the vignette so they read as airborne
   particles in front of the field but behind the broadcast lighting.

   - snow: tiny white dot with soft halo
   - ember: glowing orange pip with halo + life-fade
   - petal: rotated soft pink ellipse (with highlight)
   - shimmer: vertical wobble line, very faint (heat distortion)
   ============================================================ */
function drawAmbientParticles(particles) {
  for (const p of particles) {
    if (p.type === "snow") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r * 1.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.95)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "ember") {
      // Life-fade — particle dims as it rises
      const a = clamp(p.life / 80, 0.05, 0.85);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,140,40,${a * 0.30})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r * 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,200,80,${a})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "petal") {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.rotate(p.rot);
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,176,200,0.85)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(0, 0, p.r * 1.6, p.r * 0.7, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,210,225,0.95)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(-p.r * 0.3, -p.r * 0.2, p.r * 0.7, p.r * 0.3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    } else if (p.type === "shimmer") {
      // Heat shimmer — short vertical wobble line, very faint
      const sway = Math.sin(p.phase) * 1.5;
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,220,180,0.10)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x + sway, p.y);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x - sway, p.y + 5);
      ctx.stroke();
    } else if (p.type === "bubble") {
      // Underwater bubble — circle outline with a highlight pip
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(220,240,255,0.55)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.65)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x - p.r * 0.4, p.y - p.r * 0.4, p.r * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "leaf") {
      // Larger drifting leaf — different from the static scatter leaves
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.rotate(p.rot);
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(216,96,40,0.85)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(0, 0, p.r * 1.8, p.r * 0.9, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(120,52,18,0.6)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-p.r * 1.8, 0); ctx.lineTo(p.r * 1.8, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    } else if (p.type === "firefly") {
      // Pulsing yellow-green dot with halo
      const pulse = 0.5 + Math.sin(p.phase) * 0.5;
      const a = clamp(p.life / 200, 0.3, 1);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(180,255,120,${pulse * 0.30 * a})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(220,255,140,${pulse * 0.95 * a})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "spark") {
      // Bright neon trail — short streak with glow
      const a = clamp(p.life / 30, 0, 1);
      const isMagenta = p.hue === "magenta";
      const c1 = isMagenta ? "255,80,200" : "80,224,255";
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${c1},${0.30 * a})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x - p.vx * 3, p.y - p.vy * 3);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${0.95 * a})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x - p.vx * 2, p.y - p.vy * 2);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.stroke();
    } else if (p.type === "bat") {
      // Bat silhouette — wing flap via phase
      const wingY = Math.sin(p.phase) * 2;
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(20,15,25,0.85)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      // Body
      ctx.ellipse(p.x, p.y, 2, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      // Wings — left + right with flap
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x - 4, p.y + wingY, p.x - 7, p.y + wingY * 0.5);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x - 5, p.y + 1, p.x, p.y);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x + 4, p.y + wingY, p.x + 7, p.y + wingY * 0.5);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.x + 5, p.y + 1, p.x, p.y);
      ctx.fill();
    } else if (p.type === "drip") {
      // Cave water drip — small teardrop, brightens as it falls
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(120,200,255,0.35)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(p.x, p.y, 0.8, 1.6, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.7)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y - 0.3, 0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }
}

/* === v28.4: Stadium spectators ===
   Decorative crowd border drawn JUST OUTSIDE the playable field
   (negative coordinates for top, beyond arenaW for right, etc.) so
   the camera-fill scaling brings them into view as part of the scene.

   Each fan is a 3-color dot (head + body + occasional accent) at a
   fixed position with a small per-fan phase offset so the bobbing
   isn't synchronized. When m._crowdCheer is active (set on a kill),
   fans within crowdCheer.radius of crowdCheer.x bounce more vigorously.

   Honors LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableCrowd (settings toggle in v28.4). */
const CROWD_DEPTH = 32;        // how far back from the field edge fans extend
const CROWD_STRIDE = 11;       // spacing between fans
const CROWD_ROWS = 3;          // number of rows of seating
const CROWD_PALETTE = [
  // Pre-baked muted fan colors. Random pick per fan, deterministic via index.
  ["#7d4f3d", "#4a3625", "#a86d4d"],
  ["#4d5a8a", "#34406b", "#6f7eb5"],
  ["#6a8c5a", "#48663d", "#8baf78"],
  ["#8c5a6a", "#664050", "#b07c8c"],
  ["#5a7a8c", "#3d5b6a", "#7ca0b5"],
  ["#8c8a5a", "#666340", "#b0ad7c"],
  ["#6a4d8c", "#4d3866", "#8b6db0"],
];
let _crowdCache = null;  // cache of fan positions, recomputed if arena dims change

function _buildCrowdCache() {
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW || 1280;
  const H = COMBAT.arenaH || 720;
  const fans = [];
  // Top edge — fans facing down toward field
  for (let row = 0; row < CROWD_ROWS; row++) {
    const y = -CROWD_DEPTH + row * 9;
    const offset = (row % 2) * (CROWD_STRIDE / 2);
    for (let x = -16 + offset; x < W + 16; x += CROWD_STRIDE) {
      fans.push({ x, y, palette: CROWD_PALETTE[(x + row * 7) | 0 & 7 % CROWD_PALETTE.length || 0], side: "top" });
    }
  }
  // Bottom edge
  for (let row = 0; row < CROWD_ROWS; row++) {
    const y = H + 6 + row * 9;
    const offset = (row % 2) * (CROWD_STRIDE / 2);
    for (let x = -16 + offset; x < W + 16; x += CROWD_STRIDE) {
      fans.push({ x, y, palette: CROWD_PALETTE[((x * 3 + row * 11) | 0) & 7 % CROWD_PALETTE.length || 0], side: "bottom" });
    }
  }
  // Left edge — narrower because the canvas extends only a bit past the field
  for (let row = 0; row < CROWD_ROWS; row++) {
    const x = -CROWD_DEPTH + row * 9;
    const offset = (row % 2) * (CROWD_STRIDE / 2);
    for (let y = -16 + offset; y < H + 16; y += CROWD_STRIDE) {
      fans.push({ x, y, palette: CROWD_PALETTE[((y + row * 5) | 0) & 7 % CROWD_PALETTE.length || 0], side: "left" });
    }
  }
  // Right edge
  for (let row = 0; row < CROWD_ROWS; row++) {
    const x = W + 6 + row * 9;
    const offset = (row % 2) * (CROWD_STRIDE / 2);
    for (let y = -16 + offset; y < H + 16; y += CROWD_STRIDE) {
      fans.push({ x, y, palette: CROWD_PALETTE[((y * 3 + row * 13) | 0) & 7 % CROWD_PALETTE.length || 0], side: "right" });
    }
  }
  // Add stable per-fan random phase for bobbing
  for (let i = 0; i < fans.length; i++) {
    fans[i].phase = (i * 0.731) % (Math.PI * 2);
  }
  // Pre-resolve palette lookups (the % calc above can produce 0/undefined
  // due to JS bitwise edge cases — fall back to first palette).
  for (const f of fans) {
    if (!f.palette || !Array.isArray(f.palette)) f.palette = CROWD_PALETTE[0];
  }
  _crowdCache = { W, H, fans };
}

function drawCrowd(m) {
  if (LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableCrowd) return;
  // Build cache lazily / on resize
  const W = COMBAT.arenaW || 1280;
  const H = COMBAT.arenaH || 720;
  if (!_crowdCache || _crowdCache.W !== W || _crowdCache.H !== H) {
    _buildCrowdCache();
  }
  const fans = _crowdCache.fans;
  const t = (m && m.frame !== undefined) ? m.frame : 0;
  const cheer = m && m._crowdCheer;
  const cheerActive = cheer && (t - cheer.frame) < 30;

  // Draw stadium-back band (concrete tiers) behind the fans for depth
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(20, 22, 28, 0.7)";
  ctx.fillRect(-CROWD_DEPTH - 6, -CROWD_DEPTH - 6, W + 2*CROWD_DEPTH + 12, CROWD_DEPTH + 6);
  ctx.fillRect(-CROWD_DEPTH - 6, H, W + 2*CROWD_DEPTH + 12, CROWD_DEPTH + 6);
  ctx.fillRect(-CROWD_DEPTH - 6, -CROWD_DEPTH - 6, CROWD_DEPTH + 6, H + 2*CROWD_DEPTH + 12);
  ctx.fillRect(W, -CROWD_DEPTH - 6, CROWD_DEPTH + 6, H + 2*CROWD_DEPTH + 12);

  for (const f of fans) {
    // Bobbing — gentle sine wave
    let bob = Math.sin(t * 0.07 + f.phase) * 1.2;
    // Cheer boost: fans within cheer radius bounce harder for ~30 frames
    if (cheerActive) {
      const dx = f.x - cheer.x, dy = f.y - cheer.y;
      const d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
      if (d2 < cheer.radius * cheer.radius) {
        const cheerProgress = (t - cheer.frame) / 30;
        const cheerStrength = (1 - cheerProgress) * 4;
        bob -= Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 0.5 + f.phase * 3)) * cheerStrength;
      }
    }
    const fy = f.y + bob;
    // Body — 2px wide rectangle
    ctx.fillStyle = f.palette[0];
    ctx.fillRect(f.x - 1, fy + 1, 2, 4);
    // Head — 2x2 dot
    ctx.fillStyle = f.palette[1];
    ctx.fillRect(f.x - 1, fy - 2, 2, 2);
  }
}

function drawScenery() {
  for (const s of SCENERY) {
    if (s.type === "pillar") {
      // Stone pillar with shadow
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(s.x + 2, s.y + 14, 8, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "#3a4555";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 6, s.y - 12, 12, 24);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#4d5a6c";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 6, s.y - 12, 12, 3);
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 6, s.y + 9,  12, 3);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#2a323d";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 7, s.y - 14, 14, 2);
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 7, s.y + 12, 14, 2);
    } else if (s.type === "banner") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#2a323d";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 1, s.y - 14, 2, 28);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7a2828";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 8, s.y - 8, 16, 12);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#f7c948";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 2, s.y - 4, 4, 4);
    } else if (s.type === "rubble") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#2a323d";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 4, s.y, 8, 4);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#3a4555";
      ctx.fillRect(s.x - 6, s.y + 2, 4, 3);
      ctx.fillRect(s.x + 2, s.y + 1, 4, 3);
    }
  }
}

function drawDrops(m) {
  for (const d of m.drops) {
    const bob = Math.sin((m.frame + d.x) * 0.12) * 1.5;
    const legendary = (d.gear.scavengedCount || 0) >= 3;

    if (d.slot === "health") {
      // Pulsing red cross — visually distinct from gear drops
      const pulse = 0.7 + 0.3 * Math.sin(m.frame * 0.18 + d.x);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(239,77,77,${0.25 * pulse})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(d.x, d.y + bob, 11, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      // White cross plate
      ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
      ctx.fillRect(d.x - 6, d.y - 6 + bob, 12, 12);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ef4d4d";
      ctx.fillRect(d.x - 5, d.y - 1 + bob, 10, 2); // horizontal bar
      ctx.fillRect(d.x - 1, d.y - 5 + bob, 2, 10); // vertical bar
      continue;
    }

    // Gear drops (weapon/armor/item)
    ctx.fillStyle = legendary ? "rgba(180,109,240,0.35)" : "rgba(247,201,72,0.2)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(d.x, d.y + bob, legendary ? 11 : 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    const bgColor =
      d.slot === "weapon" ? "#5a3030" :
      d.slot === "armor"  ? "#304a5a" : "#3a5a30";
    ctx.fillStyle = bgColor;
    ctx.fillRect(d.x - 5, d.y - 5 + bob, 10, 10);
    if (legendary) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#b46df0";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(d.x - 6, d.y - 6 + bob, 12, 12);
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
    ctx.font = "9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText(d.gear.ico || "?", d.x, d.y + bob);
    ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
  }
}

function drawProjectiles(m) {
  if (!m.projectiles) return;
  for (const pr of m.projectiles) {
    const team = TEAM_BY_ID[pr.teamId];
    // === v28.5 grenade-in-flight — tumbling dark sphere + smoke trail ===
    if (pr.isGrenade) {
      const f = m.frame || 0;
      // Smoke trail — three fading puffs behind the grenade
      for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
        const tx = pr.x - pr.vx * i * 1.5;
        const ty = pr.y - pr.vy * i * 1.5;
        const alpha = (4 - i) / 8;
        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(160,160,160,${alpha})`;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(tx, ty, 2 + i * 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      // Grenade body — small tumbling oval with team-color fuse spark
      ctx.fillStyle = "#1a1a1a";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(pr.x, pr.y, 3.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = "#3a3a3a";
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - 2, pr.y - 2, 4, 4);
      // Fuse spark — pulses with team color
      const sparkOn = (f % 4) < 2;
      if (sparkOn) {
        ctx.fillStyle = team.color;
        ctx.fillRect(pr.x - 1, pr.y - 5, 2, 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
        ctx.fillRect(pr.x, pr.y - 4, 1, 1);
      }
      continue;
    }
    const isRailgun = pr.wpn?.id === "railgun";
    if (isRailgun) {
      // Long bright railgun streak — multi-segment trail with team color
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,0.65)`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(pr.x - pr.vx * 4.0, pr.y - pr.vy * 4.0);
      ctx.lineTo(pr.x, pr.y);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Team-colored outer glow
      ctx.strokeStyle = team.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(pr.x - pr.vx * 3.0, pr.y - pr.vy * 3.0);
      ctx.lineTo(pr.x, pr.y);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Bright head
      ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - 1, pr.y - 1, 3, 3);
    } else {
      // Laser pistol — fading 4-segment trail
      const tx = pr.vx, ty = pr.vy;
      // Outer dim halo
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(239,77,77,0.18)`;
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - tx * 2.4 - 2, pr.y - ty * 2.4 - 2, 4, 4);
      // Mid trail
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(239,77,77,0.45)`;
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - tx * 1.4 - 1, pr.y - ty * 1.4 - 1, 3, 3);
      // Bright body
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ef4d4d";
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - tx * 0.6 - 1, pr.y - ty * 0.6 - 1, 3, 3);
      // White-hot core
      ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
      ctx.fillRect(pr.x - 1, pr.y - 1, 2, 2);
    }
  }
}

/* === v28.5: lasting explosion effect ===
   Animates expanding shockwave + smoke billows for grenade detonations.
   Each explosion has frame (when it spawned) and maxFrames (~28). The
   normalized t = (now-frame)/maxFrames runs 0→1 across the lifetime.
   Renders three layered passes:
     1. Bright orange core shrinking + fading
     2. Outer shockwave ring expanding to full blast radius
     3. Smoke puffs at random angles, drifting outward */
function drawExplosions(m) {
  if (!m.explosions || !m.explosions.length) return;
  const now = m.frame || 0;
  for (let i = m.explosions.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const ex = m.explosions[i];
    const age = now - ex.frame;
    if (age > ex.maxFrames) {
      m.explosions.splice(i, 1);
      continue;
    }
    const t = age / ex.maxFrames;  // 0..1

    // Bright orange-red core (shrinks + fades)
    const coreR = ex.radius * 0.35 * (1 - t * 0.6);
    const coreAlpha = 0.85 * (1 - t);
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255, 180, 60, ${coreAlpha})`;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ex.x, ex.y, coreR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255, 240, 200, ${coreAlpha * 0.7})`;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ex.x, ex.y, coreR * 0.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // Outer shockwave ring (expands to full radius)
    const ringR = ex.radius * (0.4 + 0.6 * t);
    const ringAlpha = 0.65 * (1 - t);
    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255, 120, 40, ${ringAlpha})`;
    ctx.lineWidth = 3 * (1 - t * 0.5);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ex.x, ex.y, ringR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();

    // Smoke puffs — 6 fixed-angle puffs drifting outward
    if (!ex._smokeSeed) ex._smokeSeed = (ex.x * 31 + ex.y * 17) | 0;
    const numPuffs = 6;
    for (let k = 0; k < numPuffs; k++) {
      const ang = (k / numPuffs) * Math.PI * 2 + (ex._smokeSeed * 0.001);
      const drift = ex.radius * 0.6 * t;
      const px = ex.x + Math.cos(ang) * drift;
      const py = ex.y + Math.sin(ang) * drift - t * 4;  // slight rise
      const pR = ex.radius * 0.18 * (0.5 + t * 0.7);
      const pAlpha = 0.5 * (1 - t * 0.85);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(110, 100, 95, ${pAlpha})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(px, py, pR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }
}

function drawTerrain(m) {
  if (!m.terrain) return;
  for (const t of m.terrain) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.5)";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x + 2, t.y + 2, t.size, t.size);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#3a4555";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y, t.size, t.size);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#5a6678";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y, t.size, 4);
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y, 4, t.size);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#2a323d";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x + t.size - 4, t.y, 4, t.size);
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y + t.size - 4, t.size, 4);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#1f2630";
    ctx.fillRect(t.x + t.size / 2 - 1, t.y + 4, 2, t.size - 8);
    ctx.fillRect(t.x + 4, t.y + t.size / 2 - 1, t.size - 8, 2);
  }
}

function drawPowerCore(m) {
  if (!m.powerCore || m.powerCore.state !== "active") return;
  const pc = m.powerCore;
  const t = m.frame;
  const pulse = 0.7 + 0.3 * Math.sin(t * 0.15);
  const grd = ctx.createRadialGradient(pc.x, pc.y, 4, pc.x, pc.y, 30);
  grd.addColorStop(0, `rgba(247,201,72,${0.55 * pulse})`);
  grd.addColorStop(1, "rgba(247,201,72,0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = grd;
  ctx.fillRect(pc.x - 30, pc.y - 30, 60, 60);
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    const ang = (t * 0.05) + (i * Math.PI / 2);
    const x1 = pc.x + Math.cos(ang) * 8, y1 = pc.y + Math.sin(ang) * 8;
    const x2 = pc.x + Math.cos(ang) * 14, y2 = pc.y + Math.sin(ang) * 14;
    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(247,201,72,${pulse})`;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
    ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(pc.x, pc.y, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(247,201,72,${pulse})`;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(pc.x, pc.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(247,201,72,0.9)";
  ctx.font = "bold 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
  ctx.fillText("⚡POWER", pc.x, pc.y - 22);
  ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
}

/* ============================================================
   GAME MODE ENTITIES — crown (KOTH), flag + bases (CTF), tag It
   indicator. Drawn between drops and players in drawArena, so a
   carrier's player avatar appears on top of their crown/flag.
   ============================================================ */
function drawModeEntities(m) {
  if (!m) return;
  const mode = m.gameMode || "deathmatch";
  const t = m.frame;

  // ----- KOTH crown -----
  if (mode === "koth" && m.crown) {
    const c = m.crown;
    const pulse = 0.75 + 0.25 * Math.sin(t * 0.12);
    // Glow halo
    const grd = ctx.createRadialGradient(c.x, c.y, 4, c.x, c.y, 28);
    grd.addColorStop(0, `rgba(247,201,72,${0.55 * pulse})`);
    grd.addColorStop(1, "rgba(247,201,72,0)");
    ctx.fillStyle = grd;
    ctx.fillRect(c.x - 28, c.y - 28, 56, 56);
    // Crown silhouette — simple pixel-art crown
    const cx = Math.floor(c.x), cy = Math.floor(c.y);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#f7c948";
    // Base band
    ctx.fillRect(cx - 8, cy + 2, 16, 4);
    // Three points
    ctx.fillRect(cx - 8, cy - 3, 4, 5);
    ctx.fillRect(cx - 2, cy - 5, 4, 7);
    ctx.fillRect(cx + 4, cy - 3, 4, 5);
    // Jewels
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ef4d4d";
    ctx.fillRect(cx - 1, cy - 3, 2, 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#88c0d0";
    ctx.fillRect(cx - 7, cy - 1, 2, 2);
    ctx.fillRect(cx + 5, cy - 1, 2, 2);
    // "👑" label if no carrier
    if (!c.carrierId) {
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(247,201,72,${pulse})`;
      ctx.font = "bold 9px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText("CROWN", c.x, c.y - 18);
      ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    }
  }

  // ----- CTF flag -----
  if (mode === "ctf" && m.flag) {
    const f = m.flag;
    const fx = Math.floor(f.x), fy = Math.floor(f.y);
    // Pole
    ctx.fillStyle = "#3a2a1a";
    ctx.fillRect(fx, fy - 16, 2, 20);
    // Flag (color matches carrier team if held, otherwise white)
    let flagColor = "#ffffff";
    if (f.carrierTeamId) {
      flagColor = (TEAM_BY_ID[f.carrierTeamId] && TEAM_BY_ID[f.carrierTeamId].color) || "#ffffff";
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = flagColor;
    // Triangular flag waving
    const wave = Math.sin(t * 0.18) * 1.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(fx + 2, fy - 16);
    ctx.lineTo(fx + 14 + wave, fy - 13);
    ctx.lineTo(fx + 2, fy - 10);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
    // Soft glow
    if (!f.carrierId) {
      const grd = ctx.createRadialGradient(fx + 2, fy - 4, 4, fx + 2, fy - 4, 24);
      grd.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,0.30)");
      grd.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,255,255,0)");
      ctx.fillStyle = grd;
      ctx.fillRect(fx - 22, fy - 26, 48, 32);
    }
  }

  // ----- CTF bases -----
  if (mode === "ctf" && m.basesById) {
    for (const teamId in m.basesById) {
      const b = m.basesById[teamId];
      const team = TEAM_BY_ID[teamId];
      const color = (team && team.color) || "#888";
      // Base pad — colored ring on the floor
      const pulse2 = 0.6 + 0.2 * Math.sin(t * 0.08 + (teamId === m.teamAId ? 0 : Math.PI));
      ctx.strokeStyle = color;
      ctx.globalAlpha = pulse2;
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 24, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      // Inner cross-hatch
      ctx.fillStyle = color;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.20;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 22, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      // Team logo overlay
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.85)";
      ctx.font = "bold 14px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji'";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText(team ? team.emoji : "?", b.x, b.y);
      ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    }
  }

  // ----- TAG "It" indicator -----
  if (mode === "tag" && m.tag && m.tag.itId) {
    const it = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].find(p => p.id === m.tag.itId && p.alive);
    if (it) {
      // Halo on the It bot
      const pulse3 = 0.55 + 0.45 * Math.sin(t * 0.18);
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(239,77,77,${pulse3})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(it.x, it.y, 30, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      // "IT" label above
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(239,77,77,${pulse3})`;
      ctx.font = "bold 10px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText("⚡ IT", it.x, it.y - 56);
      ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    }
    // Frozen indicators on tagged bots
    for (const id in m.tag.tagFreezes) {
      const target = [...m.teamAplayers, ...m.teamBplayers].find(p => p.id === Number(id) && p.alive);
      if (!target) continue;
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(136,192,208,0.9)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(target.x, target.y, 24, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Snowflake symbol above
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(136,192,208,0.9)";
      ctx.font = "bold 12px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText("❄", target.x, target.y - 40);
      ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   CANVAS RENDERER
   Pixel-art arena: turf, scenery, players (team-colored knights
   with balloon HP), blood, corpses, drops, floating dmg numbers.
   ============================================================ */
function drawArena() {
  const W = arenaCanvas.width, H = arenaCanvas.height;
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const biome = m && m.biome ? m.biome : null;

  // Update camera target + lerp toward it. Camera tracks all alive
  // players + objectives so the broadcast view stays focused on action.
  _updateCamera(m);

  // Clear & paint background (letterbox bars when zoom > 1)
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle = "#000";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.restore();

  // World-space drawing happens inside the camera transform. We save,
  // apply translate+scale, draw everything, then restore. HUD/overlays
  // (TV bug, kill feed, vignette) draw outside this block so they stay
  // viewport-locked.
  ctx.save();
  _applyCameraTransform();

  // === FLOOR === Biome-driven base + secondary gradient
  const topColor = biome ? biome.floorTop : "#0d1018";
  const botColor = biome ? biome.floorBot : "#1a232b";
  const floorGrd = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
  floorGrd.addColorStop(0, topColor);
  floorGrd.addColorStop(0.4, _blend(topColor, botColor, 0.5));
  floorGrd.addColorStop(1, botColor);
  ctx.fillStyle = floorGrd;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // Mowing stripes — alternating bands across the field, classic stadium
  // pitch look. The stripeHue color is layered on top so it works against
  // any base. 60px-wide stripes feel right for the 1600x720 arena.
  if (biome) {
    const stripeW = 60;
    ctx.fillStyle = biome.stripeHue;
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x += stripeW * 2) {
      ctx.fillRect(x, 0, stripeW, H);
    }
  }

  // Team-colored end zones — left third tinted teamA color, right third teamB
  if (m && m.teamAId && m.teamBId) {
    const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId];
    if (ta) {
      const lg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W * 0.3, 0);
      lg.addColorStop(0, _hexA(ta.color, 0.18));
      lg.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
      ctx.fillStyle = lg;
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W * 0.3, H);
    }
    if (tb) {
      const rg = ctx.createLinearGradient(W * 0.7, 0, W, 0);
      rg.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
      rg.addColorStop(1, _hexA(tb.color, 0.18));
      ctx.fillStyle = rg;
      ctx.fillRect(W * 0.7, 0, W * 0.3, H);
    }
  }

  // === DECORATIVE SCATTER === drawn on top of the floor, beneath everything
  // else. Each sprite is a tiny shape using the biome's palette.
  if (m && m.scatter) {
    drawBiomeScatter(m.scatter);
  }

  // Field lines — biome-aware accent color
  const lineColor = biome ? biome.accents.lineColor : "rgba(247,201,72,0.06)";
  const brandColor = biome ? biome.accents.brandColor : "rgba(247,201,72,0.12)";

  // === FIELD MARKINGS ===
  // For sport-court biomes (tennis, basketball, etc), draw court-specific
  // line geometry via drawCourtMarkings. Otherwise draw the generic
  // mowing-stripe grid + center circle + penalty arcs.
  if (biome && biome.courtType) {
    drawCourtMarkings(W, H, biome.courtType, lineColor);
    // Brand mark + team mottos still go on top
    if (m && m.teamAId && m.teamBId) {
      const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId];
      if (ta) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(ta.color, 0.22);
        ctx.font = "bold 18px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.translate(110, H/2);
        ctx.rotate(-Math.PI/2);
        ctx.fillText((ta.short || "").toUpperCase(), 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      if (tb) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(tb.color, 0.22);
        ctx.font = "bold 18px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.translate(W - 110, H/2);
        ctx.rotate(Math.PI/2);
        ctx.fillText((tb.short || "").toUpperCase(), 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = brandColor;
    ctx.font = "bold 38px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText("FK", W/2, H/2);
    ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
  } else {
    // Standard arena field markings (the v21 layout)
