    // Subtle grid (much fainter when biome has decorations)
    ctx.strokeStyle = biome ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" : "rgba(247,201,72,0.06)";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < W; i += 60) {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(i, 0); ctx.lineTo(i, H); ctx.stroke();
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < H; i += 60) {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, i); ctx.lineTo(W, i); ctx.stroke();
    }

    ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(W/2, 0); ctx.lineTo(W/2, H); ctx.stroke();

    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W/2, H/2, 70, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = brandColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W/2, H/2, 55, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();

    ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(0, H/2, 90, -Math.PI/2, Math.PI/2);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(W, H/2, 90, Math.PI/2, -Math.PI/2);
    ctx.stroke();

    if (m && m.teamAId && m.teamBId) {
      const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId];
      if (ta) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(ta.color, 0.22);
        ctx.font = "bold 18px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.translate(110, H/2);
        ctx.rotate(-Math.PI/2);
        ctx.fillText((ta.short || "").toUpperCase(), 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }
      if (tb) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = _hexA(tb.color, 0.22);
        ctx.font = "bold 18px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctx.translate(W - 110, H/2);
        ctx.rotate(Math.PI/2);
        ctx.fillText((tb.short || "").toUpperCase(), 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = brandColor;
    ctx.font = "bold 38px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText("FK", W/2, H/2);
    ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
  }

  // === CLOUD SHADOWS === drift across the floor, beneath players. Subtle
  // soft-edged dark patches that move slowly. Drawn before the vignette so
  // they don't interfere with the broadcast lighting feel.
  // Skipped when the user has disabled field FX.
  const fxOff = LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableFieldFx;
  const lightOff = LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableLighting;
  if (m && m.clouds && !fxOff) {
    drawCloudShadows(m.clouds);
  }

  // === AMBIENT PARTICLES === biome-specific airborne effect (snow, embers,
  // petals, shimmer). Drawn after clouds so they appear in front of the
  // shadows, before the vignette so the broadcast lighting still rims them.
  if (m && m.ambient && m.ambient.length && !fxOff) {
    drawAmbientParticles(m.ambient);
  }

  // Sky tint vignette around edges (broadcast lighting). Biome controls
  // the warmth of the corners — orange for desert, blue for ice, etc.
  // Skipped when the user has disabled lighting.
  if (!lightOff) {
    const vignetteColor = biome ? biome.skyTint : "rgba(0,0,0,0.4)";
    const vg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W/2, H/2, Math.min(W,H) * 0.4, W/2, H/2, Math.max(W,H) * 0.7);
    vg.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
    vg.addColorStop(1, vignetteColor);
    ctx.fillStyle = vg;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  }

  // === STADIUM PITCH LIGHTS ===
  // Soft warm flares in each corner — sells the broadcast-stadium fiction.
  // Drawn AFTER the vignette so the lights cut through the dark corners.
  // Each is a radial gradient from warm white at the corner to transparent.
  if (!lightOff) {
    drawPitchLights(W, H);
  }

  // === v28.4: stadium spectators ===
  // Drawn AFTER the floor + lights but BEFORE the in-field scenery so
  // the crowd sits "outside" the playable area visually. The crowd
  // uses world-space coords (negative for top, beyond W for right) so
  // they're brought into view by the camera-fill scaling in TV mode.
  if (typeof drawCrowd === "function") drawCrowd(LEAGUE.match);

  drawScenery();

  if (!LEAGUE.match) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#5a6675";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.fillText("Awaiting next match…", W/2, H/2 - 100);
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.restore();  // close camera transform on early return
    return;
  }

  // Blood spatter (under players)
  for (const b of m.blood) {
    ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
    ctx.fillRect(Math.floor(b.x), Math.floor(b.y), 2, 2);
  }
  // Corpse markers
  for (const c of m.corpses) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#2a2a2a";
    ctx.fillRect(c.x - 5, c.y - 2, 10, 4);
    ctx.fillStyle = c.color;
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.4;
    ctx.fillRect(c.x - 6, c.y - 6, 12, 12);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#000";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(c.x - 4, c.y - 4); ctx.lineTo(c.x + 4, c.y + 4);
    ctx.moveTo(c.x + 4, c.y - 4); ctx.lineTo(c.x - 4, c.y + 4);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // Terrain obstacles (drawn over corpses but under drops/players;
  // mascots float OVER them via balloons but bodies clip on the side)
  drawTerrain(m);

  // Power Core (centerpiece objective)
  drawPowerCore(m);

  // Drops (gear lying on the field)
  drawDrops(m);

  // Mode-specific entities (crown for KOTH, flag + bases for CTF, etc.)
  // Drawn before players so a carrier's avatar appears on top.
  drawModeEntities(m);

  // Players — pixel-art mascot knights
  for (const list of [m.teamAplayers, m.teamBplayers]) {
    for (const p of list) {
      if (!p.alive) continue;
      drawMascot(p);
    }
  }

  // Projectiles (drawn over players so they're visible)
  drawProjectiles(m);

  // === v28.5 explosion rendering ===
  // Iterate m.explosions, animate expanding ring + smoke, prune expired.
  if (m.explosions && m.explosions.length) {
    const f = m.frame || 0;
    for (let i = m.explosions.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const ex = m.explosions[i];
      const age = f - ex.frame;
      if (age >= ex.maxFrames) { m.explosions.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      const t = age / ex.maxFrames;  // 0 -> 1
      const r = ex.radius * (0.3 + 0.7 * t);
      // Outer shockwave ring
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255, 180, 60, ${1 - t})`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(ex.x, ex.y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      // Inner bright disk fades faster
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255, 230, 130, ${Math.max(0, 0.7 - t * 1.5)})`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(ex.x, ex.y, r * 0.55, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      // Smoke puffs — three around the blast, each lagging the ring slightly
      for (let s = 0; s < 5; s++) {
        const pAng = (s / 5) * Math.PI * 2 + age * 0.04;
        const pR = r * 0.85;
        const px = ex.x + Math.cos(pAng) * pR;
        const py = ex.y + Math.sin(pAng) * pR;
        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(80, 80, 80, ${Math.max(0, 0.5 - t * 0.6)})`;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(px, py, 5 + t * 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  }

  // Floating damage numbers / status pings
  for (const f of m.floats) {
    ctx.fillStyle = f.color;
    ctx.font = "bold 11px 'Courier New', monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(f.life / 22, 0, 1);
    ctx.fillText(f.text, f.x, f.y);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  // End of world-space drawing — restore so HUD/overlays draw in
  // unscaled screen coordinates.
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawMascot(p) {
  const team = TEAM_BY_ID[p.teamId];
  const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
  // Float bob — gentle vertical motion to sell the balloons-on-back fiction
  const t = (LEAGUE.match?.frame || 0);
  const bob = Math.sin((t + p.id * 7) * 0.08) * 1.5;
  const x = Math.floor(p.x);
  const y = Math.floor(p.y + bob);

  // Determine sprite state — driven by the player's current condition.
  // We cache a separate avatar variant per state so transitions look
  // distinct (closed eyes when stunned, wide pupils when on last balloon).
  let state = "alive";
  if (p.stunned > 0) state = "stunned";
  else if ((p.balloonsPopped || 0) >= 2) state = "critical";

  // ----- Drop shadow (on the ground, doesn't bob, soft edged) -----
  // Two-layer: a wider soft halo + tighter inner shadow gives the impression
  // the mascot is genuinely floating above the field. Scaled up to match
  // the ~2x larger sprite footprint.
  if (!LEAGUE_CONFIG.disableLighting) {
    const shadowY = Math.floor(p.y) + 22;
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.18)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x + 1, shadowY, 22, 7, 0, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.45)";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(x, shadowY, 16, 5, 0, 0, Math.PI*2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  // ----- Balloons (HP) attached to the helmet, popping at 2/3 and 1/3 -----
  // Larger and positioned higher to clear the bigger sprite. Same physics
  // as before — three balloons, popped[i] hides the i-th one.
  const popped = p.balloonsPopped || 0;
  const balloonY = y - 48;
  const balloonOffsets = [-9, 0, 9];
  ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(220,220,220,0.6)";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    if (i < popped) continue;
    const bx = x + balloonOffsets[i];
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, y - 28);
    ctx.lineTo(bx, balloonY + 5);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    if (i < popped) continue;
    const bx = x + balloonOffsets[i];
    ctx.fillStyle = team.color;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(bx, balloonY, 5, 6.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.45)";
    ctx.fillRect(bx - 1, balloonY - 3, 2, 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = team.color;
    ctx.fillRect(bx, balloonY + 6, 1, 1);
  }

  // ----- Team-color halo behind the avatar -----
  ctx.fillStyle = team.color;
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.28;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(x, y, 22, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

  // ----- Avatar sprite (cached) — drawn centered on the player's anchor.
  // The sprite canvas is AVATAR_W × AVATAR_H; the anchor (where the
  // player's logical position lines up) is at (AVATAR_W/2, AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y).
  const sprite = (typeof AVATAR !== "undefined") ? AVATAR.get(p, state) : null;
  if (sprite) {
    // Mirror horizontally based on facing — use save/restore + scale(-1, 1)
    if (p.facing < 0) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(x, 0);
      ctx.scale(-1, 1);
      ctx.drawImage(sprite, -AVATAR_W / 2, y - AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y);
      ctx.restore();
    } else {
      ctx.drawImage(sprite, x - AVATAR_W / 2, y - AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y);
    }
  } else {
    // Fallback (shouldn't happen in a real browser): emoji rendering, kept
    // here so the function still draws SOMETHING if the avatar canvas fails.
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji'";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText("🤖", x, y + 3);
    ctx.font = "14px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji'";
    ctx.fillText(team.emoji, x, y - 9);
  }

  // ----- Weapon icon (held in hand, follows facing) -----
  const wxSign = p.facing >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
  const wpnIco = p.weapon?.ico;
  if (wpnIco) {
    ctx.font = "14px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji'";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText(wpnIco, x + 18 * wxSign, y + 4);
  }

  // ----- AI state badge (subtle, only for non-default states) -----
  if (p.aiState && p.aiState !== "ENGAGE") {
    const badge =
      p.aiState === "FLEE"       ? "🏃" :
      p.aiState === "SEEK_ITEM"  ? "🎒" :
      p.aiState === "FINISH_OFF" ? "🎯" : "";
    if (badge) {
      ctx.font = "11px ui-sans-serif, system-ui, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji'";
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
      ctx.fillText(badge, x + 18, y - 22);
    }
  }

  // ----- Stunned spark (top-of-head pulse) -----
  if (p.stunned > 0) {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#88c0d0";
    ctx.font = "bold 14px 'Courier New', monospace";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.fillText("⚡", x, y - 38);
  }

  // ----- Boost glow ring -----
  if (p.boostMods) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(247,201,72,0.7)";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, 26, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // ----- Attack flicker (white outline pulse just after firing) -----
  if (p.attackCooldown > 12) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.5)";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.strokeRect(x - 16, y - 18, 32, 36);
  }

  ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
}


function lighten(hex, amt) {
  const c = hex.replace("#", "");
  const r = parseInt(c.substr(0,2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(c.substr(2,2), 16);
  const b = parseInt(c.substr(4,2), 16);
  const f = (v) => clamp(Math.round(v + (255 - v) * amt), 0, 255);
  return `rgb(${f(r)}, ${f(g)}, ${f(b)})`;
}

function darken(hex, amt) {
  const c = hex.replace("#", "");
  const r = parseInt(c.substr(0,2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(c.substr(2,2), 16);
  const b = parseInt(c.substr(4,2), 16);
  const f = (v) => clamp(Math.round(v * (1 - amt)), 0, 255);
  return `rgb(${f(r)}, ${f(g)}, ${f(b)})`;
}

/* ============================================================
   AVATAR SYSTEM — flat-avatar-plugin-style layered mascots.

   The Pablo Stanley flat-avatar approach: compose a character from
   stacked layers (body / face / hair / accessories). For Float
   Knights the equivalent layering is:

       shadow → body+armor → cape (sniper only) → head+ears →
       face markings → eyes → mouth/beak → helmet → number badge

   Implementation:
   - Each (teamId, positionId, state) tuple produces one cached
     offscreen canvas via _renderAvatarTo(). The cache key is a
     simple pipe-delimited string. State ∈ {alive, stunned, critical}.
   - Players within the same team+position share the cached sprite.
     This keeps the cache small (~84 base avatars × 3 states ≈ 252
     canvases worst case) while still letting individuals look
     correct for their archetype.
   - During the live render (drawMascot), we compute the right
     state, look up or lazy-build the sprite, and drawImage it
     centered at (x, y). The previous balloons/halo/state-badges/
     position-color treatments still wrap the sprite, just rescaled
     to the new larger footprint.

   Avatar canvas dimensions: 56x64 logical pixels, drawn 1:1 onto
   the arena (so a player occupies ~56x64 of the 1280x720 logical
   field — about 3x the previous ~16x18 footprint, dramatically
   improving in-game readability).
   ============================================================ */

const AVATAR_W = 56;
const AVATAR_H = 64;
// Center of the rendered head/body — the sprite is anchored at its
// horizontal middle and at the body's vertical center (so the player
// position (p.x, p.y) lines up with the chest).
const AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y = 38; // y within the canvas that the player's (x, y) maps to

const AVATAR = {
  _cache: Object.create(null),

  /**
   * Get a sprite canvas for this player+state, lazily rendering on
   * first request. Returns the offscreen HTMLCanvasElement.
   * Returns null if the environment doesn't support full canvas
   * drawing (e.g. node smoke harness with mock contexts).
   */
  get(player, state) {
    const teamId = player.teamId;
    const posId = player.position;
    const key = teamId + "|" + posId + "|" + state;
    let cached = this._cache[key];
    if (cached !== undefined) return cached;  // cache miss returns null too
    if (typeof document === "undefined") {
      this._cache[key] = null; return null;
    }
    const can = document.createElement("canvas");
    can.width = AVATAR_W;
    can.height = AVATAR_H;
    const cx = (typeof can.getContext === "function") ? can.getContext("2d") : null;
    // Verify all methods we use exist before drawing — some test
    // environments stub createElement to return mocks with partial APIs.
    if (!cx ||
        typeof cx.fillRect          !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.beginPath         !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.ellipse           !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.arc               !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.fill              !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.stroke            !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.moveTo            !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.lineTo            !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.quadraticCurveTo  !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.closePath         !== "function" ||
        typeof cx.clearRect         !== "function") {
      this._cache[key] = null; return null;
    }
    _renderAvatarTo(cx, teamId, posId, state);
    this._cache[key] = can;
    return can;
  },

  clearCache() { this._cache = Object.create(null); },
};

/* ============================================================
   ANIMAL PALETTES — fur, accent, eye, nose colors per team.
   Each palette describes the species, not the team uniform; the
   uniform color comes from team.color and is layered on top.
   ============================================================ */
const ANIMAL_PALETTE = {

  bvr: { fur: "#8a5a3c", belly: "#c89a72", accent: "#5d3a22", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#a87850", tooth: "#fefcf0" },
  owl: { fur: "#b89570", belly: "#d8c098", accent: "#7a5a3c", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#5c4525", iris: "#f4c542" },

  qtz: { fur: "#2ea868", belly: "#c84050", accent: "#1a4a28", nose: "#1a1a1a", eye: "#1a1a1a", inner: "#3ad080", beak: "#f4c542", tail: "#80f0c0" },

/* ============================================================
   COMPOSITION — assemble layers onto the offscreen canvas.
   ============================================================ */
function _renderAvatarTo(cx, teamId, posId, state) {
  const team = (typeof TEAM_BY_ID !== "undefined") ? TEAM_BY_ID[teamId] : null;
  const pal = ANIMAL_PALETTE[teamId] || ANIMAL_PALETTE.fox;
  const teamColor = (team && team.color) || "#888888";

  // Body geometry — torso roughly centered, head sits above
  const bx = AVATAR_W / 2;       // body center x
  const by = AVATAR_ANCHOR_Y;    // body center y (= player anchor)
  const hx = bx;                 // head center x
  const hy = by - 16;            // head center y

  cx.clearRect(0, 0, AVATAR_W, AVATAR_H);
  cx.lineJoin = "round";
  cx.lineCap = "round";

  // 1. Soft drop shadow under the body (very subtle; main shadow is
  //    drawn by drawMascot at the world level).
  cx.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.18)";
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(bx, by + 18, 14, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();

  // 2. Cape (sniper only) — drawn behind the body so it hangs back
  if (posId === "sniper") {
    _drawCape(cx, bx, by, teamColor, pal);
  }

  // 3. Body silhouette + armor — varies by position
  _drawBodyArmor(cx, bx, by, teamColor, pal, posId);

  // 4. Head — animal-specific silhouette, ears, fur
  _drawAnimalHead(cx, hx, hy, teamId, pal);

  // 5. Face — eyes + mouth + accents, varies with state
  _drawAnimalFace(cx, hx, hy, teamId, pal, state);

  // 6. Helmet/hood — overlay drawn on top of head for tank/sniper/speed
  if (posId === "tank") {
    _drawTankHelmet(cx, hx, hy, teamColor);
  } else if (posId === "sniper") {
    _drawSniperHood(cx, hx, hy, teamColor, pal);
  } else if (posId === "speed") {
    _drawSpeedHood(cx, hx, hy, teamColor);
  }

  // 7. Position emblem on the chest (a tiny role glyph)
  _drawChestEmblem(cx, bx, by + 2, posId, teamColor);
}

/* ============================================================
   BODY + ARMOR per position. All bodies use a rounded torso
   silhouette with team-color armor; position-specific accents
   differentiate the seven classes.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawBodyArmor(cx, bx, by, teamColor, pal, posId) {
  const armor = teamColor;
  const armorDark = darken(teamColor, 0.30);
  const armorLite = lighten(teamColor, 0.25);

  // Position-driven width adjustments
  const isHeavy = posId === "tank" || posId === "brawl";
  const isLight = posId === "speed";
  const torsoW = isHeavy ? 18 : isLight ? 13 : 15;
  const torsoH = 14;

  // Main torso plate
  cx.fillStyle = armor;
  cx.beginPath();
  // rounded-rectangle torso
  const tx = bx - torsoW, ty = by - torsoH/2;
  const r = 4;
  cx.moveTo(tx + r, ty);
  cx.lineTo(tx + 2*torsoW - r, ty);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(tx + 2*torsoW, ty, tx + 2*torsoW, ty + r);
  cx.lineTo(tx + 2*torsoW, ty + torsoH - r);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(tx + 2*torsoW, ty + torsoH, tx + 2*torsoW - r, ty + torsoH);
  cx.lineTo(tx + r, ty + torsoH);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(tx, ty + torsoH, tx, ty + torsoH - r);
  cx.lineTo(tx, ty + r);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(tx, ty, tx + r, ty);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();

  // Lower plate trim (darker accent strip)
  cx.fillStyle = armorDark;
  cx.fillRect(tx, ty + torsoH - 3, torsoW * 2, 3);

  // Position-specific overlays
  if (posId === "tank") {
    // Heavy shoulder pads — rounded cubes either side
    cx.fillStyle = armorDark;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(bx - torsoW + 1, ty + 2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.arc(bx + torsoW - 1, ty + 2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.fill();
    // Center plate band
    cx.fillStyle = armorLite;
    cx.fillRect(bx - 6, ty + 2, 12, 2);
  } else if (posId === "striker") {
    // Sharp pauldron spikes
    cx.fillStyle = armorDark;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(bx - torsoW, ty + 1);
    cx.lineTo(bx - torsoW - 3, ty + 6);
    cx.lineTo(bx - torsoW + 1, ty + 5);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.moveTo(bx + torsoW, ty + 1);
    cx.lineTo(bx + torsoW + 3, ty + 6);
    cx.lineTo(bx + torsoW - 1, ty + 5);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();
  } else if (posId === "speed") {
    // Slim shoulder seams + chevron
    cx.strokeStyle = armorLite;
    cx.lineWidth = 1;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(bx - 5, ty + 8);
    cx.lineTo(bx, ty + 5);
    cx.lineTo(bx + 5, ty + 8);
    cx.stroke();
  } else if (posId === "brawl") {
    // Heavy bracers/gauntlets at sides (flanking the torso)
    cx.fillStyle = armorDark;
    cx.fillRect(tx - 3, ty + 2, 3, 10);
    cx.fillRect(tx + 2*torsoW, ty + 2, 3, 10);
    cx.fillStyle = armorLite;
    cx.fillRect(tx - 3, ty + 4, 3, 1);
    cx.fillRect(tx + 2*torsoW, ty + 4, 3, 1);
  } else if (posId === "healer") {
    // White cross on the chest — universal medic emblem
    cx.fillStyle = "#fefcf0";
    cx.fillRect(bx - 1, ty + 3, 2, 8);
    cx.fillRect(bx - 4, ty + 6, 8, 2);
  } else if (posId === "grenadier") {
    // Bandolier diagonal across chest with grenade beads
    cx.strokeStyle = armorDark;
    cx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(bx - torsoW + 2, ty + 1);
    cx.lineTo(bx + torsoW - 2, ty + torsoH - 4);
    cx.stroke();
    // Three small grenade beads
    cx.fillStyle = "#3a3c40";
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const t = (i + 1) / 4;
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(bx - torsoW + 2 + (2*torsoW - 4) * t, ty + 1 + (torsoH - 5) * t, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
    }
  }
  // sniper has its own cape drawn earlier; no extra body overlay needed
}

/* ============================================================
   CAPE — drawn behind the body for snipers.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawCape(cx, bx, by, teamColor, pal) {
  const cape = darken(teamColor, 0.45);
  const trim = darken(teamColor, 0.6);
  cx.fillStyle = cape;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(bx - 14, by - 8);
  cx.lineTo(bx - 18, by + 14);
  cx.lineTo(bx, by + 18);
  cx.lineTo(bx + 18, by + 14);
  cx.lineTo(bx + 14, by - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Cape inner shadow center fold
  cx.fillStyle = trim;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(bx, by - 6);
  cx.lineTo(bx - 1, by + 16);
  cx.lineTo(bx + 1, by + 16);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

/* ============================================================
   ANIMAL HEAD — silhouette + ears + species markings.
   Dispatches by teamId; each species draws its distinctive shape.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawAnimalHead(cx, hx, hy, teamId, pal) {
  switch (teamId) {
    case "fox": _headFox(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "bvr": _headBeaver(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "owl": _headOwl(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "shk": _headShark(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "pnd": _headPanda(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "rcc": _headRaccoon(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "frg": _headFrog(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "hed": _headHedgehog(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "kit": _headKit(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "wlf": _headWolf(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "bdg": _headBadger(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "otr": _headOtter(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "tgr": _headTiger(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "plr": _headPolarBear(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "bsn": _headBison(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "rvn": _headRaven(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "lyx": _headLynx(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "mos": _headMoose(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "cyt": _headCoyote(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    case "qtz": _headQuetzal(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
    default:    _headFox(cx, hx, hy, pal); break;
  }
}

// Fox — pointed ears, narrow tapered snout, white chin
function _headFox(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Ears — triangular, tipped darker, pink interior
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 8, hy - 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 11, hy - 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy - 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 11, hy - 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.inner;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 8, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 6, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 6, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — oval, slightly tapered to chin
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy, 9, 8.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // White snout/chin patch
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 3, 5, 4, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Beaver — round head, small rounded ears, big teeth
function _headBeaver(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Round small ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 8, hy - 7, 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 8, hy - 7, 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.inner;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 8, hy - 7, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 8, hy - 7, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Round head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Lighter muzzle area
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 3, 5, 3.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Owl — very round, big yellow-ringed eyes, small ear tufts, beak
function _headOwl(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Tiny ear tufts (peaked)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Round head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Feather mottling — three darker dots
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 4, hy + 5, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 4, hy + 5, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx, hy + 7, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Shark — pointed snout, dorsal fin, white belly underside
function _headShark(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Dorsal fin on top of head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 2, hy - 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy - 14);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — pointed-snout oval
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy, 10, 7.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // White belly underside
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 9, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Pointed snout tip
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy + 1);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 12, hy + 3);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy + 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

// Panda — white face, black eye patches, black ears
function _headPanda(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Black ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 8, hy - 8, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 8, hy - 8, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Round white head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 9.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Black eye patches (oval, tilted slightly)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx - 4, hy - 1, 3, 4, -0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.ellipse(hx + 4, hy - 1, 3, 4, 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Raccoon — black mask stripe across eyes, pointed ears
function _headRaccoon(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Pointed ears with black tips
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 8, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 10, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 4, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 10, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 9, hy - 10, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 9, hy - 10, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Head — slightly triangular
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 9, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Black mask stripe across eyes
  cx.fillStyle = pal.mask;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy - 1, 8, 2.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light snout
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 4, 2.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Frog — bulging eyes ON TOP of head, wide mouth, smooth green
function _headFrog(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Two big bulging eye DOMES on top of head — drawn before head so
  // the head silhouette overlaps slightly at the base.
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 5, hy - 7, 4.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 5, hy - 7, 4.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Head — wide blob, low forehead
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 2, 10, 7, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Lighter belly/chin underside
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 5, 8, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Hedgehog — short spikes radiating from top, pointed snout, pink nose
function _headHedgehog(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Spike crown — multiple short triangles around upper hemisphere
  cx.fillStyle = pal.spike;
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
    const ang = -Math.PI + (i / 8) * Math.PI; // top hemisphere
    const r1 = 8.5, r2 = 12;
    const sx = hx + Math.cos(ang) * r1;
    const sy = hy + Math.sin(ang) * r1;
    const tx = hx + Math.cos(ang) * r2;
    const ty = hy + Math.sin(ang) * r2;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(sx - 1.5, sy + 1);
    cx.lineTo(tx, ty);
    cx.lineTo(sx + 1.5, sy + 1);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();
  }
  // Round head with pointed snout
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 8.5, 7.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light snout
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 4.5, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Kit — triangular ears, pink nose, whiskers
function _headKit(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Triangular ears with pink interior
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 3, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 3, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.nose;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Round head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Whiskers (3 lines per side)
  cx.strokeStyle = pal.whisker;
  cx.lineWidth = 0.6;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const yo = hy + 2 + i * 1.5;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(hx - 4, yo);
    cx.lineTo(hx - 10, yo + (i - 1) * 0.8);
    cx.moveTo(hx + 4, yo);
    cx.lineTo(hx + 10, yo + (i - 1) * 0.8);
    cx.stroke();
  }
}

// Wolf — pointed ears, sharp snout, yellow eyes
function _headWolf(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Pointed ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 10, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 4, hy - 9);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 10, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 9);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle = pal.inner;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — somewhat angular
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy, 9, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light underside
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 6, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Sharp snout extension
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 3, hy + 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 3, hy + 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

// Badger — black with white stripe down center of face
function _headBadger(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Small rounded ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 8, hy - 7, 2.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 8, hy - 7, 2.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Wide low head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 9.5, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // White stripe down center of face (forehead to nose)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.stripe;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 1.5, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 1.5, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 2, hy + 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 2, hy + 6);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

// Otter — rounded head, light belly, whiskers
function _headOtter(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Tiny ears
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 7, hy - 7, 1.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 7, hy - 7, 1.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Rounded head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy, 9, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light belly/chin
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 6, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Whiskers
  cx.strokeStyle = pal.whisker;
  cx.lineWidth = 0.5;
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(hx - 3, hy + 3 + i);
    cx.lineTo(hx - 8, hy + 3 + i + (i - 0.5) * 0.5);
    cx.moveTo(hx + 3, hy + 3 + i);
    cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy + 3 + i + (i - 0.5) * 0.5);
    cx.stroke();
  }
}

// === v28.12: New species head functions ===

// Tiger — rounded orange head with black stripes, prominent ears, white chin
function _headTiger(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Triangular ears with darker tips
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 9, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 11, hy - 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 9, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 11, hy - 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Black ear interiors
  cx.fillStyle = pal.stripe;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 9, hy - 9, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 9, hy - 9, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Round head, slightly broader than fox
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy + 1, 9.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Black stripes — characteristic tiger pattern
  cx.fillStyle = pal.stripe;
  cx.beginPath();
  // Top-of-head stripe pair
  cx.fillRect(hx - 5, hy - 7, 1.4, 3);
  cx.fillRect(hx + 3.5, hy - 7, 1.4, 3);
  // Cheek stripes (curved)
  cx.fillRect(hx - 8, hy - 1, 1.2, 4);
  cx.fillRect(hx + 6.5, hy - 1, 1.2, 4);
  // Forehead stripe
  cx.fillRect(hx - 0.7, hy - 7, 1.4, 4);
  cx.fill();
  // White muzzle/chin patch
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 5, 5, 3.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Polar Bear — big round white head, very small dark eyes + nose, no visible ears
function _headPolarBear(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Subtle ears — small rounded humps on top of head, mostly hidden by head shape
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 7, hy - 7, 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 7, hy - 7, 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Pink ear interiors
  cx.fillStyle = "#f8c8c8";
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 7, hy - 7, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 7, hy - 7, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Large round white head — the biggest head silhouette in the league
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy + 1, 10.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Faint shadow under chin to give the head dimension against white
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 7, 7, 2, 0, 0, Math.PI);
  cx.fill();
  // Long muzzle — slightly cream-colored
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 5.5, 4, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Bison — massive low-slung head with curved horns and chin mane
function _headBison(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Chin mane (drawn first, behind head) — long shaggy fur under jaw
  cx.fillStyle = pal.mane;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy + 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy + 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 4, hy + 10);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy + 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy + 10);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy + 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 7, hy + 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Horns — curved upward+outward, dark
  cx.fillStyle = pal.horn;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 10, hy - 7);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx - 13, hy - 9, hx - 12, hy - 12);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx - 9, hy - 10, hx - 8, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 10, hy - 7);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx + 13, hy - 9, hx + 12, hy - 12);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx + 9, hy - 10, hx + 8, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Massive head — bulkier than other species
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 10, 9, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Top-of-head shaggy tuft (between horns)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.mane;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 3, hy - 6, 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 3, hy - 6, 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx, hy - 7, 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Lighter snout
  cx.fillStyle = pal.inner;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 5, 4.5, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Raven — sleek black head, sharp triangular beak, alert posture
function _headRaven(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Head — slightly elongated forward, sleek
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 9, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Sharp triangular beak — projects forward
  cx.fillStyle = pal.beak;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 1, hy + 3);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 7, hy + 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 1, hy + 6);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Beak split (bottom mandible faint line)
  cx.strokeStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.lineWidth = 0.5;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 1, hy + 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 7, hy + 4.5);
  cx.stroke();
  // Crown feather — small ruffle on top of head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 4, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 6, hy - 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 2, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 4, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 6, hy - 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 2, hy - 8);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Slight neck-feather ruffle (collar at base of head)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 8, hy + 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 6, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 4, hy + 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 2, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy + 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 2, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy + 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 6, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy + 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

// === v28.13: Continental expansion — Lynx, Moose, Coyote, Quetzal ===

// Lynx — silver-grey with very long tufted ears (the diagnostic feature),
// alert green eyes (set in face fn), white chin ruff
function _headLynx(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Long pointed tufted ears (black tufts on top)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 8, hy - 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 11, hy - 14);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 4, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 8, hy - 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 11, hy - 14);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Black tufts on ear tips — Lynx diagnostic
  cx.fillStyle = pal.tuft;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 11, hy - 14);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 12, hy - 17);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 13);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 11, hy - 14);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 12, hy - 17);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 13);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — slightly broader than fox, more triangular
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 9, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // White chin ruff (the Lynx's distinctive cheek-fluff)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 9, hy + 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 11, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy + 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy + 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 11, hy + 9);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy + 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // White muzzle
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 4, 4, 2.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Moose — oversized head with huge palmate antler rack, long droopy snout,
// dewlap below jaw. The biggest head silhouette in the league after the Polar Bears.
function _headMoose(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Antler rack — big palmate (paddle-shaped), drawn behind head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.horn;
  // Left antler
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 14, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 16, hy - 8);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 14, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 11, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 13, hy - 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Right antler
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 14, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 16, hy - 8);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 14, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 11, hy - 7);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 13, hy - 4);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Antler tine highlights
  cx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)";
  cx.fillRect(hx - 13, hy - 10, 1, 4);
  cx.fillRect(hx + 12, hy - 10, 1, 4);
  // Head — long oval shape
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 8.5, 9.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Long droopy snout — extends down past head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 8, 5, 4, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Dewlap (the dangling skin under the chin) — Moose-specific
  cx.fillStyle = pal.dewlap;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 12, 3, 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Lighter snout patch
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 8, 3.5, 2.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Small inner ears (mostly hidden by antlers)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx - 6, hy - 6, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.arc(hx + 6, hy - 6, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Coyote — triangular sandy head with very pointed ears, lean elongated snout,
// alert ears (more upright than Wolf, more pointed). Tan/sandy fur.
function _headCoyote(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Very pointed upright ears — distinctive sharp triangle, more pointed than Wolf
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 9, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 3, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 9, hy - 13);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 3, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Pink ear interiors
  cx.fillStyle = "#d8a890";
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 4, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 7, hy - 5);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 8, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 7);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — narrower than Wolf, more triangular shape
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 1, 8, 7.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Long lean snout (extends forward, sandy)
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 3, hy + 3);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy + 8);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 3, hy + 3);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Light cheek/chin patches
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx - 4, hy + 3, 2.5, 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.ellipse(hx + 4, hy + 3, 2.5, 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Light muzzle
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 6, 2, 1.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
}

// Quetzal — bright emerald head with crimson breast/belly, golden beak,
// distinctive long flowing tail-feathers (drape behind body).
function _headQuetzal(cx, hx, hy, pal) {
  // Long flowing tail feathers behind body — drape down past torso
  cx.fillStyle = pal.tail;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 4, hy + 4);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx - 8, hy + 14, hx - 4, hy + 22);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx - 1, hy + 14, hx, hy + 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.moveTo(hx + 4, hy + 4);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx + 8, hy + 14, hx + 4, hy + 22);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx + 1, hy + 14, hx, hy + 4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Tail-feather highlights
  cx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.25)";
  cx.lineWidth = 0.8;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 5, hy + 8);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx - 6, hy + 15, hx - 5, hy + 20);
  cx.moveTo(hx + 5, hy + 8);
  cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx + 6, hy + 15, hx + 5, hy + 20);
  cx.stroke();
  // Crest — tuft of bright green feathers on top of head
  cx.fillStyle = pal.inner;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 4, hy - 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 5, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 1, hy - 8);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy - 12);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 1, hy - 8);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 4, hy - 6);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Head — emerald round
  cx.fillStyle = pal.fur;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Crimson breast/throat patch
  cx.fillStyle = pal.belly;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(hx, hy + 5, 6, 4, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  // Golden beak — short triangular
  cx.fillStyle = pal.beak;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 1, hy + 1);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy + 3);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 1, hy + 5);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Beak split
  cx.strokeStyle = pal.accent;
  cx.lineWidth = 0.4;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 1, hy + 3);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 5, hy + 3);
  cx.stroke();
}

/* ============================================================
   FACE — eyes + mouth + nose, varies with state.
   Same eye/mouth treatment for all species since they're already
   distinguished by head silhouette; this saves 12*3 = 36 face
   variations and keeps the state distinction crisp.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawAnimalFace(cx, hx, hy, teamId, pal, state) {
  // Eye positions — owl/frog get wider-set eyes; everyone else uses default
  let eyeOffsetX = 3;
  let eyeOffsetY = -1;
  let eyeR = 1.4;
  let useIris = false;
  if (teamId === "owl") {
    eyeOffsetX = 3.5; eyeOffsetY = -1; eyeR = 2.4; useIris = true;
  } else if (teamId === "frg") {
    // Eyes on top of head (drawn within the bulging domes)
    eyeOffsetX = 5; eyeOffsetY = -7; eyeR = 1.8;
  } else if (teamId === "shk") {
    eyeOffsetX = 4; eyeOffsetY = -2; eyeR = 1;
  } else if (teamId === "wlf") {
    useIris = true;
  } else if (teamId === "bdg") {
    // Badger eyes flank the white stripe
    eyeOffsetX = 4; eyeOffsetY = -1;
  } else if (teamId === "rvn") {
    // Raven gets corvid yellow iris like Wolves; eyes set slightly forward
    useIris = true;
    eyeOffsetX = 3.5; eyeOffsetY = -1.5;
  } else if (teamId === "bsn") {
    // Bison head is bulkier — set eyes wider
    eyeOffsetX = 4.5; eyeOffsetY = -1;
  } else if (teamId === "plr") {
    // Polar bear's massive head — small dark eyes spaced wider
    eyeOffsetX = 3.5; eyeOffsetY = -1; eyeR = 1.2;
  } else if (teamId === "lyx") {
    // Lynx has alert pale-green iris (boreal cat eyes catch low light)
    useIris = true;
    eyeOffsetX = 3.2; eyeOffsetY = -1.5; eyeR = 1.5;
  } else if (teamId === "cyt") {
    // Coyote has yellow iris like Wolves but smaller, set forward
    useIris = true;
    eyeOffsetX = 3; eyeOffsetY = -2; eyeR = 1.2;
  } else if (teamId === "qtz") {
    // Quetzal — tiny bird eyes, set high above the beak
    eyeOffsetX = 2.8; eyeOffsetY = -2.5; eyeR = 1.0;
  } else if (teamId === "mos") {
    // Moose eyes are recessed and set below the antler line
    eyeOffsetX = 4; eyeOffsetY = -1; eyeR = 1.2;
  }

  if (state === "stunned") {
    // X-shaped closed eyes — universal "knocked silly"
    cx.strokeStyle = "#1a1a1a";
    cx.lineWidth = 1.3;
    for (const sign of [-1, 1]) {
      const ex = hx + eyeOffsetX * sign;
      const ey = hy + eyeOffsetY;
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.moveTo(ex - 1.5, ey - 1.5);
      cx.lineTo(ex + 1.5, ey + 1.5);
      cx.moveTo(ex + 1.5, ey - 1.5);
      cx.lineTo(ex - 1.5, ey + 1.5);
      cx.stroke();
    }
  } else if (state === "critical") {
    // Wide intense eyes — bigger pupils, slight squint
    cx.fillStyle = "#fefcf0";
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.fill();
    cx.fillStyle = "#1a1a1a";
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.fill();
  } else {
    // Default alive — small focused eyes
    if (useIris) {
      // Owl/wolf: yellow ring + black pupil
      cx.fillStyle = pal.iris || "#f4c542";
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 0.7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
      cx.fillStyle = "#1a1a1a";
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR * 0.65, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR * 0.65, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
    } else if (teamId === "frg") {
      // Frog: white sclera in dome, black pupil
      cx.fillStyle = "#fefcf0";
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR + 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
      cx.fillStyle = "#1a1a1a";
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
    } else {
      cx.fillStyle = pal.eye;
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(hx - eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.arc(hx + eyeOffsetX, hy + eyeOffsetY, eyeR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      cx.fill();
    }
  }

  // Mouth/nose — varies by team. Keep simple for readability.
  if (teamId === "bvr") {
    // Beaver: two big visible front teeth
    cx.fillStyle = pal.tooth;
    cx.fillRect(hx - 1.6, hy + 4, 1.4, 3);
    cx.fillRect(hx + 0.2, hy + 4, 1.4, 3);
    cx.strokeStyle = pal.accent;
    cx.lineWidth = 0.4;
    cx.strokeRect(hx - 1.6, hy + 4, 1.4, 3);
    cx.strokeRect(hx + 0.2, hy + 4, 1.4, 3);
  } else if (teamId === "shk") {
    // Shark: visible teeth row
    cx.fillStyle = pal.tooth;
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.moveTo(hx + 4 + i * 1.5, hy + 1);
      cx.lineTo(hx + 4.5 + i * 1.5, hy + 3);
      cx.lineTo(hx + 5 + i * 1.5, hy + 1);
      cx.closePath();
      cx.fill();
    }
  } else if (teamId === "wlf") {
    // Wolf: small visible canines + black nose tip
    cx.fillStyle = pal.nose;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(hx, hy + 7, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.fill();
    cx.fillStyle = pal.tooth;
    cx.fillRect(hx - 1.4, hy + 7.5, 0.7, 1.5);
    cx.fillRect(hx + 0.7, hy + 7.5, 0.7, 1.5);
  } else if (teamId === "frg") {
    // Frog: wide thin mouth across the bottom
    cx.strokeStyle = pal.accent;
    cx.lineWidth = 1;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(hx - 5, hy + 5);
    cx.quadraticCurveTo(hx, hy + 7, hx + 5, hy + 5);
    cx.stroke();
  } else if (teamId === "owl") {
    // Owl: tiny downward beak triangle
    cx.fillStyle = pal.iris || "#f4c542";
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(hx - 1.5, hy + 4);
    cx.lineTo(hx + 1.5, hy + 4);
    cx.lineTo(hx, hy + 7);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();
  } else {
    // Default: small black nose dot + faint mouth line
    cx.fillStyle = pal.nose;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(hx, hy + 4, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    cx.fill();
    if (state !== "stunned") {
      cx.strokeStyle = pal.accent;
      cx.lineWidth = 0.6;
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.moveTo(hx - 1, hy + 6);
      cx.lineTo(hx + 1, hy + 6);
      cx.stroke();
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   HELMETS / HOODS — drawn over head for tank/sniper/speed.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawTankHelmet(cx, hx, hy, teamColor) {
  const h = darken(teamColor, 0.5);
  const trim = lighten(teamColor, 0.2);
  // Helmet dome covers the top of the head
  cx.fillStyle = h;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy - 1, 11, Math.PI, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 11, hy + 1);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 11, hy + 1);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Visor slit
  cx.fillStyle = "#1a1a1a";
  cx.fillRect(hx - 7, hy - 2, 14, 2);
  // Trim band
  cx.fillStyle = trim;
  cx.fillRect(hx - 11, hy + 1, 22, 1);
  // Top crest spike
  cx.fillStyle = trim;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.moveTo(hx - 1.5, hy - 11);
  cx.lineTo(hx, hy - 14);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 1.5, hy - 11);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

function _drawSniperHood(cx, hx, hy, teamColor, pal) {
  const h = darken(teamColor, 0.45);
  // Hood arc draped over head, leaving face visible
  cx.fillStyle = h;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy - 2, 12, Math.PI + 0.2, -0.2);
  cx.lineTo(hx + 11, hy + 6);
  cx.lineTo(hx - 11, hy + 6);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
  // Hood front opening curve (lighter inset)
  cx.fillStyle = darken(teamColor, 0.6);
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(hx, hy + 1, 8, Math.PI + 0.4, -0.4);
  cx.closePath();
  cx.fill();
}

function _drawSpeedHood(cx, hx, hy, teamColor) {
  const h = darken(teamColor, 0.3);
  // Slim band across the forehead — like a goggle strap or headband
  cx.fillStyle = h;
  cx.fillRect(hx - 9, hy - 6, 18, 3);
  // Lens dot in center
  cx.fillStyle = "#fefcf0";
  cx.fillRect(hx - 1, hy - 5, 2, 1);
}

/* ============================================================
   CHEST EMBLEM — small colored glyph indicating role.
   ============================================================ */
function _drawChestEmblem(cx, bx, by, posId, teamColor) {
  const colors = {
    tank: "#88c0d0",
    striker: "#ef6e6e",
    speed: "#f7c948",
    brawl: "#d8b070",
    sniper: "#b46df0",
    healer: "#7fd989",
    grenadier: "#f49056",
  };
  const color = colors[posId] || "#aaaaaa";
  cx.fillStyle = color;
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(bx, by, 1.7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  cx.fill();
  cx.strokeStyle = darken(color, 0.4);
  cx.lineWidth = 0.5;
  cx.stroke();
}

/* ============================================================
   DOM RENDERERS
   ============================================================ */
function renderHeader() {
  $("#bitsAmt").textContent = LEAGUE.bits;
  $("#seasonNum").textContent = LEAGUE.season;
  $("#leagueBadge").textContent = "S" + LEAGUE.season;
  // Ticker shows current matchup or next-up cue
  const am = activeMatchTeams();
  if (LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.phase === "round") {
    $("#ticker").textContent = `LIVE · R${LEAGUE.match.currentRoundIndex}/${LEAGUE.match.bestOf} · ${TEAM_BY_ID[LEAGUE.match.teamAId].name} ${LEAGUE.match.roundsWonA}–${LEAGUE.match.roundsWonB} ${TEAM_BY_ID[LEAGUE.match.teamBId].name}`;
  } else if (LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.phase === "betweenRounds") {
    $("#ticker").textContent = `INTERMISSION · ${LEAGUE.match.roundsWonA}–${LEAGUE.match.roundsWonB} · place bets between rounds →`;
  } else if (am) {
    $("#ticker").textContent = `NEXT: ${am.slot.toUpperCase()} · ${TEAM_BY_ID[am.teamA]?.name || "?"} vs ${TEAM_BY_ID[am.teamB]?.name || "?"}`;
  } else {
    $("#ticker").textContent = `Season ${LEAGUE.season} complete. Begin next season →`;
  }
}

function renderTeamPanel(side) {
  const am = activeMatchTeams();
  if (!am) return;
  const teamId = side === "A" ? am.teamA : am.teamB;
  const team = TEAM_BY_ID[teamId];
  const players = LEAGUE.rosters[teamId];

  $("#team" + side + "emoji").textContent = team.emoji;
  $("#team" + side + "name").textContent = team.name;
  const rec = LEAGUE.records[teamId];
  $("#team" + side + "record").textContent =
    `${rec.wins}–${rec.losses} · ${rec.championships} 🏆`;

  // Alive count (active match only; otherwise show roster size)
  let alive = 0;
  if (LEAGUE.match && (LEAGUE.match.teamAId === teamId || LEAGUE.match.teamBId === teamId)) {
    const list = LEAGUE.match.teamAId === teamId ? LEAGUE.match.teamAplayers : LEAGUE.match.teamBplayers;
    alive = list.filter(p => p.alive).length;
  } else {
    alive = players.filter(p => !p.deceased).length;
  }
  const aliveEl = $("#team" + side + "alive");
  aliveEl.textContent = alive;
  aliveEl.className = "alive-count " + (alive >= 4 ? "green" : alive >= 2 ? "amber" : "red");

  // Player chips
  const list = $("#team" + side + "list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const p of players) {
    const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
    const chip = document.createElement("div");
    chip.className = "player-chip" + (p.deceased ? " deceased" : "");
    const liveP = LEAGUE.match
      ? (LEAGUE.match.teamAplayers.concat(LEAGUE.match.teamBplayers)).find(x => x.id === p.id)
      : null;
    // KO'd this round but still has lives → "dead" class (faded, not deceased)
    if (liveP && !liveP.alive && !p.deceased) chip.classList.add("dead");
    const hpFrac = liveP ? clamp(liveP.hp / liveP.stats.hp, 0, 1) : 1;
    const hpCls = hpFrac > 0.6 ? "" : hpFrac > 0.3 ? "med" : "low";

    // Lives pips (only show when in an active match)
    let livesHTML = "";
    if (liveP && !p.deceased && LEAGUE.match) {
      const totalLives = MATCH.startingLives;
      const remaining = liveP.lives;
      let pips = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < totalLives; i++) {
        pips += `<div class="pip${i >= remaining ? " spent" : ""}"></div>`;
      }
      livesHTML = `<div class="lives" title="${remaining}/${totalLives} lives">${pips}</div>`;
    }

    // === v28.1: Streak badge — shown when current streak ≥ 3 ===
    // Career-long currentStreak resets on KO. Yellow flame at 3-9, orange
    // at 10-19, red at 20+. Hot-streak signal at a glance from team panel.
    const streak = p.career.currentStreak || 0;
    let streakHTML = "";
    if (streak >= 3 && !p.deceased && (!liveP || liveP.alive)) {
      const cls = streak >= 20 ? "streak-red" : streak >= 10 ? "streak-orange" : "streak-yellow";
      streakHTML = `<span class="chip-streak ${cls}" title="${streak}-kill streak">🔥${streak}</span>`;
    }

    // === v28.1: Armor badge — restores per-armor visual identity ===
    // Small icon in chip's bottom-right corner showing what armor they wear.
    // Hidden if no armor.
    let armorHTML = "";
    if (p.armor && p.armor.ico) {
      armorHTML = `<span class="chip-armor" title="${escapeHTML(p.armor.name || p.armor.ico)}">${p.armor.ico}</span>`;
    }

    chip.innerHTML = `
      <div class="pos-icon ${pos.cls}" title="${pos.label}">${pos.icon}</div>
      <div class="name-block">
        <div class="pname">${escapeHTML(p.name)}${streakHTML}</div>
        <div class="psub">${pos.label} · K:${p.career.kills}${livesHTML ? "" : ""}</div>
      </div>
      ${livesHTML || `<div class="hpbar ${hpCls}"><div style="width:${Math.round(hpFrac * 100)}%"></div></div>`}
      ${armorHTML}
    `;
    chip.addEventListener("click", () => showPlayerProfile(p));
    list.appendChild(chip);
  }
}

function renderBracketStrip() {
  const strip = $("#bracketStrip");
  if (!strip || !LEAGUE.bracket) return;
  const order = LEAGUE.bracket._order || ["semi1", "semi2", "final"];
  // Build labels dynamically based on slot prefix
  const labelFor = (slot) => {
    if (slot.startsWith("ql")) return "Qualifier " + slot.slice(2);
    if (slot.startsWith("qf")) return "Quarterfinal " + slot.slice(2);
    if (slot.startsWith("sf")) return "Semifinal " + slot.slice(2);
    if (slot === "semi1") return "Semifinal 1";
    if (slot === "semi2") return "Semifinal 2";
    if (slot === "final") return "Final";
    return slot;
  };

  // Re-render the strip with the right number of cards
  strip.innerHTML = "";
  for (const slot of order) {
    const m = LEAGUE.bracket[slot];
    const el = document.createElement("div");
    el.className = "bracket-match";
    el.dataset.slot = slot;
    if (LEAGUE.currentSlot === slot) el.classList.add("current");
    if (m.winner) el.classList.add("done");

    const aId = m.teamA, bId = m.teamB;
    const aTeam = aId ? TEAM_BY_ID[aId] : null;
    const bTeam = bId ? TEAM_BY_ID[bId] : null;
    const aClass = m.winner === aId ? "winner" : (m.winner && m.winner !== aId ? "loser" : "");
    const bClass = m.winner === bId ? "winner" : (m.winner && m.winner !== bId ? "loser" : "");

    let aScore = "—", bScore = "—";
    if (m.winner && LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.slot === slot) {
      aScore = LEAGUE.match.killsByTeam[aId] || 0;
      bScore = LEAGUE.match.killsByTeam[bId] || 0;
    }

    el.innerHTML = `
      <div class="label">${labelFor(slot)}</div>
      <div class="team-row ${aClass}">
        <span class="em">${aTeam?.emoji || "❓"}</span>
        <span class="nm">${aTeam?.short || "TBD"}</span>
        <span class="sc">${aScore}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="vs-divider">VS</div>
      <div class="team-row ${bClass}">
        <span class="em">${bTeam?.emoji || "❓"}</span>
        <span class="nm">${bTeam?.short || "TBD"}</span>
        <span class="sc">${bScore}</span>
      </div>
    `;
    strip.appendChild(el);
  }
}

/* === ROADMAP 2.6: Ladder ranking with weighted formula ===
   Real-world references: most pro sports use a weighted formula.
   F1 uses points; NBA combines W/L with strength-of-schedule; NFL
   uses W-L-T with tiebreakers. We use a simple weighted score:
     championships×100 + wins×3 + draws×1 - losses×1
   (Championships dominate; wins are the next big driver; losses
   only break ties.)

   Returns a sorted array of { team, record, score, rank, prevRank, delta }.
   The prevRank/delta come from LEAGUE.previousLadder (set at season-end). */
function computeLadderRanking() {
  const teams = TEAMS.slice().map(t => {
    const r = LEAGUE.records[t.id];
    const score = (r.championships || 0) * 100
                + (r.wins || 0) * 3
                + (r.draws || 0)
                - (r.losses || 0);
    return { team: t, record: r, score };
  });
  teams.sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
    // Tiebreakers: more wins wins, then fewer losses, then alphabetical
    if (b.record.wins !== a.record.wins) return b.record.wins - a.record.wins;
    if (a.record.losses !== b.record.losses) return a.record.losses - b.record.losses;
    return a.team.short.localeCompare(b.team.short);
  });
  // Annotate with current rank + delta vs previous ladder snapshot
  const prev = (LEAGUE && LEAGUE.previousLadder) || {};
  for (let i = 0; i < teams.length; i++) {
    teams[i].rank = i + 1;
    teams[i].prevRank = prev[teams[i].team.id] || null;
    teams[i].delta = teams[i].prevRank ? (teams[i].prevRank - teams[i].rank) : 0;
  }
  return teams;
}
window.computeLadderRanking = computeLadderRanking;

function renderStandings() {
  const list = $("#standingsList");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  const sorted = computeLadderRanking();
  for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length; i++) {
    const { team: t, record: r, rank, delta } = sorted[i];
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.style.cssText = "display:grid;grid-template-columns:18px 12px 1fr auto;gap:6px;padding:5px 10px;font-size:11px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bg-2);";
    // Rank-change indicator: ▲ green if up, ▼ red if down, · gray if unchanged
    let deltaIcon = `<span style="color:var(--mute);font-size:9px;">·</span>`;
    if (delta > 0) {
      deltaIcon = `<span style="color:var(--grn);font-size:9px;" title="up ${delta} from last season">▲</span>`;
    } else if (delta < 0) {
      deltaIcon = `<span style="color:var(--red);font-size:9px;" title="down ${-delta} from last season">▼</span>`;
    }
    row.innerHTML = `
      <span style="color:var(--mute);">${rank}</span>
      ${deltaIcon}
      <span>${t.emoji} ${escapeHTML(t.short)}</span>
      <span style="font-family:'Courier New',monospace;color:var(--dim);">${r.wins}-${r.losses} · ${r.championships}🏆</span>
    `;
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
}

function renderArenaHeader() {
  // Smoke-test guard — DOM may be absent
  if (typeof document === "undefined" || !document.getElementById) return;
  const matchupEl = document.getElementById("matchupLabel");
  const clockEl = document.getElementById("matchClock");
  if (!matchupEl || !clockEl) return;

  if (!LEAGUE.match) {
    matchupEl.textContent = LEAGUE.currentSlot
      ? `${TEAM_BY_ID[LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot]?.teamA]?.short || "?"} vs ${TEAM_BY_ID[LEAGUE.bracket[LEAGUE.currentSlot]?.teamB]?.short || "?"}`
      : "— vs —";
    clockEl.textContent = "00:00";
    renderPhasePill();
    return;
  }
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const biomeBadge = m.biome ? ` · ${m.biome.emoji} ${m.biome.name}` : "";
  // === v28.11: Surface formation names in the matchup label ===
  // _formations is stamped by layoutTeam at round start. Show only during
  // an active round (not pre-round / between-rounds / post). The formations
  // get badged inline next to each team's short code so the user can see
  // who's running what this round.
  const fA = (m._formations && m._formations[m.teamAId]) ? m._formations[m.teamAId].toUpperCase() : "";
  const fB = (m._formations && m._formations[m.teamBId]) ? m._formations[m.teamBId].toUpperCase() : "";
  const showFormations = (m.phase === "round" || m.phase === "preRound") && fA && fB;
  const fmtA = showFormations ? ` (${fA})` : "";
  const fmtB = showFormations ? ` (${fB})` : "";
  matchupEl.textContent =
    `${TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId].emoji} ${TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId].short}${fmtA} vs${fmtB} ${TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId].short} ${TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId].emoji}${biomeBadge}`;
  const seconds = Math.floor((m.roundFrame || 0) * COMBAT.frameMs / 1000);
  clockEl.textContent = `R${m.currentRoundIndex}/${m.bestOf} ${String(Math.floor(seconds/60)).padStart(2,"0")}:${String(seconds%60).padStart(2,"0")}`;
  renderPhasePill();
}



function renderPhasePill() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined" || !document.getElementById) return;
  const phaseEl = document.getElementById("matchPhase");
  if (!phaseEl) return;
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const baseLabel = m
    ? ({"preRound":"Pre-round", "round":"LIVE", "betweenRounds":"INTERMISSION", "post":"POST"}[m.phase] || m.phase)
    : "Pre-match";
  phaseEl.textContent = baseLabel;

  // Big centered countdown overlay — drawn over the arena (NOT in the header
  // pill anymore). The overlay has its own DOM element that gets shown/hidden
  // and updated each frame.
  renderCountdownOverlay();
}

function renderCountdownOverlay() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  const arenaWrap = document.querySelector ? document.querySelector(".arena-wrap") : null;
  if (!arenaWrap) return;
  let overlay = document.getElementById("countdownOverlay");
  if (!overlay) {
    overlay = document.createElement("div");
    overlay.id = "countdownOverlay";
    overlay.className = "countdown-overlay";
    overlay.innerHTML = `
      <div class="countdown-inner">
        <div class="countdown-label" id="countdownLabel">Next Round</div>
        <div class="countdown-time" id="countdownTime">60</div>
        <div class="countdown-sub" id="countdownSub">Place bets &middot; visit the store</div>
      </div>`;
    arenaWrap.appendChild(overlay);
  }
  const canvasEl = document.getElementById("arena");
  if (typeof AUTOPLAY_PENDING !== "undefined" && AUTOPLAY_PENDING) {
    const ms = Math.max(0, AUTOPLAY_PENDING.remainingMs);
    const sec = Math.ceil(ms / 1000);
    const labelMap = { round: "Next Round", match: "Next Match", season: "Next Season" };
    document.getElementById("countdownLabel").textContent = labelMap[AUTOPLAY_PENDING.kind] || "Next";
    document.getElementById("countdownTime").textContent = (typeof AUTOPLAY !== "undefined" && !AUTOPLAY.enabled)
      ? "⏸"
      : sec;
    const subMap = {
      round:  "Place bets &middot; visit the store",
      match:  "Match break &middot; place bets &middot; check standings",
      season: "Off-season call-ups in progress &middot; new bracket forming",
    };
    document.getElementById("countdownSub").innerHTML = subMap[AUTOPLAY_PENDING.kind] || "";
    overlay.classList.add("visible");
    if (sec <= 5) overlay.classList.add("urgent");
    else overlay.classList.remove("urgent");
    if (canvasEl) canvasEl.classList.add("countdown-blur");
  } else {
    overlay.classList.remove("visible", "urgent");
    if (canvasEl) canvasEl.classList.remove("countdown-blur");
  }
}

function renderRoundStrip() {
  const stripEl = $("#roundStrip");
  if (!stripEl) return;
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) {
    stripEl.style.display = "none";
    return;
  }
  stripEl.style.display = "";
  const pipsEl = $("#roundPips");
  pipsEl.innerHTML = "";
  // Build pips for each round in best-of-N
  // Pip filled with team color if won by that team; current round pulses gold.
  for (let i = 1; i <= m.bestOf; i++) {
    const pip = document.createElement("div");
    pip.className = "pip";
    const round = m.rounds.find(r => r.index === i);
    if (round) {
      if (round.isDraw || round.winner === null) {
        pip.classList.add("draw");
        pip.style.backgroundColor = "var(--mute, #666)";
        pip.style.borderColor = "var(--mute, #666)";
      } else {
        pip.classList.add(round.winner === m.teamAId ? "won-a" : "won-b");
        const t = TEAM_BY_ID[round.winner];
        if (t) {
          pip.style.backgroundColor = t.color;
          pip.style.borderColor = t.color;
        }
      }
    }
    if (m.phase === "round" && i === m.currentRoundIndex) {
      pip.classList.add("current");
    }
    pipsEl.appendChild(pip);
  }
  $("#roundScore").textContent = `${m.roundsWonA}–${m.roundsWonB}`;
}

function renderFeed() {
  const el = $("#commentary");
  el.innerHTML = "";
  for (const item of FEED) {
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "commentary-item " + item.kind;
    div.innerHTML = `<span class="ts">${item.ts}</span><span>${escapeHTML(item.msg)}</span>`;
    el.appendChild(div);
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   SPORTSCASTER COMMENTARY RENDER
   Renders the COMMENTARY[] queue (most-recent first) into the
   dedicated sportscaster panel. Recent entries get a "fresh"
   highlight that fades after a few seconds. The panel handles
   chant lines, openers, play-by-play and color separately so
   each style reads distinctly.
   ============================================================ */
function renderCommentary() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined" || !document.getElementById) return;
  const el = document.getElementById("sportscasterList");
  if (!el) return;
  if (typeof COMMENTARY === "undefined" || !COMMENTARY.length) {
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="cc-line" style="color:var(--mute);font-size:11px;font-style:italic;justify-content:center;"><span>Awaiting next match — sportscaster on standby.</span></div>';
    return;
  }
  const now = Date.now();
  el.innerHTML = COMMENTARY.slice(0, 18).map((c, idx) => {
    const ico = c.kind === "chant"   ? "📣"
              : c.kind === "opener"  ? "🎙️"
              : c.kind === "color"   ? "🎬"
              :                        "▶";
    const flavor = c.flavor ? "flavor-" + c.flavor : "";
    const fresh = idx === 0 ? "fresh" : "";
    const ai = c.isAi ? "is-ai" : "";
    return `<div class="cc-line ${c.kind} ${flavor} ${fresh} ${ai}">
      <span class="ts">${c.ts}</span>
      <span class="ico">${ico}</span>
      <span class="body">${c.line}</span>
    </div>`;
  }).join("");
}
window.renderCommentary = renderCommentary;

/* ============================================================
   BET RENDERING
   - renderBetSummaryRail: read-only summary of active bets in right rail
   - renderBetTab: full betting interface on the Bet tab
   ============================================================ */
function renderBetSummaryRail() {
  const el = $("#betsSummaryRail");
  if (!el) return;
  const countEl = $("#activeBetCountBadge");
  const tabBadge = $("#tabBetCount");
  const count = LEAGUE.bets.length;
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = count;
  if (tabBadge) {
    tabBadge.textContent = count;
    tabBadge.style.display = count > 0 ? "" : "none";
  }
  el.innerHTML = "";
  if (!count) {
    const empty = document.createElement("div");
    empty.className = "empty";
    empty.textContent = "No active bets.";
    el.appendChild(empty);
  } else {
    for (const bet of LEAGUE.bets) {
      const div = document.createElement("div");
      div.className = "active-bet";
      const label = betLabelFor(bet);
      const tag = bet.kind === "round" ? `<span style="color:var(--blu);font-size:9px;">[round ${bet.roundIndex}]</span> ` : "";
      div.innerHTML = `${tag}<b>${bet.amount}</b> bits on ${label} @ ${bet.payoutMult}× → <b>${Math.round(bet.amount * bet.payoutMult)}</b>`;
      el.appendChild(div);
    }
  }
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.className = "place-link";
  link.dataset.goTab = "bet";
  link.textContent = (count ? "Manage / place more bets →" : "Place a bet →");
  link.addEventListener("click", (e) => { e.preventDefault(); switchTab("bet"); });
  el.appendChild(link);
}

function betLabelFor(bet) {
  if (bet.kind === "team" || bet.kind === "round") {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[bet.target];
    return t ? `${t.emoji} ${escapeHTML(t.short)}` : "?";
  }
  if (bet.kind === "player") {
    const p = Object.values(LEAGUE.rosters).flat().find(x => x.id === bet.target);
    return p ? escapeHTML(p.name) : "?";
  }
  if (bet.kind === "firstkill") {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[bet.target];
    return t ? `${t.emoji} ${escapeHTML(t.short)} 1st kill` : "?";
  }
  if (bet.kind === "totalcas") {
    return bet.target === "over" ? "5+ deaths" : "≤5 deaths";
  }
  if (bet.kind === "rounds") {
    return bet.target === "over" ? "4+ rounds" : "≤3 rounds";
  }
  return "?";
}

function renderBetTab() {
  const am = activeMatchTeams();
  const subEl = $("#betSubLine");
  if (!am) {
    subEl.textContent = "No match is set up. Bets unavailable.";
    $("#betMarketTeamRows").innerHTML = "";
    $("#betMarketRoundRows").innerHTML = "";
    $("#betMarketPlayerRows").innerHTML = "";
    if ($("#betMarketFirstKillRows")) $("#betMarketFirstKillRows").innerHTML = "";
    if ($("#betMarketTotalCasRows")) $("#betMarketTotalCasRows").innerHTML = "";
    if ($("#betMarketRoundsRows")) $("#betMarketRoundsRows").innerHTML = "";
    $("#betPagePlace").disabled = true;
    $("#betSelectionLabel").textContent = "Pick a market above";
    renderBetHistory();
    return;
  }
  const aId = am.teamA, bId = am.teamB;
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const phase = m?.phase || "preRound";

  // Sub-line states
  const matchInProgress = phase === "round" || phase === "betweenRounds";
  subEl.textContent = matchInProgress
    ? `Round ${m.currentRoundIndex} / ${m.bestOf} — ${TEAM_BY_ID[aId].short} ${m.roundsWonA}–${m.roundsWonB} ${TEAM_BY_ID[bId].short}.`
    : `Pre-match betting open for ${TEAM_BY_ID[aId].short} vs ${TEAM_BY_ID[bId].short}.`;

  const matchOver = phase === "post";
  const roundLocked = phase === "round"; // round bets locked while round is live
  $("#mwState").textContent = matchOver ? "CLOSED" : "OPEN";
  $("#rwState").textContent = roundLocked ? "LOCKED (round live)" : matchOver ? "CLOSED" : "OPEN";
  $("#lsState").textContent = matchOver ? "CLOSED" : "OPEN";

  // Match Winner market
  const teamRows = $("#betMarketTeamRows");
  teamRows.innerHTML = "";
  // === ROADMAP 2.5: Live odds during a match ===
  // If a match is in progress (between rounds OR mid-round), use live
  // odds that account for round score + remaining lives. Pre-match
  // (no match started yet, or match just finalized), fall back to
  // static teamOdds based on team strength only.
  const oddsAreLive = LEAGUE.match
    && (LEAGUE.match.phase === "round" || LEAGUE.match.phase === "betweenRounds")
    && LEAGUE.match.teamAId === aId && LEAGUE.match.teamBId === bId;
  const odds = oddsAreLive && typeof liveMatchOdds === "function"
    ? (liveMatchOdds(LEAGUE.match) || teamOdds(aId, bId))
    : teamOdds(aId, bId);
  for (const tid of [aId, bId]) {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[tid];
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "bet-team-row" + (BET_PICK.kind === "team" && BET_PICK.target === tid ? " selected" : "");
    const liveTag = oddsAreLive
      ? `<span style="font-size:8px;color:var(--gold);background:rgba(247,201,72,0.15);padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px;margin-left:6px;letter-spacing:0.5px;font-weight:700;">LIVE</span>`
      : "";
    row.innerHTML = `
      <div class="em">${t.emoji}</div>
      <div class="info">
        <div class="nm">${escapeHTML(t.name)}${liveTag}</div>
        <div class="od">Pays <b>${odds[tid]}×</b></div>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--dim);font-family:'Courier New',monospace;">${LEAGUE.records[tid].wins}-${LEAGUE.records[tid].losses}</div>
    `;
    if (!matchOver) row.addEventListener("click", () => { BET_PICK = { kind: "team", target: tid }; refreshBetSelection(); });
    teamRows.appendChild(row);
  }

  // Round Winner market (same teams, slightly lower payout)
  const roundRows = $("#betMarketRoundRows");
  roundRows.innerHTML = "";
  for (const tid of [aId, bId]) {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[tid];
    const roundPay = +(odds[tid] * 0.85).toFixed(2);
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "bet-team-row" + (BET_PICK.kind === "round" && BET_PICK.target === tid ? " selected" : "");
    row.innerHTML = `
      <div class="em">${t.emoji}</div>
      <div class="info">
        <div class="nm">Next: ${escapeHTML(t.short)}</div>
        <div class="od">Pays <b>${roundPay}×</b></div>
      </div>
    `;
    if (!matchOver && !roundLocked) row.addEventListener("click", () => { BET_PICK = { kind: "round", target: tid }; refreshBetSelection(); });
    if (matchOver || roundLocked) row.style.opacity = "0.5";
    roundRows.appendChild(row);
  }

  // Last Standing player market
  const pOdds = playerOdds(aId, bId);
  const playerRows = $("#betMarketPlayerRows");
  playerRows.innerHTML = "";
  for (const tid of [aId, bId]) {
