    const players = (LEAGUE.rosters[tid] || []).filter(p => !p.deceased);
    for (const p of players) {
      const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
      const row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "pbet-row" + (BET_PICK.kind === "player" && BET_PICK.target === p.id ? " selected" : "");
      const livesText = m && p.lives !== undefined ? ` · ${p.lives}♥` : "";
      row.innerHTML = `
        <span class="pos ${pos.cls}">${pos.icon}</span>
        <span class="nm">${TEAM_BY_ID[tid].emoji} ${escapeHTML(p.name)}<span style="color:var(--dim);font-size:10px;">${livesText}</span></span>
        <span class="od">${pOdds[p.id]}×</span>
      `;
      if (!matchOver) row.addEventListener("click", () => { BET_PICK = { kind: "player", target: p.id }; refreshBetSelection(); });
      playerRows.appendChild(row);
    }
  }

  // First Kill market — already-locked once first kill has occurred
  const firstKillTaken = !!(m && m.firstKillTeam);
  const fkStateEl = $("#fkState");
  if (fkStateEl) fkStateEl.textContent = matchOver ? "CLOSED" : (firstKillTaken ? "LOCKED (1st kill scored)" : "OPEN");
  const fkRows = $("#betMarketFirstKillRows");
  if (fkRows) {
    fkRows.innerHTML = "";
    for (const tid of [aId, bId]) {
      const t = TEAM_BY_ID[tid];
      const fkPay = +(odds[tid] * 0.95).toFixed(2);
      const row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "bet-team-row" + (BET_PICK.kind === "firstkill" && BET_PICK.target === tid ? " selected" : "");
      row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="em">${t.emoji}</div>
        <div class="info">
          <div class="nm">${escapeHTML(t.short)} draws first blood</div>
          <div class="od">Pays <b>${fkPay}×</b></div>
        </div>
      `;
      const locked = matchOver || firstKillTaken;
      if (!locked) row.addEventListener("click", () => { BET_PICK = { kind: "firstkill", target: tid }; refreshBetSelection(); });
      if (locked) row.style.opacity = "0.5";
      fkRows.appendChild(row);
    }
  }

  // Total Casualties (over/under 5.5)
  const tcStateEl = $("#tcState");
  if (tcStateEl) tcStateEl.textContent = matchOver ? "CLOSED" : "OPEN";
  const tcRows = $("#betMarketTotalCasRows");
  if (tcRows) {
    tcRows.innerHTML = "";
    for (const dir of ["over", "under"]) {
      const pay = 1.85;
      const row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "bet-team-row" + (BET_PICK.kind === "totalcas" && BET_PICK.target === dir ? " selected" : "");
      row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="em">${dir === "over" ? "📈" : "📉"}</div>
        <div class="info">
          <div class="nm">${dir === "over" ? "OVER" : "UNDER"} 5 deaths</div>
          <div class="od">Pays <b>${pay}×</b></div>
        </div>
      `;
      if (!matchOver) row.addEventListener("click", () => { BET_PICK = { kind: "totalcas", target: dir }; refreshBetSelection(); });
      else row.style.opacity = "0.5";
      tcRows.appendChild(row);
    }
  }

  // Rounds played (over/under 3.5)
  const rdStateEl = $("#rdState");
  if (rdStateEl) rdStateEl.textContent = matchOver ? "CLOSED" : "OPEN";
  const rdRows = $("#betMarketRoundsRows");
  if (rdRows) {
    rdRows.innerHTML = "";
    const opts = [
      { dir: "over",  ico: "📈", label: "OVER 3 rounds (long match)", pay: 2.10 },
      { dir: "under", ico: "📉", label: "UNDER 4 rounds (sweep)",     pay: 1.65 },
    ];
    for (const o of opts) {
      const row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "bet-team-row" + (BET_PICK.kind === "rounds" && BET_PICK.target === o.dir ? " selected" : "");
      row.innerHTML = `
        <div class="em">${o.ico}</div>
        <div class="info">
          <div class="nm">${o.label}</div>
          <div class="od">Pays <b>${o.pay}×</b></div>
        </div>
      `;
      if (!matchOver) row.addEventListener("click", () => { BET_PICK = { kind: "rounds", target: o.dir }; refreshBetSelection(); });
      else row.style.opacity = "0.5";
      rdRows.appendChild(row);
    }
  }

  refreshBetSelection();
  renderBetHistory();
}

function refreshBetSelection() {
  const labelEl = $("#betSelectionLabel");
  const placeBtn = $("#betPagePlace");
  // === v28.5: lock UI during live rounds ===
  // Mirror the placeBet backend lock so users see why the button is
  // disabled. Bets are accepted in preRound, betweenRounds, post, or
  // when no match exists yet.
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  const liveRound = m && m.phase === "round";
  if (liveRound) {
    labelEl.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--warn,#f7c948);">🔒 Betting locked — round in progress</span>';
    placeBtn.disabled = true;
    placeBtn.textContent = "Locked During Round";
    return;
  }
  // Reset button text in case it was previously locked
  placeBtn.textContent = "Place Bet";
  if (!BET_PICK.kind) {
    labelEl.textContent = "Pick a market above";
    placeBtn.disabled = true;
    return;
  }
  if (BET_PICK.kind === "team") {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[BET_PICK.target];
    labelEl.innerHTML = `Match Winner: ${t.emoji} ${escapeHTML(t.name)}`;
  } else if (BET_PICK.kind === "round") {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[BET_PICK.target];
    labelEl.innerHTML = `Round Winner: ${t.emoji} ${escapeHTML(t.name)}`;
  } else if (BET_PICK.kind === "firstkill") {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[BET_PICK.target];
    labelEl.innerHTML = `First Kill: ${t.emoji} ${escapeHTML(t.short)}`;
  } else if (BET_PICK.kind === "totalcas") {
    labelEl.innerHTML = `Casualties: ${BET_PICK.target.toUpperCase()} 5 deaths`;
  } else if (BET_PICK.kind === "rounds") {
    labelEl.innerHTML = `Rounds: ${BET_PICK.target === "over" ? "OVER 3" : "UNDER 4"} rounds`;
  } else if (BET_PICK.kind === "player") {
    const p = Object.values(LEAGUE.rosters).flat().find(x => x.id === BET_PICK.target);
    labelEl.innerHTML = `Last Standing: ${escapeHTML(p?.name || "?")}`;
  }
  placeBtn.disabled = false;
}

function renderBetHistory() {
  const body = $("#betHistoryBody");
  body.innerHTML = "";
  if (!LEAGUE.bets.length) {
    body.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;color:var(--dim);padding:14px;">No active bets. Place one above.</td></tr>`;
    return;
  }
  for (const bet of LEAGUE.bets) {
    const tr = document.createElement("tr");
    const market = bet.kind === "team" ? "Match Winner" : bet.kind === "round" ? `Round ${bet.roundIndex || "?"} Winner` : "Last Standing";
    tr.innerHTML = `
      <td>${bet.kind === "round" ? "Round " + (bet.roundIndex || "?") : "Match"}</td>
      <td>${market}</td>
      <td>${betLabelFor(bet)}</td>
      <td style="text-align:right;font-family:'Courier New',monospace;">${bet.amount}</td>
      <td style="text-align:right;font-family:'Courier New',monospace;">${bet.payoutMult}×</td>
      <td style="text-align:right;font-family:'Courier New',monospace;color:var(--gold);">${Math.round(bet.amount * bet.payoutMult)}</td>
    `;
    body.appendChild(tr);
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   STORE RENDERING — full grid on the Store tab
   ============================================================ */
function renderStore() {
  const grid = $("#storeGrid");
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = "";
  const am = activeMatchTeams();
  const roundLive = LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.phase === "round";

  // Group items by rarity for sectioned display
  const byRarity = { common: [], rare: [], legendary: [] };
  for (const item of SHOP_ITEMS) {
    (byRarity[item.rarity || "common"] || byRarity.common).push(item);
  }
  const sections = [
    { key: "common", label: "COMMON", color: "var(--ink)" },
    { key: "rare", label: "RARE",   color: "var(--pur)" },
    { key: "legendary", label: "LEGENDARY", color: "var(--gold)" },
  ];

  for (const sec of sections) {
    if (!byRarity[sec.key].length) continue;
    const header = document.createElement("div");
    header.className = "store-rarity-header";
    header.innerHTML = `<span style="color:${sec.color};">●</span> <span>${sec.label}</span>`;
    grid.appendChild(header);

    for (const item of byRarity[sec.key]) {
      const btn = document.createElement("button");
      btn.className = "store-card rarity-" + (item.rarity || "common");
      btn.disabled = LEAGUE.bits < item.cost || roundLive || !am;
      if (btn.disabled) btn.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
      const targetTag = item.target === "opp"
        ? `<div class="store-target-tag">debuff opponent</div>`
        : item.target === "specific_player"
        ? `<div class="store-target-tag store-target-targeted">pick one knight</div>`
        : `<div class="store-target-tag store-target-buff">buff your team</div>`;
      btn.innerHTML = `
        <div class="ico">${item.ico}</div>
        <div class="nm">${escapeHTML(item.nm)}</div>
        ${targetTag}
        <div class="cost">${item.cost} bits</div>
        <div class="desc">${escapeHTML(item.desc)}</div>
      `;
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        if (!am) return;
        if (item.target === "specific_player") {
          // Open knight-picker modal — caller chooses which opponent to sandbag
          if (typeof openSandbagPicker === "function") openSandbagPicker(item, am);
          return;
        }
        const ok = buyItem(item.id, am.teamA);
        if (ok) renderAll();
      });
      grid.appendChild(btn);
    }
  }
}

function renderAll() {
  renderHeader();
  renderTeamPanel("A");
  renderTeamPanel("B");
  renderBracketStrip();
  renderRoundStrip();
  renderStandings();
  renderArenaHeader();
  renderFeed();
  renderCommentary();
  renderBetSummaryRail();
  renderBetTab();
  renderStore();
  renderTicker();
}

function renderTicker() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined" || !document.getElementById) return;
  const scoresEl = document.getElementById("tickerScores");
  const contentEl = document.getElementById("tickerContent");
  if (!scoresEl || !contentEl) return;

  // ---- LIVE SCORE PANEL ----
  // Show: current match score + each pending bracket-slot score
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  let scoresHTML = "";
  if (m && m.teamAId) {
    const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId];
    const liveBadge = m.phase === "round" ? `<span class="live">●LIVE</span>` : "";
    scoresHTML += `
      <div class="ticker-score-pill">${liveBadge}
        <span class="em">${ta.emoji}</span>
        <span style="color:${ta.color};">${ta.short}</span>
        <span style="color:var(--ink);">${m.roundsWonA}</span>
        <span style="color:var(--dim);">–</span>
        <span style="color:var(--ink);">${m.roundsWonB}</span>
        <span style="color:${tb.color};">${tb.short}</span>
        <span class="em">${tb.emoji}</span>
      </div>`;
  }
  // Recent completed matches this season
  if (LEAGUE.matchHistory && LEAGUE.matchHistory.length) {
    const thisSeason = LEAGUE.matchHistory.filter(mh => mh.season === LEAGUE.season).slice(-3);
    for (const mh of thisSeason) {
      const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[mh.teamA], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[mh.teamB];
      const w = TEAM_BY_ID[mh.winner];
      if (!ta || !tb || !w) continue;
      scoresHTML += `
        <div class="ticker-score-pill" style="opacity:0.7;">
          <span class="em">${ta.emoji}</span>
          <span>${ta.short}</span>
          <span style="color:var(--dim);">${mh.score}</span>
          <span>${tb.short}</span>
          <span class="em">${tb.emoji}</span>
        </div>`;
    }
  }
  if (!scoresHTML) {
    scoresHTML = `<div class="ticker-score-pill"><span style="color:var(--dim);">SEASON ${LEAGUE.season} · NO MATCH LIVE</span></div>`;
  }
  scoresEl.innerHTML = scoresHTML;

  // ---- ROLLING COMMENTARY TRACK ----
  // Pull last ~30 feed entries and render them as a single horizontal stream.
  // Adds champion announcement, lost-item callouts, top streaks for color.
  const items = [];
  // Champion of last season
  if (LEAGUE.history.length) {
    const last = LEAGUE.history[LEAGUE.history.length - 1];
    const c = TEAM_BY_ID[last.champion];
    if (c) items.push(`<span class="item season">🏆 S${last.season} CHAMPION: ${c.emoji} ${c.short}</span>`);
  }
  // Top streaks across the league
  const allKnights = [];
  for (const tid in LEAGUE.rosters) {
    for (const p of LEAGUE.rosters[tid]) {
      if (!p.deceased && (p.career.currentStreak || 0) >= 3) allKnights.push(p);
    }
  }
  allKnights.sort((a, b) => (b.career.currentStreak || 0) - (a.career.currentStreak || 0));
  for (const k of allKnights.slice(0, 3)) {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[k.teamId];
    items.push(`<span class="item kill">🔥 ${t.emoji} ${escapeHTML(k.name)} on a ${k.career.currentStreak}-kill streak</span>`);
  }
  // Lost legendary items
  if (LEAGUE.lostItems && LEAGUE.lostItems.length) {
    const recent = LEAGUE.lostItems.slice(-3);
    for (const li of recent) {
      items.push(`<span class="item item-feed">📉 ${li.ico || "·"} ${escapeHTML(li.name)} lost (${li.scavengedCount}× scavenged)</span>`);
    }
  }
  // Recent feed entries
  if (typeof FEED !== "undefined" && FEED.length) {
    const recentFeed = FEED.slice(-12);
    for (const f of recentFeed) {
      const txt = (f.text || "").replace(/[\r\n]/g, " ");
      const cls = f.type === "kill" ? "kill" : f.type === "item" ? "item-feed" : f.type === "match" ? "match" : f.type === "season" ? "season" : "";
      items.push(`<span class="item ${cls}">${escapeHTML(txt)}</span>`);
    }
  }
  // Standings teaser
  const sortedTeams = TEAMS.slice().sort((a, b) => {
    const ra = LEAGUE.records[a.id], rb = LEAGUE.records[b.id];
    if (rb.championships !== ra.championships) return rb.championships - ra.championships;
    return rb.wins - ra.wins;
  }).slice(0, 3);
  for (const t of sortedTeams) {
    const r = LEAGUE.records[t.id];
    items.push(`<span class="item">${t.emoji} ${t.short} ${r.wins}–${r.losses}${r.draws ? `–${r.draws}` : ""}</span>`);
  }

  // Repeat content twice so the marquee loop has continuous feel
  const html = items.join('<span class="sep">●</span>');
  contentEl.innerHTML = html + '<span class="sep">●</span>' + html + '<span class="sep">●</span>';
}

/* ============================================================
   UTILS
   ============================================================ */
function escapeHTML(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({
    "&":"&amp;", "<":"&lt;", ">":"&gt;", '"':"&quot;", "'":"&#39;"
  }[c]));
}

let BET_PICK = { kind: null, target: null };

/* ============================================================
   BIOME PREVIEW — render a single biome to a thumbnail canvas.

   The Lore tab's Stadiums section needs a small live preview of
   each of the 33 biomes. Strategy:
   1. Render the biome at full arena dimensions (1280×720) on an
      offscreen canvas, using the same drawBiomeScatter and
      drawCourtMarkings routines the main render uses. To do this
      without rewriting those routines, we temporarily redirect
      the global `ctx` to the offscreen canvas, draw, then restore.
   2. drawImage the offscreen canvas onto the small display canvas
      with high-quality downsampling. This preserves visual fidelity
      (sprites would be unreadable if we drew directly at thumbnail
      scale because stroke widths would round to sub-pixel sizes).
   3. Use a local PRNG for scatter generation so we don't consume
      values from BIOME_RNG (which would perturb the live sim).

   Each biome's preview is deterministic — same biome id always
   produces the same scatter layout — by seeding the local PRNG
   from a hash of the biome id.
   ============================================================ */

// Local mulberry32 — independent from the sim's BIOME_RNG so previews
// don't consume randomness from the live simulation.
function _previewRng(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return function next() {
    a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

// Hash a biome id to a 32-bit seed for reproducible previews
function _biomeIdHash(id) {
  let h = 0x9E3779B9;
  for (let i = 0; i < id.length; i++) {
    h = ((h ^ id.charCodeAt(i)) * 0x01000193) >>> 0;
  }
  return h;
}

// Generate scatter sprites for a biome at full arena dimensions, using a
// local PRNG. Coordinates match what drawBiomeScatter expects.
function _generatePreviewScatter(biome, W, H) {
  const out = [];
  const rng = _previewRng(_biomeIdHash(biome.id));
  const KEEP = 75; // power-core keep-out radius
  const cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2;
  for (const type in biome.scatter) {
    const cfg = biome.scatter[type];
    let placed = 0, tries = 0;
    while (placed < cfg.count && tries < cfg.count * 4) {
      tries++;
      const x = rng() * W;
      const y = rng() * H;
      // Power-core keep-out — match the live sim's keep-out radius
      const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy;
      if (dx*dx + dy*dy < KEEP*KEEP) continue;
      out.push({
        type, x, y,
        rot: rng() * Math.PI * 2,
        r: rng() * 1.5,
        c1: cfg.color1, c2: cfg.color2,
      });
      placed++;
    }
  }
  return out;
}

// Render a biome to a small display canvas. Uses a hidden offscreen
// canvas at full arena resolution, then drawImage's it down. This
// reuses drawBiomeScatter and drawCourtMarkings unchanged — we just
// temporarily redirect the global ctx.
const _biomePreviewOffscreen = (typeof document !== "undefined")
  ? document.createElement("canvas") : null;
if (_biomePreviewOffscreen) {
  _biomePreviewOffscreen.width = 1280;
  _biomePreviewOffscreen.height = 720;
}

function drawBiomePreview(displayCanvas, biome) {
  if (!displayCanvas || !biome || !_biomePreviewOffscreen) return;
  // Verify the offscreen canvas has a real 2D context with the methods we
  // need. In some test environments (e.g. node smoke harness) document
  // exists but createElement returns a mock without a working getContext.
  const offCtx = (typeof _biomePreviewOffscreen.getContext === "function")
    ? _biomePreviewOffscreen.getContext("2d") : null;
  if (!offCtx || typeof offCtx.createLinearGradient !== "function") return;
  // Same defensive check on the display canvas
  const dctx = (typeof displayCanvas.getContext === "function")
    ? displayCanvas.getContext("2d") : null;
  if (!dctx || typeof dctx.drawImage !== "function") return;

  const W = _biomePreviewOffscreen.width, H = _biomePreviewOffscreen.height;

  // Save the live ctx, redirect to offscreen
  const saved = ctx;
  ctx = offCtx;

  try {
    // Floor gradient — top→bottom
    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
    grad.addColorStop(0, biome.floorTop);
    grad.addColorStop(1, biome.floorBot);
    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Mowing stripes (subtle alternating bands)
    const stripeCount = 12;
    const stripeH = H / stripeCount;
    ctx.fillStyle = biome.stripeHue;
    for (let i = 0; i < stripeCount; i += 2) {
      ctx.fillRect(0, i * stripeH, W, stripeH);
    }

    // Court markings or generic field grid
    const lineColor = biome.accents.lineColor;
    const brandColor = biome.accents.brandColor;
    if (biome.courtType) {
      drawCourtMarkings(W, H, biome.courtType, lineColor);
    } else {
      // Light grid + center vertical line + center circle (not the full
      // arena treatment, but visually consistent enough for thumbnails)
      ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)";
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      for (let i = 0; i < W; i += 60) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(i, 0); ctx.lineTo(i, H); ctx.stroke();
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < H; i += 60) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, i); ctx.lineTo(W, i); ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(W/2, 0); ctx.lineTo(W/2, H); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W/2, H/2, 70, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
    }

    // Scatter (full sprite library — drawBiomeScatter handles all 29 types)
    const scatter = _generatePreviewScatter(biome, W, H);
    drawBiomeScatter(scatter);

    // Vignette / sky tint to give the preview some depth
    const vg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W/2, H/2, Math.min(W,H) * 0.4, W/2, H/2, Math.max(W,H) * 0.7);
    vg.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
    vg.addColorStop(1, biome.skyTint);
    ctx.fillStyle = vg;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  } finally {
    // Always restore the live ctx, even if drawing threw
    ctx = saved;
  }

  // Now downsample to the display canvas (dctx was hoisted above)
  dctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
  dctx.imageSmoothingQuality = "high";
  dctx.clearRect(0, 0, displayCanvas.width, displayCanvas.height);
  dctx.drawImage(_biomePreviewOffscreen, 0, 0, displayCanvas.width, displayCanvas.height);
}
window.drawBiomePreview = drawBiomePreview;

</script>

<!-- ============================================================
     UI — Part B
     Event handlers, modals, cloud sync, main loop, bootstrap
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
'use strict';

/* ============================================================
   SPORTSCASTER — AI ENHANCEMENT VIA ai-text-plugin
   When the plugin is present (imported in perchance-top.txt as
   `aiTextPlugin = {import:ai-text-plugin}`), live commentary
   lines fire FIRST from the static phrase pools so the user
   never waits, then we fire-and-forget an AI generation that
   may swap in a more dynamic line if it returns in time.

   Architecture:
   - Static line shows immediately on commentate() call
   - If aiTextPlugin is callable, we issue a request with the
     event context as a prompt, and on success replace the
     entry's `line` field + re-render.
   - We rate-limit (no more than 1 AI request per 1500ms) so
     bursty events don't hammer the plugin.
   - We cache by prompt-hash so repeated identical events
     (e.g. multiple first-kills across seasons) don't re-prompt.
   ============================================================ */
const AI_CACHE = new Map();
let AI_LAST_FIRE_AT = 0;
const AI_RATE_LIMIT_MS = 1500;

function _aiPromptFor(ctx) {
  // Build a tight, controlled prompt. We want short outputs (< 18 words).
  if (!ctx || !ctx.type) return null;
  if (ctx.type === "matchOpen") {
    return `You are a sportscaster for "Float Knights" — a cute mascot tournament where animals in armor fight with futuristic weapons. Write ONE excited 12-word opener for ${ctx.teamA.name} versus ${ctx.teamB.name}. No quotes. Punchy. Sportscaster voice.`;
  }
  if (ctx.type === "firstKill") {
    return `Sportscaster voice. ${ctx.attacker.name} from team ${ctx.attacker.teamId.toUpperCase()} just scored the FIRST KILL of the match against ${ctx.defender.name}. Write ONE line, under 14 words, hyped, no quotes.`;
  }
  if (ctx.type === "lastKnight") {
    return `Sportscaster voice. ${ctx.knight.name} is the LAST knight standing for team ${ctx.team.short}, against a full enemy team. ONE line, under 14 words, dramatic, no quotes.`;
  }
  if (ctx.type === "matchEnd") {
    const margin = Math.abs(ctx.score[0] - ctx.score[1]);
    const tone = margin >= 3 ? "dominant" : "close";
    return `Sportscaster voice. ${ctx.winner.name} just beat ${ctx.loser.name} ${ctx.score[0]}-${ctx.score[1]} (${tone} win). ONE line, under 14 words, no quotes.`;
  }
  return null;
}

// Override the no-op stub from the sim layer with the real implementation
// that uses the ai-text-plugin if it's been imported at the perchance-top.
function requestAiCommentaryEnhancement(entry, ctx) {
  if (typeof aiTextPlugin === "undefined") return;
  if (typeof aiTextPlugin !== "object" && typeof aiTextPlugin !== "function") return;
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - AI_LAST_FIRE_AT < AI_RATE_LIMIT_MS) return;
  AI_LAST_FIRE_AT = now;

  const prompt = _aiPromptFor(ctx);
  if (!prompt) return;

  // Cache hit — use immediately
  const cacheKey = ctx.type + "::" + (ctx.attacker?.name || ctx.knight?.name || ctx.winner?.name || "x");
  if (AI_CACHE.has(cacheKey)) {
    entry.line = AI_CACHE.get(cacheKey);
    entry.isAi = true;
    if (typeof renderCommentary === "function") renderCommentary();
    return;
  }

  // Fire-and-forget. The plugin's actual API: aiTextPlugin.getResponse(prompt)
  // returns a Promise<string> (per Perchance docs). Some versions are
  // callable directly. Be defensive.
  let req;
  try {
    if (typeof aiTextPlugin === "function") {
      req = aiTextPlugin(prompt);
    } else if (typeof aiTextPlugin.getResponse === "function") {
      req = aiTextPlugin.getResponse(prompt);
    } else if (typeof aiTextPlugin.generate === "function") {
      req = aiTextPlugin.generate({ instruction: prompt });
    } else {
      return;
    }
  } catch (e) { return; }

  if (!req || typeof req.then !== "function") return;

  req.then(out => {
    if (!out) return;
    let text = typeof out === "string" ? out : (out.text || out.output || out.response || null);
    if (!text) return;
    text = String(text).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
    if (text.length > 200) text = text.slice(0, 200);
    if (!text) return;
    AI_CACHE.set(cacheKey, text);
    entry.line = text;
    entry.isAi = true;
    if (typeof renderCommentary === "function") renderCommentary();
  }).catch(() => { /* swallow; static line stays */ });
}

/* ============================================================
   SPEED CONTROL
   ============================================================ */
let SPEED = 1;
let lastTick = 0;
let pendingTickAcc = 0;

function setSpeed(s) {
  SPEED = s;
  $$(".controls .grp button").forEach(btn => {
    btn.classList.toggle("active", parseFloat(btn.dataset.spd) === s);
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   MODAL SYSTEM
   ============================================================ */
let MODAL_AFTER_CLOSE = null;

function openModal(title, bodyHTML, footerHTML, onCloseCallback) {
  $("#modalTitle").textContent = title;
  $("#modalBody").innerHTML = bodyHTML;
  $("#modalFooter").innerHTML = footerHTML || "";
  $("#modalBack").classList.add("show");
  MODAL_AFTER_CLOSE = onCloseCallback || null;
}
function closeModal() {
  $("#modalBack").classList.remove("show");
  const cb = MODAL_AFTER_CLOSE;
  MODAL_AFTER_CLOSE = null;
  if (cb) cb();
}

$("#modalClose").addEventListener("click", closeModal);
$("#modalBack").addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  if (e.target.id === "modalBack") closeModal();
});

/* ============================================================
   INTERMISSION OVERLAY (after each match)
   ============================================================ */
/* ============================================================
   ROUND INTERMISSION — non-blocking
   Posts a summary to the commentary feed, fires toast notifications,
   and schedules an auto-advance countdown. User can manually skip
   via the countdown pill in the arena header.
   ============================================================ */
function showRoundIntermission() {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;
  const lastRound = m.rounds[m.rounds.length - 1];
  if (!lastRound) return;
  const winner = TEAM_BY_ID[lastRound.winner];

  // Summary in feed
  feed("match", `📊 Match score after R${m.currentRoundIndex}: ${TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId].short} ${m.roundsWonA}–${m.roundsWonB} ${TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId].short}.`);

  // Permanent casualties this round → individual toasts
  const thisRoundCasualties = LEAGUE.graveyard
    .filter(g => g.season === LEAGUE.season && g.slot === m.slot && g.round === m.currentRoundIndex);
  for (const c of thisRoundCasualties) {
    toast(`☠️ <b>${escapeHTML(c.name)}</b> (${TEAM_BY_ID[c.teamId].short}) ran out of lives. Slain by ${escapeHTML(c.killer)}.`, "lose", { ico: "☠️", ttl: 8000 });
  }

  // === v28.5: refresh UI at intermission start ===
  // This unlocks the bet button (round phase ended → betweenRounds),
  // refreshes the odds rail with post-round live odds, and updates
  // the bet history readout. Without this the UI stays in "round live"
  // state until the next round begins.
  if (typeof renderAll === "function") renderAll();

  // Schedule next round (auto-advances after 60s if AUTOPLAY enabled)
  scheduleAutoAdvance("round", () => {
    continueAfterRound();
    renderAll();
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   MATCH INTERMISSION — non-blocking
   ============================================================ */
function showIntermission() {
  const m = LEAGUE.match;
  if (!m) return;
  const winner = TEAM_BY_ID[m.winnerId];
  const isFinal = m.slot === "final";
  // Generalized slot label — handles 4-team (semi/final), 8-team (qf/sf/final),
  // and 12-team (qualifier/qf/sf/final) brackets without crashing on unknowns.
  const slotLabels = {
    "qual1": "Qualifier 1", "qual2": "Qualifier 2", "qual3": "Qualifier 3", "qual4": "Qualifier 4",
    "qf1": "Quarterfinal 1", "qf2": "Quarterfinal 2", "qf3": "Quarterfinal 3", "qf4": "Quarterfinal 4",
    "sf1": "Semifinal 1", "sf2": "Semifinal 2",
    "semi1": "Semifinal 1", "semi2": "Semifinal 2",
    "final": "Final",
  };
  const matchLabel = slotLabels[m.slot] || (m.slot ? m.slot.toUpperCase() : "Match");

  if (winner) {
    // Match-end toast: green if user won bets on this team
    toast(`🏆 ${winner.emoji} <b>${escapeHTML(winner.name)}</b> win ${matchLabel} ${m.roundsWonA}–${m.roundsWonB}.`, "info", { ico: "🏆", ttl: 8000 });
  } else {
    // Draw — no winner, but match still finalized (advancement was coin-flipped).
    const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamAId], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamBId];
    const taTxt = ta ? `${ta.emoji} ${escapeHTML(ta.short)}` : "?";
    const tbTxt = tb ? `${tb.emoji} ${escapeHTML(tb.short)}` : "?";
    toast(`🤝 ${matchLabel} ends in a DRAW — ${taTxt} ${m.roundsWonA}–${m.roundsWonB} ${tbTxt}. Advancement decided by coin-flip.`, "warn", { ico: "🤝", ttl: 8000 });
  }

  if (isFinal) {
    // Season end will trigger from finalizeSeason; auto-schedule there.
    scheduleAutoAdvance("match", () => {
      continueAfterMatch();
      renderAll();
    });
  } else {
    scheduleAutoAdvance("match", () => {
      continueAfterMatch();
      renderAll();
    });
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   SEASON-END MODAL (champion banner)
   ============================================================ */
function showSeasonEnd(championId, runnerupId, casualtyCount) {
  const champ = TEAM_BY_ID[championId];
  // Big celebratory toast (longer TTL)
  toast(`🏆 SEASON ${LEAGUE.season} CHAMPIONS: ${champ.emoji} <b>${escapeHTML(champ.name)}</b>! ${casualtyCount} knights buried this season.`, "info", { ico: "🏆", ttl: 10000 });
  // Schedule next season
  scheduleAutoAdvance("season", () => {
    beginNextSeason();
    renderAll();
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   PERSONAL SANDBAG PICKER MODAL (v28.1, ROADMAP 3.9)
   Shows the opposing team's living roster as a clickable list. On
   click, calls buyTargetedItem(itemId, knightId) to apply the
   per-knight boost. Closes immediately on selection.
   ============================================================ */
function openSandbagPicker(item, am) {
  if (!item || !am || !LEAGUE.bracket || !LEAGUE.currentSlot) return;
  // Opposing team is the one we ARE NOT (am.teamA is "us" by convention)
  const oppId = am.teamB;
  const opp = TEAM_BY_ID[oppId];
  if (!opp) return;
  const roster = LEAGUE.rosters[oppId].filter(p => !p.deceased);

  let html = `
    <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--dim);margin-bottom:10px;line-height:1.5;">
      Pick ONE knight on <b>${opp.emoji} ${escapeHTML(opp.name)}</b> to sandbag for the upcoming match.
      Their SPD will drop by <b>0.8</b>. Quiet, surgical, and deniable. Cost: <b>${item.cost} bits</b>.
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--dim);margin-bottom:8px;">
      Your bits: <b style="color:var(--gold);">${LEAGUE.bits}</b>
    </div>
  `;
  if (LEAGUE.bits < item.cost) {
    html += `<div style="color:var(--red);font-size:11px;text-align:center;padding:10px;">Insufficient bits.</div>`;
  }
  html += `<div class="sandbag-pick-list">`;
  for (const p of roster) {
    const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
    const tooSlow = p.stats.spd <= 1.5;
    html += `
      <button class="sandbag-pick-row" data-knight-id="${p.id}" ${LEAGUE.bits < item.cost ? "disabled" : ""}>
        <span class="sb-pos">${pos.icon}</span>
        <span class="sb-name">${escapeHTML(p.name)}</span>
        <span class="sb-stats">${pos.label} · SPD ${p.stats.spd.toFixed(1)}${tooSlow ? " (already slow)" : ""}</span>
      </button>
    `;
  }
  html += `</div>`;

  openModal(`⚓ ${item.nm}`, html, `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Cancel</button>`);

  // Wire each row to call buyTargetedItem
  setTimeout(() => {
    const rows = document.querySelectorAll(".sandbag-pick-row");
    rows.forEach(row => {
      row.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const knightId = +row.getAttribute("data-knight-id");
        const ok = buyTargetedItem(item.id, knightId);
        if (ok) {
          closeModal();
          if (typeof renderAll === "function") renderAll();
          if (typeof toast === "function") {
            const knight = LEAGUE.rosters[oppId].find(p => p.id === knightId);
            toast(`Personal sandbag placed on <b>${knight?.name || "?"}</b>.`, "info");
          }
        }
      });
    });
  }, 10);
}
window.openSandbagPicker = openSandbagPicker;

/* ============================================================
   PLAYER PROFILE MODAL
   ============================================================ */
function showPlayerProfile(p) {
  const team = TEAM_BY_ID[p.teamId];
  const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
  const status = p.deceased
    ? `<span style="color:var(--red);">⚰ DECEASED · S${p.deceasedSeason} · slain by ${escapeHTML(p.deceasedBy || "?")}</span>`
    : `<span style="color:var(--grn);">ACTIVE</span>`;
  const w = p.weapon, a = p.armor, it = p.item;
  const gearRow = (g, slotLabel) => {
    if (!g) return "";
    const dn = g.displayName || g.name;
    const scavenged = !!g.displayName;
    const scavCount = g.scavengedCount || 0;
    const legendary = scavCount >= 3;
    const badge = legendary
      ? `<span style="color:var(--pur);font-size:10px;font-weight:700;">⭐LEGENDARY (${scavCount}×)</span>`
      : scavenged ? `<span style="color:var(--gold);font-size:9px;">● scavenged</span>` : "";
    return `<tr>
      <th>${slotLabel}</th>
      <td>${g.ico || "·"} ${escapeHTML(dn)} ${badge}</td>
    </tr>`;
  };

  // K/D ratio
  const kills = p.career.kills || 0;
  const deaths = p.career.deaths || 0;
  const kd = deaths === 0 ? (kills > 0 ? "∞" : "0.00") : (kills / deaths).toFixed(2);

  // Kill log: who they killed (search graveyard for entries killed by p.name)
  const victims = LEAGUE.graveyard
    .filter(g => g.killer === p.name || g.killer?.startsWith(p.name + " "))
    .slice(-8)
    .reverse();
  const victimList = victims.length ? victims.map(v => {
    const vt = TEAM_BY_ID[v.teamId];
    return `<li>${vt.emoji} ${escapeHTML(v.name)} <span style="color:var(--dim);">(S${v.season} R${v.round || "?"})</span></li>`;
  }).join("") : `<li style="color:var(--dim);font-style:italic;">no recorded victims</li>`;

  // Streak section
  const longestStreak = p.career.longestStreak || 0;
  const currentStreak = p.career.currentStreak || 0;
  const streakHTML = currentStreak >= 3
    ? `<div style="margin-top:8px;text-align:center;background:rgba(247,201,72,0.1);padding:8px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid rgba(247,201,72,0.3);">
         🔥 <b>ON A ${currentStreak}-KILL STREAK</b>
       </div>` : "";

  const body = `
    <div style="text-align:center;font-size:48px;">${pos.icon}</div>
    <div style="text-align:center;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;">${escapeHTML(p.name)}</div>
    <div style="text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--dim);margin-bottom:14px;">
      ${team.emoji} ${escapeHTML(team.name)} · ${pos.label} · Debut S${p.debutSeason}
    </div>
    ${streakHTML}
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;">
      <div style="background:var(--bg-2);padding:8px;border-radius:4px;text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);">KILLS</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:var(--gold);">${kills}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background:var(--bg-2);padding:8px;border-radius:4px;text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);">DEATHS</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:var(--red);">${deaths}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background:var(--bg-2);padding:8px;border-radius:4px;text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);">K/D</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;">${kd}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background:var(--bg-2);padding:8px;border-radius:4px;text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);">LONGEST STREAK</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:${longestStreak >= 5 ? "var(--gold)" : "var(--ink)"};">${longestStreak}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <table class="dt" style="margin-top:10px;">
      <tr><th>Status</th><td>${status}</td></tr>
      ${gearRow(w, "Weapon")}
      ${gearRow(a, "Armor")}
      ${gearRow(it, "Item")}
      <tr><th>HP / DMG / DEF / SPD</th><td class="num">${p.stats.hp} / ${p.stats.dmg} / ${p.stats.def} / ${p.stats.spd.toFixed(2)}</td></tr>
      <tr><th>Crit</th><td class="num">${(p.stats.crit * 100).toFixed(1)}%</td></tr>
      <tr><th>Scavenged</th><td class="num">${p.career.scavenged || 0}</td></tr>
      <tr><th>Matches won / played</th><td class="num">${p.career.matchesWon} / ${p.career.matchesPlayed}</td></tr>
      <tr><th>Seasons played</th><td class="num">${p.career.seasonsPlayed}</td></tr>
    </table>
    ${victims.length ? `
      <h4 style="margin:14px 0 6px;font-size:11px;color:var(--gold);letter-spacing:1px;">RECENT VICTIMS</h4>
      <ul style="margin:0;padding-left:20px;font-size:11px;line-height:1.6;color:var(--ink);">${victimList}</ul>
    ` : ""}
    <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);margin-top:10px;text-align:center;">
      <span style="color:var(--gold);">●</span> scavenged ·
      <span style="color:var(--pur);">⭐</span> legendary (3+ scavenges)
    </div>
  `;
  const title = p.deceased ? "⚰ Knight Memorial" : "Knight Profile";
  openModal(title, body, `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Close</button>`);
}

/* ============================================================
   KNIGHT MEMORIAL MODAL (ROADMAP 2.4)
   Extended career obituary for deceased knights. Shows full weapon
   lineage (every gear piece ever wielded), seasons played, victims,
   and the cause of death. Used by graveyard click handler.
   ============================================================ */
function showKnightMemorial(p) {
  if (!p) return;
  // For ALIVE knights, fall back to regular profile
  if (!p.deceased) { showPlayerProfile(p); return; }

  const team = TEAM_BY_ID[p.teamId];
  const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
  const debutSeason = p.debutSeason || 1;
  const deceasedSeason = p.deceasedSeason || LEAGUE.season;
  const seasonsPlayed = Math.max(1, deceasedSeason - debutSeason + 1);
  const kills = p.career.kills || 0;
  const deaths = p.career.deaths || 0;
  const longestStreak = p.career.longestStreak || 0;
  const scavenged = p.career.scavenged || 0;

  // Find all victims from the graveyard
  const victims = LEAGUE.graveyard
    .filter(g => g.killer === p.name || (g.killer && g.killer.startsWith && g.killer.startsWith(p.name + " ")))
    .reverse();

  // Find this knight's own death entry in the graveyard (most recent)
  const obit = LEAGUE.graveyard.slice().reverse().find(g => g.name === p.name && g.teamId === p.teamId);

  // Gear lineage from career.gearLog (logged on each scavenge)
  const lineage = (p.career.gearLog || []).slice();

  let body = `
    <div style="text-align:center;font-size:48px;line-height:1;margin-bottom:4px;">⚰</div>
    <div style="text-align:center;font-size:15px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.5px;">
      ${escapeHTML(p.name)}
    </div>
    <div style="text-align:center;font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);margin-bottom:14px;">
      ${team.emoji} ${escapeHTML(team.name)} · ${pos.icon} ${pos.label}
    </div>
    <div style="background:var(--bg-2);padding:10px;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid var(--line);margin-bottom:12px;">
      <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--gold);letter-spacing:1px;margin-bottom:4px;">EPITAPH</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;font-style:italic;line-height:1.5;">
        ${obit
          ? `Slain in S${obit.season}${obit.round ? ` Round ${obit.round}` : ""}${obit.slot ? ` of the ${obit.slot}` : ""}<br>by <b>${escapeHTML(obit.killer || "unknown")}</b>${obit.weapon ? ` with a ${escapeHTML(obit.weapon)}` : ""}.`
          : `Lost in S${deceasedSeason}.`}
      </div>
    </div>

    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:6px;margin-bottom:12px;">
      <div style="background:var(--bg-2);padding:8px;border-radius:4px;text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--dim);letter-spacing:0.5px;">SEASONS</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:var(--ink);">${seasonsPlayed}</div>
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--dim);">S${debutSeason}–S${deceasedSeason}</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background:var(--bg-2);padding:8px;border-radius:4px;text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--dim);letter-spacing:0.5px;">KILLS</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:var(--gold);">${kills}</div>
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--dim);">${deaths} K/D loss</div>
      </div>
      <div style="background:var(--bg-2);padding:8px;border-radius:4px;text-align:center;">
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--dim);letter-spacing:0.5px;">LONGEST STREAK</div>
        <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:${longestStreak >= 10 ? "var(--gold)" : "var(--ink)"};">${longestStreak}</div>
        <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--dim);">${longestStreak >= 10 ? "🔥 elite" : "career-best"}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  // === Weapon lineage section ===
  body += `<h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px;color:var(--gold);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;">⚔ WEAPON LINEAGE</h3>`;
  // Always include the starting gear as the first row
  if (p.startingWeapon) {
    body += `<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--dim);margin-bottom:4px;">
      Debuted with <b style="color:var(--ink);">${p.startingWeapon.ico || "·"} ${escapeHTML(p.startingWeapon.name)}</b>
    </div>`;
  }
  if (lineage.length > 0) {
    body += `<table class="dt" style="font-size:10px;">
      <tr><th>Season</th><th>Slot</th><th>Gear</th><th>Lineage</th></tr>`;
    for (const g of lineage) {
      body += `<tr>
        <td>S${g.season}</td>
        <td>${g.slot}</td>
        <td>${g.ico || "·"} ${escapeHTML(g.to)}</td>
        <td style="color:var(--dim);font-size:9px;">${(g.scavenged || 0) >= 3 ? `<span style="color:var(--pur);">⭐ ${g.scavenged}×</span>` : g.scavenged + "×"}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }
    body += `</table>`;
  } else {
    body += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);font-style:italic;">No scavenged gear during career.</div>`;
  }

  // === Victims section ===
  body += `<h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px;color:var(--red);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;">⚔ FELLED IN BATTLE (${victims.length})</h3>`;
  if (victims.length > 0) {
    body += `<ul style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);padding-left:16px;margin:0;">`;
    for (const v of victims.slice(0, 20)) {
      const vt = TEAM_BY_ID[v.teamId];
      body += `<li>${vt ? vt.emoji : "·"} ${escapeHTML(v.name)} <span style="color:var(--dim);">(S${v.season}${v.round ? ` R${v.round}` : ""})</span></li>`;
    }
    if (victims.length > 20) body += `<li style="font-style:italic;">…and ${victims.length - 20} more.</li>`;
    body += `</ul>`;
  } else {
    body += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);font-style:italic;">No recorded kills.</div>`;
  }

  body += `<div style="text-align:center;font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);margin-top:14px;font-style:italic;">
    "Three balloons. Three lives. One legacy."
  </div>`;

  openModal("⚰ Memorial — " + p.name, body, `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Close</button>`);
}
window.showKnightMemorial = showKnightMemorial;

/* ============================================================
   TEAMS DIRECTORY MODAL
   ============================================================ */
function showTeams() {
  let html = "";
  for (const t of TEAMS) {
    const r = LEAGUE.records[t.id];
    const roster = LEAGUE.rosters[t.id];
    const aliveCount = roster.filter(p => !p.deceased).length;
    html += `
      <div style="margin-bottom:14px;">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding-bottom:6px;margin-bottom:6px;">
          <span style="font-size:24px;">${t.emoji}</span>
          <div style="flex:1;">
            <div style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHTML(t.name)}</div>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--dim);">${r.wins}–${r.losses} · ${r.championships}🏆 · ${aliveCount}/5 alive</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <table class="dt">
          <tr><th>Pos</th><th>Name</th><th>HP</th><th>DMG</th><th>DEF</th><th>SPD</th><th>K</th></tr>
          ${roster.map(p => {
            const pos = POS_BY_ID[p.position];
            const cls = p.deceased ? `style="color:var(--mute);text-decoration:line-through;"` : "";
            return `<tr ${cls}>
              <td>${pos.icon}</td>
              <td>${escapeHTML(p.name)}</td>
              <td class="num">${p.stats.hp}</td>
              <td class="num">${p.stats.dmg}</td>
              <td class="num">${p.stats.def}</td>
              <td class="num">${p.stats.spd.toFixed(1)}</td>
              <td class="num">${p.career.kills}</td>
            </tr>`;
          }).join("")}
        </table>
      </div>
    `;
  }
  openModal("Teams", html, `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Close</button>`);
}

/* ============================================================
   HISTORY MODAL
   ============================================================ */
function showHistory() {
  if (!LEAGUE.history.length && (!LEAGUE.matchHistory || !LEAGUE.matchHistory.length)) {
    openModal("History", `<div style="color:var(--dim);text-align:center;padding:20px;">No completed seasons yet.</div>`,
      `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Close</button>`);
    return;
  }
  let html = "";

  // Season-level history
  if (LEAGUE.history.length) {
    html += `<h3 style="margin:0 0 8px;color:var(--gold);font-size:13px;letter-spacing:1px;">SEASONS</h3>`;
    html += `<table class="dt">
      <tr><th>S</th><th>Champion</th><th>Runner-up</th><th>Casualties</th></tr>`;
    for (const h of LEAGUE.history.slice().reverse()) {
      const c = TEAM_BY_ID[h.champion], r = TEAM_BY_ID[h.runnerup];
      html += `<tr>
        <td>${h.season}</td>
        <td>${c.emoji} ${escapeHTML(c.short)}</td>
        <td>${r.emoji} ${escapeHTML(r.short)}</td>
        <td class="num">${h.casualties}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }
    html += `</table>`;
  }

  // Match-level history (with seeds — proves determinism, useful for replay)
  if (LEAGUE.matchHistory && LEAGUE.matchHistory.length) {
    html += `<h3 style="margin:14px 0 8px;color:var(--gold);font-size:13px;letter-spacing:1px;">MATCH LOG</h3>`;
    html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);margin-bottom:6px;">Each match is identified by a deterministic seed — same seed always plays out identically.</div>`;
    html += `<table class="dt" style="font-size:11px;">
      <tr><th>S</th><th>Slot</th><th>Matchup</th><th>Score</th><th>Winner</th><th>K☠</th><th style="font-family:'Courier New',monospace;">Seed</th></tr>`;
    for (const mh of LEAGUE.matchHistory.slice().reverse().slice(0, 30)) {
      const w = TEAM_BY_ID[mh.winner];
      const ta = TEAM_BY_ID[mh.teamA], tb = TEAM_BY_ID[mh.teamB];
      const seedHex = "0x" + (mh.seed >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
      html += `<tr>
        <td>${mh.season}</td>
        <td>${escapeHTML(mh.slot)}</td>
        <td>${ta.emoji} vs ${tb.emoji}</td>
        <td>${mh.score}</td>
        <td>${w.emoji} ${escapeHTML(w.short)}</td>
        <td class="num">${mh.casualties.length}</td>
        <td style="font-family:'Courier New',monospace;color:var(--dim);">${seedHex}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }
    html += `</table>`;
    if (LEAGUE.matchHistory.length > 30) {
      html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);text-align:center;margin-top:6px;">Showing 30 of ${LEAGUE.matchHistory.length} matches.</div>`;
    }
  }

  // Genesis seed footer
  if (LEAGUE.genesisSeed) {
    const gHex = "0x" + (LEAGUE.genesisSeed >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
    html += `<div style="margin-top:10px;font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);text-align:center;">League genesis seed: <code style="color:var(--gold);">${gHex}</code> — share this seed to let others run the same league.</div>`;
  }

  openModal("League History", html, `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Close</button>`);
}

/* ============================================================
   GRAVEYARD MODAL
   ============================================================ */
function showGraveyard() {
  if (!LEAGUE.graveyard.length) {
    openModal("Graveyard", `<div style="color:var(--dim);text-align:center;padding:20px;">No casualties yet. The carnage begins soon.</div>`,
      `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Close</button>`);
    return;
  }
  let html = `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);margin-bottom:6px;">Click a name to view their memorial.</div>`;
  html += `<table class="dt">
    <tr><th>S</th><th>R</th><th>Name</th><th>Team</th><th>Slain by</th></tr>`;
  for (const g of LEAGUE.graveyard.slice().reverse()) {
    const t = TEAM_BY_ID[g.teamId];
    // Find the actual roster entry (deceased players are still in roster, just .deceased=true)
    const rosterEntry = (LEAGUE.rosters[g.teamId] || []).find(p => p.name === g.name);
    const clickAttr = rosterEntry
      ? `onclick='openMemorialFor(${g.teamId ? `"${g.teamId}"` : "null"}, ${JSON.stringify(g.name)})' style="cursor:pointer;"`
      : "";
    html += `<tr ${clickAttr}>
      <td>S${g.season}</td>
      <td>${g.round || "—"}</td>
      <td><span style="color:var(--gold);text-decoration:underline;">${escapeHTML(g.name)}</span></td>
      <td>${t.emoji} ${escapeHTML(t.short)}</td>
      <td>${escapeHTML(g.killer)}</td>
    </tr>`;
  }
  html += `</table>`;

  // Lost-items section
  if (LEAGUE.lostItems && LEAGUE.lostItems.length) {
    html += `<h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px;color:var(--gold);font-size:12px;letter-spacing:1px;">📉 LOST LEGENDARY ITEMS</h3>`;
    html += `<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--dim);margin-bottom:6px;">Items left on the field at match end after 2+ scavenges. Forever lost.</div>`;
    html += `<table class="dt" style="font-size:11px;">
      <tr><th>S</th><th>Item</th><th>Scavenged</th><th>Last carrier</th></tr>`;
    for (const li of LEAGUE.lostItems.slice().reverse().slice(0, 20)) {
      html += `<tr>
        <td>S${li.season}</td>
        <td>${li.ico || "·"} ${escapeHTML(li.name)}</td>
        <td class="num">${li.scavengedCount}×</td>
        <td>${escapeHTML(li.lostBy)}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }
    html += `</table>`;
  }

  openModal("Graveyard (" + LEAGUE.graveyard.length + ")", html, `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Close</button>`);
}

// Helper used by inline graveyard onclick handlers
function openMemorialFor(teamId, name) {
  if (!teamId) return;
  const p = (LEAGUE.rosters[teamId] || []).find(x => x.name === name);
  if (!p) return;
  // === ROADMAP 2.4: Use full memorial modal for deceased knights ===
  if (p.deceased && typeof showKnightMemorial === "function") {
    showKnightMemorial(p);
  } else {
    showPlayerProfile(p);
  }
}
window.openMemorialFor = openMemorialFor;

/* ============================================================
   BRACKET MODAL (full-size view)
   ============================================================ */
function showBracket() {
  const b = LEAGUE.bracket;
  const matchHTML = (slot, label) => {
    const m = b[slot];
    const aT = m.teamA ? TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamA] : null;
    const bT = m.teamB ? TEAM_BY_ID[m.teamB] : null;
    const aCls = m.winner === m.teamA ? "winner" : (m.winner ? "loser" : "");
    const bCls = m.winner === m.teamB ? "winner" : (m.winner ? "loser" : "");
    const isCurrent = LEAGUE.currentSlot === slot;
    return `
      <div class="bracket-match ${isCurrent ? 'current' : ''} ${m.winner ? 'done' : ''}" style="margin:0;">
        <div class="label">${label}${isCurrent ? ' · LIVE' : ''}</div>
        <div class="team-row ${aCls}">
          <span class="em">${aT?.emoji || "❓"}</span>
          <span class="nm">${aT?.name || "TBD"}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="vs-divider">VS</div>
        <div class="team-row ${bCls}">
          <span class="em">${bT?.emoji || "❓"}</span>
          <span class="nm">${bT?.name || "TBD"}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  };
  const body = `
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:12px;">
      <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px;">
        ${matchHTML("semi1", "Semifinal 1")}
        ${matchHTML("semi2", "Semifinal 2")}
      </div>
      <div style="text-align:center;color:var(--dim);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;">↓</div>
      ${matchHTML("final", "Final")}
    </div>
  `;
  openModal(`Season ${LEAGUE.season} Bracket`, body, `<button onclick="closeModal()" class="secondary">Close</button>`);
}

/* ============================================================
   CLOUD SYNC (Perchance uploadPlugin)
   ============================================================ */
/* ============================================================
   CLOUD SYNC — schema v13 (v28.13 continental expansion)
   ============================================================
   The save payload has been completely rewritten for v28.13. With the
   20-team continental expansion, the bracket-size jump from 4→12, the
   12-effect stadium grid, and the prior-species lore retcon, the save
   format from any earlier version is no longer compatible — old saves
   reference team IDs that don't exist (or, conversely, miss team IDs
   the current League uses), and the bracket structure has changed
   shape twice since v: 6.

   Rather than ship layered backfills that paper over breakage, this
   version refuses saves with v < SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION outright and
   asks the user to reset their League. Anyone with a v: 13 sync code
   can pull cleanly; anyone with an older code gets a clear error
   message and a path forward (Reset League → start fresh).
   ============================================================ */
const SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 13;

function serializeLeague() {
  const cleanRosters = {};
  for (const tid of Object.keys(LEAGUE.rosters)) {
    cleanRosters[tid] = LEAGUE.rosters[tid].map(p => ({
      id: p.id,
      name: p.name,
      teamId: p.teamId,
      position: p.position,
      stats: p.stats,
      weapon: p.weapon,
      armor: p.armor,
      item: p.item,
      career: p.career,
      deceased: p.deceased,
      deceasedSeason: p.deceasedSeason,
      deceasedBy: p.deceasedBy,
      debutSeason: p.debutSeason,
    }));
  }
  return JSON.stringify({
    // === Schema metadata ===
    v: SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    gameVersion: "v28.13",        // displayed game version, for diagnostics
    savedAt: new Date().toISOString(), // wall-clock timestamp of the push
    teamPool: TEAMS.map(t => t.id), // snapshot of which teams existed at save time

    // === League state ===
    season: LEAGUE.season,
    bits: LEAGUE.bits,
    rosters: cleanRosters,
    graveyard: LEAGUE.graveyard,
    records: LEAGUE.records,
    bracket: LEAGUE.bracket,
    currentSlot: LEAGUE.currentSlot,
    history: LEAGUE.history,
    previousLadder: LEAGUE.previousLadder || {},
    matchHistory: LEAGUE.matchHistory,
    lostItems: LEAGUE.lostItems || [],
    genesisSeed: LEAGUE.genesisSeed,
    startedAt: LEAGUE.startedAt,
    lastTickAt: LEAGUE.lastTickAt,
    pendingBoosts: LEAGUE.pendingBoosts,
    pendingTargetedBoosts: LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts || {},
    bets: LEAGUE.bets,

    // === Tunables ===
    leagueConfig: typeof LEAGUE_CONFIG !== "undefined" ? LEAGUE_CONFIG : null,
    autoplay: typeof AUTOPLAY !== "undefined" ? {
      cooldownRoundMs: AUTOPLAY.cooldownRoundMs,
      cooldownMatchMs: AUTOPLAY.cooldownMatchMs,
      cooldownSeasonMs: AUTOPLAY.cooldownSeasonMs,
      enabled: AUTOPLAY.enabled,
    } : null,
    matchCfg: typeof MATCH !== "undefined" ? {
      bestOf: MATCH.bestOf,
      roundsToWin: MATCH.roundsToWin,
      startingLives: MATCH.startingLives,
    } : null,

    // === User identity ===
    syncName: ($("#syncName") && $("#syncName").value) || "",
  });
}

function deserializeLeague(jsonStr) {
  const data = JSON.parse(jsonStr);

  // === Hard version gate ===
  // The schema was rewritten in v28.13 (continental expansion + lore retcon).
  // Saves from earlier versions reference team IDs and bracket structures
  // that no longer match the current League. Rather than ship layered
  // backfills, we refuse outright.
  const v = data.v || 0;
  if (v < SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    throw new Error(
      "This sync code is from an older League format (v" + v + "; current is v" +
      SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION + "). The League schema was rewritten for the v28.13 " +
      "Continental Expansion. Please reset your League and start fresh — old " +
      "saves are not compatible with the current 20-team format."
    );
  }
  if (v > SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    // Forward-compat: a save from a future version. Try to load and let the
    // user discover any incompatibilities — but warn them.
    console.warn(
      "Sync code is from a newer League format (v" + v +
      "; this client is v" + SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION + "). Loading anyway."
    );
  }

  // === Core League state ===
  LEAGUE.season = data.season || 1;
  LEAGUE.bits = data.bits || 0;

  // Rehydrate rosters with default match-time fields. The match-time fields
  // (x/y/vx/vy, hp, alive, target, cooldowns, stunned, balloons popped) are
  // always reset on load — they only matter mid-match and a sync code is
  // expected to be loaded between matches.
  LEAGUE.rosters = {};
  for (const tid of Object.keys(data.rosters || {})) {
    LEAGUE.rosters[tid] = data.rosters[tid].map(p => ({
      ...p,
      x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0,
      hp: p.stats ? p.stats.hp : 100,
      alive: !p.deceased,
      target: null,
      attackCooldown: 0,
      facing: 1,
      stunned: 0,
      itemUsed: false,
      boostMods: null,
      balloonsPopped: 0,
    }));
  }

  // === Team-pool reconciliation ===
  // If the current TEAMS pool has teams the save doesn't, backfill empty
  // rosters/records for them so the rest of the code (Roster tab, ladder,
  // bracket draws) doesn't crash on missing keys. This handles forward-pool
  // drift (someone pulls a save right after a team-expansion patch).
  for (const team of TEAMS) {
    if (!LEAGUE.rosters[team.id]) {
      LEAGUE.rosters[team.id] = POSITIONS.map(p => makePlayer(team.id, p));
    }
  }

  LEAGUE.graveyard = data.graveyard || [];
  LEAGUE.records = data.records || {};

  // Backfill records for any team the save didn't cover
  for (const team of TEAMS) {
    if (!LEAGUE.records[team.id]) {
      LEAGUE.records[team.id] = { wins: 0, losses: 0, draws: 0, championships: 0 };
    }
  }

  LEAGUE.bracket = data.bracket || null;
  LEAGUE.currentSlot = data.currentSlot || null;

  // If the loaded bracket references teams that don't exist in the current
  // TEAMS pool, the save is from a divergent fork — rebuild the bracket
  // from current TEAMS rather than crash on missing references mid-match.
  if (LEAGUE.bracket && LEAGUE.bracket._order) {
    const currentTeamIds = new Set(TEAMS.map(t => t.id));
    let bracketHasGhostTeams = false;
    for (const slotKey of LEAGUE.bracket._order) {
      const slot = LEAGUE.bracket[slotKey];
      if (slot) {
        if (slot.teamA && !currentTeamIds.has(slot.teamA)) { bracketHasGhostTeams = true; break; }
        if (slot.teamB && !currentTeamIds.has(slot.teamB)) { bracketHasGhostTeams = true; break; }
      }
    }
    if (bracketHasGhostTeams) {
      console.warn("Loaded bracket references teams not in current pool; rebuilding bracket from current TEAMS.");
      buildBracket();
    }
  }

  // Restore tunables — only fields known to current LEAGUE_CONFIG/AUTOPLAY/MATCH
  if (data.leagueConfig && typeof LEAGUE_CONFIG !== "undefined") {
    Object.assign(LEAGUE_CONFIG, data.leagueConfig);
  }
  if (data.autoplay && typeof AUTOPLAY !== "undefined") {
    Object.assign(AUTOPLAY, data.autoplay);
  }
  if (data.matchCfg && typeof MATCH !== "undefined") {
    Object.assign(MATCH, data.matchCfg);
  }

  LEAGUE.history = data.history || [];
  LEAGUE.previousLadder = data.previousLadder || {};
  LEAGUE.matchHistory = data.matchHistory || [];
  LEAGUE.lostItems = data.lostItems || [];
  LEAGUE.genesisSeed = data.genesisSeed || ((Math.random() * 0xFFFFFFFF) >>> 0);
  LEAGUE.startedAt = data.startedAt || Date.now();
  LEAGUE.lastTickAt = data.lastTickAt || Date.now();
  LEAGUE.pendingBoosts = data.pendingBoosts || {};
  LEAGUE.pendingTargetedBoosts = data.pendingTargetedBoosts || {};
  LEAGUE.bets = data.bets || [];
  LEAGUE.match = null;

  // Recompute _idCounter from highest player id so newly minted knights
  // don't collide with existing ones.
  let maxId = 0;
  for (const r of Object.values(LEAGUE.rosters)) {
    for (const p of r) if (p.id > maxId) maxId = p.id;
  }
  _idCounter = maxId + 1;

  if ($("#syncName") && data.syncName) $("#syncName").value = data.syncName;
}

function setSyncStatus(msg, kind) {
  const el = $("#syncStatus");
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.className = "status" + (kind ? " " + kind : "");
}

async function pushSave() {
  // Per the Perchance upload-plugin API: `uploadPlugin` is imported as a
  // single CALLABLE function. Signature: `await uploadPlugin(blob)` returns
  // `{ url, size, error }`.
  if (typeof uploadPlugin !== "function") {
    setSyncStatus("uploadPlugin not loaded — top editor needs `uploadPlugin = {import:upload-plugin}`", "err");
    return;
  }
  const name = $("#syncName").value.trim();
  if (!name) {
    setSyncStatus("Pick a username first", "err");
    return;
  }
  setSyncStatus("Saving…");
  try {
    const json = serializeLeague();
    const sizeKb = (json.length / 1024).toFixed(1);
    // uploadPlugin expects a Blob. Wrap our JSON string with the right MIME.
    const blob = new Blob([json], { type: "application/json" });
    const result = await uploadPlugin(blob);
    if (!result) {
      setSyncStatus("Upload returned nothing", "err");
      return;
    }
    if (result.error) {
      setSyncStatus("Upload failed: " + result.error, "err");
      return;
    }
    if (!result.url) {
      setSyncStatus("Upload succeeded but returned no URL", "err");
      return;
    }
    $("#syncCode").value = result.url;
    try { localStorage.setItem("fk_syncUrl", result.url); } catch {}
    try { localStorage.setItem("fk_syncName", name); } catch {}
    setSyncStatus(
      "Saved (" + sizeKb + " KB · schema v" + SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION + ").",
      "ok"
    );
  } catch (e) {
    setSyncStatus("Save error: " + (e.message || e), "err");
  }
}

async function pullSave() {
  const url = $("#syncCode").value.trim();
  if (!url) {
    setSyncStatus("Paste a sync code (URL) first", "err");
    return;
  }
  setSyncStatus("Loading…");
  try {
    const fetchFn = (typeof superFetch === "function") ? superFetch : fetch;
    const res = await fetchFn(url);
    if (!res.ok) {
      setSyncStatus("Fetch failed: HTTP " + res.status, "err");
      return;
    }
    const text = await res.text();

    // Peek at the schema version BEFORE deserialization mutates state, so we
    // can show a friendly version-mismatch message without leaving LEAGUE in
    // a half-rehydrated state. deserializeLeague itself also throws on old
    // schemas — this peek just lets us format a nicer message earlier.
    let peekVersion = null;
    let peekGameVersion = null;
    let peekSavedAt = null;
    try {
      const peek = JSON.parse(text);
      peekVersion = peek.v;
      peekGameVersion = peek.gameVersion;
      peekSavedAt = peek.savedAt;
    } catch {}

    if (peekVersion != null && peekVersion < SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
      const fromGame = peekGameVersion ? " (from " + peekGameVersion + ")" : "";
      setSyncStatus(
        "This sync code is from an older League format" + fromGame + " (schema v" +
        peekVersion + " → current is v" + SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION + "). The format was " +
        "rewritten for the v28.13 Continental Expansion. Please Reset League and " +
        "start fresh — old saves are not compatible.",
        "err"
      );
      return;
    }

    deserializeLeague(text);

    try { localStorage.setItem("fk_syncUrl", url); } catch {}
    LEAGUE.match = null;
    if (LEAGUE.currentSlot) startMatch();
    renderAll();

    // Build a friendly success message
    const ageMsg = peekSavedAt ? (" · saved " + _formatRelativeTime(peekSavedAt)) : "";
    const verMsg = peekGameVersion ? (" · " + peekGameVersion) : "";
    setSyncStatus("Loaded successfully" + verMsg + ageMsg + ".", "ok");
  } catch (e) {
    setSyncStatus("Load failed: " + (e.message || e), "err");
  }
}

/* Format an ISO timestamp as a relative time ("3h ago", "2d ago"). Used in
   pull-status messages so users can see how recent the synced state is. */
function _formatRelativeTime(isoStr) {
  try {
    const then = new Date(isoStr).getTime();
    if (!then || isNaN(then)) return "";
    const ago = Date.now() - then;
    if (ago < 60000) return "just now";
    if (ago < 3600000) return Math.round(ago / 60000) + "m ago";
    if (ago < 86400000) return Math.round(ago / 3600000) + "h ago";
    if (ago < 86400000 * 30) return Math.round(ago / 86400000) + "d ago";
    return new Date(isoStr).toLocaleDateString();
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   EVENT BINDINGS
   ============================================================ */
function bindEvents() {
  // Speed buttons
  $$(".controls .grp button").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => setSpeed(parseFloat(btn.dataset.spd)));
  });

  // Skip button removed — game is fully autoplay now. Manual control via
  // speed buttons (4× to fast-forward) and AUTOPLAY toggle.

  $("#btnReset").addEventListener("click", () => {
    if (!confirm("Reset the entire league? All rosters, history, bits, and saved progress will be wiped.")) return;
    if (typeof clearLocal === "function") clearLocal();
    initLeague();
    LEAGUE.match = null;
    FEED.length = 0;
    feed("season", "🚨 League reset. New season begins.");
    startMatch();
    renderAll();
  });

  // Top-level tab navigation (Game / Bet / Store / League / Settings / About)
  $$(".nav-tabs button").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      switchTab(btn.dataset.tab);
      // Auto-close mobile dropdown after selecting
      const nav = document.getElementById("tabNav");
      if (nav) nav.classList.remove("open");
    });
  });
  // Hamburger toggle for narrow viewports
  const navToggle = document.getElementById("navToggle");
  if (navToggle) {
    navToggle.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const nav = document.getElementById("tabNav");
      if (nav) nav.classList.toggle("open");
    });
    // Click outside to close
    document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      const nav = document.getElementById("tabNav");
      if (nav && nav.classList.contains("open") && !nav.contains(e.target)) {
        nav.classList.remove("open");
      }
    });
  }
  // Any element with data-go-tab="..." also switches tab on click
  document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    const t = e.target.closest("[data-go-tab]");
    if (t) { e.preventDefault(); switchTab(t.dataset.goTab); }
  });

  // Place bet (Bet tab)
  const placeBtn = $("#betPagePlace");
  if (placeBtn) {
    placeBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const amt = parseInt($("#betPageAmount").value, 10);
      if (!amt || amt <= 0) {
        setSyncStatus("Invalid bet amount", "err");
        return;
      }
      if (!BET_PICK.kind || BET_PICK.target == null) return;
      const ok = placeBet(BET_PICK.kind, BET_PICK.target, amt);
      if (ok) {
        BET_PICK = { kind: null, target: null };
        placeBtn.disabled = true;
        renderAll();
      }
    });
  }

  // Modal triggers — League Office panel removed; equivalent data
  // is now available in the League tab. Leaving the show* functions
  // defined (callable programmatically) but no longer wired to a UI.

  // Autoplay toggle
  const apToggle = $("#autoplayToggle");
  if (apToggle) {
    apToggle.addEventListener("click", () => {
      AUTOPLAY.enabled = !AUTOPLAY.enabled;
      apToggle.classList.toggle("on", AUTOPLAY.enabled);
      toast(AUTOPLAY.enabled ? "Autoplay <b>ON</b> — game advances on its own." : "Autoplay <b>OFF</b> — countdown paused.", AUTOPLAY.enabled ? "info" : "warn");
    });
  }


  // Sync
  $("#btnPush").addEventListener("click", pushSave);
  $("#btnPull").addEventListener("click", pullSave);

  // Restore last sync URL/name from localStorage
  try {
    const u = localStorage.getItem("fk_syncUrl");
    const n = localStorage.getItem("fk_syncName");
    if (u) $("#syncCode").value = u;
    if (n) $("#syncName").value = n;
  } catch {}

  // Settings tab — bind range labels and Apply button
  bindSettings();
}

function bindSettings() {
  const ranges = [
    { id: "setRoundCooldown",  get: () => Math.round(AUTOPLAY.cooldownRoundMs  / 1000), label: v => v + "s" },
    { id: "setMatchCooldown",  get: () => Math.round(AUTOPLAY.cooldownMatchMs  / 1000), label: v => v + "s" },
    { id: "setSeasonCooldown", get: () => Math.round(AUTOPLAY.cooldownSeasonMs / 1000), label: v => v + "s" },
    { id: "setBestOf", get: () => MATCH.bestOf,
      label: v => v + " (first to " + Math.ceil(v / 2) + ")" },
    { id: "setLives",  get: () => MATCH.startingLives,
      label: v => v + (v === 1 ? " life" : " lives") },
  ];
  for (const r of ranges) {
    const el = $("#" + r.id);
    if (!el) continue;
    el.value = r.get();
    const valEl = $("#" + r.id + "Val");
    if (valEl) valEl.textContent = r.label(+el.value);
    el.addEventListener("input", () => {
      if (valEl) valEl.textContent = r.label(+el.value);
    });
  }
  const bracketSel = $("#setBracketFmt");
  if (bracketSel) {
    bracketSel.value = String((typeof LEAGUE_CONFIG !== "undefined" && LEAGUE_CONFIG.bracketSize) || 4);
  }

  // Game mode selector (v28). Populate from current LEAGUE_CONFIG.gameMode
  // and show/hide the mode-specific sub-options based on the active value.
  const modeSel = $("#setGameMode");
  const kothPointsSel = $("#setKothPoints");
  const ctfCapsSel = $("#setCtfCaps");
  const kothRow = $("#kothPointsRow");
  const ctfRow = $("#ctfCapsRow");
  function _updateModeSubRows() {
    const v = modeSel ? modeSel.value : "deathmatch";
    if (kothRow) kothRow.style.display = (v === "koth") ? "" : "none";
    if (ctfRow)  ctfRow.style.display  = (v === "ctf")  ? "" : "none";
  }
  if (modeSel) {
    modeSel.value = (LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG.gameMode) || "deathmatch";
    modeSel.addEventListener("change", _updateModeSubRows);
  }
  if (kothPointsSel && LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig) {
    kothPointsSel.value = String(LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.koth.pointsToWin || 50);
  }
  if (ctfCapsSel && LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig) {
    ctfCapsSel.value = String(LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.ctf.capsToWin || 5);
  }
  _updateModeSubRows();

  // Visual toggles — apply IMMEDIATELY on change (no need to hit Apply for
  // these, because they're cheap visual settings and the user wants to
  // see the effect right away). Persist via saveLocal.
  const toggles = [
    { id: "setDisableShake",    key: "disableShake"    },
    { id: "setDisableFieldFx",  key: "disableFieldFx"  },
    { id: "setDisableLighting", key: "disableLighting" },
    { id: "setDisableCrowd",    key: "disableCrowd"    },  // v28.4 stadium spectators
    { id: "setHighFrameRate",   key: "highFrameRate"   },  // v28.5 30/60fps cap
  ];
  for (const tog of toggles) {
    const el = $("#" + tog.id);
    if (!el) continue;
    el.checked = !!(LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG[tog.key]);
    el.addEventListener("change", () => {
      if (typeof LEAGUE_CONFIG !== "undefined") LEAGUE_CONFIG[tog.key] = el.checked;
      // Wipe the lingering shake transform if it was on when shake was disabled
      if (tog.key === "disableShake" && el.checked && typeof document !== "undefined") {
        const wrap = document.querySelector(".arena-wrap");
        if (wrap) wrap.style.transform = "";
      }
      // === v28.5: reset frame-rate accumulator on toggle ===
      // Without this, switching from 30→60 waits up to one frame for
      // the accumulator to roll over before the new cap takes effect.
      if (tog.key === "highFrameRate" && typeof loop !== "undefined") {
        loop._drawAcc = 0;
      }
      if (typeof saveLocal === "function") saveLocal();
    });
  }

  const applyBtn = $("#settingsApply");
  if (applyBtn) {
    applyBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      AUTOPLAY.cooldownRoundMs  = (+$("#setRoundCooldown").value)  * 1000;
      AUTOPLAY.cooldownMatchMs  = (+$("#setMatchCooldown").value)  * 1000;
      AUTOPLAY.cooldownSeasonMs = (+$("#setSeasonCooldown").value) * 1000;
      MATCH.bestOf = +$("#setBestOf").value;
      MATCH.roundsToWin = Math.ceil(MATCH.bestOf / 2);
      MATCH.startingLives = +$("#setLives").value;
      const newBracketSize = +($("#setBracketFmt")?.value || 4);
      const oldBracketSize = (typeof LEAGUE_CONFIG !== "undefined" && LEAGUE_CONFIG.bracketSize) || 4;
      if (typeof LEAGUE_CONFIG !== "undefined") LEAGUE_CONFIG.bracketSize = newBracketSize;

      // Game mode + per-mode config (v28)
      const newMode = ($("#setGameMode")?.value) || "deathmatch";
      const oldMode = (LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG.gameMode) || "deathmatch";
      if (LEAGUE_CONFIG) {
        LEAGUE_CONFIG.gameMode = newMode;
        if (LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig) {
          if (LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.koth) {
            LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.koth.pointsToWin = +($("#setKothPoints")?.value || 50);
          }
          if (LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.ctf) {
            LEAGUE_CONFIG.modeConfig.ctf.capsToWin = +($("#setCtfCaps")?.value || 5);
          }
        }
      }
      saveLocal();
      const msg = $("#settingsApplyMsg");
      if (msg) {
        msg.textContent = "✓ Applied — effective at next round/match/season.";
        setTimeout(() => { msg.textContent = ""; }, 4000);
      }
      if (typeof toast === "function") toast("Settings applied. New cadence takes effect on next event.", "info");
      if (newBracketSize !== oldBracketSize && typeof toast === "function") {
        toast(`Bracket size changing to ${newBracketSize} teams next season.`, "warn");
      }
      if (newMode !== oldMode && typeof toast === "function") {
        const modeNames = { deathmatch: "Deathmatch", koth: "King of the Hill", ctf: "Capture the Flag", tag: "Melee Tag" };
        toast(`Game mode changing to <b>${modeNames[newMode] || newMode}</b> at next match.`, "warn");
      }
    });
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   TOAST NOTIFICATION SYSTEM
   Bottom-right stack. Kinds: "win" (green), "lose" (red),
   "warn" / "info" (yellow / blue). Auto-dismisses after 6s.
   ============================================================ */
function toast(msg, kind, opts) {
  kind = kind || "info";
  opts = opts || {};
  const stack = $("#toastStack");
  if (!stack) return;
  const el = document.createElement("div");
  el.className = "toast " + kind;
  const icoMap = { win: "💸", lose: "💔", warn: "⚠️", info: "ℹ️" };
  el.innerHTML = `
    <span class="ico">${opts.ico || icoMap[kind] || "ℹ️"}</span>
    <div class="body">${msg}</div>
    <button class="close-x" aria-label="dismiss">×</button>
  `;
  const dismiss = () => {
    el.classList.add("fade-out");
    setTimeout(() => { if (el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el); }, 380);
  };
  el.querySelector(".close-x").addEventListener("click", dismiss);
  stack.appendChild(el);
  const ttl = opts.ttl || 6000;
  setTimeout(dismiss, ttl);
}
// Make available to the simulation layer
window.toast = toast;

/* ============================================================
   AUTO-PLAY — countdown between rounds/matches/seasons
   ============================================================ */
const AUTOPLAY = {
  enabled: true,
  cooldownRoundMs: 60000,   // 1 minute between rounds
  cooldownMatchMs: 60000,   // 1 minute between matches
  cooldownSeasonMs: 60000,  // 1 minute between seasons
};
// Pending auto-action: {kind: "round"|"match"|"season", remainingMs, action}
let AUTOPLAY_PENDING = null;

function scheduleAutoAdvance(kind, action) {
  const cd = kind === "round"  ? AUTOPLAY.cooldownRoundMs  :
             kind === "match"  ? AUTOPLAY.cooldownMatchMs  :
                                 AUTOPLAY.cooldownSeasonMs;
  AUTOPLAY_PENDING = { kind, remainingMs: cd, totalMs: cd, action };
  renderArenaHeader();
}
function skipCountdown() {
  if (!AUTOPLAY_PENDING) return;
  const a = AUTOPLAY_PENDING.action;
  AUTOPLAY_PENDING = null;
  try { a(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  renderAll();
}
function tickAutoplay(dt) {
  if (!AUTOPLAY_PENDING) return;
  if (!AUTOPLAY.enabled) return; // user turned it off — wait for click
  AUTOPLAY_PENDING.remainingMs -= dt;
  if (AUTOPLAY_PENDING.remainingMs <= 0) skipCountdown();
}

/* ============================================================
   FAST-FORWARD — catch up the sim by N ms of wall-clock time.
   Used when the user comes back to a tab they left running for a while:
   the league plays itself in the background, and on resume we replay
   what would have happened in deterministic lockstep.

   Hard-capped at 1 hour of wall-clock catch-up to prevent runaway
   replays after extended absences. Also bounded by a 5-second
   wall-clock budget (so a slow device doesn't freeze on resume).
   ============================================================ */
function fastForwardSim(ms) {
  if (ms <= 0) return { matchesPlayed: 0, simulatedMs: 0, capped: false };
  const cap = 60 * 60 * 1000; // 1 hour
  const capped = ms > cap;
  let remaining = Math.min(ms, cap);

  const wallStart = (typeof performance !== "undefined" && performance.now)
    ? performance.now() : Date.now();
  const wallBudget = 5000; // give up after 5s wall time so the page doesn't hang

  const startMatchCount = (LEAGUE.matchHistory || []).length;
  const startSeason = LEAGUE.season;

  let safety = 0;
  while (remaining > 0 && safety++ < 1000000) {
    // Wall-clock safety check
    const wallNow = (typeof performance !== "undefined" && performance.now)
      ? performance.now() : Date.now();
    if (wallNow - wallStart > wallBudget) {
      console.warn(`fastForwardSim: hit ${wallBudget}ms wall budget, stopping early (${remaining}ms unsimulated)`);
      break;
    }
