    if (LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.phase === "round") {
      // Active round — tick the sim
      tickMatch();
      remaining -= COMBAT.frameMs;
    } else if (AUTOPLAY_PENDING && AUTOPLAY.enabled) {
      // We're in a cooldown countdown — burn through it
      const stepMs = Math.min(remaining, AUTOPLAY_PENDING.remainingMs);
      AUTOPLAY_PENDING.remainingMs -= stepMs;
      remaining -= stepMs;
      if (AUTOPLAY_PENDING.remainingMs <= 0) {
        const action = AUTOPLAY_PENDING.action;
        AUTOPLAY_PENDING = null;
        try { action(); } catch (e) { console.error("autoplay action failed", e); break; }
      }
    } else {
      // No match, no cooldown — sim is idle (e.g., post-final, awaiting season)
      // Try to bootstrap
      if (!LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.currentSlot && typeof startMatch === "function") {
        startMatch();
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    matchesPlayed: ((LEAGUE.matchHistory || []).length - startMatchCount),
    seasonsPlayed: LEAGUE.season - startSeason,
    simulatedMs: Math.min(ms, cap) - Math.max(0, remaining),
    capped,
    unsimulated: Math.max(0, remaining),
  };
}
window.fastForwardSim = fastForwardSim;

/* ============================================================
   TAB SWITCHING — Game / Bet / Store / About
   ============================================================ */
function switchTab(tabId) {
  $$(".tab-content").forEach(el => el.classList.toggle("active", el.dataset.tab === tabId));
  $$(".nav-tabs button").forEach(btn => btn.classList.toggle("active", btn.dataset.tab === tabId));
  if (tabId === "bet" && typeof renderBetTab === "function") renderBetTab();
  if (tabId === "store" && typeof renderStore === "function") renderStore();
  if (tabId === "league" && typeof renderLeagueTab === "function") renderLeagueTab();
  if (tabId === "game" && typeof renderAll === "function") renderAll();
  if (tabId === "lore" && typeof bindLoreSubtabs === "function") bindLoreSubtabs();
}

/* Lore sub-tab navigation — switch between Histories / Bios / Diaries /
   etc. by toggling the .active class on the right .lore-section. The
   lore-tab buttons are inside the Lore tab content; we bind once on first
   visit (idempotent guard via a flag). */
let _loreSubtabsBound = false;
function bindLoreSubtabs() {
  if (_loreSubtabsBound) return;
  _loreSubtabsBound = true;
  const buttons = document.querySelectorAll(".lore-tab");
  buttons.forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const target = btn.dataset.loretab;
      document.querySelectorAll(".lore-tab").forEach(b =>
        b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.loretab === target));
      document.querySelectorAll(".lore-section").forEach(s =>
        s.classList.toggle("active", s.dataset.loresection === target));
      // Lazy-render biome previews on Stadiums tab activation. Each canvas
      // stores its own rendered flag so we don't redraw on every tab switch.
      if (target === "stadiums") {
        if (typeof renderStadiumLore === "function") renderStadiumLore();
        if (typeof renderStadiumPreviews === "function") renderStadiumPreviews();
      }
    });
  });
}

/* Render the live biome previews on each canvas in the Stadiums section.
   Idempotent: each canvas stores `dataset.rendered = "1"` after first draw
   so subsequent calls are no-ops. Drawing happens via drawBiomePreview()
   from the render module, which uses the same scatter+court routines as
   the live game (just on a small offscreen canvas, then downsampled). */
function renderStadiumPreviews() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined" || typeof drawBiomePreview !== "function") return;
  const canvases = document.querySelectorAll(".lore-stadium-preview");
  canvases.forEach(canvas => {
    if (canvas.dataset.rendered === "1") return;
    const id = canvas.dataset.biomeId;
    const biome = (typeof BIOME_BY_ID !== "undefined") ? BIOME_BY_ID[id] : null;
    if (!biome) return;
    drawBiomePreview(canvas, biome);
    canvas.dataset.rendered = "1";
  });
}
window.renderStadiumPreviews = renderStadiumPreviews;

/* === v28.10: Stadium Atlas with Effect picker ===
   The factory now produces 12 cities × 8 sports × 9 effects = 864
   stadium variations. Showing all of them at once would be excessive
   — instead we show the 96 "stock" stadiums (default effect) by
   default and provide a picker that re-renders the grid with any
   selected effect overlay. Each picker selection shows 96 cards
   grouped by city. */
function renderStadiumLore(effectFilter) {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  const grid = document.getElementById("loreStadiumGrid");
  if (!grid) return;
  if (typeof BIOMES === "undefined" || typeof CITY_PALETTES === "undefined") return;

  // Default to the 'default' effect on first call
  const activeEffect = effectFilter || grid.dataset.effectFilter || "default";
  grid.dataset.effectFilter = activeEffect;

  const html = [];

  // === Picker bar ===
  // One button per effect; the active one is highlighted.
  if (typeof EFFECT_TYPES !== "undefined") {
    html.push(`<div class="stadium-effect-picker" style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 6px; margin: 0 0 16px 0; padding: 10px 12px; background: var(--bg-1); border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 8px;">`);
    html.push(`<div style="width:100%; font-size: 12px; letter-spacing: 1.2px; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--mute); margin-bottom: 4px;">Effect Overlay</div>`);
    for (const ek of Object.keys(EFFECT_TYPES)) {
      const ef = EFFECT_TYPES[ek];
      const isActive = ek === activeEffect;
      const label = (ef.namePrefix || "Stock");
      const emoji = ef.emoji || "🏟️";
      html.push(`<button class="stadium-effect-btn" data-effect="${_escHtml(ek)}" style="
        padding: 6px 12px;
        background: ${isActive ? "var(--gold)" : "var(--bg-2)"};
        color: ${isActive ? "var(--bg-0)" : "var(--ink)"};
        border: 1px solid ${isActive ? "var(--gold)" : "var(--line)"};
        border-radius: 6px;
        font-size: 12px;
        font-weight: 600;
        cursor: pointer;
        transition: all 0.15s;
      ">${emoji} ${_escHtml(label)}</button>`);
    }
    html.push(`</div>`);
  }

  // === Filter biomes by effect ===
  const filtered = BIOMES.filter(b => (b.effectKey || "default") === activeEffect);

  // === Grid by city ===
  const cityKeys = Object.keys(CITY_PALETTES);
  for (const ck of cityKeys) {
    const city = CITY_PALETTES[ck];
    const cityBiomes = filtered.filter(b => b.cityKey === ck);
    if (!cityBiomes.length) continue;
    html.push(`<div class="lore-stadium-section">`);
    html.push(`<h4>${_escHtml(city.name)} <span style="color: var(--mute); font-weight: 400; font-size: 13px;">(${city.code} · ${cityBiomes.length} venues)</span></h4>`);
    html.push(`<p style="color: var(--mute); margin: 4px 0 12px 0; font-size: 13px;">${_escHtml(city.lore)}</p>`);
    for (const b of cityBiomes) {
      html.push(_stadiumCardHtml(b));
    }
    html.push(`</div>`);
  }

  grid.innerHTML = html.join("");
  grid.dataset.rendered = "1";

  // Wire up picker buttons — re-render grid with selected effect on click
  const btns = grid.querySelectorAll(".stadium-effect-btn");
  btns.forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const ek = btn.dataset.effect;
      // Clear rendered flag so canvases redraw fresh for new biomes
      grid.dataset.rendered = "0";
      renderStadiumLore(ek);
    });
  });

  // Trigger preview rendering on the new canvases
  if (typeof renderStadiumPreviews === "function") renderStadiumPreviews();
}
window.renderStadiumLore = renderStadiumLore;

function _stadiumCardHtml(b) {
  const ambient = b.ambient ? `${b.ambient} ambient` : "no ambient";
  const court   = b.courtType ? `${b.courtType} markings` : "open field";
  const meta    = `${b.cityCode || "neutral"} · ${court} · ${ambient}`;
  const sportLore  = b.sportLore  || "";
  const cityLore   = b.cityLore   || "";
  const effectLore = b.effectLore || "";
  return `
    <div class="lore-stadium-card">
      <canvas class="lore-stadium-preview" data-biome-id="${_escHtml(b.id)}" width="220" height="124"></canvas>
      <div>
        <h3>${b.emoji || "🏟️"} ${_escHtml(b.name)}</h3>
        <div class="meta">${_escHtml(meta)}</div>
        ${sportLore ? `<p>${_escHtml(sportLore)}</p>` : ""}
        ${effectLore && b.effectKey !== "default" ? `<p>${_escHtml(effectLore)}</p>` : ""}
        ${cityLore  ? `<p class="city-lore">${_escHtml(cityLore)}</p>` : ""}
      </div>
    </div>`;
}

function _escHtml(s) {
  if (s == null) return "";
  return String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}

/* Render the canon LEAGUE LEGENDS + LEGENDARY LOST LOOT sections inside
   the League tab. (These used to live in Settings; v19 moved them into
   League where they fit thematically.) Called from renderLeagueTab. */
function renderLeagueLegends() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined" || !document.getElementById) return;
  const grid = document.getElementById("leagueLegendsGrid");
  if (grid && typeof LEGACY_HEROES !== "undefined") {
    grid.innerHTML = LEGACY_HEROES.map(h => {
      const t = TEAM_BY_ID[h.teamId];
      return `
        <div class="settings-legend-card">
          <div class="settings-legend-head">
            <span class="emoji">${t?.emoji || "·"}</span>
            <div>
              <div class="nm">${escapeHTML(h.name)}</div>
              <div class="meta">${t?.short || "—"} · S${h.season} R${h.round} · ${escapeHTML(h.position)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="killer">⚔ ${escapeHTML(h.killer)}</div>
          <div class="career">${h.career.kills}K · ${h.career.deaths}D · streak ${h.career.longestStreak}</div>
          <div class="epitaph">${escapeHTML(h.epitaph)}</div>
        </div>`;
    }).join("");
  }
  const lootList = document.getElementById("leagueLegacyLootList");
  if (lootList && typeof LEGACY_LOOT !== "undefined") {
    const sorted = LEGACY_LOOT.slice().sort((a, b) => b.scavengedCount - a.scavengedCount);
    lootList.innerHTML = sorted.map(li => `
      <div class="settings-legend-loot">
        <div class="ico">${li.ico}</div>
        <div class="body">
          <div class="nm">${escapeHTML(li.name)}</div>
          <div class="chain">Carried by: ${li.lineage.map(escapeHTML).join(' <span class="arrow">→</span> ')}</div>
          <div class="epitaph">${escapeHTML(li.epitaph)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="count"><div class="big">${li.scavengedCount}×</div><div class="lbl">SCAV</div></div>
      </div>`).join("");
  }
}
window.renderLeagueLegends = renderLeagueLegends;

function renderLeagueTab() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined" || !document.getElementById) return;

  // Hero stats
  const oldest = TEAMS.reduce((min, t) => (t.founded < (min.founded || 9999) ? t : min), {});
  const subEl = document.getElementById("leagueHeroSub");
  if (subEl) {
    subEl.textContent = `${TEAMS.length} teams · founded ${oldest.founded || 2015} · ${LEAGUE.season > 1 ? (LEAGUE.season - 1) + " seasons completed" : "season 1 in progress"} · perma-death tournament sim`;
  }
  const heroStats = document.getElementById("leagueHeroStats");
  if (heroStats) {
    heroStats.innerHTML = '' +
      '<div class="league-hero-stat"><div class="lbl">Season</div><div class="val">' + LEAGUE.season + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="league-hero-stat"><div class="lbl">Matches</div><div class="val">' + ((LEAGUE.matchHistory || []).length) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="league-hero-stat"><div class="lbl">Casualties</div><div class="val">' + LEAGUE.graveyard.length + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="league-hero-stat"><div class="lbl">Lost Items</div><div class="val">' + ((LEAGUE.lostItems || []).length) + '</div></div>';
  }

  // Teams grid
  const sortedTeams = TEAMS.slice().sort((a, b) => {
    const ra = LEAGUE.records[a.id] || {}, rb = LEAGUE.records[b.id] || {};
    if ((rb.championships||0) !== (ra.championships||0)) return (rb.championships||0) - (ra.championships||0);
    if ((rb.wins||0) !== (ra.wins||0)) return (rb.wins||0) - (ra.wins||0);
    return (ra.losses||0) - (rb.losses||0);
  });
  const grid = document.getElementById("leagueTeamsGrid");
  if (grid) {
    grid.innerHTML = sortedTeams.map(t => {
      const r = LEAGUE.records[t.id] || {};
      const champStr = r.championships > 0 ? "🏆".repeat(Math.min(r.championships, 5)) : "";
      const motto = t.motto ? `<div class="league-team-motto">"${escapeHTML(t.motto)}"</div>` : "";
      const lore = t.lore ? `<div class="league-team-lore">${escapeHTML(t.lore)}</div>` : "";
      return `
        <div class="league-team-card" style="--team-color: ${t.color};">
          <div class="league-team-head">
            <div class="league-team-emoji">${t.emoji}</div>
            <div>
              <div class="league-team-name">${escapeHTML(t.name)}</div>
              <div class="league-team-meta">EST. ${t.founded || "—"} · ${t.short}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          ${motto}
          ${lore}
          <div class="league-team-record">
            <span class="stat-label">W</span><span class="stat-val">${r.wins||0}</span>
            <span class="stat-label">L</span><span class="stat-val">${r.losses||0}</span>
            <span class="stat-label">D</span><span class="stat-val">${r.draws||0}</span>
            <span style="margin-left:auto;" class="stat-trophy">${champStr}</span>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join("");
  }

  // Stats breakdown
  const statsTable = document.getElementById("leagueStatsTable");
  if (statsTable) {
    const rows = TEAMS.map(t => {
      const roster = LEAGUE.rosters[t.id] || [];
      const livingRoster = roster.filter(p => !p.deceased);
      const totalKills = roster.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.career.kills || 0), 0);
      const totalDeaths = LEAGUE.graveyard.filter(g => g.teamId === t.id).length;
      const totalScav = roster.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.career.scavenged || 0), 0);
      const longestStreak = Math.max(0, ...roster.map(p => p.career.longestStreak || 0));
      const r = LEAGUE.records[t.id] || {};
      return {
        team: t,
        wins: r.wins || 0, losses: r.losses || 0, draws: r.draws || 0,
        championships: r.championships || 0,
        kills: totalKills, deaths: totalDeaths, scavenged: totalScav,
        longestStreak, roster: livingRoster.length,
      };
    }).sort((a, b) => (b.championships - a.championships) || (b.wins - a.wins));

    const bestKills = Math.max(...rows.map(r => r.kills));
    const bestStreak = Math.max(...rows.map(r => r.longestStreak));
    const bestScav = Math.max(...rows.map(r => r.scavenged));

    statsTable.innerHTML =
      '<thead><tr>' +
        '<th>Team</th><th>W</th><th>L</th><th>D</th><th>🏆</th>' +
        '<th>Kills</th><th>Deaths</th><th>Scav.</th><th>Streak</th><th>Active</th>' +
      '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
      rows.map(r => `<tr>
        <td><div class="team-cell"><span style="font-size:18px;">${r.team.emoji}</span> <span style="color:${r.team.color};font-weight:700;">${r.team.short}</span></div></td>
        <td class="num">${r.wins}</td>
        <td class="num">${r.losses}</td>
        <td class="num">${r.draws}</td>
        <td class="num ${r.championships ? "num-best" : ""}">${r.championships}</td>
        <td class="num ${r.kills === bestKills && bestKills > 0 ? "num-best" : ""}">${r.kills}</td>
        <td class="num">${r.deaths}</td>
        <td class="num ${r.scavenged === bestScav && bestScav > 0 ? "num-best" : ""}">${r.scavenged}</td>
        <td class="num ${r.longestStreak === bestStreak && bestStreak > 0 ? "num-best" : ""}">${r.longestStreak}</td>
        <td class="num">${r.roster}/5</td>
      </tr>`).join("") +
      '</tbody>';
  }

  // Hall of Fallen Heroes
  const hall = document.getElementById("leagueHall");
  if (hall) {
    // Combine current-league graveyard with LEGACY_HEROES (canon seed data).
    // Current generation entries are sorted ABOVE legacy ones.
    // LIVE Hall — only knights from THIS league (legacy entries get
    // their own dedicated section below). v19 split: stops the redundant
    // "current + legacy mixed together" presentation.
    const livingFallen = LEAGUE.graveyard.map(g => {
      const rosterEntry = (LEAGUE.rosters[g.teamId] || []).find(p => p.name === g.name);
      return {
        ...g,
        career: rosterEntry?.career || { kills: 0, longestStreak: 0 },
        team: TEAM_BY_ID[g.teamId],
      };
    }).sort((a, b) => (b.career.kills - a.career.kills) || (b.season - a.season));

    if (!livingFallen.length) {
      hall.innerHTML = '<div class="league-hall-empty">No casualties yet in your league. The League Legends below are pre-seeded canon.</div>';
    } else {
      hall.innerHTML = livingFallen.slice(0, 60).map(h => `
        <div class="league-hall-card" data-team="${h.teamId}" data-name="${escapeHTML(h.name)}">
          <div class="name">${h.team ? h.team.emoji : "·"} ${escapeHTML(h.name)}</div>
          <div class="meta">${h.team?.short || "—"} · S${h.season} R${h.round || "?"} · ${escapeHTML(h.position || "")}</div>
          <div class="killer">⚔ slain by ${escapeHTML(h.killer || "?")}</div>
          <div class="career-line">${h.career.kills} kills · streak ${h.career.longestStreak}</div>
        </div>`).join("");

      hall.querySelectorAll(".league-hall-card").forEach(card => {
        card.addEventListener("click", () => {
          if (typeof openMemorialFor === "function") openMemorialFor(card.dataset.team, card.dataset.name);
        });
      });
    }
  }

  // LIVE Biggest Loot Losses — only this league's lost items.
  const lootEl = document.getElementById("leagueLootList");
  if (lootEl) {
    const liveLoot = (LEAGUE.lostItems || []).slice().sort((a, b) => b.scavengedCount - a.scavengedCount);

    if (!liveLoot.length) {
      lootEl.innerHTML = '<div class="league-loot-empty">No legendary items lost in your league yet. The canon entries above are seeded.</div>';
    } else {
      lootEl.innerHTML = liveLoot.slice(0, 25).map(li => `
        <div class="league-loot-card">
          <div class="ico">${li.ico || "·"}</div>
          <div class="body">
            <div class="name">${escapeHTML(li.name)}</div>
            <div class="lineage">Lost in S${li.season} ${escapeHTML(li.slot)} · last carried by <b>${escapeHTML(li.lostBy || "the void")}</b></div>
          </div>
          <div class="scav-count">
            <div class="big">${li.scavengedCount}×</div>
            <div class="lbl">SCAVENGED</div>
          </div>
        </div>`).join("");
    }
  }

  // Render the dedicated canon-legends sections (between Hall and Loot).
  if (typeof renderLeagueLegends === "function") renderLeagueLegends();

  // Render the performance trend chart (initial draw + bind controls)
  if (typeof renderTrendChart === "function") renderTrendChart();
}
window.renderLeagueTab = renderLeagueTab;

/* ============================================================
   PERFORMANCE TRENDS — adjustable-scope normalized line chart.

   Three scopes (window of matches considered):
     "last30"  — most recent 30 matches across all teams
     "season"  — only matches from LEAGUE.season
     "all"     — every recorded match in matchHistory

   Three metrics, all naturally bounded:
     "winrate"   — running cumulative win rate within the window (0–100%)
     "roundrate" — running cumulative round-win % within the window (0–100%)
     "cumwins"   — raw cumulative wins, normalized to (max across teams) → 0–100%

   For each team a separate line is plotted on a single axis. The x axis
   shows match index within the chosen window; the y axis is 0–100. Each
   team gets its canon color. The legend below the chart toggles team
   visibility (click a team name to hide that line).

   Implementation:
   - We walk LEAGUE.matchHistory once, in order, building a per-team
     running tally inside the chosen window. The series for each team
     is a list of {x, y} points, one point added every match the team
     participated in.
   - We then render the chart on a 2D canvas with a manual axis grid +
     line passes. Tooltips on hover show team + metric value at that
     match index.
   ============================================================ */
const TREND = {
  scope: "last30",   // "last30" | "season" | "all"
  metric: "winrate", // "winrate" | "roundrate" | "cumwins"
  hidden: new Set(), // team ids whose line is hidden via the legend
  _bound: false,
};

function _trendBuildSeries() {
  // Returns: { series: { teamId: [{x,y}, ...] }, windowSize, totalMatches }
  // - x is the 0-based match index within the chosen window
  // - y is the metric value at that point (0-100, already normalized)
  const hist = (typeof LEAGUE !== "undefined" && LEAGUE.matchHistory) || [];
  let windowed = hist;
  if (TREND.scope === "last30") {
    windowed = hist.slice(-30);
  } else if (TREND.scope === "season") {
    const cur = (typeof LEAGUE !== "undefined" ? LEAGUE.season : 1);
    windowed = hist.filter(m => m.season === cur);
  }

  const series = {};
  for (const t of TEAMS) series[t.id] = [];

  // Per-team running accumulators (within window only)
  const tally = {};
  for (const t of TEAMS) tally[t.id] = { played: 0, wins: 0, roundsWon: 0, roundsLost: 0 };

  windowed.forEach((m, idx) => {
    // Parse score "A-B"
    let scoreA = 0, scoreB = 0;
    if (typeof m.score === "string") {
      const parts = m.score.split("-").map(s => parseInt(s, 10) || 0);
      scoreA = parts[0] || 0; scoreB = parts[1] || 0;
    }
    const ta = m.teamA, tb = m.teamB;
    if (!tally[ta] || !tally[tb]) return;

    tally[ta].played++; tally[tb].played++;
    if (m.winner === ta) tally[ta].wins++;
    if (m.winner === tb) tally[tb].wins++;
    tally[ta].roundsWon  += scoreA; tally[ta].roundsLost += scoreB;
    tally[tb].roundsWon  += scoreB; tally[tb].roundsLost += scoreA;

    // Push a sample for each participating team at this point
    for (const id of [ta, tb]) {
      const t = tally[id];
      let y = 0;
      if (TREND.metric === "winrate") {
        y = t.played > 0 ? (t.wins / t.played) * 100 : 0;
      } else if (TREND.metric === "roundrate") {
        const tot = t.roundsWon + t.roundsLost;
        y = tot > 0 ? (t.roundsWon / tot) * 100 : 0;
      } else if (TREND.metric === "cumwins") {
        y = t.wins; // un-normalized; we'll normalize globally below
      }
      series[id].push({ x: idx, y });
    }
  });

  // For cumulative-wins metric, normalize so the max ever-seen across
  // teams maps to 100. (If everyone is at 0, the chart is just a flat
  // line at 0, which is correct and informative.)
  if (TREND.metric === "cumwins") {
    let maxW = 0;
    for (const id in series) {
      for (const p of series[id]) maxW = Math.max(maxW, p.y);
    }
    if (maxW > 0) {
      for (const id in series) {
        for (const p of series[id]) p.y = (p.y / maxW) * 100;
      }
    }
  }

  return { series, windowSize: windowed.length, totalMatches: hist.length };
}

function _trendDrawChart(canvas, data) {
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  const padL = 56, padR = 18, padT = 18, padB = 38;
  const plotW = W - padL - padR;
  const plotH = H - padT - padB;

  // Background
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)";
  ctx.fillRect(padL, padT, plotW, plotH);

  // Y-axis grid (0/25/50/75/100) + labels
  ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.06)";
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(220,220,220,0.55)";
  ctx.font = "11px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "right";
  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
    const y = padT + (plotH * i / 4);
    const v = 100 - i * 25;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(padL, y); ctx.lineTo(padL + plotW, y);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillText(v + "%", padL - 8, y);
  }

  // X-axis baseline
  ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(padL, padT + plotH);
  ctx.lineTo(padL + plotW, padT + plotH);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(padL, padT);
  ctx.lineTo(padL, padT + plotH);
  ctx.stroke();

  // X-axis labels — match index. With small windows show every match;
  // with larger ones thin out so labels don't overlap.
  const N = Math.max(1, data.windowSize);
  const xStep = plotW / Math.max(1, N - 1);
  let labelStride = 1;
  if (N > 6)  labelStride = Math.ceil(N / 6);
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(220,220,220,0.55)";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.textBaseline = "top";
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    if (i % labelStride !== 0 && i !== N - 1) continue;
    const x = padL + (N === 1 ? plotW / 2 : i * xStep);
    ctx.fillText("#" + (i + 1), x, padT + plotH + 6);
  }

  // Axis labels (rotated y-axis, plain x-axis)
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(180,180,180,0.7)";
  ctx.font = "10px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
  ctx.textAlign = "center";
  ctx.fillText("match # within window", padL + plotW / 2, H - 8);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(14, padT + plotH / 2);
  ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);
  const ylabel = TREND.metric === "winrate" ? "win rate %"
              : TREND.metric === "roundrate" ? "round win %"
              : "cumulative wins (normalized)";
  ctx.fillText(ylabel, 0, 0);
  ctx.restore();

  // Plot the lines
  // Draw shadow lines first (faint, for hidden teams) — well, we just skip
  // hidden ones entirely. Then for each visible team plot its series.
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.lineCap = "round";
  ctx.lineJoin = "round";

  // For each visible team draw its line
  for (const t of TEAMS) {
    if (TREND.hidden.has(t.id)) continue;
    const pts = data.series[t.id];
    if (!pts || !pts.length) continue;
    ctx.strokeStyle = t.color;
    ctx.beginPath();
    let started = false;
    for (const p of pts) {
      const px = padL + (N === 1 ? plotW / 2 : p.x * xStep);
      const py = padT + plotH - (p.y / 100) * plotH;
      if (!started) { ctx.moveTo(px, py); started = true; }
      else ctx.lineTo(px, py);
    }
    ctx.stroke();

    // Endpoint dot for visual emphasis
    const last = pts[pts.length - 1];
    if (last) {
      const px = padL + (N === 1 ? plotW / 2 : last.x * xStep);
      const py = padT + plotH - (last.y / 100) * plotH;
      ctx.fillStyle = t.color;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(px, py, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }
}

function _trendDrawLegend(legendEl, series) {
  if (!legendEl) return;
  legendEl.innerHTML = TEAMS.map(t => {
    const muted = TREND.hidden.has(t.id) ? " muted" : "";
    const pts = series[t.id] || [];
    const last = pts.length ? pts[pts.length - 1].y : 0;
    const label = TREND.metric === "cumwins"
      ? Math.round(last) + "%"
      : last.toFixed(0) + "%";
    return `<span class="trend-legend-item${muted}" data-tid="${t.id}">
      <span class="trend-legend-swatch" style="background:${t.color};"></span>
      <span class="trend-legend-emoji">${t.emoji}</span>
      <span>${t.short}</span>
      <span style="color:var(--mute);font-family:Courier New, monospace;font-size:10px;">${label}</span>
    </span>`;
  }).join("");
  legendEl.querySelectorAll(".trend-legend-item").forEach(el => {
    el.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const id = el.dataset.tid;
      if (TREND.hidden.has(id)) TREND.hidden.delete(id);
      else TREND.hidden.add(id);
      renderTrendChart();
    });
  });
}

function renderTrendChart() {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  const canvas = document.getElementById("trendCanvas");
  if (!canvas) return;

  // Bind controls (once)
  if (!TREND._bound) {
    TREND._bound = true;
    document.querySelectorAll("#trendScope .trend-btn").forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        TREND.scope = btn.dataset.scope;
        document.querySelectorAll("#trendScope .trend-btn").forEach(b =>
          b.classList.toggle("active", b === btn));
        renderTrendChart();
      });
    });
    document.querySelectorAll("#trendMetric .trend-btn").forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        TREND.metric = btn.dataset.metric;
        document.querySelectorAll("#trendMetric .trend-btn").forEach(b =>
          b.classList.toggle("active", b === btn));
        renderTrendChart();
      });
    });
  }

  const data = _trendBuildSeries();
  const empty = document.getElementById("trendEmpty");
  if (empty) empty.style.display = data.windowSize === 0 ? "flex" : "none";

  _trendDrawChart(canvas, data);
  _trendDrawLegend(document.getElementById("trendLegend"), data.series);

  const footer = document.getElementById("trendFooter");
  if (footer) {
    const scopeText = TREND.scope === "last30" ? "the last 30 matches"
                  : TREND.scope === "season"  ? `season ${LEAGUE && LEAGUE.season || 1}`
                  : "all-time history";
    footer.textContent = `Showing ${data.windowSize} match(es) from ${scopeText} · ${data.totalMatches} match(es) recorded total. Click a team in the legend to hide its line.`;
  }
}
window.renderTrendChart = renderTrendChart;

/* ============================================================
   MAIN GAME LOOP
   ============================================================ */
function loop(ts) {
  if (!lastTick) lastTick = ts;
  const dt = ts - lastTick;
  // Cap dt to avoid runaway simulation if the tab was backgrounded for a
  // long time. The proper catch-up is in boot(); the main loop just needs
  // to advance smoothly. 250ms is ~4 frames worth.
  const realDt = ts - (lastTick - dt);
  const cappedDt = Math.min(dt, 250);
  lastTick = ts;

  // === v28.5 FPS cap ===
  // Default: cap render at 30fps. Cuts draw work in half on devices that
  // run rAF at 60fps (most). The sim tick rate (20fps via tick
  // accumulator below) is independent — gameplay speed is identical
  // either way. Toggle via Settings → highFrameRate (60fps).
  // Sim ticks happen BEFORE the early-return so dropped frames don't
  // drop sim updates.
  const highFrameRate = (LEAGUE_CONFIG && LEAGUE_CONFIG.highFrameRate);
  const targetFrameMs = highFrameRate ? (1000 / 60) : (1000 / 30);

  // Auto-advance countdown ticks in real-time, regardless of match state
  tickAutoplay(cappedDt);

  if (SPEED > 0 && LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.match.phase === "round") {
    // Slow-mo: when slowMoFrames > 0 (set on the match-deciding KO), the
    // sim runs at 35% speed so the broadcast lingers on the moment.
    let effSpeed = SPEED;
    if (LEAGUE.match.slowMoFrames > 0) effSpeed *= 0.35;
    pendingTickAcc += cappedDt * effSpeed;
    while (pendingTickAcc >= COMBAT.frameMs) {
      tickMatch();
      pendingTickAcc -= COMBAT.frameMs;
      if (!LEAGUE.match || LEAGUE.match.phase !== "round") {
        pendingTickAcc = 0;
        break;
      }
    }
  } else {
    pendingTickAcc = 0;
  }

  // Track wall-clock activity for catch-up on next page load
  LEAGUE.lastTickAt = Date.now();

  // === FPS cap gate — drops draws but never ticks ===
  // Accumulate elapsed time toward target frame interval; only redraw
  // when at least targetFrameMs has passed since the last actual draw.
  loop._drawAcc = (loop._drawAcc || 0) + cappedDt;
  if (loop._drawAcc >= targetFrameMs) {
    loop._drawAcc = loop._drawAcc % targetFrameMs;  // preserve remainder
    drawArena();
    if (typeof applyShake === "function") applyShake();
    // Lightweight UI updates each frame
    renderArenaHeader();
    // Throttled full UI render (every ~250ms)
    if (!loop._lastFull || ts - loop._lastFull > 250) {
      loop._lastFull = ts;
      renderTeamPanel("A");
      renderTeamPanel("B");
      renderHeader();
    }
  }

  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

/* ============================================================
   PUBLIC API (for debugging / Perchance integration)
   ============================================================ */
window.FLOAT_KNIGHTS = {
  league: LEAGUE,
  startMatch, beginNextSeason, finalizeSeason,
  placeBet, buyItem, pushSave, pullSave,
  feed, renderAll,
  reset: () => {
    initLeague(); FEED.length = 0; LEAGUE.match = null; startMatch(); renderAll();
  },
};

/* ============================================================
   BOOTSTRAP
   ============================================================ */
/* ============================================================
   LOCAL PERSISTENCE — auto-save to localStorage on key events
   so progress, bits, bets, and rosters survive page reloads.
   Cloud sync (uploadPlugin) is still available for cross-device.

   v28.13: LOCAL_SAVE_KEY bumped from fk_save_v1 → fk_save_v13 to
   match the rewritten cloud-sync schema. Old fk_save_v1 entries are
   left untouched (the user can still recover them from localStorage
   manually if they want, but the game won't try to load them — they
   reference team IDs that no longer exist after the LY 9 continental
   expansion). On first boot under v28.13, we proactively clear the
   v1 key to free up storage quota.
   ============================================================ */
const LOCAL_SAVE_KEY = "fk_save_v13";
const LOCAL_SAVE_KEY_PRIOR = "fk_save_v1";
let _localSaveTimer = null;

// One-shot cleanup of the prior schema's localStorage entry. Idempotent —
// runs on every boot but only acts once (since after removal there's
// nothing left to remove).
try {
  if (localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_SAVE_KEY_PRIOR)) {
    localStorage.removeItem(LOCAL_SAVE_KEY_PRIOR);
    console.info("[v28.13] Cleared incompatible local save (fk_save_v1).");
  }
} catch {}

function saveLocal() {
  // Debounce — don't write more than once per 800ms during burst events
  if (_localSaveTimer) return;
  _localSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    _localSaveTimer = null;
    try {
      const json = serializeLeague();
      localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_SAVE_KEY, json);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("localStorage save failed:", e);
    }
  }, 800);
}

function loadLocal() {
  try {
    const json = localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_SAVE_KEY);
    if (!json) return false;
    deserializeLeague(json);
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    // deserializeLeague throws on incompatible schemas. Silently discard
    // the broken save — the user gets a fresh League on this boot and
    // future saves overwrite the corrupt entry.
    console.warn("[v28.13] localStorage save was incompatible, starting fresh:", e.message || e);
    try { localStorage.removeItem(LOCAL_SAVE_KEY); } catch {}
    return false;
  }
}

function clearLocal() {
  try { localStorage.removeItem(LOCAL_SAVE_KEY); } catch {}
}

// Expose so the simulation layer can call after every state change
window.saveLocal = saveLocal;

function boot() {
  bindEvents();
  let restored = false;
  try { restored = loadLocal(); } catch {}
  if (!restored) {
    initLeague();
    feed("season", `Season ${LEAGUE.season} begins. Bracket set.`);
    startMatch();
  } else {
    feed("season", `📂 Restored from local save — season ${LEAGUE.season}, ${LEAGUE.bits} bits.`);

    // CATCH UP — fast-forward the sim by however long the tab was closed
    const elapsed = Date.now() - (LEAGUE.lastTickAt || Date.now());
    if (elapsed > 5000 && (LEAGUE.match || LEAGUE.currentSlot)) {
      // Only fast-forward if a meaningful amount of time has passed (>5s)
      // AND we have something to advance.
      const summary = fastForwardSim(elapsed);
      if (summary.matchesPlayed > 0 || summary.seasonsPlayed > 0) {
        const minutes = Math.round(summary.simulatedMs / 60000);
        const parts = [];
        if (summary.seasonsPlayed > 0) parts.push(`${summary.seasonsPlayed} season${summary.seasonsPlayed === 1 ? "" : "s"}`);
        if (summary.matchesPlayed > 0) parts.push(`${summary.matchesPlayed} match${summary.matchesPlayed === 1 ? "" : "es"}`);
        const msg = `⏩ Caught up ~${minutes} min — ${parts.join(", ")} happened while you were away.`;
        feed("season", msg);
        if (typeof toast === "function") toast(msg, "info", { ico: "⏩", ttl: 9000 });
      } else if (summary.simulatedMs > 30000) {
        const minutes = Math.round(summary.simulatedMs / 60000);
        if (typeof toast === "function") toast(`⏩ Caught up ~${minutes} min of league time.`, "info", { ico: "⏩", ttl: 6000 });
      }
      if (summary.capped) {
        if (typeof toast === "function") toast(`Catch-up capped at 1 hour. Some events not replayed.`, "warn", { ttl: 8000 });
      }
    }

    if (typeof toast === "function") toast("Restored from local save", "info");
    if (!LEAGUE.match && LEAGUE.currentSlot) startMatch();
  }
  LEAGUE.lastTickAt = Date.now();
  renderAll();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
if (document.readyState !== "loading") boot();

</script>
